### [1] [A recursive self-improvement framework for electron microscopy analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32325)
*Yaotian Yang,Sijia Tao,Yuping Zhang,Jiangjie Qiu,Qinghua Zhang,Xiao Chen,Fei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhyEvo-Mic-RSI——一个物理引导（physics-guided）、专家反馈驱动的递归自我改进（recursive self-improvement）框架，将一次性人工修正转化为可复用、可验证的显微图像分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电子显微（electron microscopy）衬度中解析缺陷、界面等局域原子结构高度依赖研究者判断，导致效率低、标准主观。

Method: 通过带版本继承（versioned inheritance）地更新可执行算子、参数与路由规则，实现自我演进；在给定研究者定义任务下，系统无需专家标签即可依据图像特征选择分析路径，并在固定预算内测试与继承代码更新。

Result: 融合物理先验与专家反馈后，原子点缺陷检测（atomic point-defect detection）优于更大规模模型；跨材料界面覆盖（interface coverage）在10%面积预算内由5.13%提升至72.11%；应用于未见研究的显微图像时可分离相互接触的纳米颗粒（nanoparticles）。

Conclusion: 该工作推动计算显微学（computational microscopy）从孤立模型与静态脚本迈向可自我演进、能累积人类科学专业知识的平台。

Abstract: Resolving local atomic structures such as defects and interfaces from electron microscopy contrast relies heavily on researcher judgement, leading to low efficiency and subjective standards. Here we present PhyEvo-Mic-RSI, a physics-guided and expert-informed recursive self-improvement framework that turns one-off human corrections into reusable, verifiable capabilities by updating executable operators, parameters and routing rules with versioned inheritance. Given researcher-defined tasks, the system selects analysis paths from image features without expert labels, while testing and inheriting code updates under fixed budgets. Combining physical priors with expert feedback improves atomic point-defect detection over larger models and raises cross-material interface coverage from 5.13% to 72.11% within a 10% area budget. Adapting the framework to micrographs from unseen studies further separates touching nanoparticles by aligning optimization with particle-level measurements. This work moves computational microscopy beyond standalone models and static scripts towards self-evolving platforms that accumulate human scientific expertise

</details>

### [2] [Reliability of Language-Model Judges for Quantitative Scientific Question Answering: Evidence from Perovskite Photovoltaics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31269)
*Qiwei Han,Chi Zhou,Gaoxiang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在钙钛矿光伏（perovskite photovoltaics）定量合成基准上评测自动化模型评审（model judge）的可靠性。结果显示，自动评分高度依赖预计算的参考答案键；缺失参考时评分保真度显著崩塌，甚至颠倒系统排名，故物理科学中的模型评审须以结构化测量记录预计算的基准真值为前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强语言模型（retrieval-augmented language models）的评测日益依赖自动模型评审（automated model judges），但其在定量科学合成任务上的可靠性尚未被系统测量。该研究旨在量化此类评审在缺少参考真值时的可信度。

Method: 构建钙钛矿光伏定量基准，755 个参考答案（ground-truth answers）均由 45,441 条实验器件记录确定性地计算得出。对来自六种检索架构（retrieval architectures）的 4,530 条自由文本答案，在参考增强（reference-augmented）、证据增强（evidence-augmented）与无辅助（unassisted）三种条件下，评测一个前沿评审（Claude Opus）与两个本地开源权重评审（Qwen3-14B、Qwen3-8B）。

Result: 提供参考键时，前沿评审近乎完美复现真值（Cohen's κ=0.982±0.003）并保持架构排名（τ=1.00）。撤除参考后一致性骤降（κ=0.557±0.022），检索文献段落几乎无改善（κ=0.576±0.024）；无参考时本地评审接近随机（κ=0.087 与 0.112），排名出现倒置（τ≤0.20）。此外，SQL 增强生成引发评审盲区：无参考时前沿评审接受 46.6% 的错误 SQL 答案，将架构准确率高估为 66.4%（真实值 52.6%）。

Conclusion: 物理科学中的基于模型的评测（model-based evaluation）不能依赖非结构化文本检查；严谨的基准测试要求以结构化测量记录预计算的参考真值作为评分依据。

Abstract: Evaluating retrieval-augmented language models increasingly relies on automated model judges, yet their reliability on quantitative scientific synthesis remains largely unmeasured. Here, we evaluate automated grading on a quantitative benchmark for perovskite photovoltaics, where all 755 ground-truth answers are deterministically computed from 45,441 experimental device records. We evaluate a frontier judge (Claude Opus 5.5) alongside two local open-weight judges (Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-8B) across 4,530 free-text answers from six retrieval architectures under three regimes: reference-augmented (with the computed key), evidence-augmented (with retrieved passages and query logs), and unassisted (question and answer alone). Equipped with the reference key, Claude Opus reproduces ground truth near-perfectly (Cohen's $κ= 0.982 \pm 0.003$) and preserves exact architectural rankings ($τ= 1.00$). Withholding the reference causes evaluation fidelity to collapse: frontier agreement drops to $κ= 0.557 \pm 0.022$, and providing retrieved literature passages yields virtually no improvement ($κ= 0.576 \pm 0.024$). Without reference keys, local judges degrade to near-random agreement ($κ= 0.087$ and $0.112$), inverting the leaderboard to rank a hallucinating closed-book baseline first ($τ\le 0.20$). Furthermore, SQL-augmented generations induce severe evaluative blind spots: absent a reference, the frontier judge accepts 46.6% of erroneous SQL answers, overestimating architecture accuracy as 66.4% against its true 52.6%. These findings demonstrate that model-based evaluation in the physical sciences cannot rely on unstructured text inspection; rigorous benchmarking requires reference ground truth precomputed from structured measurement records.

</details>

### [3] [Exact nonstationary heat-excess statistics in the weakly asymmetric Kipnis--Marchioro--Presutti model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31303)
*Baruch Meerson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用宏观涨落理论（macroscopic fluctuation theory, MFT），精确求解了弱不对称 Kipnis–Marchioro–Presutti（KMP）模型中初始局域热脉冲所产生的过剩热量（heat excess）的完整长时间大偏差统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用多体系统中精确的非定常大偏差（large-deviation）统计结果仍然稀缺，尤其缺乏对非平衡、非平稳热输运涨落的解析刻画。

Method: 采用宏观涨落理论（MFT）框架，构造一个显著的非局域正则变换（nonlocal canonical transformation），将弱不对称 MFT 问题（连同其初始与终态条件）映射到此前已精确求解的对称问题上。

Result: 所得速率函数（rate function）是对称情形的显式非线性形变（nonlinear deformation）；解亦推广至在任意观测点之外测量热量的情形。

Conclusion: 该非局域正则变换实现了弱不对称与对称 MFT 问题之间的精确对应，从而给出非定常热涨落统计的显式解析结果，并具备向任意观测位置推广的灵活性。

Abstract: Exact nonstationary large-deviation statistics remain scarce for interacting many-body systems. Here we employ the macroscopic fluctuation theory (MFT) to determine the complete long-time statistics of heat excess produced by an initially localized heat pulse in the weakly asymmetric Kipnis--Marchioro--Presutti model. We find a remarkable nonlocal canonical transformation which maps the weakly asymmetric MFT problem, including its initial and terminal conditions, onto the symmetric problem, previously solved exactly. The resulting rate function is an explicit nonlinear deformation of its symmetric counterpart. We also extend the solution to heat measured beyond an arbitrary observation point.

</details>


### [4] [Condensates and Edge Effects in the Generalised Takayasu Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31325)
*Angad Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将一维 Takayasu 凝聚-扩散模型推广到一般化输入过程，并系统刻画电荷模型（CM）与配对注入模型（PIM）中团簇电荷的幂律分布、截断尺度、凝聚体形成及边界依赖的分布函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在一维 Takayasu 质量模型（MM）中观察到并精确刻画了大凝聚体与新颖幂律，作者希望将此类结果推广到一般化输入过程，并理解不同注入规则（单体注入与配对注入）下凝聚与幂律行为的差异。

Method: 提出将 MM 分布函数翻译到任意空间与时间无关注入过程的模型的关系式；并在系统尺寸为 L 的一维电荷模型（CM，±1 电荷单体等概率独立注入）与配对注入模型（PIM，相邻格点同时注入 +1 与 -1）中进行解析刻画与稳态、距离依赖分布分析。

Result: CM 中团簇电荷分布为 P(q)~|q|^{-5/3}，稳态在 t≫L² 时于 |q|~L^{3/2} 处截断，并形成净电荷介于 -√(Lt) 与 √(Lt) 的大凝聚体；距边界 x 处的分布从小团簇的 |q|^{-5/3} 过渡到 |q|>x^{3/2} 时的 |q|^{-7/3}。PIM 中分布为 P(q)~|q|^{-2}，稳态于 |q|~L 处截断且无大凝聚体生长；距最大团簇或开放边界 x 处，小团簇为 |q|^{-2}，大团簇（|q|>x）为 |q|^{-3}。

Conclusion: 边界与凝聚体均起到类似“边缘（edge）”的作用，导致距离依赖的分布函数；注入方式（独立单体注入 vs 配对注入）决定了幂律指数、截断尺度以及是否形成宏观凝聚体。

Abstract: Recently, in the 1D Takayasu Mass Model (MM) of diffusion-aggregation with input, large condensates and a novel power law were observed in simulations, and later characterised exactly. We extend these studies to the Takayasu model with generalised input, and provide a relation to translate the distribution function of the MM to any model with a space and time independent injection process. In the 1D Charge Model (CM) of a system of size $L$, where $q=+1$ and $-1$ charge monomers are injected with equal probability at each site in each time step, there is a power law distribution of cluster charge $P(q)\sim |q|^{-5/3}$. This distribution cuts off at $|q|\sim L^{3/2}$ in the steady state for long times $t\gg L^2$ and a large charge condensate forms with a net charge between $-\sqrt{Lt}$ and $\sqrt{Lt}$. A distance dependent distribution function is observed around the charge condensate and near open boundaries, both of which act like an edge in the system, analogous to the observations in the MM. The charge distribution measured at a distance $x$ from the edge transitions from the previously known CM bulk power law $P(q)\sim |q|^{-5/3}$ for small clusters ($|q|< x^{3/2}$), to a novel power law $P(q)\sim |q|^{-7/3}$ for larger clusters ($|q|>x^{3/2}$). In the 1D Pair Injection Model (PIM), where $+1$ and $-1$ charge monomers are injected simultaneously at adjacent sites, the power law distribution $P(q)\sim |q|^{-2}$ cuts off at $|q|\sim L$ in the steady state for long times, and no large growing condensate is formed. We still observe a distance dependent distribution measured from the largest clusters, or an open boundary, with a bulk power law $P(q)\sim |q|^{-2}$ for small clusters ($|q|< x$), and an edge power law $P(q)\sim |q|^{-3}$ for larger clusters ($|q >x$).

</details>


### [5] [What time is it in Assembly Space? Cronin and Walker's "The Physics of Causation" fails elementary dimensional analysis (Comment on arXiv:2601.00515)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31339)
*Philip Moriarty*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文批评Cronin与Walker的"组装指数（assembly index）"理论存在量纲（dimensional）不一致：将无量纲计数与"连续时间"等同，且未提供将指数换算为时钟时间的时标（timescale），据此论证组装理论无法刻画因果关系（causation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者意在检验组装理论（assembly theory）对因果关系的量化主张是否成立，特别是其将"组装指数"与"由时钟测量的连续时间"相等同的做法是否自洽。

Method: 采用概念与量纲分析（dimensional analysis）的论证方式，指出原文将同一参数同时用作速率（rate）、时间间隔（time interval）与纯数（pure number），并考察其组装方程（assembly equation）中指数加权（exponential weighting）的来源。

Result: 分析表明：指数加权只能通过引入原文从未给出的时标获得，否则等价于把时钟时间重铸为无量纲的组装步数计数，此时所谓"推导"退化为伯努利（Bernoulli）十七世纪的复利极限（compound-interest limit）。

Conclusion: 由于缺乏速率或能量尺度（energy scale），该理论无法预测对象形成的快慢与条件，而"有限时间内形成（formation in finite time）"恰是其区分自发与"被选择"对象的标准，故组装理论无法处理因果关系问题。

Abstract: Cronin and Walker claim that causation is quantified by an ``assembly index'', a count of the steps needed to build an object. Remarkably, they equate ``continuous time (as measured by clocks)'' to a multiple of the index, which is a dimensionless number. Their argument treats one parameter as simultaneously a rate, a time interval, and a pure number, and provides no timescale to convert index to clock time. This dimensional incoherence betrays, at best, an ill-conceived and ill-formed theory, and fatally undermines two central claims. First, the exponential weighting at the heart of what they call the assembly equation can be obtained only by supplying a timescale, which they never do, or by recasting clock time as a dimensionless count of assembly steps. In the latter case, their ``derivation'' trivially reduces to Bernoulli's seventeenth-century compound-interest limit. Second, lacking rates or energy scales, their account cannot predict how quickly, or under what conditions, anything forms, and yet formation ``in finite time'' is their criterion for distinguishing spontaneous from ``selected'' objects. Assembly theory is thus incapable of addressing causation.

</details>


### [6] [Localization in one-dimensional magnetic quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31384)
*Andrew Osborne,Rhine Samajdar,Andrew A. Houck*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维准周期外场晶格中图拓扑如何调控金属—绝缘体转变，展示临界局域化、指数局域化、平带共存及仅由图结构产生的可解迁移率边，并揭示相互作用驱动局域态趋向扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Aubry-André-Harper 模型（Aubry-André-Harper model）的金属—绝缘体转变源于最近邻跃迁与准周期在位势之间的能量竞争。本文旨在探究底层图（graph）拓扑如何介导绝缘相与导电相之间的竞争，从而超越单纯能量竞争的图像。

Method: 构建受准周期外场（quasiperiodic external field）作用的一维晶格模型，通过调节底层图拓扑设计不同局域化行为；并采用玻色子动力学（bosonic dynamics）的精确对角化（exact-diagonalization）计算考察在位相互作用（on-site interactions）的影响。

Result: 给出所有色散本征态（dispersive eigenstates）呈临界局域化（critically localized）的实例，以及本征态呈指数局域化（exponentially localized）的模型；证明上述现象可与构成平带（flat band）的大量简并有限支撑本征态共存；并构造出仅由图结构产生、可精确求解的迁移率边（mobility edges）。

Conclusion: 与常规 Aubry-André-Harper 情形类似，在位相互作用驱动局域本征态趋向扩展（extendedness）。该工作为研究磁干涉（magnetic interference）与相互作用如何共同约束准周期晶格中的输运提供了框架。

Abstract: The celebrated Aubry-André-Harper model undergoes a metal-insulator transition akin to Anderson localization due to an energetic competition between nearest-neighbor hopping and a quasiperiodic on-site potential. In this work, we consider the problem of a one-dimensional lattice subject to a quasiperiodic external field, where the topology of the underlying graph mediates the competition between insulating and conducting phases. In particular, we present examples for which all dispersive eigenstates are critically localized, as well as models in which they are exponentially localized. We also demonstrate the coexistence of these phenomena with an extensive set of degenerate finitely supported eigenstates forming a flat band. Finally, we construct models with exactly solvable mobility edges arising only from graph structure. Going beyond these single-particle results, we show---using exact-diagonalization calculations of the bosonic dynamics---that, as in the conventional Aubry-André-Harper case, on-site interactions drive localized eigenstates toward extendedness. Our results provide a framework for exploring how magnetic interference and interactions jointly constrain transport in quasiperiodic lattices.

</details>


### [7] [Upper and lower bounds for the critical point of the three-dimensional Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31872)
*Zhongpu Qiu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明三维立方格（Z^3）上最近邻伊辛铁磁体（Ising ferromagnet）的临界点（critical point）满足 0.2164203058 < K_c < 0.2293910295，端点由显式有理数给出，是目前最锐利的严格界之一，并将经典界收窄约 3.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典严格界（Fisher 1967；Fröhlich–Simon–Spencer 1976）给出的区间较宽，需要更锐利的严格上下界，并使严格区间包含数值估计值。

Method: 下界改进 Fisher 的自避走（self-avoiding-walk）论证：利用最短路径禁阻其顶点张成键，以局部关联积分（local correlation integral）与有限记忆转移界（finite-memory transfer bound）沿路径求和；上界证明系数含最近邻关联的红外界（infrared bound），保留零傅里叶模为未知量并用小壳层自旋的精确局部关系强制其为正。

Result: 得到 0.2164203058 < K_c < 0.2293910295（等价 1.541630483 < e^{2K_c} < 1.582145851），区间宽度较经典结果缩小约 3.5 倍且包含数值估计；副产品是 Z^3 的连接常数（connective constant）至多为 4.73195。

Conclusion: 有限不等式以精确有理数算术验证，热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）的过渡经解析证明，从而给出 Z^3 伊辛模型临界点的严格、可验证且目前最锐利的区间估计。

Abstract: We prove that the critical point $K_c$ of the nearest-neighbour Ising ferromagnet on $\mathbb{Z}^3$ satisfies $0.2164203058<K_c<0.2293910295$, equivalently $1.541630483<e^{2K_c}<1.582145851$, with both endpoints given by explicit rational numbers. To our knowledge these are the sharpest rigorous bounds available. They improve the classical bounds of Fisher (1967) and of Fröhlich, Simon and Spencer (1976), narrowing the rigorously known interval by a factor of about 3.5, and the interval contains the numerical estimate of the critical point. For the lower bound we refine Fisher's self-avoiding-walk argument. A shortest path in the high-temperature expansion forbids all bonds spanned by its vertices, and the resulting gain is estimated through local correlation integrals and summed over paths by a finite-memory transfer bound. As a by-product, the connective constant of $\mathbb{Z}^3$ is shown to be at most $4.73195$. For the upper bound we prove an infrared bound whose coefficient involves the nearest-neighbour correlation. We keep the zero Fourier mode as an unknown and force it to be positive by exact local relations for the spins on a small shell. The finite inequalities entering the proof are verified in exact rational arithmetic, and the passage to the thermodynamic limit is proved analytically.

</details>


### [8] [From Growth Inhibition to Fluctuations: Testing an Ising Model of Bacterial Responses to Antibiotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31609)
*Pietro Cicuta,Morten Kals,Nir S. Gov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究以三种细菌对多种抗生素的生长响应为实验体系，检验生化反应网络可映射为有效伊辛模型（Ising model）的假说，发现伊辛模型在描述种群生长速率异质性（PGRH）上显著优于传统Hill模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论提出复杂生化反应网络可展现出由少量参数编码集体相互作用的涌现协同性（emergent cooperativity），并可用有效伊辛模型描述，但该映射此前缺乏实验检验。

Method: 测量三种细菌对不同细胞靶点抗生素的响应，将伊辛描述与常规Hill模型（Hill model）进行对比，同时考察平均生长速率响应与种群生长速率异质性（population growth-rate heterogeneity, PGRH）两方面。

Result: 两种模型对平均生长速率随抗生素浓度变化的拟合效果相当，但伊辛模型对PGRH的描述明显更优；拟合得到的伊辛相互作用强度与Hill指数系统相关，并随抗生素靶点距蛋白质生产机器的功能距离增加而增大。

Conclusion: 结果为用有效协同伊辛相互作用描述复杂生化反应网络提供了实验支持，并提示细菌生长对抗生素的集体响应反映了其底层代谢网络的组织结构。

Abstract: Complex networks of biochemical reactions have been proposed to exhibit emergent cooperativity that can be described by an effective Ising model, with a small number of parameters encoding collective interactions. Here this mapping is experimentally tested using the response of three bacterial species to a range of antibiotics acting on different cellular targets. The Ising description is compared to the conventional Hill model, examining both the mean growth-rate response and, importantly, its population growth-rate heterogeneity (PGRH). While both models describe the mean growth-rate dependence on antibiotic concentration comparably well, we find that the Ising model provides a markedly better description of PGRH, offering an additional and more stringent test of the collective description. The fitted Ising interaction strength is systematically related to the Hill exponent and increases with the functional distance of the antibiotic target from the protein-production machinery. These results provide experimental support for describing complex biochemical reaction networks through effective cooperative Ising interactions, and suggest that the collective response of bacterial growth to antibiotics reflects the organization of the underlying metabolic network.

</details>


### [9] [Classifying exact local-equilibrium solutions of the Boltzmann equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32468)
*Nagi Khalil,Jorge Bravo-Gadea*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文在 \(d\) 维欧氏空间（Euclidean space）中，对 Boltzmann 动理学方程（Boltzmann kinetic equation）存在精确局部平衡分布（local equilibrium distribution）解的所有流体力学状态（hydrodynamic states）与外场情形给出完整分类：温度须空间均匀，速度场为刚性平动、径向膨胀/压缩与刚性转动的叠加，外力加速度须为保守场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部平衡分布通常仅被视为动理学（kinetic）与流体力学（hydrodynamic）近似的基础工具，其作为 Boltzmann 方程精确解成立的条件此前缺乏系统刻画；本文旨在明确这些例外情形并给出严格的判定准则。

Method: 采用解析分类方法（analytic classification）：在 \(d\) 维欧氏空间中求解使局部平衡分布精确满足 Boltzmann 动理学方程的温度场、速度场与外加速度所需满足的条件，并由此确定密度剖面与时间演化。

Result: 温度 \(T\) 必须空间均匀；速度场为刚性平动（rigid translation）、径向膨胀或压缩（radial expansion or compression）与刚性转动（rigid rotation）之和；外加速度须为保守的，但在随流体平动与转动的参考系中可含时间依赖。密度剖面由外势确定，其时间依赖由可调温度函数 \(T(t)\) 支配。由此重现经典“谐振呼吸子”（harmonic breather），并获得涉及静力、呈类似 Haff 定律（Haff's law）的代数冷却（algebraic cooling）的解，尽管系统中存在弹性碰撞（elastic collisions）。

Conclusion: 该分类明确了局部平衡精确成立的特殊情形，并为动理学与流体力学近似（kinetic and hydrodynamic approximations）的评估提供严格准则。

Abstract: We provide a complete classification, in a \(d\)-dimensional Euclidean space, of all hydrodynamic states and external forces for which a local equilibrium distribution constitutes an exact solution to the Boltzmann kinetic equation. We demonstrate that the temperature \(T\) must be spatially uniform, that the velocity field is the sum of a rigid translation, a radial expansion or compression, and a rigid rotation, and that the external acceleration must be conservative, though time-dependent within a reference frame that translates and rotates along with the fluid. The density profile is then determined by the external potential, while its temporal dependence is governed by any temperature function \(T(t)\) that can be generated through an appropriate adjustment of the external acceleration. We recover the classic ``harmonic breather'' and obtain solutions involving static forces that exhibit algebraic cooling, similar to Haff's law, despite the occurrence of elastic collisions. Our classification identifies the exceptional circumstances under which local equilibrium is exact and provides rigorous criteria for evaluating kinetic and hydrodynamic approximations.

</details>


### [10] [Exact Response Theory based on Karhunen-Loève Expansions for Brownian Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32038)
*Matteo Colangeli,Lamberto Rondoni,Pasquale Vozza*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将精确响应理论（Exact Response Theory, ERT）推广至由热白噪声驱动的过阻尼布朗振子（overdamped Brownian oscillator），借助 Karhunen–Loève 级数与谐波辅助相位构造增广相空间，得到耗散函数并分析方差响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确响应理论（ERT）此前主要针对确定性或特定动力学系统建立，其在随机强迫、尤其是过阻尼噪声驱动系统中的应用尚不清晰，需要给出一个可操作的构造框架。

Method: 以 Karhunen–Loève 级数（Karhunen–Loève series）表示随机强迫，并用谐波辅助相位（harmonic auxiliary phase）将其嵌入自治的增广相空间（augmented phase space）；在固定模式幅值下构造 ERT 耗散函数（dissipation function），再对幅值与初始相位平均以恢复随机平均量。

Result: 在有限谱截断下推导了方差的响应；解析表明，在均匀相位采样下，全谱白噪声极限与基于生成元（generator-based）的突然温度变化结果一致；与一阶线性响应理论（first-order linear response theory）的比较显示后者一般会失效，若噪声诱导方差对噪声幅值呈二次依赖，则线性响应完全遗漏该贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作为过阻尼布朗振子提供了 ERT 分析框架，并可推广至更一般的强迫形式；对非马尔可夫动力学（non-Markovian dynamics）的扩展则需另行开展收敛性与正则性分析。

Abstract: We extend Exact Response Theory (ERT) to an overdamped Brownian oscillator driven by thermal white noise. The stochastic forcing is represented by a Karhunen--Loève series and embedded into an autonomous, augmented phase space using a harmonic auxiliary phase. This representation allows us to construct the ERT dissipation function for fixed mode amplitudes and recover stochastic averages by averaging over the amplitudes and the initial phase. For the variance, we derive the response at finite spectral truncation and show analytically that, with uniform phase sampling, the full-spectrum white-noise limit reproduces the generator-based result for a sudden temperature change. Furthermore, a comparison with first-order linear response theory highlights how this may fail in general. In particular, if the noise-induced variance is quadratic in the noise amplitude, it is completely omitted by linear response. The analysis provides a framework for this model and for studying more general forcing; extensions to non-Markovian dynamics require separate convergence and regularity analyses.

</details>


### [11] [Network topology imposes spectral constraints on collective nonequilibrium responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32130)
*Dingjie Wu,Yaozu Wang,Rong Wang,Ying Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文基于微观可逆性原理与网络上的线性朗之万动力学，提出短时互相关不对称性与稳态热流的模式分解，揭示非平衡的两类来源，并给出由加权拉普拉斯算子谱决定的不平衡上下界及脑结构连接组的拓扑优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体非平衡动力学（collective nonequilibrium dynamics）同时依赖网络拓扑（network topology）与随机驱动（stochastic driving），但拓扑在随机驱动响应与非平衡涌现中的作用尚需系统理解。

Method: 从微观可逆性（microscopic reversibility）与网络上的线性朗之万动力学（linear Langevin dynamics）出发，对短时互相关的不对称性与稳态热流（heat currents）作模式分解（mode decomposition）；对具有独立局域驱动的互易网络（reciprocal networks）推导两个非平衡响应算子（nonequilibrium response operators），并分析其谱性质。

Result: 分解显示非平衡源于单模式涨落与不同模式间相关涨落两类机制；所得响应算子为与随机驱动无关的加权拉普拉斯算子（weighted Laplacians），其谱在固定驱动异质性下决定非平衡的可达上下界——不对称强度在任意偏离平衡处精确成立，熵产生率（entropy production rate）在近平衡时领先阶成立；人脑结构连接组（structural connectomes）的层级模块化组织拓宽了非平衡工作范围，静息态脑活动支持维持较低非平衡水平的拓扑优势。

Conclusion: 研究为理解生物网络组织及设计具有目标非平衡工作点的网络提供了基础。

Abstract: Collective nonequilibrium dynamics in Langevin systems depend on both network topology and stochastic driving. Understanding the role of topology in responses to stochastic driving and in the emergence of nonequilibrium is essential. Starting from the principle of microscopic reversibility and linear Langevin dynamics on networks, we derive a mode decomposition for both the asymmetry of short-time cross-correlations and heat currents at steady state. This decomposition reveals two sources of nonequilibrium---fluctuations of individual modes and correlated fluctuations between distinct modes. For reciprocal networks with independent local driving, we derive two nonequilibrium response operators that yield explicit expressions for the global asymmetry intensity and the entropy production rate. These operators are weighted Laplacians independent of stochastic driving. Their spectra determine attainable upper and lower bounds on nonequilibrium at fixed driving heterogeneity. These bounds are exact for asymmetry intensity at arbitrary deviations from equilibrium and hold to leading order near equilibrium for entropy production rate. Finally, we find that the hierarchical modular organization of human structural connectomes broadens the brain's nonequilibrium operating range, with resting-state brain activity supporting this topological advantage for sustaining a lower nonequilibrium level. Our study provides a basis for understanding biological network organization and designing networks with targeted nonequilibrium operating points.

</details>


### [12] [Dynamic scaling behaviors induced by periodic drivings at continuous Ising quantum transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32301)
*Andrea Pelissetto,Davide Rossini,Ettore Vicari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在重整化群框架下建立周期驱动量子多体系统（quantum many-body systems）在连续量子相变（continuous quantum phase transitions）附近的非平衡标度理论，区分破坏与保持 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性的两类驱动协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期驱动（periodic driving）在零温连续量子相变附近诱导的非平衡标度行为（out-of-equilibrium scaling behaviors），并厘清不同驱动对称性对临界标度的影响。

Method: 以量子 Ising 链（受周期纵向场）与 Kitaev 费米子线（周期化学势，等价于周期横向场 Ising 链）为范例，在重整化群（renormalization-group, RG）框架下构建有限尺寸与热力学极限下的动态标度理论（dynamic scaling theory），识别与驱动幅度和周期相关的标度变量（scaling variables），并区分破坏与保持全局 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性的驱动协议。

Result: 两类驱动呈现不同的标度行为，其幂律指数（power-law exponents）由相应微扰的 RG 维度决定；并通过平滑单频驱动与不连续方波（square-wave）周期驱动的数值模拟验证所提出的动态标度理论。

Conclusion: 周期驱动诱导的非平衡临界标度可由 RG 维度和对称性分类统一刻画，动态标度理论在有限尺寸与热力学极限下、对不同驱动波形均得到数值支持。

Abstract: We investigate the out-of-equilibrium scaling behaviors induced by periodic driving protocols in quantum many-body systems near continuous quantum phase transitions at zero temperature. We consider two paradigmatic one-dimensional systems: the quantum Ising chain subject to periodic longitudinal fields and the Kitaev fermionic wire with periodically driven chemical potentials, equivalent to a quantum Ising chain with a periodic transverse field. Within a renormalization-group (RG) framework, we develop a dynamic scaling theory for both finite-size systems and the thermodynamic limit, identifying scaling variables associated with the driving amplitude and period. We distinguish between protocols that break the global ${\mathbb Z}_2$ symmetry by coupling to the order parameter and those that preserve it. These two classes of driving exhibit distinct scaling behaviors, with power-law exponents determined by the RG dimensions of the corresponding perturbations. We verify the proposed dynamic scaling theory through numerical simulations of both smooth single-frequency and discontinuous square-wave periodic drivings.

</details>


### [13] [On fluctuation properties of the cumulative current in gTASEP on open chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32495)
*N. C. Pesheva,N. Zh. Bunzarova,E. K. Mitsova,A. M. Povolotsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文报告广义完全非对称简单排他过程（gTASEP）在开放链、Liggett 边界条件下累积电流涨落性质的初步数值研究结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定累积电流 J_T 的二阶标度累积量（second scaled cumulant）C2 在长时间极限下对系统尺寸 L 的依赖关系，并比较开放与周期两种几何构型。

Method: 对含粒子聚集控制附加项的 gTASEP 进行大规模数值模拟（numerical simulations），计算时间积分电流 J_T 的二阶标度累积量 C2 随 L 的变化。

Result: 最大电流相（maximum current phase）中的 C2 初步结果与关于 gTASEP 在链段上具有 Kardar-Parisi-Zhang 普适性（universality）的理论预测相符。

Conclusion: gTASEP 的电流涨落在最大电流相下与 KPZ 普适类预测一致；结果为初步性质，开放与周期几何的完整比较有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We present preliminary results of our numerical study of the fluctuation properties of the cumulative current, JT, in the generalized totally asymmetric simple exclusion process (gTASEP) on open chains with Liggett boundary conditions. GTASEP is the ordinary TASEP (a model of 1D- traffic on a single track, having many diverse applications), where an additional term controlling clustering of the particles is added. Extensive numerical simulations are performed to determine the dependence of the second scaled cumulant C2 of the time integrated current, JT, on the system size, L, in the long-time limit, allowing for comparison between open and periodic geometries. Preliminary results for C2 in the maximum current phase show agreement with theoretical predictions about the Kardar-Parizi-Zhang universality of the gTASEP on a segment.

</details>


### [14] [Lattice models of chiral random motion: A short review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32743)
*E. Kalz,J. Wojcik,N. Sherf,Y. Roichman,R. Metzler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该综述指出，在晶格上实现手性（chirality）需要记忆（memory），并以近期提出的手性随机行走（chiral random walk）模型为模板，用内部自由度描述行走者，从而将持续性行走、手性行走、run-and-tumble 运动及循环随机运动统一到同一框架中，并讨论了记忆如何转移到环境与路径层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性（即系统性旋转偏置）广泛存在于细菌游动、旋转胶体、磁场下带电粒子扩散等随机运动模式中，且在二维下使随机输运呈现“奇异（odd）”性质；然而自1960年以来不同领域多次独立引入带旋转偏置的晶格行走模型，却缺乏共同语言与概念联系。

Method: 采用综述与概念统一的路径：以晶格模型为可精确求解的研究平台，以近期提出的手性随机行走模型为模板，为行走者赋予内部自由度（internal degree of freedom），并施加手性偏置更新规则（chiral biased updating rule），据此推导其完整扩散张量（diffusion tensor）。

Result: 在该图像下，手性行走者的完整扩散张量变为奇异（odd），即获得反对称部分——其不改变均方位移（mean-squared displacement）但会修改通量与边界流；同时，持续性行走与手性行走、run-and-tumble 运动及循环随机运动可在该框架内统一描述。

Conclusion: 记忆可进一步被“外化”：进入环境层面体现为 Lorentz 晶格气体（Lorentz lattice gases）、动力学行走（kinetic walks）、转子行走（rotor walks）与随机奇异环境；进入路径层面则体现为螺旋自回避行走（spiral self-avoiding walks）。该视角为长期分散于不同学科的手性晶格行走模型提供了统一的概念语言。

Abstract: Chirality, a systematic rotational bias, is ubiquitous in many random motion patterns, from bacteria cells, that swim in circles near surfaces and spinning colloids, to charged particles diffusing under the influence of magnetic fields. In two dimensions, chirality renders random transport \textit{odd}: the diffusion tensor acquires an antisymmetric part that leaves the mean-squared displacement invariant but modifies fluxes and boundary currents. Lattice models for random motion are a natural setting to study chirality, since they are exactly solvable, connect directly to heterogeneous media, cellular automata, and programmable robots, and in discrete time admit fully chiral limits with a localised bulk and edge transport. Lattice walks with a rotational bias have been introduced in different communities several times since 1960, yet without a common language or conceptual connection between them. Here we review these models from the perspective that on the lattice, chirality requires memory. Taking the recently introduced chiral random walk model as a template, we describe the walker with an internal degree of freedom. With a chiral biased updating rule, the full diffusion tensor of this chiral walker turns odd. Within this picture, we can unite persistent and chiral walks, run-and-tumble motion, and cyclic random motions in one framework. We discuss how the memory is followed into the environment; in Lorentz lattice gases, kinetic and rotor walks, and random odd environments; and into the path; in spiral self-avoiding walks.

</details>


### [15] [Ordering Information in Multispecies Long-Range Swap Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32766)
*Eunghyun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文量化了整数格点上相互作用粒子左至右物种序 $W_t$ 对初始构型所揭示的信息量，给出互信息的长时间渐近展开 $C_U/t+O(t^{-3/2})$，并揭示短时信息损失与输出律的非对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于刻画相互作用粒子系统的物种序（species order）随时间如何泄露初始构型信息，即度量 $W_t$ 与初始构型之间的互信息，并比较不同相互作用规则与组分下的信息衰减机制。

Method: 对长程交换下具有不同物种的粒子，通过删除较强粒子得到具有独立泊松增量（independent Poisson increments）的坐标；对有限支撑初始律，将函数 $U$ 与 $W_t$ 的互信息展开，系数由高斯序统计量（Gaussian order statistics）的均值及给定 $U$ 下初始压缩坐标的条件均值确定；并在相邻格点、均匀初始序下计算任意物种数的短时信息损失 $t\log(1/t)$ 项系数。

Result: 长时间互信息为 $C_U/t+O(t^{-3/2})$，$C_U$ 可为零；三粒子情形中，重复较弱而非较强物种使 $t^{-1}$ 系数增至三倍，尽管二者组分熵与初始序熵相同；等粒子阻塞或推挤给出相同的长时间系数，但非均匀极限输出律（limiting output law）相反；普通多种群 TASEP 中固定格点的两个不同粒子则呈 $t^{-1/2}$ 衰减。

Conclusion: 物种序对初始构型的信息衰减由系数 $C_U$ 与相互作用细节刻画，且长时间系数并不由组分熵或初始序熵决定；阻塞与推挤规则影响极限输出律的方向，普通 TASEP 情形呈现更慢的 $t^{-1/2}$ 信息衰减。

Abstract: We measure how much the left-to-right species order $W_t$ of interacting particles on the integer lattice reveals about their initial configuration. For distinct species under long-range swaps, deleting stronger particles gives coordinates with independent Poisson increments. For a finitely supported initial law, the mutual information between any function $U$ of the initial configuration and $W_t$ is $C_U/t+O(t^{-3/2})$ as $t\to\infty$, where $C_U$, which may vanish, is determined by the means of Gaussian order statistics and the conditional means of the initial compressed coordinates given $U$. On consecutive sites with a uniform initial order, we compute the coefficient of the $t\log(1/t)$ term in the short-time information loss for arbitrary species counts. For three particles in this setting, repeating the weaker rather than the stronger species triples the long-time $t^{-1}$ coefficient, although both compositions have the same composition entropy and initial-order entropy. These long-time coefficients are the same whether equal particles block or push each other, but the nonuniform limiting output law is reversed. In the ordinary multispecies totally asymmetric simple exclusion process (TASEP), two distinct particles at fixed sites with a uniform initial order instead give $t^{-1/2}$ decay.

</details>


### [16] [Entanglement Asymmetry and the Quantum Mpemba Effect in Inhomogeneous Quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32934)
*Maëlle Olivier,Filiberto Ares,Pasquale Calabrese*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究考察自旋-1/2 XX 链在初始两半以不同倾角铁磁态制备时纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）的动力学演化，结合广义流体力学（generalized hydrodynamics, GHD）与准粒子图像，并与精确费米子关联矩阵计算对比，发现非均匀初态下量子姆潘巴效应（quantum Mpemba effect）依赖于子系统位置，且存在超越领头阶流体力学的精确动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠不对称性是探测对称性破缺及其量子淬火后动力学恢复的有力工具，但既有研究多集中于均匀初态，从非均匀初始构型出发的演化尚未被充分探索。

This effect now depends on the subsystem position: while the homogeneous behavior is recovered when the subsystem lies entirely within one of the initially tilted halves, the entanglement-asymmetry curves for subsystems straddling the interface can exhibit multiple crossings or no crossings at all. A notable exception occurs for subsystems entirely within an initially non-tilted half. In this case, GHD predicts vanishing asymmetry, but the exact dynamics generates a small, nonzero asymmetry that can develop a plateau before decaying toward zero. Result: 理论预测的稳态电流在 $(v_0,\tau)$ 平面的大部分区域与模拟吻合，包括电流随 $v_0$、$\tau$ 的非单调变化及其与链简正模式（normal modes）的共振（在大 $\tau$、小 $v_0$ 时最显著）。存在临界推进速度 $v_c$：当粒子穿越环的频率离开声子带（phonon band）时，$v_0<v_c$ 电流随持续时间增大而增长，$v_0>v_c$ 则按 $(v_0^2\tau)^{-1}$ 衰减。

Abstract: We study the energy current through a harmonic chain whose left end is coupled, via a short-range contact potential, to a medium of overdamped run-and-tumble particles moving on a ring, while the right end is attached to a zero-temperature Langevin reservoir. The activity is parametrized by the propulsion speed $v_0$ and the persistence time $τ$. Using nonequilibrium response theory at weak system-medium coupling, we predict the stationary current and simulations reproduce it over a large part of the $(v_0,τ)$ plane. That includes the possible nonmonotonicity of the current as function of $v_0$ and $τ$, and occurrence of resonances with the normal modes of the chain, most significantly at large $τ$ and small $v_0$. There is a critical propulsion speed $v_c$ at which the ring-crossing frequency of the particles leaves the phonon band: for $v_0<v_c$ the current grows with persistence, while for $v_0>v_c$ it decays as $(v_0^2τ)^{-1}$. We also explain where the theoretical prediction fails, especially due to an oscillatory instability originating from a sufficiently strong negative friction induced on the leftmost oscillator. Finally, the persistent medium produces strongly non-Gaussian velocity statistics and long-range, oscillating velocity correlations in the bulk of the chain.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文为Hounkonnou–Bukweli Kyemba的$\mathcal{R}(p,q)$-变形量子代数（deformed quantum algebras）建立beta函数理论：借助Jackson型级数表示定义两类变形beta函数，给出其闭式表达、交换缺陷、渐近行为、Bohr–Mollerup型刻画、对数凸性判据与变形Turán恒等式，并统一涵盖多种已知变形框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathcal{R}(p,q)$-变形量子代数（$\mathcal{R}(p,q)$-deformed quantum algebras）虽已提出，但尚缺乏与之配套的beta函数（beta function）体系；该文旨在填补这一空缺，将经典beta函数的整套解析性质推广到结构函数由两个变形函数$\varphi_1>\varphi_2>0$生成的情形。

Method: 基于变形积分（deformed integral）的Jackson型级数表示，在$[0,1]$上由变形幂$(1\ominus\varphi_2 t)^{y-1}$定义第一类beta函数（并延拓至复指数），在半直线上由$(1\oplus\varphi_1 t)^{-x-y}$定义第二类beta函数；第二类借助Ramanujan的${}_1\psi_1$求和（Ramanujan ${}_1\psi_1$ summation）计算。

Result: 第一类beta函数等于$\varphi_1^{(y-1)(2x+y-2)/2}\Gamma_{\mathcal{R}(p,q)}(x)\Gamma_{\mathcal{R}(p,q)}(y)/\Gamma_{\mathcal{R}(p,q)}(x+y)$，并得到一般平移恒等式、交换缺陷（exchange defect）$\varphi_1^{(y-x)(x+y-1)/2}$、至多相差常数的对称化、渐近展开及变形Euler积分—变形Gauss超几何函数（deformed Gauss hypergeometric function）与变形Gauss求和。第二类函数涉及第二个变形gamma函数，与前者相差一个单周期theta商，故在一参数情形下交换缺陷仍存在。此外证明变形gamma函数的Bohr–Mollerup型刻画与乘法公式，其正半轴对数凸性当且仅当$\varphi_1\geq1$，并给出正规化beta函数的联合对数凸性与变形Turán恒等式，计算相关变形beta分布的矩。

Conclusion: 该理论在规范不变性（gauge invariance）意义下覆盖Jagannathan–Srinivasa Rao、Chakrabarti–Jagannathan、广义$q$-Quesne及Hounkonnou–Ngompe等变形，所有恒等式经三十位有效数字的数值验证，构成上述变形框架下beta函数与gamma函数性质的统一表述。

Abstract: We develop the theory of the beta functions attached to the $\mathcal{R}(p,q)-$deformed quantum algebras of Hounkonnou and Bukweli Kyemba, for structure functions whose deformed numbers are generated by two deformation functions $φ_{1}>φ_{2}>0$. Working with the Jackson type series representation of the deformed integral, we define a first kind beta function on $[0,1]$ from the deformed power $(1\ominusφ_{2}t)^{y-1}$, extended to complex exponents, and a second kind beta function on the half line from $(1\oplusφ_{1}t)^{-x-y}$. The first kind function equals $φ_{1}^{(y-1)(2x+y-2)/2}Γ_{\mathcal{R}(p,q)}(x)Γ_{\mathcal{R}(p,q)}(y)/Γ_{\mathcal{R}(p,q)}(x+y)$ for all complex arguments with positive real parts; we derive its general shift identity, its exchange defect $φ_{1}^{(y-x)(x+y-1)/2}$, a commutative symmetrisation unique up to a constant, its asymptotics and a deformed Euler integral for a deformed Gauss hypergeometric function, which yields a deformed Gauss summation. The second kind function is evaluated through Ramanujan's ${}_{1}ψ_{1}$ summation and involves a second deformed gamma function, differing from the first one by a one periodic theta quotient, so that its exchange defect persists in the one parameter case. We prove a Bohr Mollerup type characterisation and a multiplication formula for the deformed gamma function, show that it is logarithmically convex on the positive half line if and only if $φ_{1}\geq1$, obtain the joint logarithmic convexity of normalised beta functions with a deformed Turán identity, and compute the moments of the associated deformed beta distributions. Gauge invariance covers the Jagannathan Srinivasa Rao, Chakrabarti Jagannathan, generalized $q-$Quesne and Hounkonnou Ngompe deformations, and every identity is confirmed numerically to thirty digits.

</details>

*Andreas Fring*

Main category: nlin.PS

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）仅刻画形状模的小振幅振荡，其非线性行为及与连续谱（continuous spectrum）的耦合机制尚不清楚，尤其是高次谐波落入连续谱时是否产生辐射需要澄清。

Result: 二次与三次谐波虽位于线性化涨落算符（linearised fluctuation operator）的连续谱内，相应非齐次方程仍存在精确局域特解；非线性源项在两谐波频率处的在壳重叠为零，从而抑制辐射响应（radiative response）。

Conclusion: 结果表明高阶可积对称性可约束线性谱本已允许的非线性共振（nonlinear resonance），为可积模型中形状模的非线性稳定性提供了对称性层面的解释。

Abstract: We investigate nonlinear excitations of the discrete shape mode about a static complex soliton of the integrable Bullough-Dodd model. Extending the linear stability analysis to third order in the excitation amplitude, we construct explicit spatially localised corrections to the shape-mode oscillation and determine its nonlinear frequency shift using the Poincaré-Lindstedt method. Although both the second and third harmonics lie inside the continuous spectrum of the linearised fluctuation operator, the corresponding inhomogeneous equations admit exact localised particular solutions. We demonstrate that the nonlinear sources have vanishing on-shell overlaps with the scattering states at both harmonic frequencies, suppressing the corresponding radiative responses. We further investigate the role of integrability and show how a higher commuting symmetry of the Bullough-Dodd equation provides a perturbative selection-rule interpretation of these cancellations. Our results illustrate how higher integrable symmetries can constrain nonlinear resonances that are otherwise permitted by the linear spectrum.

</details>

*Shaoxuan Chen,Su Yang,Simon J. Haward,Amy Q. Shen,Panayotis G. Kevrekidis*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该文提出数据驱动框架，从稀疏稳态测量中学习黏弹性流动的分岔结构二维参数依赖正规形（normal form），以不对称参数为状态变量、间隙比与Weissenberg数为控制参数，结合平衡约束、局部稳定性、反射对称性与迭代负样本，重构实验相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏弹性流动（viscoelastic flow）绕过并列微圆柱时呈现对称、汇聚、发散与不对称发散等多种流态及双稳、三稳区域，但其分岔结构缺乏可解释的低维模型；同时实验通常只能获得稀疏的稳态测量而无轨迹数据，需要评估此类数据可推断的动力学信息上限。

Method: 以流动不对称参数 I'、I'' 为状态变量，间隙比 G 与 Weissenberg 数 Wi 为控制参数，构建二维参数依赖正规形；在无轨迹数据条件下，通过平衡约束与局部稳定性约束将测得稳态强制为学习向量场的稳定平衡点；比较多项式与神经网络表示，并直接将物理反射对称性嵌入模型；另引入迭代负采样（negative-sample）过程以抑制未被测量约束的多余稳定平衡点。

Result: 所得模型恢复了主要的几何依赖流态转变及实验相图的整体结构；同时纳入平衡数据、局部稳定性、物理对称性与负样本的模型与实验相图吻合度最高，其余偏差源于仅有稀疏平衡测量所导致的信息不足。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了从稀疏平衡测量推断非线性流动动力学的潜力与局限，并强调物理约束与额外动力学信息对重构复杂分岔结构（bifurcation structure）的重要性。

Abstract: Viscoelastic flow past two side-by-side microcylinders has been shown to exhibit geometry-dependent transitions among symmetric, converging, diverging, and asymmetric-diverging flow states, together with regions of bistability and tristability. Here, we develop a data-driven framework for learning a two-dimensional, parameter-dependent normal form for this bifurcation structure from sparse measurements of stable flow states. We use the flow asymmetry parameters $I'$ and $I''$ as state variables, with the gap ratio $G$ and Weissenberg number $Wi$ as control parameters. In the absence of trajectory data, the measured steady states are enforced as stable equilibria of the learned vector field through equilibrium and local-stability constraints. We examine both polynomial and neural-network representations of the associated normal form and incorporate the physical reflection symmetry directly into the model. The resulting models recover the main geometry-dependent transitions and the overall structure of the experimental phase diagram. To reduce additional stable equilibria that are not constrained by the measurements, we introduce an iterative negative-sample procedure that refines the predicted equilibrium structure. Combining equilibrium data, local stability, physical symmetry, and negative samples yields the closest agreement with the experimental phase diagram among the models considered. The remaining discrepancies reflect the limited information available, solely in the form of sparse equilibrium measurements. Overall, the framework aims to showcase a prototypical example of both the potential and the limitations of inferring nonlinear flow dynamics from sparse equilibrium measurements and highlight the importance of physical constraints and additional dynamical information for reconstructing complex bifurcation structures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波族群研究（gravitational-wave population studies）中的选择效应量化需要数百万次昂贵的信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR）计算，传统波形与内积方法计算成本过高，需要快速且精确的替代模型。

Conclusion: 基于 ρ∝1/d_L 的精确距离重标度支持目录级可探测性重复评估；完整流水线可作为可复现基准，并在公开表格数据集上显示超越引力波领域的竞争力，未来可扩展至探测器网络与特定历元灵敏度。

Abstract: Quantifying selection effects in gravitational-wave population studies requires millions of costly signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) calculations. We present $Φ$asmaNet, a compact neural architecture for structured tabular regression, with staged spectral refinement as an explicit architectural prior. Learned input tokens are refined through ordered attention-based $Φ$-Stages, each augmented by a $Φ$-Refractor subspace-mixing module, then revisited through gated top-down $Φ$-Feedback. Applied to the 11-dimensional parameters of precessing, higher-mode binary black hole signals (IMRPhenomXPHM), it predicts phase-averaged optimal SNR for a single detector at fixed Advanced LIGO design sensitivity. With approximately 732k parameters, it outperforms MLP, 1D-ResNet and Transformer-encoder baselines under matched training across $10^5$--$10^6$ samples. At $10^6$ samples, it achieves $100-\mathrm{MAPE}=98.73\%$ on the held-out test set, $99.29\%$ after rank-preserving monotonic (PCHIP) calibration, and $99.39\%$ ($R^2=0.9998$) in the detection-relevant regime $ρ>8$, with stable accuracy across independent dataset realizations. On a single GPU, it sustains approximately $1.2\times10^5$ SNR evaluations per second and processes a $10^6$-injection catalogue end-to-end in approximately 8.3 s, about two orders of magnitude faster than conventional waveform-and-inner-product calculations on a 16-core CPU. Exact distance rescaling through $ρ\propto1/d_L$ supports repeated catalogue-wide detectability evaluations. The fully documented pipeline is intended as a reproducible benchmark, while results on public tabular datasets indicate competitiveness beyond gravitational-wave applications. Although demonstrated for a fixed single-detector configuration, the construction supports extensions to detector networks and epoch-specific sensitivities. (Abridged)

</details>

*Beaudoin Simon,Bonnefoy Quentin,Marrani Alessio,Rausch de Traubenberg Michel,Saulquin Victor*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 该文从两个六元环构成的六棱柱（hexagonal prism）出发，通过固定正整数边重数的加倍构造（doubling construction）生成一簇不定 Kac–Moody 代数，并利用棱柱的谱精确确定每个代数的余秩（corank）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅表明目标是通过组合构造产生一族不定 Kac–Moody 代数，并借助棱柱谱对其代数不变量（余秩）给出精确刻画。

Method: 构造方法为：以两个六元环组成六棱柱，施加带固定正整数边重数的加倍构造，得到代数族；随后由棱柱的谱（spectrum）确定族中每个代数的精确余秩，并对十二节点代数分析其两个中心方向与前两个分次层（graded levels）。

Result: 在每个非零奇偶类（parity class）上，余秩呈指数增长，但其与矩阵规模之比趋于零；对十二节点代数，识别出两个中心方向，描述了前两个分次层，并刻画了其酉最高权模（unitary highest-weight modules）的整型中心荷（integral central charges）。

Conclusion: 棱柱谱可有效控制该加倍构造所得不定 Kac–Moody 代数族的余秩行为，且十二节点情形具有可显式描述的中心结构与低阶分次结构。

Abstract: We form a hexagonal prism from two six-cycles and iterate a doubling construction with a fixed positive integer edge multiplicity, producing a family of indefinite Kac--Moody algebras. The prism's spectrum determines the exact corank of every algebra in the family. Along each nonvanishing parity class, the corank grows exponentially, while its ratio to the matrix size tends to zero. For the twelve-node algebra we identify the two central directions, describe the first two graded levels, and characterize the integral central charges of its unitary highest-weight modules.

</details>

### [24] [Strain-enabled dynamical magnetoelectric coupling in BaFe$_{12}$O$_{19}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31413)
*ChanJu You,Jayanti Higgins,Zhiren He,Yilin Evan Li,Harikrishnan KP,Sören Buchenau,Christo Guguschev,Mario Brützam,Jiaqiang Yan,David Mandrus,David A. Muller,Darrell G. Schlom,Guru Khalsa,Ankit S. Disa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过将共振振动激发（resonant vibrational excitation）与外延应变（epitaxial strain）结合，在应变钡六铁氧体（BaFe12O19, BaM）薄膜中实现了室温光诱导多铁态（multiferroic state），揭示了一种由应变诱导对称性破缺所开启的隐藏动力学磁电耦合（dynamical magnetoelectric coupling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光激发是调控与创造非平衡物相（non-equilibrium phases）的有力手段，但可用调控路径受平衡态体系对称性与相互作用的内在限制。作者旨在通过原子结构工程（atomic-structural engineering）重新设计这些路径，以获得隐藏的动力学性质。

Method: 将共振振动激发（中红外声子激发）与外延应变结合，作用于技术相关的六方亚铁磁体钡六铁氧体（BaFe12O19, BaM）薄膜；采用时间分辨二次谐波产生（time-resolved second harmonic generation）与磁光克尔效应（magneto-optical Kerr effect）测量，并结合结构分析与基于第一性原理的理论。

Result: 在应变BaM薄膜中，中红外声子激发同时导致铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization）增强与磁矩减弱，而块体材料中无此现象；结构分析与第一性原理理论表明，该效应源于应变诱导的Fe3+离子位移，其破坏局域对称性并促成新兴的动力学磁电耦合。

Conclusion: 强晶格激发与应变诱导的对称性破缺对实现该动力学耦合均不可或缺，为设计原本无法获得的非平衡现象提供了重要范式（template）。

Abstract: Optical excitation has emerged as a powerful tool for manipulating and creating non-equilibrium phases of matter. Yet, the pathways available for optical control are intrinsically limited by the symmetries and interactions of the equilibrium system. Here, we demonstrate that these pathways can be redesigned through atomic-structural engineering to access hidden dynamical properties. By combining resonant vibrational excitation with epitaxial strain, we realize a light-induced, room-temperature multiferroic state in strained thin films of the technologically relevant hexagonal ferrimagnet, barium hexaferrite (BaFe$_{12}$O$_{19}$, BaM). Time-resolved second harmonic generation and magneto-optical Kerr effect measurements reveal an enhanced ferroelectric polarization together with a weakening of the magnetic moment upon exciting mid-infrared phonons in strained BaM thin films, but not in the bulk material. Structural analysis and first-principles-based theory show that this effect is enabled by strain-generated displacement of Fe$^{3+}$ ions, which break the local symmetry and facilitate an emergent dynamical magnetoelectric coupling. Both the strong lattice excitation and strain-induced symmetry breaking are essential for enabling this dynamical coupling, promising an important template for designing otherwise inaccessible non-equilibrium phenomena.

</details>


### [25] [Translation Method Bounds for 3D Isotropic Conductivity--Shear Modulus and the Search for Attaining Microstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31503)
*Justin Tahmassebpur,Tomás Arias*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用平移方法（translation method）计算三维各向同性两相复合材料的电导率—剪切模量交叉性质界（cross-property bounds），得到迄今最紧的界，并通过单元胞拓扑优化检验其可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在回答交叉性质界（conductivity–shear-modulus bounds）能否被实际微结构达到，此前缺乏系统性的可达性评估。

Method: 采用平移方法（translation method）推导交叉性质界；对数千个不同初始结构开展单元胞拓扑优化（unit-cell topology optimization），并借助将宏观材料视为随机取向晶粒各向同性集合的有效介质近似（effective-medium approximation）与各向同性界进行比较。

Result: 获得目前最紧的平移界；综合性能最优的设计一致表现为双连续（bicontinuous）、类三周期极小曲面（triply periodic minimal surface-like）的微结构，但与平移界之间仍存在虽小却显著（small but notable）的差距。

Conclusion: 最优类三周期极小曲面微结构接近但未达到平移界，表明该界可能并非严格可达，其紧致性与可达性之间仍留有值得进一步研究的间隙。

Abstract: We compute conductivity--shear-modulus cross-property bounds in three-dimensional isotropic two-phase composites using the translation method, and obtain the tightest bounds reported to date. To assess attainability, we perform unit-cell topology optimization on thousands of distinct initial structures and compare optimized designs to the isotropic bounds using an effective-medium approximation that models the macroscopic material as an isotropic assembly of randomly oriented grains. The best jointly performing designs are consistently bicontinuous, triply periodic minimal surface-like microstructures; however, a small but notable gap to the translation bound remains.

</details>


### [26] [Electrical Degradation of Vertical $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ Schottky Barrier Diodes Under Au-Ion Irradiation to a Fluence of $1.3 \times 10^7$ cm$^{-2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31512)
*Botong Li,Imteaz Rahaman,Bingcheng Da,Houqiang Fu,Michael A. Scarpulla,Kai Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究系统考察了高能Au离子辐照对垂直β-Ga₂O₃肖特基势垒二极管（Schottky barrier diode, SBD）电学退化与时间相关电流行为的影响，提出基于电荷俘获（charge trapping）的模型解释电流增强与快慢弛豫过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需评估重离子辐照（heavy-ion irradiation）对β-Ga₂O₃ SBD器件性能与瞬态电流响应的影响，理解辐照诱发电学退化与时间相关电流弛豫的关系。

Method: 在-100 V恒定反偏下进行两次连续Au离子辐照，测量正反向电学特性及辐照期间与束流终止后的瞬态电流；采用单指数与双指数（double-exponential）拟合分析弛豫过程，并提出基于电荷俘获的模型。

Result: 辐照后器件正向导通退化、比导通电阻（specific on-resistance）增大、反向漏电流增加、表观载流子浓度降低；辐照期间漏电流显著上升并出现瞬态电流尖峰，束流终止后呈快慢两阶段弛豫。第二次辐照后快、慢弛豫时间常数分别由33.50 s增至40.64 s、由190.35 s增至282.29 s。

Conclusion: 重复辐照导致恢复动力学延长；所提电荷俘获模型通过有效肖特基隧穿势垒（Schottky tunneling barrier）的调制与逐步恢复解释了电流增强及快慢弛豫行为，为重离子诱发电学退化与时间相关电流弛豫的关系提供了认识。

Abstract: The electrical degradation and time-dependent current behavior of vertical $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ Schottky barrier diodes (SBDs) subjected to energetic Au-ion irradiation were systematically investigated. Two successive irradiation exposures were conducted under a constant reverse bias of $-100$ V to evaluate the effects of heavy-ion irradiation on device performance and transient current response. Following irradiation, the devices exhibited degraded forward conduction, increased specific on-resistance, enhanced reverse leakage current, and reduced apparent carrier concentration. During irradiation, pronounced increases in leakage current and transient current spikes were observed. Following each beam termination, the leakage current exhibited an initial rapid decay followed by a slower relaxation process. Single-exponential fitting identified distinct fast and slow relaxation components, while a double-exponential model provided a more comprehensive description of the overall current decay. After the second irradiation exposure, the characteristic time constants of the fast and slow relaxation processes increased from 33.50 to 40.64 s and from 190.35 to 282.29 s, respectively, indicating prolonged recovery dynamics under repeated irradiation. A charge-trapping-based model is proposed to explain the irradiation-induced current enhancement and subsequent fast and slow relaxation processes through modulation and gradual recovery of the effective Schottky tunneling barrier. These findings provide insights into the relationship between heavy-ion-induced electrical degradation and time-dependent current relaxation in $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ SBDs.

</details>


### [27] [Density-dependent photocarrier recombination in $γ$-InSe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31537)
*Vivek Jain,Salman Ahsanullah,Neema Rafizadeh,Gbenga S. Agunbiade,Hui Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用飞秒瞬态反射光谱（femtosecond transient reflectance spectroscopy）研究γ-InSe的光生载流子动力学，通过建立差分反射率与载流子密度的经验关系，定量区分一阶与二阶复合过程，并解析出热载流子冷却（hot-carrier cooling）的超快时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: γ-InSe中光生载流子的复合机制及其密度依赖尚缺乏定量刻画，需要明确一阶与非线性（二阶）复合在其中的相对贡献。

Method: 采用飞秒瞬态反射光谱测量γ-InSe，考察差分反射率（differential reflectance）随注入载流子密度（injected carrier density）的变化，据此建立光学响应与载流子密度的经验关系，用于复合动力学的定量分析。

Result: 载流子衰减呈现显著的密度依赖，表明非线性复合贡献可观；高密度下由二阶复合主导，速率常数为3.8×10⁻⁶ cm³ s⁻¹；低密度下衰减近似指数型，载流子寿命约7 ps；并分辨出带边响应（band-edge response）的初始上升，时间常数小于1 ps，归因于热载流子冷却。

Conclusion: 结果表明γ-InSe中存在一阶与二阶复合的竞争，并给出了其超快光生载流子动力学的定量参数，为理解该材料的载流子输运与复合行为提供依据。

Abstract: We studied the photocarrier dynamics in $γ$-InSe using femtosecond transient reflectance spectroscopy. By measuring the dependence of the differential reflectance on the injected carrier density, we established an empirical relation between the optical response and carrier density, allowing quantitative analysis of the recombination dynamics. The carrier decay exhibits a strong density dependence, indicating a substantial contribution from nonlinear recombination. At high carrier densities, the dynamics are dominated by a second-order recombination process, with a rate constant of $3.8\times10^{-6}~\mathrm{cm^3\,s^{-1}}$. At low densities, the decay becomes approximately exponential, with a carrier lifetime of about 7 ps. We also resolved an initial rise of the band-edge response with a time constant of less than 1 ps, which is attributed to hot-carrier cooling. These results reveal the competition between first-order and second-order recombination in $γ$-InSe and provide quantitative insights into its ultrafast photocarrier dynamics.

</details>


### [28] [Photocatalytic Water Splitting in GeC/ReS$_2$ van der Waals Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31672)
*Parisa Beyranvand,Ismaeil Abdolhosseini Sarsari,Noejung Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）系统研究GeC/ReS₂范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructure），确认直接Z型（direct Z-scheme）机制为界面电荷转移的主导机制，并证明其在不同pH下可有效驱动析氧反应（oxygen evolution reaction, OER）与析氢反应（hydrogen evolution reaction, HER），且应变可显著提升太阳能制氢（solar-to-hydrogen, STH）效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光催化水分解（photocatalytic water splitting）是绿色制氢的重要途径，但光生电子-空穴对（photogenerated electron-hole pairs）的快速复合严重抑制效率；构建范德华异质结可抑制复合，然而在交错能带（staggered band）异质结中，电荷转移可能经由type-II或直接Z型机制，判定哪种机制占主导至关重要。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）系统研究GeC/ReS₂异质结，通过稳定性计算、能带结构分析、能带对齐（band alignment）与吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）路径分析，判别界面电荷转移机制，并考察不同pH条件下的OER/HER能量势垒及应变对STH效率的影响。

Result: 该体系具有良好热力学、动力学与热稳定性，间接带隙为1.38 eV，显著增强可见光吸收；分析表明直接Z型机制为主导界面电荷转移路径。在光照下，type-II与Z型路径均可在4≤pH≤14范围自发驱动OER，但Z型势垒显著更低；type-II机制不适于HER，而Z型机制在0≤pH≤14全范围内均可有效促进HER。无应变条件下STH效率达41.40%，远高于10%的商业化门槛，+6%拉伸应变下进一步提升至60.68%。

Conclusion: Z型、应变可调的GeC/ReS₂异质结是一种有前景且高效的全解水（overall water splitting）光催化剂。

Abstract: Photocatalytic water splitting is considered as one of the most promising approaches for the green hydrogen production. But the rapid recombination of photogenerated electron-hole pairs severely inhibits its photocatalytic efficiency. Construction of van der Waals heterostructures is a good way to suppress this recombination. However, in staggered band heterostructures, the charge transfer could occur either through the type-II or the direct Z-scheme mechanism. Therefore it is very important to identify which mechanism is dominant. In the present work, we systematically study the GeC/ReS$_2$ heterostructure using first-principles density functional theory (DFT). The system exhibits good thermodynamic, dynamic and thermal stability and an indirect band gap of 1.38 eV which greatly enhances the visible-light absorption. We find that the direct Z-scheme mechanism is the dominant interfacial charge transfer by analysing band alignment and Gibbs free energy pathways. The oxygen evolution reaction (OER) can be driven spontaneously in the pH range $4 \le \text{pH} \le 14$ under light irradiation for both type-II and Z-scheme pathways but the energy barrier is much lower for the Z-scheme mechanism. Moreover, the type-II mechanism is not suitable for hydrogen evolution reaction (HER), but the Z-scheme mechanism can promote HER well in the entire pH range ($0 \le \text{pH} \le 14$). Remarkably, the strain-free (pristine) condition exhibits a solar-to-hydrogen (STH) efficiency of 41.40\%, well above the 10\% threshold for commercial viability. The STH efficiency is further enhanced to 60.68\% under a tensile strain of +6\%. To summarise, the Z-scheme strain-tunable GeC/ReS$_2$ heterostructure is a promising and efficient photocatalyst for overall water splitting.

</details>


### [29] [AI-assisted formal proofs for static and time-dependent density functional theory on lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32370)
*Miguel A. L. Marques,Irene D'Amico,Stefano Pittalis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作证明：在任意连通格子上（非空非满填充、跳跃振幅同号、任意密度-密度相互作用），Hohenberg-Kohn 定理的失效情形虽存在，但只是零 Lebesgue 测度的闭稀疏集；非简并共享基态的密度集余维至少为二。相关结论亦适用于 Kohn-Sham 势与自旋密度泛函理论，并在含时密度泛函理论中给出初始态选取、势的局部存在唯一性等结果，全部定理在 Lean 4 中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hohenberg-Kohn 定理（Hohenberg-Kohn theorem）在格点上失效：相差非常数的两个势可以共享同一基态，这动摇了密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）在格点模型中的严格基础。该文旨在刻画此类例外势的“稀有性”，并厘清含时情形下初始态与势映射的适定性。

Method: 考虑任意连通格子上无自旋费米子（spinless fermions），填充非空且非满，跳跃振幅取同一符号，密度-密度相互作用任意。通过对势空间与密度空间的测度与拓扑分析（零 Lebesgue 测度、闭稀疏集、余维估计），建立例外集的结构性刻画，并将论证推广至 Kohn-Sham 势（Kohn-Sham potential）与自旋密度泛函理论（spin-density functional theory）。含时部分则构造反例并给出局部存在性与唯一性证明。全部命题在证明助手 Lean 4 中形式化并经机器检验。

Result: 与相差非常数的势共享基态的势构成闭的、无处稠密的零 Lebesgue 测度集；非简并共享基态对应的密度集余维至少为二。对 Kohn-Sham 势与自旋密度泛函理论（仅多出磁场均匀平移这一自由度）同样成立。含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory）中，并非每个连通格子的基态都可作为密度-势映射的初始态；Penz 与 van Leeuwen 给出的方形格上两费米子基态即为反例。但对几乎所有静态势，每个实基态（含自旋分辨密度）均可作初始态；并证明含时 Kohn-Sham 势在时间局部存在、可容许初始态势的局部存在唯一性、可延拓至可容许态集边界，以及正则化 Kohn-Sham 迭代的收敛性。

Conclusion: Hohenberg-Kohn 定理在格点上的失效属于测度意义下的例外而非通例，从而为格点 DFT 的密度-势映射提供了严格的基础性保障；含时情形下初始态的可容许性亦为“几乎所有”意义下成立。所有结论经 Lean 4 机器验证，提升了结果的形式可靠性。

Abstract: The Hohenberg-Kohn theorem fails on lattices, where two potentials that differ by more than a constant can share a ground state. We prove that such potentials are exceptional. We consider spinless fermions on any connected lattice, at any filling other than empty or full, with hopping amplitudes of a common sign and any density-density interaction. The potentials whose ground state is shared with a potential differing by more than a constant lie in a closed, nowhere dense set of zero Lebesgue measure. The densities of non-degenerate shared ground states form a set of codimension at least two. The statement about the potentials also holds for the Kohn-Sham potential and for spin-density functional theory, where a uniform shift of the magnetic field is the only additional freedom. In time-dependent density functional theory on lattices, we show that not every ground state of a connected lattice can serve as the initial state of the density-potential map. A counterexample is the ground state of two fermions on a square that Penz and van Leeuwen found as a counterexample to the Hohenberg-Kohn theorem. For almost every static potential, however, every real ground state can serve as an initial state, also for spin-resolved densities. We also give formal proofs that the time-dependent Kohn-Sham potential exists, locally in time, for almost every static Kohn-Sham potential, and of known results: local existence and uniqueness of the time-dependent potential for an admissible initial state, continuation in time up to the boundary of the set of admissible states, and the convergence properties of a regularized Kohn-Sham iteration. All theorems and propositions are formalized in the proof assistant Lean 4 and checked by computer.

</details>


### [30] [Magic-layer scenario for predicting non-van der Waals two-dimensional materials: The case of double-layer honeycomb structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31748)
*Shota Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将“幻数（magic number）”团簇概念推广为“幻数层（magic-layer）”概念，用以识别难以从母体三维晶体中剥离的非范德华（non-vdW）二维材料，并通过第一性原理计算验证其作为预测性描述符的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华（van der Waals, vdW）团簇中存在因特定尺寸而异常稳定的“幻数”现象，但非vdW二维（2D）材料因层间结合强、难以剥离，缺乏可预判其能否实验实现的稳定性判据，这构成本研究的出发点。

### [34] [Continuum model for disconnection structures of hetero-interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32412)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 模型预测的间断网络与界面倾角与实验、原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）及理论预测吻合良好；系统分析进一步揭示了异质界面结构性质对倾角与取向差角的函数依赖关系。

Conclusion: 以广义Frank-Bilby约束下的界面能最小化为核心的连续介质框架，可有效预测多类异质界面的平衡结构并刻画其角度依赖规律。

Abstract: Hetero-interfaces, as planar defects between dissimilar materials, accommodate the lattice misfit through networks of line defects of disconnections (a combination of structural ledges/step and dislocation characters). Comparisons with experiments, atomistic simulations and theoretical predictions show that our continuum model provides excellent predictions of the hetero-interface structures including disconnection networks and inclination, misorientation angles. Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 将平均场键模型（mean-field bond model）从单组分 fcc 晶格推广到多化学物种、多亚晶格晶体，无需统计采样即可预测有限温度热力学，并在 Morse 型势的分子动力学模拟中验证一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原平均场键模型仅适用于单组分 fcc 晶格，而复杂晶体（多物种、多亚晶格）的有限温度热力学通常依赖昂贵的统计采样（如分子动力学（molecular dynamics））。

Conclusion: 该推广模型可在无统计采样条件下描述复杂晶体的有限温度热力学；结合第一性原理（first principles）及基础模型（foundation models）参数化的键合势，有望达到从头算（ab initio）精度。

Abstract: We extend our previously introduced mean-field bond model from unary fcc lattices to crystals with multiple chemical species and sublattices, accurately predicting finite-temperature thermodynamics without statistical sampling. ### [36] [Spin-Group versus Magnetic-Group Selection Rules for Nonlinear Photocurrents in Compensated Ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32618)
*Fu Li,Vikrant Chaudhary,Weiyi Pan,Hongbin Zhang,Harish K. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文比较自旋群（spin-group）与磁群（magnetic-group）对称性对多铁性补偿亚铁磁体GaFeO₃中非线性电荷与自旋光电流选择定则的差异，指出仅考虑自旋群对称性会遗漏SOC激活的电流成分，磁群对称性对完整描述是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光激发可实现无接触、超快（ultrafast）的电荷与自旋电流，但允许何种非线性光电流（nonlinear photocurrents）由对称性决定。自旋群对称性仅描述无自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）极限，而磁群对称性才包含SOC，二者对选择定则的差异及其物理后果尚需在具体材料中厘清。

Method: 以多铁性补偿亚铁磁体（multiferroic compensated ferrimagnet）GaFeO₃为对象，分别采用自旋群对称性与磁群对称性两套框架，推导并比较电荷光电流与自旋光电流的选择定则（selection rules），区分无SOC与含SOC两种情形。

Result: 无SOC时仅允许线性位移电流（linear shift current）与圆形注入电流（circular injection current），且电荷与自旋分量相同，故每个电荷电流均沿磁矩方向自旋极化。引入SOC后，这些电荷电流几乎不变，但激活磁线性注入（magnetic linear injection）与圆形位移（circular shift）电荷电流，并产生偏振垂直于磁矩的纯自旋流（pure spin currents）。铁电翻转（ferroelectric switching）会反转线性位移与圆形注入电荷电流及其自旋极化。

Conclusion: 两套对称性描述给出不同的电荷与自旋光电流选择定则；磁群对称性对于补偿亚铁磁体中非线性电荷与自旋光电流的完整描述具有关键意义，且铁电极化可作为一种调控手段反转相关电流与自旋极化。

Abstract: Optical excitation offers a contact-free, ultrafast route to charge and spin currents, with symmetry determining which nonlinear photocurrents are allowed and whether they carry charge, spin, or both. SOC leaves these charge currents nearly unchanged, activates magnetic linear injection and circular shift charge currents, and enables pure spin currents polarized perpendicular to the moments. Ferroelectric switching reverses the linear shift and circular injection charge currents and their spin polarization. These findings establish the importance of magnetic-group symmetry for a complete description of nonlinear charge and spin photocurrents in compensated ferrimagnets.

</details>


### [37] [Constrained Allen-Cahn phase-field topology optimization of flexoelectric devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32620)
*Hao Zhen,Sheng Mao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立基于相场（phase-field）方法的约束拓扑优化（topology optimization）框架，用于微尺度挠曲电（flexoelectric）器件的连续介质建模设计；以输出电压为目标函数，并纳入制造偏差的worst-case考量与谱约束（spectral constraints），在压力传感器、弯曲传感器及三维薄膜器件上获得显著电压提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型器件需要以性能为导向的设计手段，而挠曲电（flexoelectric）器件在微尺度下的构型优化缺乏系统性的数值设计框架。研究旨在提供一个能够兼顾器件级材料分布、输出性能与可制造性的约束拓扑优化工具。

Method: 在小变形假设下采用连续介质模型，构建相场（phase-field）框架进行器件级材料分布优化，以输出电压为目标，并支持电容或静电能目标。制造偏差通过规定几何实现上的worst-case目标处理，同时施加谱约束（spectral constraints）以保持结构连通性；框架应用于二维压力与弯曲传感器及三维薄膜器件。

Result: 优化后标称输出电压约为初始值的4.8倍（压力传感器）和2.5–3.4倍（弯曲传感器）。电压增益主要来自机械诱导源电荷（source charge）的增加，电容（capacitance）降低提供随构型变化的附加贡献。腐蚀（erosion）分析表明，结构构件局部变窄时连通性保持良好，输出电压接近标称构型。

Conclusion: 该优化框架可有效提升挠曲电（flexoelectric）器件的输出电压并保持结构连通性与制造鲁棒性。结合具体微纳加工工艺，可为器件构型筛选与原型开发提供数值依据。

Abstract: Topology optimization provides an effective approach to exploring performance-oriented designs for miniature devices. In this work, a phase-field framework for constrained topology optimization of microscale flexoelectric devices is developed using a continuum model under the assumption of infinitesimal deformations. The material distribution is optimized at the device level with the output voltage as the objective. The framework also accommodates objectives based on capacitance or stored electrostatic energy. Manufacturing variations are incorporated through a worst-case objective over the prescribed geometric realizations, with spectral constraints imposed to maintain the structural connectivity. The framework is applied to two-dimensional pressure and bending sensors and a three-dimensional thin-film device. The optimized nominal output voltages reach approximately 4.8 times the initial value for the pressure sensor and 2.5-3.4 times the initial values for the bending sensors. The voltage gains arise primarily from increased mechanically induced source charge, with capacitance reduction providing an additional contribution that varies with the device configuration. Erosion analyses of representative designs further show that structural connectivity is well preserved when structural members become locally narrower, with the output voltage remaining close to that of the nominal configuration. Combined with specific microfabrication processes, this optimization framework is expected to provide a numerical basis for configuration screening and prototype development of flexoelectric devices.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Heterostructure interfaces created by stacking two-dimensional (2D) materials outline an appealing route towards achieving advanced electronic and magnetic functionalities at the nanoscale.   <summary>Details</summary>

  <summary>Details</summary>

By contrast, $2V_\mathrm{Cr}+2V_\mathrm{N}$ in CrN remains stabilised, with its association energy decreasing from $+1.188$ to $+0.431$ eV per vacancy. Result: 在磁转变温度以下，五个拉曼活性晶格振动中的四个（一个Eg与三个Tg模式）频率显著偏离Klemens模型预言；所提两子晶格共线反照磁体模型可同时再现冷却进入AFM相时声子的硬化（hardening）与软化（softening）。此外给出两个磁振子模式的能量-温度与能量-磁场依赖，发现高能模（约3.5 meV）在交叉偏振配置下呈意外的二重各向异性（twofold anisotropy），低能模（约2.6 meV）呈微弱但确定的软化趋势。

Conclusion: 自旋-声子耦合是MnTe₂中PM/AFM相变处声子频率重整化的合理解释，简单共线反照磁模型已具描述能力；磁振子各向异性与低能模软化的确切起源尚不明确，作者提出局域原子环境对称性降低（symmetry reduction）作为可能机制供进一步讨论。

Abstract: Recently, two modes of spin wave excitations (magnons) were detected in Raman scattering spectra of MnTe2. It is an unconventional magnet that features a pyrite-type crystal structure hosting a non-collinear, non-coplanar antiferromagnetic (AFM) spin order below the Néel temperature. In this brief communication, the focus is primarily on the effect of spin ordering on lattice dynamics (phonons) that has been established through temperature (5 - 300 K), linear polarization angle (0 deg - 360 deg) and external magnetic field (up to 16 T) dependent measurements. It has been found that below the magnetic transition temperature, the frequencies of four out of five Raman-active lattice vibrations (i.e., Eg and three Tg phonon modes) revealed significant departure from the mechanism of the three-phonon anharmonic decay described by Klemens model. Here, we propose a new model considering spin-phonon coupling to explain frequency renormalization of the phonon part of the Raman spectrum upon the para-/antiferro- (PM/AFM) magnetic phase transition change in MnTe2. Through employing Green's function formalism, we show that a simple two-sublattice collinear antiferromagnet approach follows well both spin ordering induced hardening and softening of phonon modes observed in AFM phase of MnTe2 upon cooling. In addition to unexpected twofold anisotropy of the higher energy magnon mode (approx. 3.5 meV) unveiled in crossed configuration, our magneto-optical measurements revealed a subtle, yet definite softening trend for the lower energy mode (approx. 2.6 meV). Although the precise reasons for these effects remain unclear, we put forward a symmetry reduction in the local atomic surroundings as a possible mechanism for discussion.

</details>

### [49] [A mathematical perspective on nonplanar on-shell forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10041)
*Artyom Lisitsyn,Elizabeth Pratt,Melissa Sherman-Bennett,Jaroslav Trnka*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文将原仅针对平面图（planar graphs）的壳层形式（on-shell forms）研究推广到任意图，给出某类壳层形式的行列式公式（determinantal formula），并阐明其与 Castravet–Tevelev 引入的 $M_{0,n}$ 超树除子（hypertree divisors）的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壳层形式是粒子物理中出现的 Grassmannian 上的微分形式，其数学研究此前主要局限于图是平面图（planar graph）的情形，因而可借助 Postnikov 研究全非负 Grassmannian（totally nonnegative Grassmannian）时发展的组合工具；对任意图的情形尚缺乏相应工具与结果。

Method: 首先讨论如何把平面图情形下的多种组合工具推广到任意图（arbitrary graphs），随后对一类最早出现于物理文献的壳层形式证明其行列式公式（determinantal formula），并分析该类形式与 $M_{0,n}$ 的超树除子（hypertree divisors）之间的联系。

Result: 论文给出了将平面图工具推广至任意图的方案，建立了某一类壳层形式的行列式公式，并说明该类形式与 Castravet–Tevelev 定义的 $M_{0,n}$ 超树除子相关联。

Conclusion: 壳层形式的研究可超越平面图限制；任意图情形下的工具推广与行列式公式为理解物理中出现的壳层形式提供了数学基础，并将其与 $\overline{M}_{0,n}$ 上超树除子的代数几何对象联系起来。

Abstract: On-shell forms are differential forms on the Grassmannian which arise in particle physics. They are defined using bipartite graphs with $n$ distinguished ``boundary'' vertices. Mathematical investigation of on-shell forms has largely focused on the case where the graph is planar, in which case one can utilize combinatorial tools pioneered by Postnikov in the study of the totally nonnegative Grassmannian. In this article, we investigate on-shell forms for arbitrary graphs. We first discuss how to extend various tools from the planar case to arbitrary graphs. We then prove a determinantal formula for a class of on-shell forms which first appeared in physics literature. We explain the relation between this class of forms and the hypertree divisors of $M_{0,n}$, introduced by Castravet--Tevelev.

</details>

*Coline Emprin,Alex Takeda*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文在 $\mathbb{R}^m$ 上局部常值预分解代数（locally constant prefactorization algebras）的上同调中构造了一个泊松括号（Poisson bracket），作为任意维数 $m\geqslant 2$ 下形式性（formality）的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有二维泊松括号（Bruinsma–Schenkel–Vicedo）仅适用于二维情形，作者希望将其推广至高维，从而为更一般的预分解代数形式性问题提供统一的障碍工具。

Method: 构造结合了彩色障碍序列（colored obstruction sequences）与链环球面（linking spheres），推广二维括号；并据此证明非形式性（non-formality）结论。

Result: 将李代数分解包络（Lie algebra factorization envelopes）的非形式性定理推广到所有高维；并对李代数及其紧化（compactifications）、$L_\infty$-代数推广、自由 BF 型理论（free BF-type theories）、高维阿贝尔 Chern–Simons 理论及其紧化得到新的非形式性结果。

Conclusion: 所构造的高维泊松括号为预分解代数形式性提供了维度无关的障碍，并统一扩展了一类量子场论模型的非形式性结论。

Abstract: We construct a Poisson bracket on the cohomology of locally constant prefactorization algebras on $\mathbb{R}^m$ that provides an obstruction to formality in every dimension $m\geqslant 2$. Our construction combines colored obstruction sequences to formality with linking spheres and generalizes the two-dimensional bracket of Bruinsma, Schenkel, and Vicedo. We use it to extend their non-formality theorem for Lie algebra factorization envelopes to all higher dimensions. We also establish new non-formality results for factorization envelopes of Lie algebras, their compactifications and generalizations to $L_\infty$-algebras, free BF-type theories, higher abelian Chern-Simons theories and their compactifications.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个构建三阶段预测-校正有限体积（finite volume）格式的广义框架，用于求解含刚性松弛源项（stiff relaxation source terms）的双曲平衡律（hyperbolic balance laws），并通过理论分析与数值实验确定自由参数 α 的可行范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含刚性松弛源项的双曲平衡律，需要在保持中心型格式简洁性的同时，为源项离散引入可调的灵活性，并给出参数选取的理论依据。

Method: 将中心 Nessyahu-Tadmor（NT）框架推广至 PDE 的非齐次部分，源项积分采用待定系数法（undetermined coefficient approach）引入自由参数 α；随后开展统一理论分析，考察一致相容性（uniform consistency）、渐近性质、A-稳定性（A-stability）与 L-稳定性（L-stability），并联合各理论约束确定 α 的公共可行范围。

Result: 理论分析给出了使格式满足相容性、渐近性质及 A-/L-稳定性的 α 可行区间；数值上通过光滑解算例评估精度，通过不同数学模型的一维基准问题与现有格式对比，并借助十个二维黎曼问题（Riemann problems）考察激波捕捉、复杂波相互作用及计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提广义框架在统一理论约束下可为含刚性松弛源项的双曲平衡律提供参数可调的稳健数值格式，其精度、稳定性与激波分辨能力经多维基准算例得到验证。

Abstract: This study develops a generalized framework for constructing a three-stage predictor-corrector finite volume scheme for solving hyperbolic balance laws with stiff relaxation source terms. The proposed scheme extends the central Nessyahu-Tadmor (NT) framework for the nonhomogeneous part of PDE and the integrals of the source terms are computed using an undetermined coefficient approach to introduce a free parameter \(α\), which offers flexibility in formulation. A unified theoretical analysis is then carried out to characterize the properties of the resulting scheme and determine the admissible range of \(α\). Specifically, uniform consistency and asymptotic properties are investigated along with $A$ -stability and $L$-stability, with the corresponding theoretical constraints combined to establish a common admissible range for the free parameter. The numerical performance of the scheme is subsequently evaluated through a sequence of test problems. Smooth-solution tests are used to assess its accuracy, while one-dimensional benchmark problems arising from different mathematical models are considered to examine its performance in comparison with existing numerical schemes. The assessment is further extended to ten two-dimensional Riemann problems to investigate the ability of the scheme to capture shocks and complex wave interactions along with computational cost.

</details>


### [52] [Convergence of Implicit Particle Methods for Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32784)
*Yunhong Deng,Wilfrid Gangbo,Li Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明隐式粒子方法（implicit particle methods）用于 Wasserstein 梯度流（Wasserstein gradient flows）时，在时间步长固定、粒子数趋于无穷下的收敛性，并给出 blob 方法在粒子数与正则化参数同时极限下的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 JKO 变分时间离散与粒子近似结合时，内能（internal energy）非线性依赖概率密度，无法直接在经验测度（empirical measures）上求值，这构成粒子到连续极限分析的核心障碍，需建立相应的收敛理论。

Method: 方案结合 Jordan–Kinderlehrer–Otto（JKO）变分时间离散与概率测度的粒子近似；内能近似采用三种途径：基于有序粒子间距的一维密度重构、基于核密度重构的 blob 方法（blob method）、以及借助变量替换公式由推前关系恢复密度的输运映射（transport-map）表述。分析工具为 Γ-收敛（Γ-convergence）与紧性论证（compactness arguments）。

Result: 建立了离散变分问题及其极小元的收敛性；后两种方法适用于任意空间维度且不要求区域紧致；对 blob 方法，在标度条件 $N\varepsilon_N^d\to\infty$ 下证明了粒子数趋于无穷与正则化参数消失的同时收敛。

Conclusion: 结果为含非线性内能的隐式 JKO 格式提供了变分形式的粒子到连续极限（particle-to-continuum limit），补充了经典动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the convergence of implicit particle methods for Wasserstein gradient flows as the number of particles tends to infinity, with the time step held fixed. The schemes combine the Jordan--Kinderlehrer--Otto (JKO) variational time discretization with particle approximations of the evolving probability measure. The main challenge lies in approximating the internal energy, which depends nonlinearly on the probability density and cannot, in general, be evaluated directly on empirical measures. We consider three approaches: a one-dimensional density reconstruction based on the spacings between ordered particles; a blob method using kernel-based density reconstruction; and a transport-map formulation in which the density is recovered from the pushforward relation through the change-of-variables formula. The latter two approaches apply in arbitrary spatial dimensions without requiring the spatial domain to be compact. For the blob method, we also establish simultaneous convergence as the particle number tends to infinity and the regularization parameter vanishes, under the scaling $N\varepsilon_N^d\to\infty$. Our analysis combines $Γ$-convergence and compactness arguments to establish convergence of the discrete variational problems and their minimizers. These results complement classical dynamical mean-field theory by providing a variational particle-to-continuum limit for implicit JKO schemes involving nonlinear internal energies.

</details>

*Sascha H. Hauck,Nis-Luca van Hülst,Matthias Kabel,Dieter Jaksch,Nicolas R. Gauger*
