  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于超快速傅里叶变换（superfast Fourier transform, SFFT）的 TT 谱求解器仅适用于周期问题，非周期边界条件下的对应表示尚缺乏研究。

Method: 基于离散余弦/正弦变换的量子线路构造，从共享的 TT 算子表示推导出 SFCT 与 SFST，并证明其 TT 秩被 6r_S 界定（r_S 为给定精度下 SFFT 的秩）。

Result: 在网格规模至 2^26 点时实测秩稳定在约 32；Rayleigh-Bénard 对流中谱压力投影使 Poisson 求解较预处理物理空间 TT 求解器加速最多 7 倍且精度相当；三维计算均匀化较现有 TT 方法加速 2-6 倍、内存降低 4-7 倍。

Conclusion: 结果建立了面向非周期边界条件谱偏微分方程（PDE）求解器的高效 TT 框架。

Abstract: Tensor train (TT) methods enable high-resolution computational mechanics simulations by exploiting low-rank structure in discretized fields and operators. While the superfast Fourier transform (SFFT) has enabled efficient TT-based spectral solvers for periodic problems, corresponding representations for non-periodic boundary conditions remain underexplored. We introduce the superfast cosine and sine transforms (SFCT and SFST), extending the TT spectral framework to homogeneous Neumann and Dirichlet boundary conditions, respectively. Building on a quantum-circuit construction of the discrete cosine and sine transforms, we derive both transforms from a shared TT operator representation and prove that their TT ranks are bounded by $6r_S$, independently of the discretization order, where $r_S$ is the rank of the SFFT at the prescribed accuracy. The measured ranks plateau near 32 for grid sizes up to $2^{26}$ points. For Rayleigh-Bénard convection, spectral pressure projection achieves up to a 7-fold speedup in the Poisson solve relative to a preconditioned physical-space TT solver while maintaining comparable accuracy. Three-dimensional computational homogenization achieves a 2-6-fold speedup and a 4-7-fold reduction in memory consumption compared with the existing TT-based approach. The results establish an efficient TT framework for spectral PDE solvers with non-periodic boundary conditions.

</details>

*Rashmi Ranjan Routaray,Ali Ghojavand,Eric Bousquet*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文用 DFT+U 系统研究了单层 CrS$_2$ 的 1H、1T、1T$'$、1T$''$ 四种多型体（polymorph）在 Hubbard 相互作用 $U=0$–$8$ eV 与双轴应变 $-10\%$ 至 $+10\%$ 下的结构、磁性与电子性质，揭示了 1H 相从非磁半导体经反铁磁半导体到金属的相变序列及其驱动机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联二维磁体中竞争性电子与磁相之间的平衡常由在位库仑相互作用（on-site Coulomb interaction）强度决定，而该参数本身并无唯一定义，导致预测的基态可能随参数取值而变；单层 CrS$_2$ 正处于这一区间，存在能量接近、性质从非磁半导体到磁性金属的多种多型体，因此需系统考察其参数依赖性。

Method: 在广义梯度近似（generalized gradient approximation, GGA）框架下采用 DFT+U，计算单层 CrS$_2$ 的 1H、1T、1T$'$、1T$''$ 四种多型体的结构、磁性与电子性质，扫描 $U=0$–$8$ eV 与双轴应变 $-10\%$–$+10\%$；并用以局域轨道（localized-orbital）实现的独立计算进行交叉验证。

Result: 1T 衍生结构在整个参数范围内保持金属性与磁性，$U$ 主要起能谱重整化（spectral renormalization）作用；1H 相则随 $U$ 增大依次经历非磁半导体、反铁磁半导体（antiferromagnetic semiconducting）到金属的转变，带隙闭合而局域 Cr 磁矩单调增大，固定 $U$ 下的双轴拉伸应变可诱发类似的转变序列。两条调控路径上，两个不等价最近邻 Cr–Cr 交换通道逐渐分离并交叉，平行自旋通道转为占优，并伴随呼吸型键歧化（breathing-type bond disproportionation）。

Conclusion: 局域轨道实现的独立计算重现了整体演化趋势，但给出偏移的临界 $U$ 与应变值，表明相图具有稳健性，同时转变阈值存在实现依赖性（implementation dependence）。

Abstract: In correlated two-dimensional magnets the balance between competing electronic and magnetic phases is often set by the strength of the on-site Coulomb interaction, so that the predicted ground state can hinge on a parameter that is not uniquely defined. Monolayer CrS$_2$ lies squarely in this regime, hosting several energetically close polymorphs whose character ranges from non-magnetic semiconducting to magnetically ordered metallic. We therefore investigate, using DFT$+U$ within the generalized gradient approximation, the structural, magnetic, and electronic properties of the 1H, 1T, 1T$'$, and 1T$''$ polymorphs of monolayer CrS$_2$ under varying Hubbard interactions ($U=0$--$8$~eV) and biaxial strain ($-10\%$ to $+10\%$). The 1T-derived structures remain metallic and magnetic throughout, with $U$ acting mainly as a spectral renormalization. The 1H phase behaves differently, passing from a non-magnetic semiconductor through an antiferromagnetic semiconducting regime before metallizing, so that the gap closes as $U$ increases while the local Cr moment grows monotonically. Biaxial tensile strain at fixed $U$ induces a similar sequence of transitions. Along both tuning pathways, the two inequivalent nearest-neighbor Cr--Cr exchange channels progressively separate and cross, with the parallel-spin channel becoming dominant, accompanied by a breathing-type bond disproportionation. Independent calculations with a localized-orbital implementation reproduce the overall evolution but yield shifted critical values of $U$ and of strain, demonstrating the robustness of the phase diagram while highlighting the implementation dependence of the transition thresholds.

</details>

### [55] [Spatial switching near a four-loop homoclinic bouquet: a mechanism for multi-bump bound states in coupled nonlinear Schrödinger systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31846)
*Sajjad Bakrani*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文研究耦合非线性薛定谔（CNLS）系统中驻波在分量间逐脉冲切换的机制，通过四维哈密顿系统的四环同宿轨道束（bouquet of homoclinic loops）构造稳定/不稳定不变流形，并证明其横截相交蕴含无穷多多峰同宿轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究耦合非线性薛定谔（coupled nonlinear Schrödinger, CNLS）系统中局域驻波（localized standing wave）在不同分量之间逐脉冲切换的可能性，需要理解对应驻波约化系统的同宿结构与多峰轨道存在性。

Method: 对 CNLS 系统作驻波约化，得到具有两个反射对称性的四维哈密顿系统（Hamiltonian system），其中四个基本脉冲对应鞍点的四环同宿轨道束；在鞍点特征值 0<λ1<λ2<2λ1 及适当非退化假设下，构造该轨道束的稳定与不稳定不变流形（invariant manifolds）。

Result: 两个不变流形在鞍点所处的临界哈密顿能级（critical Hamiltonian level）中均为余维一（codimension one），并刻画轨道束附近除渐近鞍点轨道外的单侧捕获（one-sided trapping）；若两流形在轨道束之外横截相交（transversely）并产生超同宿轨道（super-homoclinic orbits），则存在无穷多条通向鞍点的多峰同宿轨道（multi-bump homoclinic orbits）。

Conclusion: 多峰同宿轨道的存在性对应于 CNLS 系统的多峰局域驻波，从而为分量间脉冲切换现象提供同宿轨道框架下的解释；结论依赖特征值条件与非退化、横截性假设。

Abstract: We study how a localized standing wave of a coupled nonlinear Schrödinger (CNLS) system can switch between components from one pulse to the next. The standing wave reduction of the CNLS system yields a four-dimensional Hamiltonian system with two reflection symmetries, in which four elementary pulses correspond to a bouquet of homoclinic loops to a saddle. For saddle eigenvalues $\pmλ_{1},\pmλ_{2}$ satisfying $0<λ_{1} < λ_{2} < 2λ_1$, and under suitable nondegeneracy assumptions, we construct stable and unstable invariant manifolds of the four-loop bouquet. Apart from trajectories asymptotic to the saddle in the corresponding time direction, these manifolds characterize one-sided trapping near the bouquet within the critical Hamiltonian level, namely the level set containing the saddle equilibrium. Both manifolds have codimension one in this level. We prove that if they intersect transversely away from the bouquet, producing super-homoclinic orbits, then there exist infinitely many multi-bump homoclinic orbits to the saddle. These orbits correspond to multi-bump localized standing waves of the CNLS system.

</details>

### [56] [Absence of $σ$-criticality in Short-Range Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31584)
*C. M. Newman,D. L. Stein*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文证明在任意维度下，Edwards-Anderson Ising自旋玻璃（spin glass）中两种σ-临界性（σ-criticality）均不出现，从而彻底排除了σ-临界性作为多重基态对存在的充分条件，并提出一个严格的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态对多重性（ground state pair multiplicity）问题长期未决，而σ-临界性曾被视作其存在的充分条件；厘清该条件是否成立有助于缩小多重性问题的判据范围。

Method: 通过分析单个耦合（coupling）在特定基态中的“柔性”（flexibility），区分第一类与第二类σ-临界性；在第二类情形下考察某键是否落入正密度其他键的临界液滴边界（critical droplet boundaries），并给出理论证明。

Result: 证明第二类σ-临界性在任意维度的EA自旋玻璃中同样不存在，与先前对第一类的排除结果合并，完全排除了σ-临界性的存在。

Conclusion: 该结果虽未解决基态对多重性问题，但移除了其一个充分条件；作者进而提出基态对多重性的严格必要条件。

Abstract: The stability of ground states in the Edwards-Anderson Ising spin glass in dimensions two and higher against perturbations of a single coupling can be quantified through the ``flexibility'' of that coupling in a particular ground state. The property of $σ$-criticality is one in which varying a single coupling can alter the flexibilities of a (strictly) positive density of couplings in an infinite system, and its presence provides a sufficient condition for the presence of multiple ground state pairs. In a previous paper it was shown for the EA spin glass that $σ$-criticality of the first kind, in which the critical droplet of a bond has a positive-density interface with the ground state, was ruled out in any dimension. The remaining possibility, called $σ$-criticality of the second kind, is one in which a bond lies in the critical droplet boundaries of a positive density of other bonds. We show here that this kind of $σ$-criticality is also absent, ruling out altogether the presence of $σ$-criticality in the EA spin glass. While this doesn't resolve the question of multiplicity of ground state pairs, it removes a sufficient condition for its presence. We then propose a rigorous necessary condition for ground state pair multiplicity.

</details>


### [57] [Conformality lost in Anderson localization in infinite dimensions, and regained in finite dimensions: a phenomenological theory of the Anderson transition in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32725)
*Antonello Scardicchio,Carlo Vanoni*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文指出高维（d→∞）安德森局域化（Anderson localization）的近期结果可纳入"共形性丧失（conformality lost）"框架，即重整化群（RG）两个不动点碰撞并变为复数，从而使BKT型双参数标度流控制全部可观测量，并给出ε=1/d展开下的唯象RG方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于空间维度d→∞时安德森局域化的研究与"共形性丧失"及BKT型双参数标度流的猜想之间存在关联，本文旨在将二者统一框架化，并由此构造可用的微扰展开。

Method: 将高维安德森局域化问题映射到共形性丧失场景（RG两不动点碰撞并变为复数），据此论证Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless（BKT）型双参数标度流控制所有可观测量；并开展ε=1/d≪1的RG方程ε展开。

Result: 得到唯象的重整化群方程，该方程能够拟合d≳4维度的数值结果。

Conclusion: 高维安德森局域化可自然纳入共形性丧失与BKT型双参数标度框架，且基于ε=1/d展开的唯象RG方程在中高维（d≳4）与数值结果一致。

Abstract: We show that the recent works on Anderson localization when the number of spatial dimensions $d\to\infty$ can be framed into the scenario dubbed it conformality lost [Kaplan et al., Phys. Rev. D 80, 125005 (2009)], which occurs when two fixed points of the RG collide and become complex. This framing makes it natural for a two-parameter scaling flow à la Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) to control all the observables of the theory as conjectured in [Vanoni et al., PNAS 121, e2401955121 (2024)]. We also use this as a starting point for an $ε$ expansion of the RG equations in $ε=1/d\ll 1$, and find phenomenological RG equations which fit the numerical results in dimensions $d\gtrsim 4$.

</details>

### [58] [Machine learning for trigger, reconstruction and generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31311)
*Marco Giacalone*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该综述总结了 LHCP2026 前一年 ALICE、ATLAS、CMS 与 LHCb 在 LHC 数据处理全链条上的机器学习进展，涵盖生成式模拟加速、实时异常检测触发、量子化喷注标记器、基于经验标度律的味标记以及面向 HL-LHC 的 Transformer 与图神经网络重建算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习（machine learning, ML）已逐步嵌入 LHC 数据处理链的各个环节，从事件产生到最终重建；实验合作组近一年成果密集，需按工作流脉络进行系统梳理，以呈现当前技术前沿与面向高亮度 LHC（HL-LHC）的准备情况。

Method: 采用综述（review）形式，按实验工作流三阶段组织：事件产生与探测器模拟、实时触发选择（trigger selection）、在线/离线重建（online/offline reconstruction），汇总四大实验合作组公开的最新 ML 方法。

Result: 代表性成果包括：生成模型将 ALICE 特定探测器部件模拟加速最高达六个数量级；CMS AXOL1TL 与 ATLAS GELATO 无监督异常检测（anomaly detection）触发已在 Run 3 运行；面向 CMS Phase-2 Level-1 触发的量子化（quantised）Deep Sets 喷注标记器；LHCb 基于 ML 的喷注能量修正与味标记；ATLAS 首次给出基于 Transformer 的味标记器经验标度律（scaling laws）；以及 ATLAS GN3、MaskFormer、LHCb GarNet、CMS MLPF 等面向 HL-LHC 的重建算法。

Conclusion: ML 已从离线分析工具延伸至触发与模拟等在线环节并实现实际部署；Transformer 与图神经网络（graph neural network）成为 HL-LHC 重建的主流架构方向，标度律研究则提示味标记性能可被经验性预测。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) is increasingly embedded in every stage of the LHC data-processing chain, from event generation to final reconstruction. This contribution reviews the latest ML developments from the ALICE, ATLAS, CMS and LHCb collaborations that were made public in the year preceding LHCP2026, following the workflow of the experiments: event generation and detector simulation, real-time trigger selection, and online/offline reconstruction. Highlights include generative models that accelerate the simulation of specific ALICE detector components by up to six orders of magnitude, the CMS AXOL1TL and ATLAS GELATO unsupervised anomaly- detection triggers now operating in Run 3, a quantised Deep Sets jet tagger for the CMS Phase-2 Level-1 trigger, ML-based jet energy correction and flavour tagging at LHCb, the first empirical scaling laws for transformer-based flavour taggers in ATLAS, and several transformer and graph- based reconstruction algorithms (ATLAS GN3 flavour tagging and MaskFormer tracking, LHCb GarNet, CMS MLPF) developed for HL-LHC operation.

</details>

Conclusion: CEDVR可作为CMS物理学家实用的可视化工具，并借助内置样本事件服务于教育与公众传播；该工具面向消费级硬件，具有较好的可及性。

Abstract: Collision event displays are widely used in high-energy physics (HEP) experiments, enabling researchers to visualize and debug raw and reconstructed event data, and to communicate aspects of individual events to broader audiences. Such event displays are typically viewed on a conventional two-dimensional computer screen. To enhance immersion and interactivity with CMS data, we have developed a virtual reality (VR) software package named CEDVR (for CMS Event Display in Virtual Reality) that uses widely-available VR headsets and hand controllers. This application provides an immersive detector-coordinate view and a novel, complementary LEGO-style view. CEDVR provides easy access to physics object properties and object filtering, and incorporates novel VR features for navigation and object selection. Compatible with consumer-grade Quest headsets, CEDVR is a useful visualization tool for CMS physicists, and contains built-in sample events that support educational and public outreach initiatives.

</details>

*Tianyu Liang,Hulin Wang,Kai Chen,Dongliang Zhang,Chaosong Gao,Xiangming Sun,Feng Liu,Jun Liu,Hui Zhang,Chengui Lu,Yichen Yang,Chengxin Zhao,Hao Qiu*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该摘要报告 NνDEx 实验中基于高压 ⁸²SeF₆ 负离子时间投影室（time projection chamber, TPC）的双载流子到达时间分离方法，比较 FFT 去卷积（deconvolution）与直接波形拟合（waveform fitting）两种重建算法在模拟 Topmetal-S 电荷灵敏放大器（CSA）波形上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无中微子双贝塔衰变（neutrinoless double-beta decay）搜索需要无需外部触发即可确定事例纵向坐标的重建方案；在两载流子假设下，SeF₅⁻ 与 SeF₆⁻ 的到达时间分离可用于定位，但需评估不同信号处理算法的精度。

Method: 在固定放大器响应与采样 CSA 成形两种条件下，对模拟波形分别采用 FFT 去卷积与直接波形拟合，比较单点沉积接受率、到达时间分离 RMSE、数值深度刻度（depth calibration）后的 z 残差；并以 Geant4 事例进行全径迹（full-track）拟合，统计保留 pad 数与事um级深度 RMSE，同时以事件中心化 RMS 作为径迹形状诊断。

Result: 固定响应下直接拟合与 FFT 分别接受 873 个中 873 与 855 个单点沉积，共同事例上时间分离 RMSE 为 0.180 与 0.222 ms；深度刻度后 848 个共同事例的 z 残差均值为 0.036 与 0.075 cm，RMSE 为 0.192 与 0.208 cm。采样成形下两者接受数分别为 866/873 与 855；一个位于读出端附近、被直接拟合接受而 FFT 未接受的异常事例带来 184 ms 分离误差，使采样响应直接拟合总体 RMSE 升至 6.25 ms。全径迹拟合在 100 个 Geant4 事例中保留 1,810 个 pad 的 1,185 个，事件级深度 RMSE 中位数 0.184 cm，事件中心化 RMS 中位数 0.102 cm。

Conclusion: 固定响应下直接拟合在时间分离与深度精度上略优于 FFT 去卷积，但采样成形下对近读出端事例较为脆弱；绝对深度仍依赖公共事例时间，且需用探测器数据验证扩散（diffusion）、电子学噪声与 pad 间响应的影响。

Abstract: The N$ν$DEx experiment aims to search for neutrinoless double-beta decay of \(^{82}\)Se with a high-pressure \(^{82}\)SeF\(_6\) negative-ion time projection chamber. In the assumed two-carrier model, the arrival-time separation of SeF\(_5^-\) and SeF\(_6^-\) can estimate the longitudinal coordinate without an external trigger. We compare FFT deconvolution and direct waveform fitting on simulated Topmetal-S charge-sensitive-amplifier waveforms.
  With the fixed amplifier response, direct fitting and FFT accepted 873 and 855 of 873 geometry-selected single depositions, respectively. On the 855 common events, the arrival-time separation RMSEs were 0.180 and 0.222 ms. Numerical depth calibration gave mean \(z\) residuals of 0.036 and 0.075 cm and RMSEs of 0.192 and 0.208 cm on 848 common events. With sampled CSA shaping, FFT accepted the same 855 waveforms and direct fitting accepted 866 of 873. On the four-way common sample of 855 events, the direct-fit separation RMSE was 0.180~ms with fixed shaping and 0.217~ms with sampled shaping. An accepted near-readout direct fit outside the FFT sample had a 184~ms separation error, giving an RMSE of 6.25~ms over all accepted sampled-response direct fits.
  With the fixed response, the full-track fit retained 1,185 of 1,810 observed pads in 100 selected Geant4 events. The median event-level depth RMSE was 0.184 cm. Subtracting each event's mean Monte Carlo residual gave a median event-centred RMS of 0.102 cm as a diagnostic of relative track shape. The absolute depth remains sensitive to the common event time. Validation with detector data is needed to assess the effects of diffusion, electronics noise, and inter-pad response.

</details>

Abstract: The kagome superconductors AV3Sb5 (A=K, Rb, Cs) host multiband superconductivity (SC) within an unconventional charge density wave (CDW) parent phase, yet the relationship between the two remains unresolved. Through time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (tr-ARPES) experiments on CsV3Sb5 and KV3Sb5, we observe coherent band oscillations driven by coupling to a low-energy CDW amplitude mode at 3.1 THz. We find this coupling to be highly band-selective, differing by a factor of over 2 even between bands with dominant V-3d character. We reproduce these results with frozen-phonon density functional theory calculations, offering insight towards the relation between differing Fermi surface characters and our observed band selectivity. Furthermore, we observe differences in coupling channels between KV3Sb5 and CsV3Sb5. These results establish CDW amplitude mode fluctuations as a band-selective low-energy coupling channel that intimately contributes to the interplay between CDW and SC in the AV3Sb5 compounds.

### [62] [Tunneling signatures of the Lifshitz transition in a superconducting vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31371)
*Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该摘要研究清洁第二类超导体中涡旋线上 Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon 束缚态形成的一维微带在磁场诱导下的 Lifshitz 转变，理论上预测了零偏压电导在阈值场 B* 附近呈现一维 van Hove 奇异性，并指出涡旋核心内形成弱相互作用的一维自旋极化费米气体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋核心中最低微带与费米能级之间的微隙（minigap）E₀ ~ Δ²/E_F 可被塞曼能（Zeeman energy）补偿，从而驱动能带穿越费米面；此前缺乏针对该 Lifshitz 转变的隧穿谱理论及相互作用效应的系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在量子极限下对所有沿涡旋的动量数值求解 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 方程，并结合热展宽与仪器展宽计算局域态密度（local density of states）；进一步将屏蔽电子相互作用投影到被填充的微带上，求取 Luttinger 参数。

Result: 涡旋中心零偏压电导在 B* 以下缺失，在 B* 附近出现具有一维 van Hove 奇异性（~B⁻¹ᐟ²）的极大值，并在更高场下减弱；下一微带在偏离中心有限距离处产生第二个起始点。零偏压信号在中心由一种自旋投影承载，在周围环区由相反自旋投影承载；Luttinger 参数接近 1。

Conclusion: 涡旋核心内形成弱相互作用的一维费米气体，其准粒子通过类比量子线与量子霍尔边缘热化的三粒子碰撞（three-particle collisions）弛豫，相应弛豫率由解析方法给出；FeSe 的 B* 估计为几特斯拉，具备实验可及性。

Abstract: The Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon states bound to a vortex line in a clean type-II superconductor form one-dimensional minibands that disperse along the vortex. The lowest miniband is separated from the Fermi level by a minigap $E_0\simΔ^2/E_F$. A magnetic field shifts the minibands of the two spin projections in opposite directions. At the threshold field $B^*$ the Zeeman energy equals $E_0$. Estimates for FeSe place $B^*$ at a few tesla. The lowest miniband of one spin projection then crosses the Fermi level, and a spin-polarized one-dimensional Fermi sea forms inside the vortex core. We develop the theory of tunneling spectra across this Lifshitz transition. We solve the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations numerically in the quantum limit for all momenta along the vortex and compute the local density of states with thermal and instrumental broadening. The zero-bias conductance at the vortex center is absent below $B^*$. It has a maximum near $B^*$ with the inverse-square-root field dependence of a one-dimensional van Hove singularity and decreases at higher fields. The next miniband produces a second onset at a finite distance from the center. The zero-bias signal is carried by one spin projection at the center and by the opposite one in the surrounding ring. We then project the screened electron interaction onto the filled miniband. The resulting Luttinger parameter is close to unity, so the core hosts a weakly interacting one-dimensional Fermi gas. Its quasiparticles relax through three-particle collisions in analogy with thermalization in quantum wires and at quantum Hall edges. The corresponding relaxation rates are established analytically.

</details>


### [63] [Eigenvalue-based computation of Richardson pairing spectra and finite-size thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31531)
*Grzegorz Biskowski,Marcin R. Piatek,Franco Ferrari*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作提出一套求解约化BCS Richardson模型的数值流程，结合本征值型Bethe方程、Baxter多项式重构与复根求解，并在有限体系中计算正则热力学量以考察单粒子能级间距对热响应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Richardson模型在有限体系下的可解性，需要一个能给出完整seniority-zero谱并可靠提取rapidity的数值方案，以便研究配对强度与单粒子谱结构如何共同影响有限尺寸热响应。

Method: 以Newton-Raphson迭代求解本征值型变量（eigenvalue-based variables）的非线性Bethe方程，通过线性系统得到Baxter多项式系数，再用Laguerre迭代配合多项式降阶（polynomial deflation）提取rapidity；对等间距、二次型与类氢非简并单粒子谱随配对强度变化进行计算，并将基准谱与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）对比。

Result: 在八能级、四对粒子的类氢谱体系中，弱耦合下热容（heat capacity）出现两个极大值：随耦合增强，低温极大上移、高温极大下移，后者在使用单位下于g≈0.03515附近不再构成独立局域极大；而等间距基准谱在所展示的耦合区间内仅有一个主导极大。

Conclusion: 该对比表明单粒子能级间距可塑造有限尺寸热响应；C++代码已公开，但在病态构型附近及较大体系尺寸下仍可能存在数值不稳定性。

Abstract: We present a numerical workflow for the reduced BCS Richardson model, combining the eigenvalue-based Bethe equations, Baxter-polynomial reconstruction, and complex root finding. Newton-Raphson iteration solves the nonlinear equations for the eigenvalue-based variables; a linear system yields the polynomial coefficients, and Laguerre iteration with polynomial deflation extracts the rapidities. We apply the procedure to equally spaced, quadratic, and hydrogen-like non-degenerate single-particle spectra as the pairing strength varies. For finite systems, we calculate canonical observables from the complete seniority-zero spectrum and check benchmark spectra against exact diagonalization. In an eight-level, four-pair system with a hydrogen-like spectrum, the heat capacity has two maxima at weak coupling. With increasing coupling, the low-temperature maximum shifts upward and the high-temperature maximum downward; the latter ceases to be a distinct local maximum near $g\simeq0.03515$ in the units used. The equally spaced benchmark instead has a single dominant maximum over the coupling interval displayed for that spectrum. This comparison illustrates how single-particle level spacings can shape finite-size thermal response. The C++ code is publicly available; numerical instabilities remain possible near ill-conditioned configurations and at larger sizes.

</details>


### [64] [Majorana flat bands from mirror-particle-hole antisymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31597)
*Justin Schirmann,Martin Rodriguez-Vega,Alexander Cerjan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Majorana flat bands, zero modes across the whole edge Brillouin zone, usually rest on chiral symmetry. We show that a fully gapped class-D superconductor can host such a flat band without chiral symmetry or time reversal, provided its superconducting gap is odd under a mirror and translation along the edge is preserved. Combined with particle--hole conjugation, the mirror forms an antiunitary antisymmetry that squares to $+1$ and preserves the momentum along the edge, so it fixes the Kitaev index of every slice of fixed edge momentum. In a gapped bulk, this slice index cannot change with the edge momentum, and the edge zero modes form a band across the whole edge Brillouin zone. We construct a four-band model with exactly these symmetries, a helical $p$-wave superconductor with Rashba coupling in an in-plane exchange field, from which no term can be removed without restoring an additional symmetry. On the mirror lines, its two mirror sectors are exact Kitaev chains. This yields a closed-form phase diagram. Besides the flat band, the model hosts a phase with two Majoranas at each end of a wire, although its class-D index is trivial, and a second-order phase with Majorana corner modes. The spectral localizer detects all these indices in real space, directly on finite-size samples as relevant for experiments. On open rectangular flakes of two widths, it reproduces the phase diagram even though translational symmetry is broken. In the nodal regions, its reading depends on the flake size, so they are left unread. The readings persist under disorder several times the bulk gap. Without translation along the edge, the symmetries protect no more than Majorana parities, and we quantify the signatures that remain specific to the antisymmetry. A minimal model of magnetic adatoms on a triplet superconductor realizes the flat-band and second-order phases.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋极化金属中的超导对传统反自旋配对（opposite-spin pairing）构成挑战，尤其当磁场增强而非抑制超导时；石墨烯额外的谷自由度（valley degree of freedom）扩大了可能配对通道，使母金属的谷占据成为理解此类超导的关键。

Method: 在 hBN 对齐的 17 层菱面堆叠石墨烯中测量面内磁场下的输运；利用正常态量子振荡（quantum oscillations）提取费米口袋；结合计算分析母能带的空间分布与静电屏蔽效应；并分析邻近的非线性金属响应与超导临界电流特征。

Result: 量子振荡显示费米口袋不等，符合谷不平衡、自旋极化的母金属；计算表明母电子口袋主要局域于下表面，且在新增费米口袋出现时受静电屏蔽保护。超导在该区间持续存在，但当载流子被极化至 hBN 对齐表面（该处母能带发生强莫尔重构，moire reconstruction）时被抑制；邻近的非线性金属响应连续演化为超导临界电流特征，并在超导转变温度以上仍存在。

Conclusion: 谷不平衡与面内磁场增强超导的组合提示磁振子辅助等自旋谷间配对的唯象图像；结果为谷不平衡金属中的配对提供实验约束，并开辟通过层选择性控制母电子态以检验磁性配对机制的途径。

Abstract: Superconductivity in spin-polarized metals challenges conventional opposite-spin pairing, particularly when a magnetic field strengthens rather than suppresses it. In graphene, the additional valley degree of freedom broadens the possible pairing channels, making the valley occupation of the parent metal central to understanding such superconductivity. Here we report in-plane-field-enhanced superconductivity in hBN-aligned 17-layer rhombohedral graphene. Normal-state quantum oscillations reveal unequal Fermi pockets consistent with a valley-imbalanced, spin-polarized parent metal. Calculations show that the parent electron pocket is predominantly localized on the bottom surface and largely preserved by electrostatic screening as additional Fermi pockets emerge. Superconductivity persists in this regime but is suppressed when carriers are polarized toward the hBN-aligned surface, where the parent bands undergo strong moire reconstruction. An adjacent nonlinear metallic response evolves continuously into superconducting critical-current features and persists above the superconducting transition temperature. The combination of valley imbalance and in-plane-field-enhanced superconductivity motivates a phenomenological magnon-assisted equal-spin intervalley pairing scenario. These findings provide experimental constraints on pairing in valley-imbalanced metals and open a route to testing magnetic pairing mechanisms through layer-selective control of the parent electronic states.

</details>


### [66] [Magnetoelectric response to a Néel domain wall in spin-orbit coupled superconducting films: Spontaneous currents, vortex pinning, and vortex-antivortex nucleation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32379)
*P. J. F. Cavalcanti,M. D. Croitoru,A. I. Buzdin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文在近邻铁磁层且含界面Rashba自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）的薄超导薄膜中，解析研究了Néel畴壁（Néel domain wall）引发的磁电耦合（magnetoelectric coupling）、自发电流、相位响应与涡旋-畴壁相互作用，并发现畴壁可形成涡度选择性钉扎势阱及无外场下的涡旋-反涡旋对成核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性织构与破缺反演对称性共存时会产生磁电耦合与自发平衡电流，但Néel畴壁对超导薄膜的具体响应（相位、电流、磁场、涡旋钉扎与成核）缺乏解析刻画，本文旨在建立该情形的解析框架。

Method: 先以周期谐波回旋（periodic harmonic cycloid）磁织构作为可解析的参考问题，讨论Tc附近序参量（order parameter）修正及Tc以下的相位响应、Pearl屏蔽（Pearl screening）与涡旋相互作用；随后求解单个Néel畴壁，推导其平衡相位分布、自发电流、磁场及涡旋-壁相互作用能的解析表达式。

Result: Néel畴壁表现为扩展的磁电源（extended magnetoelectric source），其钉扎势（pinning landscape）具有涡度选择性，并由畴壁宽度与有效屏蔽长度（effective screening length）的竞争控制；足够强的磁电耦合可使束缚的涡旋-反涡旋对（vortex-antivortex pair）相对无涡旋态在零外场下能量更有利。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了自旋轨道耦合超导薄膜中Néel畴壁诱导磁电涡旋钉扎与涡旋-反涡旋成核的解析框架，可用于理解相关钉扎与成核机制。

Abstract: We study the superconducting response induced by a Néel domain wall in a thin superconducting film proximity-coupled to a ferromagnetic layer in the presence of interfacial Rashba spin-orbit coupling. The combined action of the magnetic texture and broken inversion symmetry generates a magnetoelectric coupling that induces spontaneous equilibrium currents. To establish the basic response to a continuously rotating magnetic texture, we first consider a periodic harmonic cycloid as an analytically tractable reference problem. Near Tc, this reference problem illustrates how the magnetoelectric coupling modifies the superconducting order parameter, while below Tc it provides a simple setting for describing the phase response, Pearl screening, and the interaction with a vortex. We then turn to the main problem of this work, a single Néel domain wall, and derive analytical expressions for its equilibrium phase profile, spontaneous current, magnetic field, and vortex-wall interaction energy.We show that the Néel wall acts as an extended magnetoelectric source and produces a vorticity-selective pinning landscape controlled by the competition between the wall width and the effective screening length. We further demonstrate that sufficiently strong magnetoelectric coupling can render a bound vortex-antivortex pair energetically favorable relative to the vortex-free state, even in the absence of an applied magnetic field. Our results provide an analytical framework for understanding magnetoelectric vortex pinning and vortex-antivortex nucleation by Néel domain walls in spin-orbit coupled superconducting films.

</details>


### [67] [From Fermions to Vortices: Microscopic Simulations of Phase Ordering in a d-Wave Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32916)
*Ali Rayat,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文基于绝热变分原理（adiabatic variational principle, AVP），构建了一个从微观费米子哈密顿量出发描述超导相位有序化的框架，并在二维d波超导体的数值实验中验证其刻画涡旋动力学与二维相位有序（phase ordering）普适动力学的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从微观费米子哈密顿量（microscopic fermionic Hamiltonian）出发描述由热浴诱导的超导相位有序化（bath-induced superconducting phase ordering）仍是重大难题，且现有方法多依赖现象学金兹堡-朗道（Ginzburg-Landau）自由能泛函假设。

Method: 采用近期提出的绝热变分原理（AVP），将演化中的空间非均匀键配对场（bond-pairing fields）映射到受约束的博戈留博夫-德让纳（Bogoliubov-de Gennes）态，并由拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multipliers）确定热力学驱动力，从而在保留微观电子结构的前提下实现超导序参量的耗散演化。

Result: 从无序配对构型出发，观测到涡旋-反涡旋湮灭（vortex-antivortex annihilation）引发的相位相干（phase coherence）涌现及局域电子谱的同步演化；涡旋衰减与动力学标度（dynamical scaling）重现了二维相位有序的特征动力学，构成框架的非平凡基准检验。

Conclusion: AVP形式与具体求解器无关（solver-independent），为超导体中耗散有序化与亚稳性（metastability）的微观研究开辟路径，并可推广至条纹序与配对密度波（pair-density-wave）等交织有序体系。

Abstract: Describing bath-induced superconducting phase ordering directly from a microscopic fermionic Hamiltonian remains a formidable challenge. Here we develop a microscopic framework for this problem using the recently introduced adiabatic variational principle (AVP) and demonstrate its application to a two-dimensional $d$-wave superconductor. The central construction maps evolving, spatially inhomogeneous bond-pairing fields onto constrained Bogoliubov-de Gennes states, with the associated Lagrange multipliers determining the thermodynamic driving forces. This enables dissipative evolution of the superconducting order while retaining its microscopic electronic structure, without assuming a phenomenological Ginzburg-Landau free-energy functional. Starting from disordered pairing configurations, we resolve the emergence of phase coherence through vortex-antivortex annihilation and the accompanying evolution of local electronic spectra. Vortex decay and dynamical scaling recover the characteristic kinetics of two-dimensional phase ordering, providing a nontrivial benchmark of the framework. The solver-independent AVP formulation opens a route to microscopic studies of dissipative ordering and metastability in superconductors with intertwined stripe and pair-density-wave orders.

</details>


### [68] [Topological Anderson Superconductor in Landau Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32988)
*Uddalok Nag,Koji Kudo,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文预测：在Rashba自旋轨道耦合的常规二维超导体中，引入Anderson型在位无序可在不同Chern数的超导态转变区实现拓扑超导渗流，形成“拓扑Anderson超导态”，为无序诱导拓扑非平庸态提供了新例证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有奇数Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）Chern数的二维超导体因预言存在Majorana零模而备受关注，而磁场产生的Landau能级（Landau levels, LL）本身携带非零Chern数，因此自然的问题是：常规自旋费米子超导体能否仅靠外加磁场驱动进入拓扑态。

Method: 对具有Rashba自旋轨道耦合与在位吸引相互作用的体系自洽求解BdG方程，得到以化学势等为参数的相图；进而引入Anderson型在位无序（可展宽并混合Landau能级），考察其在超导态转变区的作用。

Result: 自洽解显示相图几乎处处为具有偶数整数Chern数的超导态，随化学势等参数变化在不同Chern数之间跃迁；在转变区引入Anderson型在位无序后，出现拓扑超导渗流（percolation）的态，即拓扑Anderson超导态。

Conclusion: 磁场单独作用倾向于给出偶数Chern数的平庸超导态，而无序可在相变区诱导拓扑超导渗流，表明无序同样是产生拓扑非平庸态的一条途径。

Abstract: There is much interest in two-dimensional superconductors with odd integer values of the Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) Chern number $C_{\rm BdG}$, often termed topological superconductors, as these are predicted to harbor Majorana zero modes. Because a magnetic field produces Landau levels (LL) with nonzero Chern numbers, it is natural to ask if a conventional two-dimensional superconductor of spinful fermions with Rashba spin-orbit coupling can be driven topological by application of a magnetic field. Self-consistent solutions of the BdG equations for an on-site attractive interaction produce a phase diagram consisting almost everywhere of superconducting states with even integer values of $C_{\rm BdG}$, transitioning from one value to another as parameters such as the chemical potential are varied. We predict that introducing Anderson-type on-site disorder, which broadens as well as mixes LLs, produces, in the transition region, states wherein topological superconductivity percolates. This topological Anderson superconducting state provides another example where disorder can produce topologically nontrivial states.

</details>


### [69] [Multistable Thermoelectric Josephson Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33041)
*Filippo Antola,Margherita Cervi,Angelo Greco,Alessandro Braggio,Giorgio De Simoni,Francesco Giazotto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作构建了一种以热驱动为核心的自动机（automaton），其元件为间隙不对称超导隧道结（gap-asymmetric superconducting tunnel junctions），既能将热量主动转换为电功率（最高 2.6 pW），又能以热稳定多稳态并编码偏置扫描方向与历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多稳态系统（multistable systems）虽可存储与处理信息，但其元件通常仅耗散驱动能量；本文旨在使元件在承担信息功能的同时主动将热能转换为电能，实现“产电型”计算元件。

Method: 采用间隙不对称超导隧道结作为基本元件，在固定加热条件下工作于零偏置或电流偏置，并叠加负载；利用磁场可调的约瑟夫森电流（Josephson current）控制可用状态，通过两个共享加热岛的结实现偏置的滞回分配。

Result: 元件在固定加热下于任一极性可输出最高 2.6 pW；零偏置下热即可稳定状态，且各状态将发电与耗散导向不同结。两个共享加热岛的结在零偏置时亦锁定于相反电压，从而编码扫描方向；在负载与电流偏置下，自动机可访问涵盖结极性组合（含零电压）的多种状态。

Conclusion: 磁场决定哪些状态可用，偏置历史决定实际占据的状态，表明该热-电耦合超导结自动机可同时实现能量转换、多稳态存储与历史依赖的状态选择。

Abstract: Multistable systems can store and process information, yet their elements usually only burn the drive energy. Here, we build an automaton whose elements actively convert heat into electrical power. Heat alone stabilizes the states at zero bias, and each state routes generation and dissipation to different junctions. The elements, gap-asymmetric superconducting tunnel junctions, deliver up to 2.6 pW at either polarity under fixed heating and support a magnetic-field-tunable Josephson current. Two junctions sharing a heated island split the bias hysteretically and, even at zero bias, lock into opposite voltages encoding the sweep direction. With a load and current bias, the automaton accesses states spanning junction polarity combinations, including zero voltage; the magnetic field sets which are available and the bias history selects the occupied one.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画三维静电系统（electrostatic systems）即带宇宙学常数的 Einstein–Maxwell 方程静态解的黎曼几何结构，在尽量弱的假设下获得分类与刚性结果。

Method: 采用局部面积极小曲面作为几何分析工具：考察视界（horizons）上有边界的此类曲面（为全测地半球）以及内部闭合的此类曲面，据此推导系统的结构限制。

Conclusion: 三维静电系统具有强刚性：视界面积、稳定性、面积–电荷不等式与拓扑形式均被严格约束，带电 Nariai 系统为临界情形，且非紧系统在严格平均凸端条件下仅有一个端。

Abstract: We study three-dimensional electrostatic systems, the Riemannian data of static solutions of the Einstein--Maxwell equations with cosmological constant $Λ$, by means of locally area-minimizing surfaces. Such a surface with non-empty boundary on the horizons is a totally geodesic hemisphere and forces the de Sitter system. A closed one in the interior forces a system of product type, one of eight models if the scalar curvature is somewhere positive on it, among them the Bertotti--Robinson, Nariai and cold and ultracold black hole systems. No compactness, asymptotic or pinching assumption is needed. In particular, closed systems without horizons are flat or compact quotients of the Nariai--Bertotti--Robinson system. For compact systems with connected horizon, the electric field vanishes even when the system is not simply connected, and the area of the horizon is either $12π/Λ$, for de Sitter, or at most $4π/Λ$. With several horizons, every horizon whose area does not exceed the sum of the others is stable, minimizes area in its homology class and satisfies an area--charge inequality, with equality only for the charged Nariai systems; its charged Hawking mass and charge therefore lie in the parameter region of the Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter black holes. Compact systems with horizons are spherical space forms with balls removed. Noncompact systems with strictly mean-convex ends have one end, and their horizons are stable and $π_1$-injective and satisfy a strict area--charge inequality.

</details>

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究给定边界 Bartnik 数据的闭合 $(n-1)$ 维流形何时能成为满足主能量条件（dominant energy condition, DEC）的紧致 $n$ 维初始数据集的边界，给出充分条件并应用于内质量下界、带边界旋量初值数据的正质量问题及类 Penrose 不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非负标量曲率填充（non-negative scalar curvature fill-in）问题推广到时空情形，即在指定边界度量与平均曲率（Bartnik 数据）下刻画 DEC 填充的存在性。

Method: 对 Bartnik 数据施加充分条件，构造以内视界（inner MOTS boundary）为边界的满足 DEC 的紧致初始数据集；并据此推导若干应用。

Result: 得到 Bartnik–Bray 内质量（Bartnik–Bray inner mass）的下界（经重新表述为真正的时空定义）；证明带边界且边界不必为陷阱（trapped）的旋量初始数据的正质量定理；并在假定时空 Penrose 不等式成立时给出以边界 Bartnik 数据表示质量正下界的类 Penrose 不等式。

Conclusion: DEC 填充的存在性为边界数据与时空质量之间提供统一框架，可导出内质量界、带边界正质量定理及类 Penrose 不等式。

Abstract: Given a closed $(n-1)$-dimensional manifold equipped with prescribed spacetime Bartnik data, we ask when this can be realised as the boundary of a compact $n$-dimensional initial data set satisfying the dominant energy condition (DEC). This is a natural generalisation of the problem of non-negative scalar curvature fill-ins, where the boundary metric and mean curvature are prescribed. We give conditions on the Bartnik data that suffice to prove the existence of such DEC fill-ins with inner MOTS boundary, and give some applications of these fill-ins.
  Our first application of these is a lower bound on the Bartnik--Bray inner mass, which we slightly reformulate here to be a genuine spacetime definition. Our other applications include proving a positive mass theorem for spin initial data with boundary that need not be trapped, and a spacetime Penrose-like inequality that gives a positive lower bound on the mass of an initial data set with boundary in terms of its boundary Bartnik data assuming the spacetime Penrose inequality holds.

</details>


### [72] [Energy and topology of Ricci-flat 4-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33132)

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明：任意完备 Ricci 平坦（Ricci-flat）4-流形 $(M,g)$ 上，曲率算子（curvature operator）的 $L^2$ 范数 $\frac{1}{8\pi^2}\int_M|R_g|_g^2\,dv_g$ 与第二贝蒂数（second Betti number）$b_2(M)$ 之差被一个通用常数 $C$ 控制，且无需体积增长、无穷远模型或曲率衰减等假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在极弱假设（不假定体积增长、无穷远处模型或曲率衰减）下，建立完备 Ricci 平坦 4-流形的 $L^2$ 曲率范数与拓扑不变量 $b_2(M)$ 之间的定量联系，从而对奇异 4-流形（如 exotic $\mathbb R^4$）的度量存在性给出限制。

Method: 证明改进了 Cheeger–Gromov 与 Cheeger–Tian 的"切块法（chopping method）"，其核心是对穷竭流形的各区域边界之拓扑进行定量控制。

Result: 得到双边不等式 $b_2(M)-C\leqslant\frac{1}{8\pi^2}\int_M|R_g|_g^2\,dv_g\leqslant b_2(M)+C$；由此推出不可数多个 exotic $\mathbb R^4$ 不容许完备 Ricci 平坦度量，且具有有限 $b_2$ 的完备非紧超凯勒（hyperkähler）度量的端（ends）可由 Sun–Zhang 的结果完全分类。

Conclusion: 该结果将 $L^2$ 曲率能量与 $b_2$ 拓扑量在极弱假设下联系起来，并为 Bamler 分解中长生四维 Ricci 流（Ricci flows）的"薄部（thin part）"提供了信息。

Abstract: We show that there is $C>0$ such that any complete Ricci-flat $4$-manifold $(M,g)$ satisfies $$ b_2(M)-C\leqslant\frac1{8π^2}\int_M|R_g|_g^2\,dv_g\leqslant b_2(M)+C, $$ where $R$ is the curvature operator. No assumption is made on volume growth, model at infinity or curvature decay. It follows that uncountably many exotic $\mathbb R^4$ admit no complete Ricci-flat metric, and that complete noncompact hyperkähler metrics with finite $b_2$ have ends classified by Sun-Zhang. This also provides information about the thin part of immortal four-dimensional Ricci flows in Bamler's decomposition. The proof refines the chopping method of Cheeger-Gromov and Cheeger-Tian by bounding the topology of boundaries of regions exhausting the manifold.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文研究带理想边界与一个锥奇点的圆盘上双曲0-度量的Teichmüller空间，证明其对所有β>0同构于S^1\Diff(S^1)，并通过Atiyah-Bott构造得到自然闭2-形式ω_β：非整数β时为辛形式并对应椭圆Virasoro余伴随轨道，正整数β=n时为预辛形式并下降到例外Virasoro轨道上的KKS辛形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画带锥奇点（锥角θ=2πβ）的圆盘双曲0-度量所构成Teichmüller空间的几何与辛结构，并厘清其与Virasoro余伴随轨道（Virasoro coadjoint orbit）之间的对应关系，尤其是整数β时出现的例外情形。

Method: 对所有β>0建立Teich^β(\overline{D})≅S^1\Diff(S^1)的同构，并借助Atiyah-Bott构造诱导出自然闭2-形式（closed 2-form）ω_β；随后分析非整数β与整数β=n两种情形下该形式的性质，并讨论锥奇点度量的无穷小等距（infinitesimal isometry）李代数sl_2(R)及其积分行为。

Result: 非整数β时ω_β为辛形式（symplectic form），Teich^β(\overline{D})可等同于相应的椭圆Virasoro余伴随轨道；正整数β=n时ω_β为预辛形式（presymplectic form），并下降到例外Virasoro轨道PSU^(n)(1,1)\Diff(S^1)上的Kirillov-Kostant-Souriau辛形式。无穷小等距由sl_2(R)给出，其在边界上积分为PSU^(n)(1,1)，但当n>1时在内部不成立。

Conclusion: 相应Teichmüller空间的预辛结构能够恢复出边界上的群PSU^(n)(1,1)，并下降到对应的Virasoro余伴随轨道，这一现象被视为值得注意的结果。

Abstract: We study Teichmüller spaces of hyperbolic \(0\)-metrics on a disk with ideal boundary and a marked point carrying a conical singularity with angle $θ= 2πβ$. For all $β>0$, we show that $\rm{Teich}^β(\overline{\mathbb D})\cong S^1\backslash \rm{Diff}(S^1)$, and we construct natural closed 2-forms $ω_β$ induced by the Atiyah-Bott construction. For non-integer $β$, the form $ω_β$ is symplectic, and one can identify $\rm{Teich}^β(\overline{\mathbb D})$ with the corresponding elliptic Virasoro coadjoint orbit.
  For positive integers $β=n$, $ω_β$ is presymplectic, and it descends to the Kirillov-Kostant-Souriau symplectic form on the exceptional Virasoro orbit $\mathrm{PSU}^{(n)}(1,1)\backslash\rm{Diff}(S^1)$. Infinitesimal isometries of a hyperbolic \(0\)-metric on a disk with a conical singularity with angle $θ=2πn$ are given by the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb R)$ which integrates to the group $\mathrm{PSU}^{(n)}(1,1)$ on the boundary of the disk but not in the interior for $n>1$. It is remarkable that the presymplectic structure of the corresponding Teichmüller spaces recovers this group and descends to the corresponding Virasoro coadjoint orbit.

</details>

### [74] [Parton Physics in the Schwinger Model from Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31749)
*Mari Carmen Bañuls,Krzysztof Cichy,C. -J. David Lin,Manuel Schneider*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作提出在哈密顿量框架下、直接于闵可夫斯基空间（Minkowski space）利用张量网络（tensor networks）计算部分子分布函数（PDFs）、光锥分布振幅（LCDAs）与衰变常数；在施温格模型（Schwinger model）中验证了归一化、动量求和规则与对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDFs 与 LCDAs 由含光前方向威尔逊线（Wilson line）的矩阵元定义，难以在欧几里得格点（Euclidean lattice）计算中直接获得，因此需要发展可直接在闵可夫斯基空间求解部分子函数的途径。

Method: 采用哈密顿量框架，以张量网络（键维 bond dimension 系统性控制表达力）表示希尔伯特空间态；将光前威尔逊线实现为格点上的逐步演化，即外部电荷的空间平移与作用于矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）的 Trotter 化时间演化步交替进行；通过改变并外推格点间距、体积、键维、规范场截断与 Trotter 步长来量化系统误差。

Result: 在施温格模型中针对多个费米子质量计算了费米子与反费米子 PDFs、LCDAs 及矢量介子衰变常数；纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）增长缓慢使中等键维已足够；所得分布满足预期的归一化、动量求和规则与对称性质。

Conclusion: 结果表明张量网络方法可用于更一般情形下光前矩阵元的计算，为在闵可夫斯基空间中直接获取部分子函数提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) encode the non-perturbative structure of hadrons in terms of their partonic constituents. They are defined through matrix elements containing parton operators that are connected by a Wilson line along a light-front direction, which makes them difficult to access in Euclidean lattice calculations. We demonstrate how parton functions can be directly obtained in Minkowski space using the Hamiltonian framework. We compute fermion and antifermion PDFs, LCDAs, and decay constants of the vector meson in the Schwinger model for several fermion masses. States in Hilbert space are efficiently represented by tensor networks, with an expressivity that is systematically controlled by a bond dimension. The light-front Wilson line is implemented as a stepwise evolution on the lattice that alternates spatial shifts of an external charge with Trotterized time-evolution steps acting on a matrix product state. All systematic uncertainties are quantified by varying and extrapolating in the lattice spacing, volume, bond dimension, gauge-field truncation, and Trotter step width. The slow growth of the entanglement entropy makes moderate bond dimensions sufficient, which supports the feasibility of using tensor networks for computing light-front matrix elements in more general settings. We confirm that the computed distributions fulfill the expected normalizations, momentum sum rules, and symmetry properties.

</details>


### [75] [Prospects and limitations of adaptive variational quantum algorithms for lattice field theories: a case study in 2+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33138)
*Nico Dichter,Andrea Bulgarelli,Lena Funcke,Stefan Kühn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究变分量子算法 ADAPT-VQE 能否从 1+1 维格点规范理论推广到更高维度，并识别出其两类一般性局限，随后在 2+1 维自由 Wilson 费米子模型上给出首次应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADAPT-VQE 此前仅用于制备 1+1 维格点规范理论的真空态，其向更高维度的可扩展性尚不明确，需要系统考察算法在高维格点场论中的适用性与潜在障碍。

Method: 分析并指出 ADAPT-VQE 的两类局限：其一，当量子比特哈密顿量与初始态均为实数（即满足量子比特层次的时间反演（time-reversal）不变性）时，所有实值算符池（operator pool）算符的梯度消失而永不被选中；其二，算法可能收敛到激发本征态。并以 2+1 维自由 Wilson 费米子（free Wilson fermions）作为首个格点场论应用实例，通过向算符池加入电荷共轭对称性（charge conjugation symmetry）及组合不同算符池与合适初始态进行改进。

Result: 在 2+1 维自由 Wilson 费米子中两类障碍均实际出现；加入电荷共轭对称性可改善结果，而组合不同算符池并配合合适初始态，可在整个质量范围内同时恢复基态与第一激发态。

Conclusion: 研究结果为将 ADAPT-VQE 应用于 1+1 维以外的格点场论（lattice field theories）提供了实用指导准则。

Abstract: The variational quantum algorithm ADAPT-VQE has previously been used to prepare the vacuum of lattice gauge theories in $1+1D$. In this paper, we study whether it extends to higher dimensions. We identify two general limitations of ADAPT-VQE. First, if the qubit Hamiltonian and the initial state are real, i.e., invariant under qubit-level time-reversal, all real-valued pool operators have vanishing gradients and are never selected. Second, the algorithm can converge to excited eigenstates. We illustrate both limitations, also providing examples from literature, and present a first application of ADAPT-VQE to a lattice field theory in $2+1D$, namely free Wilson fermions, where both obstacles occur. Adding charge conjugation symmetry to the operator pool improves the results, and combining different pools with suitable initial states recovers the ground and first excited states over the full mass range. These findings give practical guidelines for applying ADAPT-VQE to lattice field theories beyond $1+1D$.

</details>

*Abdelhak Djouadi,John Ellis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究低 tanβ 下重中性希格斯共振信号与 QCD 背景的干涉效应，指出其在现有 LHC 排除限中影响有限，但在高亮度 LHC 探测的质量区间将变得重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LHC 对最小超对称标准模型中重中性希格斯玻色子的限制基于窄宽度近似（narrow-width approximation），未包含纯希格斯信号与 QCD 背景之间的干涉，而该干涉在低 tanβ 时尤为相关。

Method: 在领头阶（leading order）但保留精确顶夸克质量依赖的条件下，计算 CP 偶 H 与 CP 奇 A 共振贡献同非共振胶子聚变振幅在 gg→VV、gg→hh 与 gg→Zh 过程中的干涉；纵向振幅由 Goldstone 玻色子等效定理（Goldstone-boson equivalence theorem）获得。

Result: 四个搜寻道的线形由共振与非共振振幅峰值之比这一复参数 κ 决定；由于 H、A 与末态耦合弱并被顶夸克衰变展宽，在 0.5–1 TeV 区间 |κ| 降至几个量级，干涉使信号率最多提升数十个百分点并扭曲线形。

Conclusion: 对当前 ATLAS（及推测的 CMS）Run II 排除限，净效应总体较小，仅在 H→hh 轮廓的高质量端较明显；但对高亮度 LHC 将探测的质量区间，该干涉效应变得重要。

Abstract: The LHC limits on the heavy neutral Higgs bosons of the minimal supersymmetric extension of the Standard Model, which are particularly relevant at low values of the ratio of vacuum expectation values $\tanβ$, have been obtained in the narrow-width approximation, without including the interference between the pure Higgs signal and the QCD background. We study the interference of the resonant heavy neutral CP-even $H$ and CP-odd $A$ boson contributions with the non-resonant gluon-fusion amplitudes in the processes $gg\to VV$ with $V=W,Z$, $gg \to hh$ and $gg\to Zh$ at low $\tanβ$, $1\le \tanβ\le 6$. We work at leading order but with the exact top-quark mass dependence, and with the longitudinal $VV$ and $Zh$ amplitudes obtained from the Goldstone-boson equivalence theorem that lead to loop form factors similar to those appearing in $gg\to hh$. The lineshapes in the four search channels are governed by a simple complex parameter $κ$, the ratio of the resonant to non-resonant amplitudes at the peak. Because the $H$ and $A$ bosons couple weakly to the considered final states, while being broadened by their decays to top quarks, $|κ|$ drops to values of a few for masses of $0.5$--$1$ TeV, even though the total widths remain at the percent level. The interference then increases the signal rates by up to tens of per cent, and distorts their lineshapes. For the present ATLAS (and presumably CMS) Run II exclusion limits, the net effect is nevertheless rather modest, except at the high-mass end of the $H\to hh$ contour, but it becomes important for the masses that will be probed at the high-luminosity LHC.

</details>

*Abdelhak Djouadi,John Ellis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，若存在与顶夸克耦合较大的赝标希格斯玻色子 $A$（质量接近 $2m_t$），它必然与顶偶素（toponium）在颜色单态 ${}^1S_0$ 通道发生干涉；干涉由单一复数参数 $\kappa$ 控制，幺正性（unitarity）要求 ${\rm Im}\,\kappa=-1$，导致 $A$ 共振线型被完全扭曲，阈值超出不能视为顶偶素与 $A$ 贡献的简单相加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS 与 ATLAS 在阈值附近观测到 $t\bar t$ 事件超出，与赝标顶偶素态 $\eta_t$ 的形成相符；而耦合较大的赝标希格斯玻色子 $A$ 也可能贡献部分超出并隐藏于其中。作者意在指出这种情形下 $A$ 与顶偶素之间不可忽略的干涉效应。

Method: 采用领头阶精确振幅（其相对相位由幺正性固定），并将库仑重求和（Coulomb resummation）相干地应用于 QCD 背景、$A$ 信号及其干涉。结果表明线型由峰值处共振与非共振振幅之比这一单一复数 $\kappa$ 控制。

Result: 当 $A$ 衰变为顶夸克时幺正性严格给出 ${\rm Im}\,\kappa=-1$，干涉使共振速率降低 $2/|\kappa|^2$，并在略高于 $M_A$ 处产生 ${}^1S_0$ 振幅的精确零点。$\kappa$ 与 $A$ 的顶夸克耦合无关；计入库仑增强宽度后，$M_A=350$–$380$ GeV 时 $|\kappa|\approx1.5$–$1.7$，$A$ 共振被完全扭曲。

Conclusion: 若 $A$ 解释观测超出的 50%（25%），它会使顶偶素峰增强约 10%–70%（5%–35%）；阈值超出与顶偶素和 $A$ 贡献之和存在显著差异，因此简单叠加的解读不成立。

Abstract: The CMS and ATLAS collaborations have observed an excess of $t\bar t$ events near threshold that is consistent with the formation of the pseudoscalar toponium state $η_t$. A pseudoscalar Higgs boson $A$ with a large coupling to top quarks could contribute part of this excess and the presence of the $A$ boson could be concealed. We point out that, if such a state exists with $M_A$ close to $2m_t$, it necessarily interferes with toponium, since both live in the colour--singlet ${}^1S_0$ channel and share the same Coulomb final--state interaction. Using exact leading--order amplitudes, whose relative phase is fixed by unitarity, and Coulomb resummation applied coherently to the QCD background, the $A$ boson signal and their interference, we show that the line shape is controlled by a single complex number $κ$, the ratio of the resonant and non--resonant amplitudes at the peak. Unitarity fixes ${\rm Im}\,κ=-1$ exactly when $A$ decays into top quarks, so that the interference reduces the resonant rate by $2/|κ|^2$ and produces an exact zero of the ${}^1S_0$ amplitude just above $M_A$. The parameter $κ$ does not depend on the $A$ coupling to top quarks and, with the Coulomb--enhanced width we find $|κ|\approx1.5$--$1.7$ for $M_A=350$--$380$ GeV and, hence, the $A$ resonance is completely distorted. For an $A$ boson accounting for 50\% (25\%) of the observed excess, it enhances the toponium peak by about 10 to 70\% (5 to 35\%), and the threshold excess differs strongly from the sum of the toponium and $A$ contributions.

</details>

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究翘曲额外维度（warped extra dimension）中产生的暗物质（dark matter, DM），指出其在红外端表现为 Z' 型门户（Z' portal），并探讨弱耦合、强耦合及超对称（supersymmetry）扩展三类情形下暗物质的可探测性，认为未来 CTAO 等高能天文观测有望给出首个额外维度迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来直接探测将进入中微子雾（neutrino fog）、间接探测可达 TeV 级热遗迹，可能迎来 WIMP 暗物质搜寻的最后一轮推动；作者希望研究这类实验所能发现的、源于翘曲额外维度的暗物质模型。

Method: 在自下而上（bottom-up）框架下将红外端暗物质建模为 Z' 型门户，分析电弱耦合、TeV 级质量暗物质的可存活性与可探测性，并讨论其由翘曲额外维度大统一理论（GUT）完成的紫外完备化，以及受超复合希格斯（Super-Composite Higgs）启发引入超对称的扩展方案。

Result: 电弱耦合、TeV 级质量的暗物质在自下而上框架中依然可行且可被探测，但需暗物质与 Z' 质量间约 20% 的调节（tuning）；若二者强耦合且更重（约 10 TeV）则可放宽调节。强耦合 Z' 门户恰由翘曲额外维度 GUT 紫外完备化，暗物质稳定性是抑制质子衰变的副产品，KK 标度约 10 TeV。所识别的变体中除重子数（baryon-number）规范玻色子外所有规范场与暗物质耦合被压低，显著压低直接探测率但仍可通过间接探测发现。

Conclusion: 为缓解约 10 TeV KK 标度带来的小等级问题（little hierarchy problem），在额外维度 GUT 框架中引入超对称后同样存在暗物质候选者，其媒介为弱耦合、TeV 质量的 Z'。所有情形下近期发现均有可能，例如借助已运行的切伦科夫望远镜阵列天文台（CTAO），提示额外维度的首个线索可能来自高能天空。

Abstract: The coming years will see what could be the final push in the search for WIMP dark matter (DM), with direct detection reaching into the neutrino fog and indirect detection reaching TeV-scale thermal relics. We study a class of models that these experiments could discover: DM emerging from a warped extra dimension. In the IR, these scenarios appear generically as a $Z'$-type portal. We show that DM with electroweak couplings and TeV scale masses remains viable and discoverable within such a bottom-up context. However, the models require a 20\% tuning between the DM and $Z'$ masses, a tuning that is relaxed if the DM and $Z'$ are strongly coupled and heavier ($\sim$$10$ TeV). In fact, it is the strongly-coupled $Z'$-type portal which is UV-completed by the framework of a warped extra-dimensional Grand Unified Theory (GUT), where DM stability is a spin-off of suppressing proton decay, resulting in a $\sim$$10$ TeV Kaluza-Klein (KK) scale. We identify a variant of this framework where all gauge fields except a baryon-number gauge boson have suppressed couplings to the DM; this model significantly suppresses the direct detection rate, but it remains discoverable through indirect detection. Further, to address the little hierarchy problem associated with $\sim$$10$ TeV KK scale, inspired by the Super-Composite Higgs, we augment our extra-dimensional GUT setup with supersymmetry. We show that this model also possesses a DM candidate, now with a weakly-coupled and $\sim$${\rm TeV}$ mass $Z'$ mediator. In all cases, a near-term discovery remains possible, for instance with the now online Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO), suggesting that our first hint of extra dimensions could emerge from the high-energy sky.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密卡西米尔实验（precision Casimir experiments）对短程力（short range forces）极为敏感，但此前仅能约束与自旋无关的相互作用（spin-independent interactions），轴子中介的自旋相关力尚未被直接探测。

Conclusion: 反向极化磁补偿源差分测量使卡西米尔实验成为直接搜寻轴子中介单极-偶极力的有效手段，显著拓展对自旋相关短程相互作用的探测能力。

Abstract: Precision Casimir experiments enable highly sensitive probes of short range forces, but have thus far constrained only spin-independent interactions. We show that measurements between reversed polarizations of a magnetically compensated source enable direct searches of axion-mediated monopole-dipole forces. The spin force reverses sign, while the common Casimir, gravitational and electrostatic backgrounds broadly cancel. Compensated sources need no intervening shielding, allowing large probes at micrometer gaps. Casimir experiments could probe the couplings $g_p^e g_s^{N}$ and $g_p^e g_s^{e}$ at $λ\sim1-100~μ{\rm m}$ orders of magnitude beyond existing laboratory searches.

</details>

The resulting four-lepton final state separates into the charge-balanced channel $e^+e^-μ^+μ^-$ and the same-sign same-flavour channels $e^\pm e^\pmμ^\mpμ^\mp$. Both channels thus probe couplings below the present bound. The two signal rates are equal before selection; the difference in reach follows from the different backgrounds.

### [81] [When heavy vectors meet top partners: HVTP, a unified framework for composite Higgs searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31393)
*Natascia Vignaroli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文为复合希格斯模型（composite Higgs models）中矢量共振态（vector resonances）与顶夸克伴侣（top partners）提供统一描述框架，并公开发布MadGraph的UFO模型HVTP，将最新LHC搜索结果映射到模型参数空间，指出自然情形下现有搜寻灵敏度显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量共振态与顶夸克伴侣同属复合希格斯模型的强耦合扇区，且部分复合性（partial compositeness）将其相互作用与产生顶夸克质量的混合联系起来，但实验搜寻通常将二者分开处理，缺乏统一框架。

Result: 在自然情景中顶夸克伴侣轻于矢量共振态，矢量主要衰变为伴侣而变宽，标准搜寻灵敏度大幅下降，而矢量介导的伴侣对产生率可超过QCD产生率；基准点上（伴侣接近现有上限、电弱三重态约4 TeV）73–83%的三重态衰变含伴侣，矢量介导产生最高达QCD率的三倍。

Conclusion: HL-LHC可通过扩展至大宽度的共振搜寻及含共振产生的伴侣对搜寻探测该区域，后者亦可揭示伴侣谱；非极小陪集（non-minimal coset）情形下的赝标量（pseudoscalar）会改变顶夸克伴侣的标准衰变，已纳入HVTP_eta扩展。

Abstract: Vector resonances and top partners are characteristic new states of composite Higgs models: they belong to the same strongly coupled sector, and partial compositeness ties their interactions to the mixings that generate the top mass. Experimental searches, however, usually treat them separately. We provide a common framework for both and release HVTP (Heavy Vectors and Top Partners), a public UFO model for MadGraph in which an electroweak triplet, a hypercharge vector and a colour octet couple to a custodial top-partner multiplet, with all couplings obtained from the exact diagonalisation of the mass matrices. The models and the inputs of our results are available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24345218. We translate the latest LHC searches at 13 and 13.6 TeV into the parameter space of the model. In natural scenarios the top partners, which are directly tied to the Higgs mass and to the residual tuning, are lighter than the vector resonances; the vectors then decay mostly into partners and become broad: the standard searches lose much of their sensitivity, while the vectors produce partner pairs at rates that can exceed QCD production. For example, at our benchmark points, with partners near their present limits and an electroweak triplet at about 4 TeV, 73-83% of the triplet decays contain partners, and vector-mediated production reaches up to three times the QCD rate. At the HL-LHC this region can be probed by resonance searches extended to large widths and by partner-pair searches that include resonant production; the latter also reveal the partner spectrum. </details>


### [82] [OmniX: A first jet and event model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31403)
*Anna Hallin,Gregor Kasieczka,Henning Rose*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: OmniX 是一种可在单一模型中同时处理单喷注（single jet）与完整事例（full event）的基础模型架构，在喷注级与事例级数据集上联合训练后，其生成与分类性能可持平或超越仅喷注或仅事例的同等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粒子物理基础模型原型多仅作用于单喷注的组成粒子，既难以支撑完整事例尺度的物理问题（如区分不同物理过程），也因无法捕捉全局关联（global correlations）而限制性能。

Method: 提出 OmniX 架构，在同一个模型内统一处理单喷注与完整事例，并采用喷注级数据集 JetClass、Top-tagging 与事例级数据集 Collide-2V 进行联合训练与评估。

Result: 在生成（generation）与分类（classification）任务上，OmniX 联合训练于单喷注和完整事例后，性能与同等条件的纯喷注模型（jet-only）和纯事例模型（event-only）持平或更优。

Conclusion: 通过单一模型跨尺度覆盖喷注与事例，OmniX 使基础模型得以服务于完整事例尺度的物理问题，同时保持甚至提升下游任务表现，体现出联合训练的可摊销收益。

Abstract: Foundation models are a new approach to building machine learning algorithms for particle physics. Their central idea is that a single model can be adapted to a variety of downstream tasks, amortizing training costs. So far, most foundation model prototypes have operated on the constituents of a single jet. This limits their usefulness for physics problems at the scale of full events, such as distinguishing different processes, and restricts their performance, as global correla- tions are invisible to the model. Here, we introduce OmniX, an architecture that operates on both single jets and whole events within a single model. Using the jet-level JetClass and Top-tagging datasets and the event-level Collide-2V dataset, we demonstrate that OmniX trained jointly on single jets and full events matches or exceeds otherwise equivalent jet-only and event-only models in generation and classification.

</details>

Result: 给出完整 NLO 结果，并通过与特殊极限的比较，评估来自 $α_s(M)$ 与 $M^2/Q^2$ 的潜在大修正所导致的误差量级。

Conclusion: 完整 NLO 共线因子化计算可用于量化此前近似处理引入的不确定性，为重介子遍举产生过程的理论精度评估提供依据。

Abstract: Gluon tomography of the nucleon through hard-exclusive electroproduction of $J/ψ$ has been one of the key motivations for Electron-Ion Collider. We present a full next-to-leading order collinear factorization for the hard exclusive production of heavy mesons that retains the meson mass as well as full light-cone distribution amplitude. We compare the full NLO result with the special limits and estimate the errors due to potentially-large corrections from $α_s(M)$ and $M^2/Q^2$ at this order.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在考察不同的排序条件（ordering conditions）与归一化处理（normalization treatments）如何影响该过程的理论描述，并评估预言对 UPDF（非积分部分子分布）方案选择的敏感性。

Abstract: We investigate $Z$-boson production in Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$ within the $k_t$-factorization framework, using four nuclear transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution (TMD) prescriptions, namely LO-MRW, VO-MRW, MVO-MRW, and MKMRW. The aim of this study is to examine how different ordering conditions and normalization treatments influence the theoretical description of this process. We calculate the rapidity and transverse-momentum distributions using the parton-level KaTie event generator and compare the predictions with CMS measurements and collinear NLO calculations. This comparison allows us to assess the sensitivity of the predicted distributions to the choice of UPDF prescription.

Conclusion: 上述比较揭示了末步演化（last-step-evolution）核 TMD 模型用于质子-核碰撞 $Z$ 玻色子产生的潜力与局限。

Abstract: The production of dileptons in proton-lead collisions probes the transverse-momentum structure of partons in a nuclear environment. The models describe the overall trends near the $Z$ resonance, while the virtuality constraint in NLO-MRW changes the high-transverse-momentum behavior. At lower dilepton masses, both prescriptions underestimate parts of the data, particularly the NLO-MRW result. These comparisons illustrate both the potential and the limitations of last-step-evolution nuclear TMD models for $Z$ boson production in proton-nucleus collisions.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三希格斯产生对希格斯势形状与顶夸克相关反常耦合敏感，是检验电弱对称性破缺机制的重要通道。现有分析多聚焦于双希格斯过程，缺乏对包含κ_3、κ_4及顶夸克反常耦合的完整参数化描述，因此需要在κ框架（κ-framework）下进行系统研究。

Conclusion: 微分测量（differential measurements）有望破除参数简并（degeneracies），提升未来三希格斯搜寻的灵敏度。该参数化框架为三希格斯产生作为希格斯势与反常顶夸克耦合的探针提供了实用且可扩展的分析工具。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of triple-Higgs production within the $κ$-framework as a probe of the Higgs potential and anomalous top-Higgs couplings. Applying them to LHC Run 2 data and high-luminosity LHC projections, we quantify the impact of $κ_t$, $κ_{2t}$, and $κ_{3t}$ on the constraints on $κ_3$ and $κ_4$, and derive the first direct constraint on $κ_{3t}$ from existing LHC data. We further show that parameter points yielding nearly identical inclusive rates can give rise to different kinematic distributions, demonstrating the potential of differential measurements to resolve degeneracies and enhance the sensitivity of future triple-Higgs searches.

Result: 给出使暗物质丰度达到参考值的初始相位角（对应 F_χ≈10^17 GeV 与零温质量 m_{χ,0}=3×10^-20 eV），并约束禁闭群体的能量贡献。示例性暴胀子背景可满足真空与等曲率检验，但以大入口修正可控为条件；冷隐藏历史上要求隐藏到可见的重加热比例接近 5×10^-12。第二个无规范反常模型用两个带电希格斯单态给出相同物理相位能标。

Abstract: We study a gauge realization of an ultralight axion with a hidden $SU(2)$ interaction, an Abelian vector portal, a charged Higgs and a Stückelberg field. The vector absorbs one scalar combination and leaves a physical phase. Its normalization is fixed by the Higgs and Stückelberg scales, while hidden confinement generates its mass. Keeping this phase ultralight requires an additional symmetry: the displayed gauge charges alone allow large phase-dependent scalar interactions. We quantify the suppression required of these interactions. Pure-$SU(2)$ lattice results relate the susceptibility to physical confinement scales; one- and two-loop running illustrate the dependence on the chosen high-energy coupling. We derive the coherent radiation-era density and exhibit an initial angle giving the reference dark-matter abundance for $F_χ\simeq10^{17}\,\GeV$ and zero-temperature mass $m_{χ,0}=3\times10^{-20}\,\eV$. We follow the confined population through number-changing and number-conserving limits and bound its energy contribution. An illustrative inflaton background satisfies the stated vacuum and isocurvature tests, conditional on control of a large portal correction to its potential. Its cold hidden history requires a hidden-to-visible reheating fraction near $5\times10^{-12}$, whose microscopic origin remains an input. A second, gauge-anomaly-free model gives the same physical phase scale using two charged Higgs singlets. These constructions specify conditional cosmological examples and the additional protection and reheating mechanisms needed to complete them.

### [88] [Superdaisy Resummation in Theories with Broken Gauge Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31359)
*Martin Münzenberg,Carlos Tamarit*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将部分修饰重求和方案推广至自发破缺规范理论的规范玻色子与鬼场部分，并在标准模型中通过耦合的间隙方程自洽求解标量、规范玻色子与鬼场的完全热质量，发现横向规范模在确定电弱相变性质中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规高温处理仅保留主导的 O(T²) 近似，可能不足以刻画规范玻色子热质量及电弱相变（electroweak phase transition）性质，因此需保留热自能（thermal self-energies）的完整质量依赖并进行自洽求解。

Method: 将部分修饰重求和（partial dressing resummation）扩展至规范与鬼场（gauge and ghost）扇区，通过一组耦合的间隙方程（gap equations）自洽确定标量、规范玻色子与鬼场的热质量，并应用于标准模型（Standard Model）。

Result: 超出 O(T²) 的修正在规范玻色子热质量上尤为显著，而对标量谱的移动相对较小；纵向扇区的自洽规范修饰降低了有限温度希格斯有效势（finite-temperature Higgs effective potential）的势垒，而横向扇区引入重求和后势垒消失，似与格点（lattice）结果一致地排除一阶相变。

Conclusion: 由于横向激发（transverse excitations）变强耦合，横向扇区的重求和在定量上并不可靠；但结果凸显横向模在决定电弱相变性质中的核心作用。

Abstract: We extend the partial dressing resummation scheme for the finite-temperature effective potential to the gauge and ghost sectors of spontaneously broken gauge theories and apply the resulting framework to the Standard Model. Going beyond the usual high-temperature treatment, we retain the full mass dependence of the thermal self-energies and determine the scalar, gauge-boson and ghost masses self-consistently from a coupled system of gap equations. The solutions show that corrections beyond the leading $\mathcal{O}(T^2)$ approximation are particularly relevant for the gauge-boson thermal masses, while they induce a comparatively small shift in the scalar spectrum for the benchmark studied. The self-consistent gauge-boson dressing in the longitudinal sector reduces the barrier in the finite-temperature Higgs effective potential. Implementing the resummation in the transverse sector removes the barrier and would seem to exclude a first-order phase transition in accordance with lattice results. While such resummation is not quantitatively reliable, due to the transverse excitations becoming strongly coupled, the results showcase the crucial role played by transverse modes in determining the nature of the electroweak phase transition.

</details>


### [89] [Perturbative unitarity bounds from qubit entanglement in scalar scattering and QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32842)
*Shiuli Chatterjee,Kamila Kowalska,Enrico Maria Sessolo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在微扰 $S$-矩阵（perturbative $S$-matrix）框架下，对 $2\to 2$ 相对论散射中通过追踪掉动量自由度所得量子信道（quantum channel）的性质进行了系统分析，并利用纠缠（entanglement）的共度（concurrence）给出耦合常数的微扰性上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画相对论散射过程中量子信道的基本性质，并探索纠缠量能否为耦合常数提供独立于传统判据的微扰性（perturbativity）限制。

Method: 在味标量接触相互作用（flavored scalar contact interactions）与量子电动力学（QED）两种含量子比特（qubit）自由度的情形下，重点考虑具有横向极化（transverse polarization）的初态轻子，确定信道幺正性（unitarity）成立的条件，并用共度计算量子比特间的纠缠。

Result: 由纠缠的最大允许值可提取耦合常数的微扰性上界；所得限制往往比相应的分波展开（partial-wave expansion）界限更具约束力，并可规避后者因前向散射（forward scattering）奇异行为带来的困难。

Conclusion: 纠缠约束是一种有效且常更严格的微扰性判据，能够补充甚至优于分波展开方法，为相对论散射中量子信道性质的研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: In the framework of $2\to 2$ relativistic scattering described by the perturbative $S$-matrix, we perform a comprehensive analysis of the properties of quantum channels obtained by tracing out the momentum from the scattered state. We work in two distinct scenarios with qubit degrees of freedom: flavored scalar contact interactions and QED, focusing in particular on initial-state leptons with transverse polarization. We identify the conditions that make the quantum channel unitary, calculate the entanglement of the qubits through the concurrence, and use its maximally allowed value to extract perturbativity upper bounds on the coupling constants. We find that the limits are often more constraining than the corresponding partial-wave expansion bounds, and that they allow us to bypass some of the obstacles associated in the latter with the singular behavior of forward scattering.

</details>


### [90] [CANON: Agent-Assisted Construction of Canonical Differential Equations for Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33100)
*Dong-shan Jian,Yuanche Liu,Yan-qing Ma,Bo-xuan Shi,Yu Wu,Zihao Wu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出 CANON——一个由智能体（agent）辅助的框架，用于在多圈费曼积分（multi-loop Feynman integrals）正则微分方程（canonical differential equation）方法中自动完成正则基（canonical basis）的搜索与验证流程，并在二圈与三圈积分族上做了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造正则基（canonical basis）是多圈费曼积分计算中的关键步骤，但现有方法众多且无单一通用方法，获取有效基往往需要反复尝试与验证，效率较低。因此需要使这一“搜索—验证”过程更便捷高效的手段。

Method: CANON 采用智能体辅助的工作流（workflow）：智能体选择方法并调用相应工具，修正基的提案或相关的微分方程系统（differential-equation system）；随后由独立程序验证智能体结果是否有效，若验证失败则要求智能体重试。框架包含专门的状态管理工具（state-management tool），以支持需要多个子智能体（sub-agents）参与的长程基搜索工作，其模块化架构（modular architecture）便于后续扩展。

Result: 摘要仅说明将该框架应用于若干二圈（two-loop）与三圈（three-loop）积分族（integral families）进行演示，未给出具体的成功率、耗时或对比数据。

Conclusion: CANON 通过智能体与独立验证程序的分工及模块化、可扩展的设计，为多圈费曼积分中正则基的搜索与验证提供了更便利、更高效的途径，并具备支持长程搜索与后续扩展的能力。

Abstract: The construction of canonical basis is an important procedure in the calculation of multi-loop Feynman integrals via the canonical differential equation methods. Currently, there are various different methods for this purpose. Among them, no single method is universally applicable. Thus, obtaining a valid basis often requires repeated attempts and verification. In this paper, we present CANON, an agent-assisted framework designed to make this search-and-verification process more convenient and more efficient. In this workflow, the agent selects methods and calls the corresponding tools to revise the basis proposal or the associated differential-equation system. Independent programs then verify whether the agent's result is valid and ask the agent to retry if verification fails. This framework contains a specific state-management tool to supports long-range basis searching works which requires multiple sub-agents. Its modular architecture enables future extensions to the framework itself. In this paper, we demonstrate this framework by applying it to some two-loop and three-loop integral families.

</details>


### [91] [$QQ\bar Q\bar Q$ Quark System and Gauge/String Duality II: String Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33140)

TL;DR: 本文是系列工作的第二篇，基于规范/弦对偶（gauge/string duality）将全重四夸克态（fully heavy tetraquark）的构造推广到含两个等质量轻味的SU(3)规范理论，并在矩形与共线几何下解析与数值计算最低两个Born-Oppenheimer势。

Conclusion: 规范/弦对偶构造可自然推广至含两个等质量轻味的SU(3)情形，所揭示的临界尺度与弦张力普适、断裂距离非普适的结论，为理解四夸克态中的弦动力学提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: This is the second of two companion papers in which we continue developing the construction of the fully heavy tetraquark system using the gauge/string duality. Here, we extend this construction to model $SU(3)$ gauge theory with two light flavors of equal mass. As an application, we analyze the two lowest Born-Oppenheimer potentials, both analytically and numerically, for rectangular and collinear geometries. Our analysis reveals several critical scales associated with string reconnection and breaking, as well as junction annihilation and pinching. In addition, we find that the string tension is universal, whereas the string breaking distance is not.

</details>

### [92] [Self-gravitating dust clumps: linear theory sets the threshold, the onset time and the spacing of their formation, then slow coarsening erases this pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31327)
*Yohann Trivino*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 该工作通过无量纲数值模型研究行星形成中尘埃颗粒在自身引力下聚集成团块的条件与间距，用自适应网格二阶有限体积法求解并结合图像分割识别团块，发现团块形成起始时间与主导波长可由线性稳定性理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星在原行星盘（protoplanetary disk）中形成，其中一步是尘埃颗粒在自身引力下聚集成致密团块（clumps），需预测这些团块何时出现及其间距。

Method: 构建含有限程引力（finite-range gravity）、气体阻力减速、湍流扩散（turbulent spreading）及最大堆积体积填充（volume filling）约束的无量纲模型，仅依赖引力数（gravitational number）χ与平均体积分数（mean volume fraction）ρ_b 两个参数；采用自适应笛卡尔网格（adaptive Cartesian mesh）上的二阶有限体积法求解，并将各场视为图像，用 Otsu 阈值（Otsu threshold）与分水岭变换（watershed transform）分离团块。

Result: 代码质量守恒误差在 10^-15 以内且呈二阶收敛；两组实验设计共 192 次模拟（2≤χ≤40，0.05≤ρ_b≤0.7）。线性稳定性理论（linear stability theory）可预测团块形成起始，起始时间 t_on≈3.0/σ*（σ* 为最强增长模增长率），主导波长与最强放大波长偏差在 10% 以内，团块面积分数等于 ρ_b，随后发生缓慢粗化（coarsening）；敏感性分析表明方差主要归因于 χ；自适应网格仅小幅降低计算成本，因团块充满整个计算域。

Conclusion: 线性稳定性理论足以预测团块的起始时间与特征间距，团块面积分数由 ρ_b 决定且后续经历缓慢粗化，系统行为主要受引力数 χ 控制；由于团块占据整个域，自适应网格的计算节省有限。

Abstract: Planets form in disks of gas and dust. One step of their formation is the gathering of dust grains into dense clumps, under their own gravity. This work tries to predict when these clumps appear and how far apart they are. The grains attract each other through a gravity of finite range. The gas slows them down, and its turbulence spreads them out. The grains also cannot go beyond maximum packing: this is volume filling. The dimensionless model depends on two numbers. The first one is the gravitational number $χ$, which compares attraction with turbulent spreading. The second one is the mean volume fraction $ρ_b$, which measures the amount of grains. We solve the model with second-order finite volumes on an adaptive Cartesian mesh. We then treat each field as an image. An Otsu threshold and a watershed transform isolate the clumps. The code conserves mass to within $10^{-15}$ and converges at second order. Two designs of experiments give 192 simulations in total, with $2\leχ\le40$ and $0.05\leρ_b\le0.7$. Linear stability theory predicts the start of clump formation. The instability starts at time $t_{on}\simeq3.0/σ^*$, where $σ^*$ is the growth rate of the most amplified mode. The dominant wavelength equals the most amplified wavelength to within $10\%$. The area fraction of the clumps equals $ρ_b$. The clumps then go through slow coarsening. A sensitivity analysis attributes most of the variance to $χ$. The adaptive mesh reduces the computational cost only a little, because the clumps fill the whole domain.

</details>
