### [93] [Late-time cosmic acceleration from matter inhomogeneities: constraints from DESI DR2 and SNe Ia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31374)
*Mariano Cadoni,Andrea Cozzumbo,Riccardo Murgia,Matteo Pili,Mirko Pitzalis,Andrea P. Sanna,Matteo Schulz,Ilaria Usai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次给出宇宙Misner-Sharp（MS）质量（Misner-Sharp mass）的观测约束，基于一个精确广义相对论（General Relativity）解：该解在早期退化为FLRW宇宙学并兼容CMB数据，在晚期通过MS质量的尺度依赖红移分布自然产生宇宙加速。由DESI重子声学振荡（BAO）与Ia型超新星（SNe Ia）数据推得的MS质量给出与ΛCDM一致的宇宙学常数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宇宙加速通常依赖微扰框架下的暗能量（dark energy）解释，缺乏对非线性宇宙结构形成（nonlinear cosmic structure formation）的非微扰描述及对MS质量的观测约束。本文旨在以精确GR解为基础，首次从观测上约束宇宙的MS质量，并为暗能量问题提供可检验的替代方案。

Method: 采用新提出的精确广义相对论解，对非线性结构形成作非微扰描述；该解在满足统计均匀各向同性（statistical homogeneity and isotropy）的早期退化为标准FLRW宇宙学并容纳CMB数据。利用DESI的重子声学振荡（Baryon Acoustic Oscillation, BAO）数据与Ia型超新星（Type Ia Supernovae）数据反演MS质量，并分析其在大尺度渐近极限（asymptotic limit）所产生的扩展项。

Result: 在尺度≲200 Mpc上，MS质量的尺度依赖红移分布刻画了对均匀各向同性的偏离，并自然引出宇宙加速；由BAO与SNe Ia得到的MS质量对应一个与ΛCDM结果相符的宇宙学常数值。

Conclusion: 该精确解可作为暗能量谜题（dark energy puzzle）的一种可检验替代解释；多探针策略（multi-probe strategies），尤其是CMB透镜效应（CMB lensing）与晚期积分Sachs-Wolfe效应（late Integrated Sachs-Wolfe effect），有望最终检验这一方案。

Abstract: We present the first observational constraints on the Misner-Sharp (MS) mass of the universe, within a newly proposed exact General Relativity solution providing a non-perturbative description of nonlinear cosmic structure formation. At early times, when statistical homogeneity and isotropy are satisfied, our model reduces to usual FLRW cosmology, accommodating Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) data. At late times, the departure from homogeneity and isotropy on scales $\lesssim 200$ Mpc -- codified in the scale-dependent redshift distribution of the MS mass -- naturally induces cosmic acceleration, through an extensive term generated by the MS mass asymptotic limit on large scales. We derive the MS mass from Baryon Acoustic Oscillation data from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) and Type Ia Supernovae, obtaining a value of the cosmological constant in agreement with $Λ$CDM results. We outline broader implications and future observational prospects, highlighting how multi-probe strategies -- particularly CMB lensing and the late Integrated Sachs-Wolfe effect -- can ultimately test this solution to the dark energy puzzle.

</details>


### [94] [Multiscale Zoom-in Lattice Simulation for Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32753)
*Xinpeng Wang,Angelo Caravano,Leander Thiele,Misao Sasaki*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 提出三维放大（zoom-in）格点方法，通过逐代细化子网格并继承大尺度扰动，使暴胀涨落模拟可覆盖从绝热次视界到越过视界的宽广尺度范围，将可追踪时长从几个e-folds延长至数十个e-folds。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点模拟（lattice simulation）是研究暴胀涨落非线性演化的有力工具，但宇宙指数膨胀使得单一固定网格难以同时覆盖动力学相关的宽广尺度范围，限制了可模拟的e-folds数目。

Method: 构建三维zoom-in格点方法：每隔若干e-folds将网格细化为一个或多个子网格，注入小尺度功率并由前代继承大尺度涨落；每次放大平移所解析的波段而保持动态范围固定。

Result: 在演化50个e-folds的Starobinsky基准模型中，曲率功率谱（curvature power spectrum）与线性理论一致；在含振荡特征的模型中，zoom-in模拟复现了高分辨率固定网格运行的峰值，并显示出尺度依赖的非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）。

Conclusion: 该方法将格点模拟可跟随暴胀涨落的时长由几个e-folds扩展至数十个e-folds，并能在宽广尺度上可靠复现高分辨率结果，适用于研究带特征的暴胀模型中的非线性与尺度依赖非高斯性。

Abstract: Lattice simulations are a powerful tool for studying the nonlinear evolution of inflationary fluctuations. The exponential expansion, however, makes it challenging to cover a broad range of scales relevant for the dynamics, limiting how many e-folds a single fixed grid can follow. We develop a three-dimensional zoom-in lattice method to follow inflationary fluctuations over a broad range of scales from the adiabatic subhorizon regime through horizon crossing. Every few e-folds, the grid is refined into one or more subgrids, with small-scale power injected and large-scale fluctuations inherited from previous generations. Each zoom shifts the resolved band while keeping its dynamic range fixed. This extends the duration over which lattice simulations can follow inflationary fluctuations from a few e-folds to tens of e-folds. In a Starobinsky benchmark evolved for 50 e-folds, the curvature power spectrum agrees with linear theory. In a model with an oscillatory feature, the zoom-in simulations recover the peak seen in a high-resolution fixed-grid run and show scale-dependent non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [95] [In-in formalism & $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-product: no contribution from total time derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31272)
*Yohei Ema,Muzi Hong,Ryusuke Jinno,Kyohei Mukaida*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 基于路径积分（path integral）论证：在in-in形式中，总时间导数（total time derivatives）在暴胀关联子（inflationary correlators）的评估点处并不产生非平凡贡献，该评估点处的表观边界项（boundary terms）完全抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中有时主张总时间导数会对评估点（即暴胀结束时）的关联子产生非平凡贡献，本文旨在驳斥这一说法。

Method: 先建立含总时间导数系统的正则量子化（canonical quantization）与正规化路径积分表述，进而推导闭时路径（closed time path）上正确的算符排序，即$\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering。

Result: 在$\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering下，评估点（即in-in等高线的转向点）仅为中间点而非广义路径积分等高线的端点；前向与后向分支在转向点处的任何表观边界贡献均完全抵消，并通过重新分析文献中的例子（特别关注此前被忽略的与之相关的接触项（contact terms））予以演示。

Conclusion: 总时间导数不会对评估点处的暴胀关联子产生非平凡贡献，先前的主张不成立；正确算符排序下边界项相消。

Abstract: In the in-in formalism, total time derivatives are sometimes argued to contribute nontrivially to inflationary correlators at the evaluation point, i.e., at the end of inflation. In this paper, we disprove this claim based on the path integral. After formulating the canonical quantization and the regulated path integral of systems with total time derivatives, we derive the $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering, the correct operator ordering on the closed time path. In the $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering, the evaluation point, or the turning point of the in-in contour, is merely an intermediate point and is never an endpoint of the generalized path integral contour. As a result, any apparent boundary contributions at the turning point of the forward and backward branches completely cancel out. We demonstrate this cancellation by re-analyzing examples in the literature, with an emphasis on contact terms associated with the $\mathcal{T}_{C}^{\star}$-ordering overlooked previously.

</details>

### [96] [Quantum Landau Damping with Coulomb Repulsion in the Whole Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02040)
*Dongfen Bian,Emmanuel Grenier,Laurent Laflèche,Quoc Hung Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究线性化 Hartree 方程（linearized Hartree equation）在空间均匀稳定平衡附近的长时间行为，并探讨量子朗道阻尼（quantum Landau damping）。所得结果给出电场的时间衰减估计（对普朗克常数一致）以及量子密度向经典密度的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注线性化 Hartree 方程在稳定且空间均匀的平衡态附近的长时间动力学，目标是刻画量子朗道阻尼（quantum Landau damping）现象，并厘清量子效应对阻尼与密度演化的影响。

Method: 采用理论解析方法，围绕空间均匀稳定平衡附近的线性化 Hartree 方程做长时间渐近分析，并显式追踪对普朗克常数（Planck constant）的一致性。

Result: 获得电场（electric field）的时间衰减估计，且该估计在普朗克常数上一致；同时当普朗克常数趋于 0 时，量子密度（quantum density）在时间上一致地收敛到经典密度（classical density）。

Conclusion: 线性化层面下量子朗道阻尼得到验证，且量子—经典对应在半经典极限（semiclassical limit）中以一致于时间的方式成立。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the large time behavior of solutions to the linearized Hartree equation near a stable equilibrium which is homogeneous in space and investigate the quantum Landau damping. This allows us to get time decay of the electric field, uniformly in the Planck constant, as well as the uniform-in-time convergence of the quantum density towards the classical density as the Planck constant converges to 0.

</details>


### [97] [A lower bound on the Nusselt number in stochastically forced Rayleigh--Bénard convection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32984)
*Juraj Foldes,Jun Hyun Park*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明：在无穷普朗特数（Prandtl number）、自由滑移边界（free-slip boundaries）的二维瑞利–贝纳德对流（Rayleigh–Bénard convection）中，只要在温度方程中加入振幅 0<σ≤1 的加性噪声（additive noise），则长时平均努塞尔数（Nusselt number）的期望满足下界 Nu−1 ≥ c(σ³Ra)^{7/192}，前提是 σ³Ra 足够大；而无噪声时该下界不可能成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无噪声情形下，导热态（conductive state）对任意瑞利数（Rayleigh number）都是解且 Nu=1，因此不存在正的热输运下界；本文旨在说明适当的随机扰动可排除该平凡解，从而给出长时平均热输运的可证明下界。

Method: 采用辅助泛函方法（auxiliary functional method），并将其推广到一般马尔可夫过程（Markov processes）：证明辅助泛函的定义不等式只需在短时平均后成立。由此问题归结为证明解以一致正概率快速离开低热流、低热耗散的状态集；该性质通过投影到初始平均温度剖面线性化（linearization）的不稳定特征向量（unstable eigenvector）上得到，化为具有线性增长、加性噪声与非线性误差项的一维伊藤过程（Itô process）。

Result: 对任意确定性初始温度，当 σ³Ra ≥ C(1+log(1/σ))^{192/7} 时，期望长时平均努塞尔数满足 Nu−1 ≥ c(σ³Ra)^{7/192}，其中常数 c,C>0 仅依赖于区域参数 L。

Conclusion: 结果表明，小幅加性噪声足以在无穷普朗特数二维对流中产生可定量下界的长期平均热输运，且下界随 σ³Ra 呈幂律增长；同时所发展的短时平均辅助泛函判据对一般马尔可夫过程具有独立的方法论价值。

Abstract: We establish a lower bound on the expected long-time averaged Nusselt number for two-dimensional Rayleigh--Bénard convection at infinite Prandtl number, with free-slip boundaries and a suitable additive noise of amplitude $0<σ\leq1$ in the temperature equation. For every deterministic initial temperature, we prove that \[
  \underline{Nu}-1
  \geq c(σ^3 Ra)^{7/192}, \] provided that \[
  σ^3 Ra
  \geq C(1+\log(1/σ))^{192/7}, \] where $c,C>0$ depend only on the domain parameter $L$. Without noise, no such bound can hold, since the conductive state is a solution with $Nu=1$ for every Rayleigh number.
  The proof uses the auxiliary functional method. For general Markov processes, we show that the defining inequality of an auxiliary functional need only hold after a short time average. This reduces the problem to showing that, with uniformly positive probability, the solution quickly leaves the set of states with low heat flux and low thermal dissipation. We establish this by projecting onto an unstable eigenvector of the linearization around the initial mean temperature profile, yielding a one-dimensional Itô process with linear growth, additive noise, and nonlinear error terms.

</details>

### [98] [Defining the mathematical family tree with a minimal programming language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31775)
*Max Tegmark*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出图灵完备（Turing-complete）的九字符命令语言 MLISP，用它统一定义 88 个数学结构与 65 个理论的谱系树，并在 Lean 中形式化验证，兼顾教学、关联与基础三方面目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学结构定义方式缺乏统一展示格式与最小化人工预设（minimal human baggage），且高层形式化语言对刻画标准算术运算而言过于繁重；作者希望以自包含、显式且具构造主义（constructivist）精神的方式统一呈现数学结构及其关联。

Method: 将数学结构定义为展示（presentation）的等价类，用标准化展示表（standardized presentation tableaus）给出定义与公理化理论；有限结构以查找表定义生成函数，可数无限结构（countably infinite）则用 MLISP 计算式定义，并在 Lean 中验证定义、关键定理与模型。

Result: 定义了 88 个数学结构与 65 个理论的家族树，其下部自然分为关系、代数、逻辑、数、集合与连续统（continuum）六个主分支；Lean 代码同时验证了 MLISP 的正确性，并包含 Mathlib 2026-10-11 中 43% 的定理。例如程序 "jibjbfid" 定义减法。

Conclusion: MLISP 以极小语言实现统一的数学结构呈现与谱系关联，并通过 Lean 形式化获得严格性保障；提供交互版本与公开 Lean 代码，兼具教学与基础研究价值。

Abstract: I introduce MLISP, a Turing-complete language with nine single-character commands (a b c d f 0 i j k), and use it to define 88 mathematical structures and a family tree of 65 theories that they model, with three aims:
  Pedagogy: It presents the mathematical structures in a unified way, with standardized presentation tableaus that define them and axiomatized theories that describe them.
  Connections: It highlights how they are related in the family tree via added symbols, axioms, etc. While the full tree is obviously infinite, its lower parts naturally separate into six main branches: the relational branch, the algebraic branch, the logic branch, the number branch, the set branch and the continuum branch.
  Foundations: The definitions, key theorems and models of each entry are verified in Lean. Mathematical structures are defined as equivalence classes of presentations, which in a constructivist spirit are defined in a simple, explicit and self-contained way in standardized presentation tableaus with minimal human baggage. For finite structures, the generating functions are defined by lookup tables. For countably infinite ones, they are instead defined computationally using MLISP, since more complex languages such as Lean are massive overkill for rigorously defining standard arithmetic operations (for example, the program "jibjbfid" defines subtraction).
  An interactive version is available at https://tegmark.org/mathtree. The Lean code is public, also verifies the correctness of MLISP and includes 43% of the theorems from Mathlib 2026-10-11. \end{abstract}

</details>

### [99] [Torus breakdown and collective chaos in heterogeneously forced excitatory--inhibitory neural populations with cascaded synapses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32826)
*Gonzalo Contreras-Aso,F. Javier Marín-Rodríguez,Gabriel B. Mindlin*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 该文将Montbrió–Pazó–Roxin（MPR）框架推广到二次积分发放（QIF）神经元构成的兴奋—抑制网络，突触耦合采用二阶alpha函数双滤波，得到精确的八维平均场约化。基于双参数分岔分析识别出九种动力学区间，并揭示周期强迫下准周期、Arnold舌与混沌现象，以及"部分强迫等价于全体强迫"这一由异质性保证的普适性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（spiking networks）的精确平均场（mean-field）约化可提供由微观动力学导出的低维群体描述，优于现象学假设的模型；但现有MPR框架尚缺乏对兴奋—抑制（excitatory–inhibitory）网络与二阶突触滤波（double synaptic filter）组合的系统刻画，也缺少对强迫（forcing）下集体动力学及其鲁棒性的分析。

Method: 将MPR框架推广至通过alpha函数型二阶突触滤波耦合的QIF兴奋—抑制网络，保持约化的精确性并得到八维系统；以两群体平均兴奋性（mean excitabilities）为参数进行双参数分岔（bifurcation）分析，并对抑制群体在同宿分岔（homoclinic bifurcation）附近施加周期强迫，结合网络仿真与Lyapunov指数进行验证。

Result: 分岔分析显示由鞍结（saddle-node）、SNIC、Hopf、同宿分岔、Bautin点与极限环折返（folds of cycles）组织的九种动力学区间，包括静息与集体振荡之间的双稳（bistability），并经网络仿真确认。弱强迫即可引发准周期（quasiperiodicity）、Arnold舌（Arnold tongues）与混沌（chaos），符合环面破裂（torus breakdown）机制；以比例p、幅度A强迫部分抑制神经元与以幅度pA强迫全部神经元产生几乎相同的动力学及Lyapunov指数。

Conclusion: 部分强迫与全体强迫的等价性可由约化方程的精确重写证明，修正项为O(p(1-p)A^2)量级；异质性（heterogeneity）保证了受迫与未受迫神经元之间的收缩性，因此该性质是异质平均场群体的普适特征，而非特定模型的巧合。

Abstract: Exact mean-field reductions of spiking networks provide low-dimensional descriptions of neural populations that are derived from the microscopic dynamics, rather than postulated phenomenologically. Here we extend the Montbrió--Pazó--Roxin framework to excitatory--inhibitory networks of quadratic integrate-and-fire neurons coupled through a double synaptic filter, in which every spike elicits a second-order (alpha-function) synaptic response. The reduction remains exact and yields an eight-dimensional system. A two-parameter bifurcation analysis in the mean excitabilities of both populations reveals nine dynamical regimes, organized by saddle-node, SNIC, Hopf and homoclinic bifurcations, a Bautin point and folds of cycles, including bistability between quiescence and collective oscillations, which we confirm with network simulations. We then periodically force the inhibitory population close to the homoclinic bifurcation, where the collective rhythm is slow and effectively two-dimensional. Weak forcing already produces quasiperiodicity, Arnold tongues and chaos, consistent with torus breakdown. Remarkably, forcing only a fraction $p$ of the inhibitory neurons with amplitude $A$ yields nearly the same dynamics, including the Lyapunov exponents, as forcing all of them with amplitude $pA$. We show that this equivalence follows from an exact rewriting of the reduced equations, with corrections of order $p(1-p)A^2$, and that heterogeneity guarantees the contraction between forced and unforced neurons, making it a generic property of heterogeneous mean-field populations.

</details>

### [100] [Imaging Compressible and Incompressible Kinetic Energy of a 2d Bose-Einstein Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31502)
*Elinor Kath,Jelte Duchêne,Hanyi Jang,Floriane Arrouas,Helmut Strobel,Markus K. Oberthaler*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于速度敏感双光子跃迁（velocity-sensitive two-photon transitions）的方法，可在单次实现中同时重建玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）的二维速度场与局域原子密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测量难以在单次实现中同时获取速度场与密度信息，而描述远离平衡的量子流体动力学需要对流动动能各分量的行为进行刻画。

Method: 利用速度敏感的双光子跃迁对凝聚体进行探测，从单次实现中重建二维速度场及局域原子密度，并在量子化涡旋（quantized vortex）上对该方法进行定量基准测试。

Result: 在量子涡旋上完成定量基准验证后，将该方法应用于远离平衡的凝聚体，揭示出流动动能（flow kinetic energy）各分量行为迥异：可压缩分量（compressible component）快速衰减，而不可压缩分量（incompressible component）保持不变并主导长时动力学。

Conclusion: 该方法为单次实现中的速度场与密度联合测量提供了可行途径，并展示出流动动能的可压缩与不可压缩分解对理解非平衡凝聚体长时动力学的关键作用。

Abstract: We introduce a method based on velocity-sensitive two-photon transitions to reconstruct the two-dimensional velocity field as well as the local atomic density of a Bose-Einstein condensate in a single realization. We quantitatively benchmark the method on a quantized vortex. Applied to a condensate far from equilibrium, the method reveals the contrasting behaviors of the system's flow kinetic energy components, where the compressible component quickly decays while the incompressible one remains, dominating the long-time dynamics.

</details>


### [101] [Relaxation dynamics of the impurity momentum in a one-dimensional Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32280)
*Felipe Taha Sant'Ana*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究一维无自旋费米气体中单个可移动杂质经排斥接触相互作用耦合的系统，利用行列式重叠导出杂质动量算符及其平方在任意Bethe-ansatz本征态间矩阵元的行列式闭式；在无限耦合下证明选择规则，并分析注入费米海后的动量弛豫动力学与热力学极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在获得杂质动量算符在相互作用本征态之间的精确矩阵元，从而将平均杂质动量的谱表示（spectral representation）转化为对本征态的显式有限求和，以定量刻画杂质注入后的弛豫过程。

Method: 借助相互作用本征态与自由费米子态之间的行列式重叠（determinant overlaps），推导有限系统中杂质动量算符及其平方在任意Bethe-ansatz本征态间的行列式闭式（closed determinant formulas），并结合无限耦合下的选择规则（selection rule）分析动力学。

Result: 无限耦合下，共享同一快度（rapidity）的不同本征态之间两算符矩阵元均为零，且每个本征态携带总动量的单粒子份额。杂质注入费米海后，无限耦合下平均杂质动量在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）中完全弛豫，有限耦合下弛豫不完全；并解析了量子颤动振荡（quantum-flutter oscillations）与趋向对角系综值（diagonal-ensemble value）的过程。

Conclusion: 闭式矩阵元使平均杂质动量的谱表示成为显式求和，从而可评估注入后动力学，并将稳态动量映射为注入动量与耦合强度的函数；热力学极限及其渐近行为最终表达为Fredholm行列式的积分。

Abstract: We consider a single mobile impurity coupled by a repulsive contact interaction to a gas of free spinless fermions of equal mass in one dimension. Using the determinant overlaps between the interacting eigenstates and free-fermion states, we derive closed determinant formulas for the matrix elements of the impurity momentum operator and of its square between arbitrary Bethe-ansatz eigenstates of a finite system. At infinite coupling we prove a selection rule: both operators have vanishing matrix elements between distinct eigenstates that share the same rapidity, and every eigenstate carries the per-particle share of the total momentum. For an impurity injected into the Fermi sea we have the following: at infinite coupling the average impurity momentum relaxes completely in the thermodynamic limit, while at finite coupling relaxation remains incomplete. The closed-form matrix elements turn the spectral representation of the average impurity momentum into explicit finite sums over eigenstates. We evaluate the post-injection dynamics, resolve the quantum-flutter oscillations and the approach towards the diagonal-ensemble value, and map the stationary momentum as a function of the injection momentum and the coupling. Finally, we analyse the thermodynamic limit and its asymptotics as an integral over Fredholm determinants.

</details>


### [102] [Detecting Topological Phases Through Dynamics of Chiral Density in Momentum-space Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31878)
*Rui Tian,Xiayao He,Tianhao Wu,Dongdong Pu,Shuai Li,Bo Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于手征算符（chiral operator）动力学的谱学协议（spectroscopic protocol），用于探测动量空间（momentum-space）SSH链的拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量空间晶格（momentum-space lattices）是量子模拟（quantum simulation）的良好平台，尤其适用于模拟拓扑物态（topological states of matter），因此需要能够诊断其拓扑相的工具。

Method: 利用手征算符（chiral operator）的时间演化，证明手征密度（chiral density）的演化与局域态密度（local density of state, LDOS）直接相关，并通过傅里叶变换（Fourier transformation）重建LDOS与态密度（density of states）。

Result: 该方法可重建系统的LDOS与态密度，从而明确识别零能边缘模式（zero-energy edge modes）并验证体-边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）。

Conclusion: 该动力学方法为探索合成量子物质（synthetic quantum matter）中的拓扑相提供了一种诊断工具。

Abstract: Momentum-space lattices offer a versatile platform for quantum simulation in particular emulating topological states of matter.In this work, we propose a spectroscopic protocol via utilizing the dynamics of the chiral operator to detect the topological properties of a momentum-space Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain. We show that the time evolution of the chiral density is directly linked to the local density of state (LDOS). By performing a Fourier transformation of that, we reconstruct both the LDOS and density of states of the system, enabling the unambiguous signature of zero-energy edge modes and the bulk-boundary correspondence. This dynamical approach provides a diagnostic tool for exploring topological phases in synthetic quantum matter.

</details>


### [103] [Coherent meson-baryon conversion in a cold-atom non-Abelian lattice gauge simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31888)
*Tian-Yi Wang,Wei-Yong Zhang,Tie-Jin Zhong,Ming-Gen He,Zhao-Yu Zhou,Hui Sun,Zhen-Sheng Yuan,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用87Rb冷原子模拟器构建含动力学物质的非阿贝尔格点规范模型（non-Abelian lattice gauge model），并直接观测介子向重子/真空态转变及介子—重子-反重子对间的相干振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）的实时动力学与有限密度性质受符号问题（sign problem）困扰，传统蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）方法难以处理；数字量子模拟受线路深度与噪声限制，模拟系统中非阿贝尔规范-物质相互作用（non-Abelian gauge-matter interactions）亦难以工程实现，故需发展新平台。

Method: 以87Rb原子的两个超精细态（hyperfine states）与格点占据构型共同编码物质场与规范场，在自旋依赖光超晶格（spin-dependent optical superlattice）中定制共振隧穿，实现一维截断SU(2)量子链接模型（quantum link model）的一个规范扇区，并可独立调节质量与规范耦合。

Result: 通过缓慢调节质量扫描，跟踪初始介子态（mesonic state）向重子态（baryonic）或类真空构型的转化；在共振附近观测到介子与重子-反重子对（baryon-antibaryon pairs）之间的相干振荡，并表征转化效率随质量、规范耦合与净重子密度（net baryon density）的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了面向非阿贝尔规范动力学的原子平台，为强相互作用物质中非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics）的微观研究开辟了路径。

Abstract: Quantum chromodynamics (QCD), the non-Abelian gauge theory of quarks and gluons, provides a microscopic description of strongly interacting matter, including hadron structure and dynamics. Real-time dynamics and finite-density properties of these systems remain challenging for conventional Monte Carlo methods because of sign problems. Quantum simulation provides a complementary approach, although circuit depth and noise limit digital implementations and non-Abelian gauge-matter interactions remain difficult to engineer in analog systems. Here we realize a non-Abelian lattice gauge model with dynamical matter in an analog cold-atom simulator and directly observe its hadronic dynamics using site- and spin-resolved quantum gas microscopy. This realization is enabled by harnessing two hyperfine states and site-occupation configurations of 87Rb atoms to jointly encode both matter and gauge fields. We tailor resonant tunneling in a spin-dependent optical superlattice to implement a gauge sector of a truncated one-dimensional SU(2) quantum link model with independently tunable mass and gauge coupling. Leveraging this control, we track the conversion of an initially mesonic state into baryonic or vacuum-like configurations through slow mass ramps. Near resonance, we observe coherent oscillations between mesons and baryon-antibaryon pairs and characterize the conversion efficiency as a function of mass, gauge coupling and net baryon density. Our realization establishes an atomic platform for non-Abelian gauge dynamics, opening a route to microscopic studies of nonequilibrium dynamics in strongly interacting matter.

</details>


### [104] [Exact solution of the Bose-Fermi mixture along the unnesting line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32804)
*Soham Chandak,Aleksandra Petković,Zoran Ristivojevic*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文对具有接触排斥（contact repulsions）的一维Bose-Fermi混合模型，在Bethe ansatz方程可“去嵌套化（un-nested）”的特殊参数线上给出简化精确解，并借此得到基态能量、激发速度与Drude权重的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套Bethe ansatz（nested Bethe ansatz）虽形式可解，但方程高度耦合、难以提取物理量；作者寻求在一维Bose-Fermi混合模型中寻找可简化求解的特殊线，以便获得可解析处理的物理量。

Method: 沿Bethe ansatz方程可去嵌套化的特殊线，将模型解用已知的Lieb-Liniger模型与吸引型自旋1/2费米子Yang-Gaudin模型（平衡情形）的解来表达，从而得到解析结果。

Result: 得到基态能量、激发速度以及德鲁德权重（Drude weights）在弱相互作用与强相互作用极限下的显式解析表达式；并指出弱相互作用下该特殊线对应于自由费米子与弱相互作用玻色子两孤立体系速度相等的参数值。

Conclusion: 该工作表明嵌套Bethe ansatz解可在特定参数线上退耦为已知可积模型的组合，从而为Bose-Fermi混合物的基态与输运性质提供解析刻画，并揭示弱耦合下速度匹配的物理判据。

Abstract: A one-dimensional model of a Bose-Fermi mixture with contact repulsions is exactly solvable via nested Bethe ansatz. That formal solution is here simplified along a special line where the Bethe-ansatz equations can be unnested. The model solution is expressed in terms of the known solutions for the Lieb-Liniger model and the Yang-Gaudin model of attractive spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ fermions in the balanced case. We find explicit analytical results for the ground-state energy, the velocities of excitations, and the Drude weights at weak and strong interactions. At weak interactions, the special line corresponds to the parameter values where the isolated systems of free fermions and weakly interacting bosons have equal velocities.

</details>


### [105] [Probing the Rung-Mott Insulator in Bosonic Two-Leg Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32680)
*Christopher Parsonage,Lennart Koehn,Lorenzo Carfora,Ben Watson,Timon Hilker,Elmar Haller,Peter Kirton,Arthur La Rooij,Stefan Kuhr*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用超冷玻色原子在双腿梯子（two-leg ladder）体系中识别出半满附近由梯级占据稳定的梯级莫特绝缘体（rung-Mott insulating, RMI）相，并通过晶格调制光谱（lattice modulation spectroscopy）与数值模拟揭示其激发谱与不可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫特绝缘体（Mott insulator）通常出现在整数密度，但特定晶格几何或非局域相互作用可在分数密度下稳定莫特绝缘相；本文旨在实验上确证梯子几何中的此类分数密度绝缘相及其激发性质。

Method: 以超冷玻色原子（ultracold bosonic atoms）构建强关联双腿梯子，在半满（half-filling）附近通过位点分辨（site-resolved）测量提取梯级占据以识别 RMI 相；采用晶格调制光谱探测激发谱，并用包含驱动动力学、多激发态耦合与能量偏移的数值模拟解释观测谱；同时通过密度与化学势（chemical potential）的位点分辨分析检验其不可压缩性（incompressibility）。

Result: 实验观测到 RMI 相具有特征性的梯级占据，其调制光谱可由含驱动动力学与多激发态耦合的数值模拟定量解释，且位点分辨的密度—化学势分析显示该相不可压缩。

Conclusion: 结果给出强关联梯子体系激发动力学的直接认识，并为在可编程晶格几何（programmable lattice geometries）的低维量子系统中研究输运（transport）及进一步动力学性质奠定基础。

Abstract: Mott insulators typically appear at integer densities, but certain lattice geometries or non-local interactions can stabilise Mott insulators at fractional densities. In this work, we study strongly interacting two-leg ladders using ultracold bosonic atoms around half-filling, and identify the rung-Mott insulating (RMI) phase through its characteristic rung occupation in site-resolved measurements. We probe its excitation spectrum via lattice modulation spectroscopy, and interpret the observed spectra using numerical simulations that include the driven dynamics, coupling to multiple excited states, and energy offsets. As with other Mott insulators, the RMI phase is characterized through its incompressibility, which we probe via a site-resolved analysis of densities and chemical potentials. Our results provide direct insight into the excitation dynamics of a strongly correlated ladder system, and pave the way for studying transport and further dynamical properties in low-dimensional quantum systems with programmable lattice geometries.

</details>


### [106] [Parametric neural-network wavefunctions across a bosonic phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33150)
*Daniil S. Antonenko,Liang Fu,Leonid I. Glazman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出Boseformer，一种基于注意力机制的通用神经网络架构，用于表示玻色量子统计（bosonic quantum statistics）粒子的波函数；经变分蒙特卡罗（variational Monte Carlo）训练后可作为基础模型跨参数描述量子相变（quantum phase transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波函数表示方法通常需对每个哈密顿量参数（Hamiltonian parameter）单独训练，缺乏可跨越量子相变、统一描述超流（superfluid）与晶体（crystal）等不同物相的通用架构，因而需要构建可迁移的基础模型（foundation model）。

Method: 构建Boseformer——注意力机制（attention-based）神经网络架构，以哈密顿量参数为输入条件，输出玻色子波函数；通过变分蒙特卡罗优化训练后，直接在二维1/r³排斥相互作用（repulsive interaction）玻色子系统上验证。

Result: 训练后的模型无需针对具体参数值重新训练即可描述从超流到晶体相之间的量子相变，在二维1/r³排斥玻色子系统上成功实现该统一表示。

Conclusion: 注意力架构可作为玻色多体波函数的通用基础模型，以单一训练覆盖多个哈密顿量参数点并刻画相变，为跨相变的神经网络量子态（neural-network quantum states）表示提供可行路径；摘要未涉及其他维度或费米子系统。

Abstract: We propose Boseformer, a universal attention-based neural-network architecture for representing the wavefunctions of particles with bosonic quantum statistics, including superfluid and crystal phases. The model is trained to generate the wavefunction as a function of a Hamiltonian parameter across the quantum phase transition between these phases. Once trained with variational Monte Carlo optimization, it can serve as a foundation model describing the phase transition without the need to retrain it for individual parameter values. We demonstrate our method on a system of two-dimensional bosons with $1/r^3$ repulsive interaction.

</details>

### [107] [A source-based decomposition of thermal noise for mechanical system design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31628)
*Vermeer Bonhomme,Matthew Evans*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出集总元件（lumped-element）机械系统的热噪声（thermal noise）表述方法，将读出端总位移热噪声分解为各耗散元件的贡献之和，并据此给出比较串联与并联元件贡献、识别主导噪声源的设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热噪声限制精密机械仪器的灵敏度，而现有分析缺乏对集总元件系统中各耗散元件贡献的清晰拆分，难以直接指导设计与噪声源辨识。

Method: 将各元件的热力（thermal force）与其传递到读出端的传递函数分离，形成逐元件贡献的分解式；该方法适用于频率相关耗散（frequency-dependent dissipation）以及处于不同稳态温度、满足局部热平衡（local thermal equilibrium）的元件。

Result: 该分解给出串联与并联元件贡献的比较规则，用于识别主导噪声源，并据此确定并联与串联耗散稀释（dissipation dilution）降低位移热噪声的频率区间；在带损耗支撑的单摆、转动柔性铰（rotational flexure）和刚性安装的月球光学器件示例中，即使支撑对系统动力学影响很小，支撑噪声仍可占主导。

Conclusion: 该框架可统一构建位移噪声预算（displacement-noise budget），并通过反馈控制悬架（feedback-controlled suspension）示例说明传感与驱动噪声亦可纳入同一预算体系。

Abstract: Thermal noise limits the sensitivity of precision mechanical instruments. We present a thermal- noise formulation for lumped-element mechanical systems that writes the total displacement thermal noise at the readout as the sum of contributions from all dissipative elements. For each contribution, we separate the thermal force generated by the element from its transfer to the readout. This decomposition provides simple design rules for comparing contributions from elements in series and in parallel and identifying the dominant noise source. It applies to frequency-dependent dissipation and to elements at different steady temperatures under local thermal equilibrium. We use it to identify the frequency regimes in which parallel and series dissipation dilution reduce displacement thermal noise. We then use examples of a pendulum on a lossy support, a rotational flexure, and rigidly mounted lunar optics to show how support noise can dominate even when the support has little effect on the system dynamics. Finally, a feedback-controlled suspension illustrates how sensing and actuation noise can be included in the same displacement-noise budget.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Obstructions to Hadamard Deformations of Fourier Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21871)
*Shuler Hopkins,Remus Nicoara*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文研究 Fourier 自旋模型交换方（commuting square）在交换方空间中的形变，证明了当 n 既非素数幂也非素数两倍时，包络切空间中的某些方向无法由序列形变实现，从而给出 Fourier 矩阵缺陷（defect）的严格不等式与三次、四次、五次障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果表明对每个 n 都存在足够多的一参数解析族张成包络切空间，这与“方向可由形变实现”的直觉相符；本文旨在检验这一直觉在何种 n 下失效，并量化 Fourier 矩阵形变的受限程度。

Method: 在交换方（commuting square）空间中，用复 Hadamard 矩阵（complex Hadamard matrices）对 $\sqrt n F_n$ 作形变；通过分析包络切空间（enveloping tangent space）中单位向量能否作为序列形变的收敛方向，并结合 n 的素因子结构分类导出高阶障碍。

Result: 若 n 既非素数幂也非素数两倍，则包络切空间存在不能实现为序列形变收敛方向的单位向量；故过 $\sqrt n F_n$ 的任一可微 Hadamard 族的导数秩严格小于包络切空间维数（即 $F_n$ 的缺陷）。当 n 有三个以上不同素因子时得三次障碍，恰有两个素因子且含重复因子时得四次障碍，为两个不同奇素数之积时得五次障碍。

Conclusion: Fourier 矩阵缺陷不能由包络切空间维数完全刻画，形变方向受高阶障碍限制；结论推广了作者此前关于阶 30 的结果，说明障碍阶数由 n 的素因子结构决定。

Abstract: We study deformations of the Fourier spin model commuting square in the space of commuting squares, or equivalently deformations of the matrix $\sqrt nF_n$, where $F_n$ denotes the Fourier matrix, by complex Hadamard matrices. If $n$ is neither a prime power nor twice a prime, we show that there exist unit vectors in the enveloping tangent space that cannot be realized as directions of convergence of sequential deformations.
  Consequently, the derivative of any differentiable Hadamard family through $\sqrt nF_n$ has rank strictly smaller than the dimension of the enveloping tangent space at $\sqrt nF_n$ (i.e., the defect of $F_n$). This is particularly surprising because for every $n$ there exist enough one-parameter analytic families to provide a basis of directions in the enveloping tangent space.
  We obtain cubic obstructions when $n$ has at least three distinct prime divisors, quartic obstructions when it has exactly two and a repeated factor, and quintic obstructions when it is the product of two distinct odd primes. These results extend our order $30$ result from \cite{HN}.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Constructing physical mechanisms with artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31653)
*Vladimir A. Baulin,Austin Cook,Shagor Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个“原生方程（equation-native）”系统，读取全部250万篇arXiv文稿中的公式，证明跨学科机制（mechanism）可从方程中被编译（compile）并通过确定性计算（deterministic calculation）重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制常跨学科复现，但其运行方式、边界、参数与闭合关系散落在推导的不同步骤中，仅靠文本阅读或公式拟合的方法难以捕捉，故需直接从方程层面提取机制。

Method: 构建原生方程系统读取250万篇arXiv文稿的公式，并设计一个构造器（constructor），对机制的部件执行分离（detach）、迁移（transfer）、展开（unfold）、组合（compose）与连接（join）；当部件不匹配时，失配本身即构成缺失项（missing term）的方程。

Result: 构造器实现了多种跨域构造：将阈值反应器（threshold reactor）改造为可由弱信号写入的存储器，把振荡器（oscillator）通过各单独失效的两处改动变为开关（switch），并将节律（rhythm）转化为化学量的调节器，且每种构造均给出实验检验条件。

Conclusion: 机制可被视为可从科学文献方程中编译、并经确定性计算构造的对象，为跨学科机制发现与实验设计提供了方程层面的系统化路径。

Abstract: Mechanisms recur across the sciences: the equation for heat in a solid also describes molecular diffusion or a Brownian particle. A mechanism is rarely written in one equation; its operation, boundaries, parameters and closures are stated at different steps of a derivation, where methods that read text or fit formulas miss it. We built an equation-native system that reads the equations of all 2.5 million arXiv manuscripts, and show that mechanisms can be compiled from them and constructed by deterministic calculation. A constructor detaches, transfers, unfolds, composes and joins their parts across disciplines; where parts do not fit, the failure is an equation for the missing term. Constructions turn a threshold reactor into a memory that a weak signal writes, an oscillator into a switch through two changes that each fail alone, and a rhythm into the regulator of a chemical amount, each with conditions for an experimental test.

</details>


<div id='nucl-ex'></div>

# nucl-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Background characterization in the PIKACHU Phase 1 search for two-neutrino double-beta decay of $^{160}$Gd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31837)
*T. Omori,T. Iida,A. Gando,N. Hinohara,K. Hosokawa,S. Ishidate,M. Ishigami,T. Kishimoto,K. Mizukoshi,T. Sako,S. Sugi,M. Yoshino,K. Fushimi,K. Kamada,Y. Shoji,Y. Suga,H. Suzuki,K. Takahashi*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 在神冈地下实验室 PIKACHU 实验第一阶段中，利用 Ce:Gd₃Al₂Ga₃O₁₂ 闪烁体对 ¹⁶⁰Gd 双中微子双贝塔衰变（2ν2β）搜寻进行本底表征，给出半衰期下限 T₁/₂ ≥ 2.32×10¹⁹ 年（90% C.L.）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为搜寻 ¹⁶⁰Gd 的双中微子双贝塔衰变（two-neutrino double-beta decay）建立可靠的本底认识，明确探测器响应与污染来源，是该衰变搜寻实验的必要前提。

Method: 采用延迟符合分析（delayed-coincidence analysis）与基于 GEANT4 模拟的能谱拟合（spectral fitting）研究实验本底；并通过 ²¹⁴Po α 谱的特征性畸变识别晶体表面污染（surface contamination）。

Result: 经改进的本底模型可再现观测能谱；未观测到可归因于 ¹⁶⁰Gd 2ν2β 衰变的显著超出信号，得到 90% 置信水平下半衰期下限 T₁/₂ ≥ 2.32×10¹⁹ 年。

Conclusion: 所建立的本底模型与分析框架为 PIKACHU 实验后续阶段以发现 ¹⁶⁰Gd 2ν2β 衰变为目标的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We report a background characterization for the search for two-neutrino double-beta ($2\nu2β$) decay of $^{160}$Gd using Ce:Gd$_3$Al$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$ scintillators in the PIKACHU experiment Phase 1 at the Kamioka underground laboratory. The experimental backgrounds were investigated through delayed-coincidence analysis and spectral fitting based on GEANT4 simulations. In particular, surface contamination of the crystal was identified through the characteristic distortion of the $^{214}$Po $α$-ray spectrum, leading to an improved background model that reproduces the observed energy spectra. No significant excess attributable to $^{160}$Gd $2\nu2β$ decay was observed, yielding a lower limit of $T^{2ν}_{1/2} \geq 2.32 \times 10^{19}$ yr at 90\% C.L. The established background model and analysis framework provide a foundation for future PIKACHU phases aiming to discover $^{160}$Gd $2\nu2β$ decay.

</details>

### [111] [Tidally Induced Coda: Double Transients from Black Hole-Star Binary Encounters with Massive Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31361)
*Konstantinos Kritos,Rosalba Perna*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用SpaceHub直接少体散射实验研究黑洞-恒星双星与大质量黑洞（massive black hole, MBH）的双曲近距离交会，量化潮汐诱导偏心率触发微潮汐瓦解事件（micro-tidal disruption event, μTDE）的概率，并给出截面与双耀发时延的拟合公式及事件率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量黑洞周围的致密恒星环境可产生暂现电磁与引力波源，MBH潮汐场能强烈扰动近距离交会双星的内部轨道，但黑洞-恒星双星在此过程中的各类动力学结局及其观测特征尚缺乏系统量化。

Method: 采用少体直接散射实验（direct few-body scattering experiments），使用SpaceHub代码模拟黑洞-恒星双星与MBH的双曲交会（hyperbolic encounters），系统统计不同动力学结局的概率随双星轨道参数与MBH质量的依赖关系，重点分析潮汐诱导偏心率导致恒星被黑洞伴星瓦解、碎片随后被MBH吸积的μTDE通道。

Result: 推导出μTDE截面（cross section）与两次耀发之间时间延迟的拟合公式（fitting formulae）；对于10^6 M_⊙的MBH，时延可达数月量级；在40 Gpc光度距离内估计事件率为每年数个至约100次。

Conclusion: μTDE伴随的双耀发现象为微潮汐瓦解事件提供了独特的观测特征（observational signature），可作为识别该类事件的判据。

Abstract: Dense stellar environments surrounding massive black holes (MBHs) provide a natural setting for dynamical interactions that can produce transient electromagnetic and gravitational-wave sources. In particular, the tidal field of an MBH can strongly perturb binaries undergoing close encounters with it, driving their internal orbits to extreme eccentricities. Here, we investigate the outcomes of such interactions for binaries composed of a stellar-mass black hole (BH) and a star. Using direct few-body scattering experiments with the code SpaceHub, we explore hyperbolic encounters between BH-star binaries and MBHs. We systematically quantify the probabilities of different dynamical outcomes as functions of the binary orbital parameters and MBH mass, with particular emphasis on a channel in which the tidally induced eccentricity triggers a micro-tidal disruption event ($μ$TDE), as the star is disrupted by its BH companion, followed by accretion of the stellar debris onto the MBH. We derive fitting formulae for the $μ$TDE cross section and for the time delay between the two resulting flares, finding that the latter can be a few months for an MBH of $10^6\,M_\odot$. These double transients offer a distinctive observational signature of $μ$TDEs. We estimate event rates ranging from a few to $\sim 100\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$ within a luminosity distance of $40\,{\rm Gpc}$.

</details>


### [112] [Self-arresting vortex rearrangements in pinned spinning-down superfluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33103)
*Anantharaman Sekharipuram Viswanathan,Dipankar Bhattacharya*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究揭示超流体中量子化涡旋（quantized vortices）在降速旋转容器内发生突发集体迁移的起始、演化与终止机制：起始由最小开角的涡旋阵列扇区在壁面附近被腾空触发，边界流动减弱使迁移中止，耗竭区尺度受临界脱钉速度（critical unpinning velocity）、容器尺寸和初始转速控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流体在含钉扎中心（pinning centres）的降速旋转容器中会出现量子化涡旋的突发集体迁移，但其起始、进程与终止机制此前理解不足，且该过程可能与脉冲星自转突变（pulsar glitches）相关。

Method: 基于含钉扎中心的降速旋转超流体系统开展研究，通过追踪涡旋阵列（vortex array）在壁面附近的演化，分析涡旋逃逸（exodus）的触发条件与流动响应。

Result: 发现三点结果：1）当涡旋阵列中具有最小开角（minimum opening angle）的扇区在壁面附近被腾空时，首批涡旋逃逸发生；2）该逃逸使边界附近的超流流动强度持续下降，从而中止涡旋退出；3）形成的耗竭区（depletion region）具有明确尺寸，主要由临界脱钉速度、容器尺寸和初始旋转速率决定，并限制传递至容器的角动量。

Conclusion: 涡旋集体迁移过程由几何扇区条件触发、由边界流动衰减自限制，耗竭区尺度限制角动量传递；作者最后讨论了该机制对脉冲星自转突变（pulsar glitches）的相关意义。

Abstract: Superfluids, in a spinning-down container with pinning centres, exhibit sudden collective migration of quantized vortices. The onset, progress, and termination of this process remain poorly understood. Here, we show that: 1) the first exodus of vortices occurs when a sector of the vortex array, having a minimum opening angle, is vacated close to the wall; 2) driven by this exodus, the superfluid flow near the boundary continually decreases in strength and arrests the exit; and 3) the resultant depletion region has well-defined dimensions---governed primarily by the critical unpinning velocity, the container size, and the initial rotation rate---that limit the angular momentum transferred to the container. We conclude by discussing the relevance to pulsar glitches.

</details>

### [113] [Updated T2K measurement of neutrino oscillation parameters with the first data from the Gadolinium-loaded far detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32106)
*The T2K collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: T2K 实验利用总计约 21.4（16.3）×10²⁰ POT 的（反）中微子数据，首次纳入掺钆（Gadolinium, Gd）的 Super-Kamiokande 数据，重新测量中微子振荡参数，结果支持正常质量顺序（normal mass ordering）与近最大 CP 破坏相位（CP-violating phase）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在既有结果基础上增加数据量并首次引入掺钆远探测器数据，同时改进事例选择与系统误差处理，以更高精度约束中微子振荡参数（neutrino oscillation parameters）。

Method: 基于 T2K 远探测器 Super-Kamiokande 的（反）中微子模式数据，相比此前结果新增 1.7×10²⁰ POT，并采用改进的远探测器事例选择与系统误差处理；同时给出半频率学派（semi-frequentist）与贝叶斯（Bayesian）两种统计分析。

Result: 给出 sin²θ₂₃、Δm²₃₂、sin²θ₁₃ 与 δ_CP 的测量结果；两种分析均倾向正常质量顺序与近最大 CP 破坏相位，与中微子最大混合（maximal mixing）相容，并略微偏好 θ₂₃ 的上八分圆（upper octant）。

Conclusion: 掺钆数据与新分析方法的引入提升了振荡参数测量的统计与系统精度，结果整体支持正常质量顺序与近最大 CP 破坏，但对 θ₂₃ 八分圆的偏好仍较微弱。

Abstract: The T2K experiment presents updated measurements of neutrino oscillation parameters using $21.4(16.3)\times10^{20}$ protons on target (POT) in (anti-)neutrino mode at the far detector, Super-Kamiokande. This analysis uses an additional neutrino dataset of $1.7 \times 10^{20}$ POT compared to the previous result, and for the very first time, data collected with Super-Kamiokande doped with Gadolinium (Gd). Significant improvements were introduced in the far detector selection and systematic error treatment. Both semi-frequentist and Bayesian analyses are presented, including results on $\sin^{2} θ_{23}$, $Δm^2_{32} $, $\sin^{2} θ_{13}$ and $δ_{CP}$. Both analyses favor normal mass ordering with a nearly maximal CP-violating phase and are compatible with neutrino maximal mixing, slightly favouring the upper $θ_{23}$ octant.

</details>


### [114] [Measurements of $|V_{cd(s)}|$ , decay constants, and form factors in (semi-)leptonic $D_{(s)}$ decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32309)
*Medina Ablikim*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该会议报告基于BESIII实验积累的全球最大粲介子（charm-meson）样本，汇报了利用（半）轻子（(semi-)leptonic）D⁺与D_s⁺衰变对衰变常数（decay constants）、跃迁形状因子（transition form factors）及CKM矩阵元（CKM matrix elements）的最新测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKM矩阵元（CKM matrix elements）与衰变常数（decay constants）、跃迁形状因子（transition form factors）是检验标准模型（Standard Model）中夸克混合与强子化描述的关键量，而大统计量粲介子（charm-meson）样本是提升其测量精度的前提。

Method: 依托BESIII实验积累的全球最大粲介子样本，分析（半）轻子（(semi-)leptonic）D⁺与D_s⁺衰变过程以提取相关物理参数。

Result: 摘要仅指出报告了上述三类物理量（衰变常数、跃迁形状因子、CKM矩阵元）的最新测量结果，未给出具体数值或精度。

Conclusion: BESIII的大统计量粲介子数据为粲物理（charm physics）中衰变常数、形状因子与CKM矩阵元的高精度测定提供了基础，相关工作以会议报告（proceeding）形式汇总。

Abstract: The BESIII experiment accumulated the world's largest charm-meson samples. This proceeding presents recent measurements of decay constants, transition form factors, and CKM matrix elements from (semi-)leptonic \(D^+\) and \(D_s^+\) decays based on these datasets.

</details>

### [115] [Longitudinal-dither wavefront sensing: Calibration-free measurement of mode mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31315)
*Masayuki Nakano*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种无需外部标定的模式失配（mode mismatch）测量方法：通过纵向抖动（longitudinal dither）引入参考场，借助已有波前传感器（wavefront sensors）与信号链完成自标定，并在2 W与62 W输入功率下演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测器与注入光束之间的模式失配会降低臂腔储能功率与压缩（squeezing）水平；而校正失配需要在工作功率、腔共振状态下，以已标定尺度测量三个二阶模式（second-order modes）的复振幅，现有做法依赖外部标定。

Method: 在已用于对准控制的波前传感器上移动光束以获取正比于失配的信号；对腔施加纵向抖动，为共振模式叠加一个参考场（reference field），该参考场经由相同信号链到达传感器，从而自标定信号尺度以及传感器与执行器的古伊相位（Gouy phases），无需外部标定。

Result: 在输入功率2 W与62 W的共振运行光腔上验证：2 W下所得光学模型在模式成分上与独立测量一致，执行器古伊相位与布局模型一致且落在约1°不确定度内；62 W下将热致本征模式（eigenmode）变化分解为束腰尺寸与束腰位置的变化。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需每个受控光腔本就具备的组件，可实现运行状态下模式匹配（mode matching）的连续原位监测（in situ monitoring）。

Abstract: Mode mismatch between a gravitational-wave detector and the beams injected into it lowers the power stored in the arms and the level of squeezing. Correcting it requires the complex amplitudes of the three second-order modes, measured on a calibrated scale with the cavity on resonance at its operating power. Here we present a method that obtains a signal proportional to the mismatch by moving the beam on the wavefront sensors already used for alignment control. A longitudinal dither of the cavity adds to the resonant mode a reference field, which reaches the sensors through the same signal chains and so calibrates both the scale of this signal and the Gouy phases of the sensors and actuators, with no external calibration. We demonstrate the method on an optical cavity held on resonance in its operating state at input powers of 2 W and 62 W. At 2 W it yields an optical model that agrees with independent measurements in the mode content, and with the layout model in the actuator Gouy phase within its uncertainty of about $1^\circ$. At 62 W the thermally induced change of the eigenmode is decomposed into waist-size and waist-position changes. The method requires only components that every controlled cavity already possesses and enables continuous in situ monitoring of the mode matching in the operating state.

</details>


### [116] [Topological terahertz fields from tunable vectorial currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32586)
*Jacob Pettine,Tae Gwan Park,Yanhe Lyu,Wangke Yu,John B. Maier,Matthew Yeung,Rujuta Sane,Chun-Chieh Chang,Nuh Gedik,Yijie Shen,Hou-Tong Chen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用石墨烯等离激元超表面中的瞬态矢量电流产生并主动调控单周期太赫兹环向光脉冲，实现电场主导与磁场主导模式间连续切换并完整重构其时空拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来信息处理需要具备更大容量、内在抗噪能力且可与量子材料直接集成的光子架构；拓扑光脉冲虽被视为有希望的候选，但其独特性质的复杂性使其在产生、调控与时空完整解析上面临挑战。

Method: 在光电超表面（optoelectronic metasurface）中，通过三角对称的纳米等离激元谐振器（nanoplasmonic resonators）使入射线偏振调控石墨烯中光电流的局域方向，并以全局绕转图案实现压缩流与环流之间的连续调节；在超快激发下由瞬态矢量电流辐射出太赫兹环向脉冲（toroidal pulses）。

Result: 所产生辐射为单周期太赫兹环向脉冲，覆盖电场主导与磁场主导模式，并具有动态演化的斯格明子织构（skyrmion textures）；全时空映射与重构实现了光之环向结构的完整可视化，直接追踪闭合场面，并在单个周期内连续跟踪斯格明子数与手性（helicity）。

Conclusion: 该工作将二维矢量电流与丰富的三维电磁场结构相联系，并建立了面向高维光子信息处理与新兴材料光谱学的通用集成平台。

Abstract: Future information processing calls for photonic architectures with greater capacity, intrinsic resilience to noise, and direct integration with quantum materials and devices. Topological light pulses have recently emerged as promising candidates to meet these demands, yet the complexity underlying their unique properties also makes them challenging to generate, tune, and fully resolve in space and time. Here, we generate and actively tune topological light fields through transient vectorial currents in an optoelectronic metasurface. Incident linear polarization steers the local direction of photocurrents in graphene via nanoplasmonic resonators with triangular symmetry, while a global winding pattern enables continuous tuning between compressional and circulating charge flows. Under ultrafast excitation, these currents radiate single-cycle terahertz toroidal pulses spanning electric- and magnetic-dominant modes with dynamically evolving skyrmion textures. Full spatiotemporal mapping and reconstruction provide a complete visualization of the toroidal structure of light, directly tracing the closed field surfaces while continuously tracking skyrmion number and helicity throughout a single cycle. These results connect two-dimensional vectorial currents to rich three-dimensional electromagnetic field structures, while establishing a versatile integrated platform for higher-dimensional photonic information processing and emerging material spectroscopies.

</details>


### [117] [Packing the Bragg sphere: complete three-dimensional photonic band gaps at the refractive index of polymers (1.55)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32789)
*Alexander Itin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文通过互易空间设计（reciprocal-space design）与整数格点上的离散优化，系统搜索周期性三维结构，将完全光子带隙（complete photonic band gap）所需的折射率对比度从约1.9降至1.55，并指出更低对比度带隙的可能搜索方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前完全三维光子带隙仅在折射率对比度约1.9及以上（由杆连接金刚石晶格实现）时才被找到，而准周期结构的互易空间设计已在更低对比度下实现显著发射衰减，故希望系统性地把低对比度带隙设计推广到周期性三维结构。

Method: 在高能带之上开隙需要介电常数的强傅里叶分量（Fourier components）靠近该能带的布拉格球（Bragg sphere）；据此从布拉格球上多面体顶点的傅里叶峰出发构造大晶胞晶体，并针对密集细化的带边进行优化，在整数格点上求解离散优化问题。

Result: 优化结构在折射率对比度低至1.55（常见聚合物与空气的对比度）时即可打开完全带隙；在所测多面体中，二十-十二面体（icosidodecahedron）的有理逼近给出最有效的傅里叶峰布局。数值计算在分辨率序列下、于整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）搜索带边，确认了带隙存在。

Conclusion: 最优设计为连通多孔网络，适合用印刷聚合物开展微波测试。一个对任意结构成立的严格界（rigorous bound）并不排除更低折射率下的带隙，而二十-十二面体的后续有理逼近其布拉格区几乎精确匹配第576与2440能带，指示了寻找更低对比度带隙的方向。

Abstract: Complete 3D photonic band gaps have so far been found only at refractive-index contrasts of about 1.9 and above, reached by rod-connected diamond lattice structures. Reciprocal-space design of quasiperiodic structures recently achieved considerable emission attenuation at much lower contrasts [L. Maiwald et al., Adv. Optical Mater. 10, 2100785 (2022)]. Here we implement systematic search and reciprocal-space design of periodic 3D structures with complete band gaps, solving a discrete optimization problem on an integer lattice. A gap above a high band needs the strongest Fourier components of the dielectric close to the Bragg sphere of that band. Crystals with large cells, started from Fourier peaks at the vertices of a polyhedron on this sphere and optimized against densely refined band edges, open complete gaps down to an index contrast of 1.55, that of common polymers against air. A rational approximant of an icosidodecahedron gave the most efficient location of the Fourier peaks among the polyhedra tested. Numerical calculations confirm the gaps over a resolution series, with band edges searched over the whole Brillouin zone. The best designs are connected porous networks, suited to a microwave test with printed polymer. A rigorous bound, valid for any structure, does not exclude gaps at lower index, and the next rational approximants of the icosidodecahedron, whose Bragg zones match bands 576 and 2440 almost exactly, point to where gaps at even lower contrast may be sought.

</details>
