### [118] [HEP-Ex-Bench: Evaluating Agentic Performance in High Energy Physics Experiment Data Analyses with Sealed Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28194)
*Andrzej Novák,Yiyang Zhao,Dolores Garcia,Eric A. Moreno,Jeffrey Krupa,Víctor Bresó-Pla,Samuel Bright-Thonney,Lukas Heinrich,Michael Kagan,Philip Harris*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 提出基于盲分析（blind analysis）与信号注入测试（injection tests）的定量评估框架，用程序生成的"密封"私有数据集（sealed private datasets）检验智能体（agentic systems）所建高能物理分析流程的准确性与稳健性，并给出多模型基准对比结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统（agentic systems）被越来越多用于自动化实验高能物理（experimental high-energy physics）测量，但其结果正确性目前只能依赖大量专家评审（expert review）才能可靠判断，缺少可扩展的定量评估手段。

Method: 要求智能体在公开数据集上开发数据分析流程（data analysis pipeline），评估则在程序化生成的密封私有数据集上进行：其中注入信号（injected signals），并在真实探测器不确定度（detector uncertainties）范围内变化模拟设置；每个智能体须提交单一工作流，跨多个"密封世界"（sealed worlds）统一评估。

Result: 在基于 CMS 公开数据（CMS open data）构建的四个任务上验证该方案；最佳模型 Gemini 4 Argon 平均达到专家参考分析（expert reference analyses）得分的 60%，高于 GPT 6 Astra 的 38% 与 Claude Opus 5.5 的 31%；在其中一个任务的个别会话中，Gemini 4 Argon 超过参考分析。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为实验高能物理中科学工作的可靠基准测试（benchmarking）手段，基准已发布于 https://hep-ex-bench.github.io。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly being explored for automating experimental high-energy physics measurements, but the correctness of their results can today be reliably evaluated only via extensive expert review. To address this, we propose a method to perform detailed quantitative evaluations of agent-driven analyses through the use of blind analysis and injection tests, which are critical in a typical expert review. Agents develop a data analysis pipeline on a public dataset, while the evaluation is performed on programmatically generated sealed private datasets where signals are injected and simulation settings varied within realistic detector uncertainties. By requiring agents to provide a single workflow that is evaluated across these "sealed worlds", we are able to test the accuracy and robustness of the workflows. We demonstrate the approach on four tasks built from CMS open data and present it as a way to reliably benchmark scientific work in experimental high-energy physics. At the time of writing, the best-performing model, Gemini 4 Argon, reaches 60% of the score of the expert reference analyses on average, ahead of GPT 6 Astra at 38% and Claude Opus 5.5 at 31%. In individual sessions on one of the tasks, Gemini 4 Argon exceeds the reference. The benchmark is hosted at https://hep-ex-bench.github.io

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Free hyperboloidal evolution in 3D: gauge waves and constraint-violating gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31379)
*Alex Vañó-Viñuales*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作将基于 BSSN/Z4 表述的共形紧致化（conformal compactification）方法从球对称推广至全三维，首次实现了使用广义相对论通用表述的三维自由双曲型演化（hyperboloidal evolution），并在未来零无穷（future null infinity）成功提取信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲型演化（hyperboloidal evolution）可使数值相对论（numerical relativity）模拟直接触及未来零无穷，但此前的共形紧致化方案仅限球对称情形，缺乏三维通用表述下的实现。

Method: 扩展基于 BSSN/Z4 表述的共形紧致化方法至全三维模拟，并以规范波（gauge waves）及扰动闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）的违反约束引力波（constraint-violating gravitational waves）进行非线性演化检验。

Result: 实现被证明可靠，可在未来零无穷处成功提取信号；该工作是首个采用广义相对论通用表述的三维自由双曲型演化。

Conclusion: 结果支持将共形紧致化双曲型演化推广至三维的可行性，为在数值相对论中直接获取未来零无穷处的引力波信号提供了基础。

Abstract: Hyperboloidal evolution allows to reach future null infinity within numerical relativity simulations. This work extends the conformal compactification approach with BSSN/Z4 from spherical symmetry to full 3D simulations. This is the first free numerical hyperboloidal evolution in 3D with a common formulation of general relativity. In this first step, gauge waves and constraint-violating gravitational waves perturbing Minkowski spacetime are evolved nonlinearly and show that the implementation is reliable and signals can be successfully extracted at future null infinity.

</details>


### [120] [How to Play a Black Hole: Understanding Quasinormal Mode Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31381)
*Lucía Vélez-Tartajo,Vitor Cardoso,Jaime Redondo-Yuste*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在受控条件下研究史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）准正规模（quasinormal mode, QNM）的激发机制。无源情形下，较宽的初始数据抑制模式激发并相对增强晚期尾（late-time tail）贡献，靠近光球（light ring）的初始数据强烈抑制泛音（overtone）；坠落源（plunging source）则产生两个环降（ringdown）阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞准正规模的激发对解读引力波（gravitational-wave）信号、反推黑洞性质至关重要，但模式激发对扰动性质与动力学的依赖仍缺乏受控刻画。本文旨在建立可控的探针（probe）框架以厘清环降信号的来源。

Method: 在史瓦西背景下采用受控探针研究准正规模的激发：考察无源情形下初始数据的宽度与局域位置（含光球附近）的影响；比较坠落源产生的散射辐射；并通过在有限半径处关闭源（cutoff radius）来检验强场区（strong-field region）对信号的贡献。

Result: 无源情形下，较宽的初始数据抑制模式激发、提升晚期尾的相对贡献；靠近光球的局域初始数据显著抑制泛音而相对主导基模（fundamental mode）。坠落源导致两个不同的环降阶段，分别关联粒子坠落过程中的散射辐射与粒子穿越光球。切断源后所得波形随切断半径呈指数形式地逼近标准坠落波形。

Conclusion: 准正规模的激发显著依赖扰动的性质与动力学，且环降的主要信号源自强场区。该受控框架为研究黑洞环降的物理起源提供了系统化途径，并有助于引力波信号中黑洞参数的反演。

Abstract: Understanding the excitation of black hole quasinormal modes is essential for interpreting gravitational-wave signals and inferring black hole properties from them. In this work, we investigate the excitation of the quasinormal modes of Schwarzschild black holes under controlled probes. In the absence of a source, we find that broader initial data suppresses mode excitation, enhancing the relative contribution of the late-time tail, while data localized near the light ring strongly suppresses overtones relative to the fundamental mode. On the other hand, plunging sources give rise to two distinct ringdown stages, associated with scattered radiation generated during the particle's infall and with the particle crossing the light ring. Finally, we investigate how much of the signal is sourced from the strong-field region by switching off the source at some finite radius. The resulting waveform is exponentially close -- in cutoff radius -- to that of a standard infall. These results provide insight into the dependence of QNM excitation on the properties and dynamics of the perturbation, and offer a controlled setting in which to investigate the origin of black hole ringdown.

</details>


### [121] [On the evidence of lensing in GW231123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31389)
*Anna Liu,Mick Wright,Jef Heynen,Eungwang Seo,Isaac C. F. Wong,Disha Hegde,Laura E. Uronen,Harsh Narola,Chris Van Den Broeck,Martin Hendry,Tjonnie G. F. Li,Otto A. Hannuksela,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将 GW231123 解释为同一双黑洞并合事件的多重引力透镜像（gravitational lensing）叠加，可使四种波形模型下推断的高自旋与高质量趋于一致，透镜解释相对无透镜背景的显著性约为 3.96σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波（gravitational waves）传播路径上的大质量天体可产生引力透镜效应，但迄今尚未被观测到。GW231123 是迄今观测到的质量最大的双黑洞并合事件，其准圆轨道（quasi-circular）分析给出的高质量与高自旋参数对所用波形模型（waveform model）高度敏感，论文据此检验透镜效应能否解释这些反常特征。

Method: 将信号建模为同一并合事件多个相互重叠的透镜像（overlapping images）的叠加，并在四种波形模型上分别做拟合与比较；同时以无透镜信号样本作为背景（background）进行显著性估计。

Result: 四种波形模型均支持透镜解释；该解释削弱了对高自旋的支持，使推断的自旋与质量在不同波形模型间更为一致。与无透镜背景相比，透镜解释的估计显著性为 3.96^{+0.68}_{-0.30}σ。

Conclusion: 引力透镜是对 GW231123 颇具吸引力的解释，但当前证据强度尚不足以作出确定性断言（definitive claim）。

Abstract: Just like light, gravitational waves may be gravitationally lensed by massive objects along their travel path, however, this has not been observed so far. GW231123 is the most massive binary black-hole merger observed to-date, and quasi-circular gravitational wave analyses infer high masses and spin parameters that are particularly sensitive to the waveform model used. Here we investigate whether gravitational lensing can account for these features. We model the signal as a superposition of multiple overlapping images of the same merger and find support for lensing across four waveform models. The lensing interpretation reduces the support for these high spins, bringing both the inferred spins and masses into closer agreement across waveform models. A comparison with a background of unlensed signals gives the lensing interpretation an estimated significance of $3.96^{+0.68}_{-0.30}σ$. This makes lensing a compelling interpretation of this event but is insufficient for a definitive claim.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum black hole evaporation from thin shell collapse: a complete effective picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31399)
*Michał Bobula,Francesco Fazzini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构建一个受圈量子引力（loop quantum gravity）启发的有效模型，统一描述薄壳坍缩、黑洞形成、蒸发与遗迹动力学，且在不同有效运动方程、物质源与蒸发律下均成立。结果表明最终产物强烈依赖蒸发律：Stefan-Boltzmann 律下形成延伸至新渐近区的极值黑洞（含事件视界与柯西视界），而 Schwinger 机制下不形成视界，仅出现临时动力学俘获区并留下无视界零质量遗迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力（quantum gravity）被期望在解决经典奇点（classical singularity）并给出完整、可预测的时空图景中发挥核心作用；本文旨在构建一个能实现该期望的有效模型，统一处理薄壳坍缩、黑洞形成、蒸发及遗迹演化。

Method: 在准静态近似（quasi-static approximation）下纳入反作用（backreaction），将辐射建模为无效尘埃（null dust），并利用 Darmois-Israel 衔接条件（junction conditions）在蒸发面上匹配入射与出射通量；框架对多种有效运动方程、物质源和蒸发律具有一般适用性。

Result: 在由 Stefan-Boltzmann 定律支配的蒸发律下，蒸发的最终阶段产生一个延伸至新渐近区的极值黑洞（extremal black hole），同时具有事件视界（event horizon）与柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）。相反，在 Schwinger 方法下不形成事件视界或柯西视界，而是出现临时的动力学俘获区（dynamical trapped region），且所得时空保持整体双曲性（globally hyperbolic）。

Conclusion: 该模型表明黑洞蒸发的最终结局对蒸发律高度敏感：Schwinger 情形预测一个逃逸至未来零无穷（future null infinity）的无视界零质量遗迹（horizonless null remnant）。整体而言，所提框架在多种设定下具有一般有效性，为经典奇点消解与蒸发终态提供了统一描述。

Abstract: Quantum gravity is expected to play a central role in resolving classical singularities and providing a complete, predictive spacetime picture. In this paper, we develop an effective model inspired by loop quantum gravity that aims to fulfill this expectation, providing a unified description of thin shell collapse, black hole formation, evaporation, and remnant dynamics. The framework exhibits general validity, holding across different effective equations of motion, matter sources, and evaporation laws. Specifically, we incorporate backreaction within a quasi-static approximation, modeling the radiation as null dust and matching the ingoing and outgoing fluxes across an evaporation surface using the Darmois-Israel junction conditions. For an evaporation law governed by the Stefan-Boltzmann law, we find that the final stage of evaporation yields an extremal black hole extending into a new asymptotic region, featuring both event and Cauchy horizons. Conversely, under the Schwinger approach, no event or Cauchy horizons form, instead, a temporary dynamical trapped region emerges, and the resulting spacetime remains globally hyperbolic. Under this variant, our model predicts a horizonless null remnant escaping to future null infinity.

</details>


### [123] [Split Ends: UV Physics branches Black Hole Hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31438)
*Georg Trenkler,Leonardo G. Trombetta,Alexander Vikman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将破缺复标量场的戈德斯通模态与高斯-博内不变量耦合，会在视界附近触发对称性恢复，使黑洞偏离广义相对论解更显著，并导致同质量黑洞存在多支解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究中位移对称标量场（shift-symmetric scalar field）与高斯-博内不变量（Gauss-Bonnet invariant）耦合产生标量毛（scalar hair），但标量场性质较为简化；本文旨在考察标量场作为对称破缺势下复标量场戈德斯通玻色子（Goldstone）的情形，探讨质量间隙（mass gap）对黑洞解的影响。

Method: 在理论上将戈德斯通场与高斯-博内不变量耦合，构建带对称破缺势的复标量场模型，并针对黑洞尺度与逆质量间隙（inverse mass gap）可比的情形求解黑洞解，分析视界附近场的行为。

Result: 对于尺度与逆质量间隙相当的黑洞，带质量间隙的场在视界附近趋于恢复对称性，从而增强相对标准黑洞解的偏离；同时，同一质量的黑洞可具有最多三种不同的视界面积（horizon area）与表面引力（surface gravity）。

Conclusion: 这种解的分支（branching）结构对黑洞物理具有深远影响，并可能在引力波（gravitational waves）观测中留下可探测信号。

Abstract: Coupling a shift-symmetric scalar field to the Gauss-Bonnet invariant modifies black holes (BHs) via scalar hair. Here we consider this field to be the Goldstone of a complex scalar field with a symmetry-breaking potential. For BHs of a size comparable with the inverse mass gap, the gapped field tries to restore the symmetry close to the horizon, enhancing the departure from standard BHs. Moreover, BHs of the same mass can have up to three different horizon areas and surface gravities. This branching has profound consequences for BH physics, potentially observable in gravitational waves.

</details>


### [124] [Anisotropic Fluid Inhomogeneous Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31461)
*Mariano Cadoni,Andrea Cozzumbo,Riccardo Murgia,Matteo Pili,Mirko Pitzalis,Andrea P. Sanna,Matteo Schulz,Ilaria Usai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出基于广义相对论（General Relativity）与各向异性流体（anisotropic fluid）的非均匀宇宙学框架 AFIC，仅需一个输入函数即可精确求解爱因斯坦方程，无需小度规扰动假设。该模型可自然由物质非均匀性产生晚期加速，并为暗能量光谱仪（DESI）观测结果提供物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学依赖小度规扰动（metric perturbations）近似，难以描述宇宙结构形成的非线性区域（non-linear regime）；同时晚期加速膨胀的物理起源仍缺乏满意解释，DESI 等观测结果亦需要理论诠释。

Method: 构建由各向异性流体作为源的广义相对论非均匀宇宙学模型，以不同红移处的径向 Misner–Sharp 质量分布（Misner–Sharp mass distribution）这一单一输入函数进行参数化，并获得爱因斯坦方程的精确解（exact solutions）。

Result: 模型在早期及晚期大尺度极限下均可回到 FLRW 宇宙学；物质非均匀性可自然驱动晚期加速，其驱动项（extensive term）不进入精确度规解，因而不影响局部引力势（gravitational potential），而是作为晚期物质非均匀分布诱导的真正大尺度效应出现。

Conclusion: AFIC 模型提供了超越微扰近似的完全相对论性框架，可对 DESI 结果给出物理解释；作者给出一个简单解析实现，并据此估计了当今有效暗能量密度（effective dark energy density）。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for inhomogeneous cosmology based on General Relativity sourced by an anisotropic fluid. The model is parametrized by a single input function, the radial Misner--Sharp mass distribution at different redshifts, and admits exact solutions to Einstein's equations. Going beyond the standard assumption of small metric perturbations, it provides a fully relativistic framework to describe the non-linear regime of cosmic structure formation. The Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmology is recovered both at early times and -- on large scales only -- at late times. Our Anisotropic Fluid Inhomogeneous Cosmology (AFIC) model provides a natural mechanism through which matter inhomogeneities give rise to late-time acceleration. The extensive term driving this effect does not enter the exact metric solution and therefore does not contribute to the local gravitational potential. Rather, it emerges as a genuinely large-scale effect induced by the inhomogeneous distribution of matter at late times. This mechanism may provide a physical interpretation of the results from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI). Finally, we present a simple analytical realization of the model and use it to provide an estimate of the present-day effective dark energy density.

</details>


### [125] [Prospects for Detecting Post-merger Signals from Binary Neutron Stars with Future Gravitational-Wave Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31857)
*Jingwang Diao,Teng Zhang,Xingjiang Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究借助统一的旋近-并合-并合后（inspiral-merger-post-merger, IPM）波形与同一族群模型，比较双中子星（binary neutron star, BNS）并合后（post-merger, PM）阶段的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background, SGWB）与单事件直接探测两条途径的观测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNS并合后阶段蕴含并合遗迹与致密物质状态方程（equation of state）信息，但其观测可行性尚不明确，需要评估不同探测途径的相对前景。

Method: 采用统一的IPM波形规定与相同的天体物理族群模型，同时计算全源宽带SGWB与千赫兹PM频段SGWB，并针对包含ET、CE及专用千赫兹探测器的第三代探测器配置（third-generation detectors）评估单事件探测率。

Result: 在代表性探测器网络下，基线IPM模型预测全源宽带BNS SGWB在本地并合率区间内均超过探测阈值，而千赫兹PM频段SGWB仍低于阈值；对R₀=7.6–250 Gpc⁻³ yr⁻¹，专用千赫兹探测器每年约探测5–174个PM候选事件，所考虑的数值相对论（numerical relativity）情形则为约0.07–7 yr⁻¹。

Conclusion: 宽带SGWB较PM频段SGWB更易达到探测阈值，而单事件PM探测率对波形模型高度敏感；数值相对论情形显著压低候选率，表明并合后探测前景依赖波形建模的可靠性。

Abstract: The post-merger (PM) phase of binary neutron star (BNS) mergers carries information about the merger remnant and the dense-matter equation of state, but its observational prospects remain uncertain. We compare two approaches to accessing this emission: the stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) from the BNS population and direct PM-band detections of individually resolved mergers. Using a unified inspiral--merger--post-merger (IPM) waveform prescription and the same astrophysical population model for both calculations, we evaluate representative third-generation detector configurations involving ET, CE, and a dedicated kilohertz detector. For the representative detector networks considered, the baseline IPM model predicts that the all-source, broadband BNS SGWB exceeds the adopted detection threshold across the local merger-rate interval. In contrast, the kilohertz PM-band SGWB remains below this threshold. For individual events, over $R_0=7.6$--$250~{\rm Gpc}^{-3}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$, the baseline IPM model predicts approximately $5$--$174$ PM-band candidates per year for the dedicated kilohertz detector, whereas the numerical-relativity cases considered here yield approximately $0.07$--$7~{\rm yr}^{-1}$.

</details>


### [126] [Classical phase space distributions for quantum states in cosmology: General states, operator ordering and the geometry of fading non-commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31912)
*Alejandro Corichi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将量子场在FLRW背景下的Weyl排序期望值问题扩展为经典相空间上的概率分布ρ_Ψ，证明其正性仅对相干态与压缩态成立，并给出任意算符排序与Weyl排序偏离的量级估计及在de Sitter与暴胀后辐射期的抑制行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已证明线性场在任意FLRW背景下真空态对应相空间分布ρ_o，其矩正好是Weyl排序的n点函数；本文旨在将这一对应推广到任意态、任意算符排序与任意时刻，并评估非对易修正的实际大小，以界定经典概率描述的适用范围。

Method: 在Bargmann表示下给出再现全部Weyl排序期望值的分布ρ_Ψ的显式公式；利用线性可观测量的对易子为c数这一性质，把任意排序与Weyl排序之差写成对易子乘积；对纯高斯态用两个Hamiltonian向量场之间的单一夹角刻画相关系数、对易子与反对易子之比及压缩；并在FLRW中对齐次各向同性准自由态推广到任意（可不同）时刻。

Result: 纯态下ρ_Ψ仅对相干态与压缩态非负，且二者均为相容Kähler度量的高斯，所需高斯平滑宽度为零或恰为完整相干态宽度；非对易修正的量级由上述夹角统一约束。de Sitter中修正按e^{-N}（场与动量）及e^{-3N}/3（两时刻的场）衰减；暴胀继承态在辐射期以占据数倒数保持抑制，仅在模函数节点附近失效。

Conclusion: 正性分布ρ_Ψ刻画的是特定一族可观测量而非全部测量：同一压缩使±k模相对辐射期真空纠缠，并令位移宇称测量违反Bell不等式，故经典概率图像不能被理解为对一切测量的完备描述。

Abstract: In earlier work with Ashtekar and Kesavan we showed that, for linear fields in any FLRW background, the vacuum defines a probability distribution $ρ_o$ on the classical phase space whose moments are exactly the Weyl-ordered $n$-point functions. We extend that analysis in four directions. First, for an arbitrary state $Ψ$ we give an explicit formula, in the Bargmann representation, for the distribution $ρ_Ψ$ that reproduces all Weyl-ordered expectation values. For pure states it is non-negative precisely for coherent and squeezed states, each the Gaussian of a compatible Kähler metric, and the Gaussian smoothing needed to make it positive is either zero or the full coherent-state width. Second, for linear observables every operator ordering differs from the Weyl ordering by explicit products of $c$-number commutators. Third, for two linear observables in a pure Gaussian state, the classical correlation coefficient, the ratio of commutator to anticommutator and the squeezing are functions of a single angle between their Hamiltonian vector fields; this bounds the departure of arbitrarily ordered $n$-point functions from the moments of $ρ$. Fourth, for fields in FLRW the equality holds at arbitrary, possibly different, times and for every homogeneous isotropic quasi-free state. In de Sitter the non-commutative corrections fall as $e^{-N}$ for field and momentum and as $e^{-3N}/3$ for the field at two times. For the state inherited from inflation they stay suppressed in the radiation era, by the inverse occupation number, except near the nodes of the mode functions. The same squeezing entangles the modes $\pm\vec k$ relative to the radiation-era vacuum and makes displaced-parity measurements violate Bell inequalities, so the positive distribution describes a specified family of observables, not all measurements.

</details>


### [127] [Machine learning enables full Bayesian inference for spinning extreme-mass-ratio inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31916)
*Bo Liang,Minghui Du,Zhenwei Lyu,Hanlin Song,Jiakai Zhang,Weichen Qin,Chia-Jui Chou,Yi Yang,Jingyi Yu,Ziren Luo,Mingming Sun,Peng Xu,Yue-Liang Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对自旋极端质量比旋近（EMRI）系统，在完整先验下首次实现全部14个参数的联合反演：用生成网络（generative networks）收缩先验并揭示自旋方向的简并环，再由精确探测器似然（detector likelihood）确定后验，在模拟一年期LISA-Taiji观测中给出迄今最精确的宽先验14参数后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数EMRI参数推断预测假设源参数已知，宽先验分析也只处理简化问题；从完整先验联合反演自旋源的14个参数此前尚未解决，而这对测量中心黑洞质量自旋、探测强场环境与宇宙距离至关重要。

Method: 在增广解析拼凑模型（augmented analytic kludge model）框架下，利用生成网络收缩完整先验、发现自旋方向的简并环，并用精确探测器似然计算后验；对模拟的一年期LISA-Taiji观测数据进行分析。

Result: 在环的一个扇区内首次获得自旋EMRI基于完整先验的14参数后验；大黑洞红移质量与自旋精度达2×10^-5（质量精度超电磁测量两个数量级），致密天体红移质量精度达10^-4，其偏心逆行轨道（retrograde orbit）可区分俘获通道；距离精度0.8%、定位优于1平方度，满足暗标准汽笛宇宙学（dark-siren cosmology）需求。

Conclusion: 研究表明完整先验下自旋EMRI的高精度参数推断是可行的，且一年期相干分析能解析由辐射反作用（radiation reaction）驱动的旋近过程。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), stellar-mass compact objects spiralling into massive black holes, promise to measure the masses and spins of the central black holes, reveal the strong-field environment around them and gauge cosmic distances via millihertz gravitational-wave observations. Yet most forecasts for the parameter inference of these systems assume known source parameters, and broad-prior analyses have addressed only simplified versions of the problem. The joint inversion of all 14 parameters of a spinning source from the complete prior has remained unsolved. Here we solve it for the augmented analytic kludge model: generative networks narrow the complete prior and reveal a ring of degenerate spin directions, and the exact detector likelihood determines the posterior. For a simulated one-year LISA-Taiji observation, within one ring sector we obtain, for the first time, a 14-parameter posterior of a spinning EMRI from the complete prior, the most precise yet obtained for such a source from broad priors. The redshifted mass and spin of the massive black hole are determined to two parts in $10^5$, the mass two orders of magnitude beyond any electromagnetic measurement. The compact object's redshifted mass is measured to one part in $10^4$, and its eccentric, retrograde orbit discriminates between capture channels. The 0.8% distance and sub-square-degree localization are sufficient for dark-siren cosmology. Year-long coherent analysis resolves the inspiral driven by radiation reaction itself.

</details>


### [128] [Completing the real-space construction of the canonical harmonic coordinates of Kerr metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31994)
*Zizheng Xing,Xiaokai He,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在多极后闵可夫斯基（multipolar post-Minkowskian, MPM）形式体系内给出了精确的规范调和克尔度规（canonical harmonic Kerr metric），并证明其与已知4PM结果一致；借助形式级数环证明PM阶n≥2时度规不含拉普拉斯算子的齐次分量，从而实现所有规范矩为零的归纳构造，并与Sadu与Vanhove的独立结果交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建自旋致密天体的高精度后闵可夫斯基（post-Minkowskian, PM）波形模板需要精确的克尔度规多极展开，而规范选择与齐次（homogeneous）自由度使得该构造存在不确定性，需给出明确的规范固定方案。

Method: 采用多极后闵可夫斯基（MPM）形式体系，借助形式级数环（formal series ring）证明PM阶n≥2时度规中不出现拉普拉斯算子（Laplacian operator）的齐次分量；结合线性场与规范克尔解相等这一性质，对规范矩全为零的规范MPM度规作归纳构造；并给出将其联系到Boyer-Lindquist度规的坐标变换。

Result: 所得度规与先前构造的4PM结果相符；规范调和克尔度规到Boyer-Lindquist度规的坐标变换由复调和径向函数（complex harmonic radial function）的实部生成；与Sadu和Vanhove基于动量空间递推（momentum-space recursion）及剩余齐次自由度分类的独立构造结果一致。

Conclusion: MPM框架下存在唯一的闭式规范调和克尔度规，其在PM阶n≥2无齐次污染、规范矩可全部消去；两条独立途径得到相同结果，构成对该闭式表达的交叉验证（cross validation）。

Abstract: In this paper, we determine the exact canonical harmonic Kerr metric to all post-Minkowskian (PM) orders within the multipolar post-Minkowskian (MPM) formalism. The metric agrees with the previously constructed 4PM results. Using a formal series ring, we prove that no homogeneous components of Laplacian operator appear in this metric at PM order $n\ge2$. Together with the equality of the linear field with the canonical Kerr solution, this property allows an inductive construction of the canonical MPM metric where all gauge moments vanish. The coordinate transformation linking the canonical harmonic Kerr metric to the Boyer-Lindquist metric is generated by the real part of a complex harmonic radial function. A recent independent construction by Sadu and Vanhove using momentum-space recursion and a residual homogeneous freedom classification yields the same result, providing a cross validation of the closed-form canonical harmonic Kerr metric.

</details>


### [129] [Exact Mass Multipoles of Distorted Schwarzschild Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32283)
*Brennen Black*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在孤立视界（isolated horizon）框架下，不作线性化处理，精确计算了Weyl畸变Schwarzschild视界的几何多极矩与质量多极矩；四极畸变情形对所有ℓ给出合流超几何函数闭式解，偶极-四极-八极畸变情形给出β级数闭式解，并据此量化了低阶近似的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多依赖对畸变参数的低阶近似（线性化）来估计视界多极矩，其在多大程度上失真缺乏定量刻画；因此需要一个非微扰的精确基准来评估近似误差。

Method: 从精确积分表示出发，在孤立视界框架下对两种畸变（纯四极；偶极-四极-八极）求闭式解：纯四极情形以合流超几何函数（confluent hypergeometric functions）表达所有ℓ的结果，混合畸变情形则对奇宇称系数β作级数展开而系数在四极系数α₂上精确。

Result: 对α₂作领头阶近似会使每个无量纲质量多极矩产生约为α₂的相对误差，且几乎与ℓ无关；纯线性化下误差主要来自被线性化的四极矩而非被舍弃的高阶多极矩。一个简单的次数计数规则决定各阶畸变对每个多极矩的贡献；奇宇称分量须小于α₂²量级才可忽略。β级数截断至首个含β阶时，在|β|,|α₂|≤1/8下可再现ℓ≤6各多极矩至约10⁻³精度。

Conclusion: 结果表明低阶近似的误差具有近似普适的相对量级α₂，且主要源于四极矩的线性化；所得闭式解与次数计数规则为视界多极矩的微扰展开提供了误差控制依据。

Abstract: Using the isolated-horizon framework, we compute the geometric and mass multipole moments of the Weyl-distorted Schwarzschild horizon without linearizing the distortion. Starting from the exact integral representation we evaluate it in closed form for two cases, a pure quadrupole distortion and a dipole-quadrupole-octupole distortion. For a pure quadrupole we obtain a closed form for all $\ell$ in terms of confluent hypergeometric functions, while for a dipole-quadrupole-octupole distortion we derive a closed-form series solution in the odd-parity coefficient $β$ whose coefficients are exact in the quadrupole coefficient $α_{2}$. We use these results to understand the errors incurred by leading-order approximations. Leading-order approximations in $α_{2}$ misestimate every dimensionless mass multipole by a relative error of roughly $α_{2}$, nearly independent of $\ell$, and for pure linearization most of the error lies in the linearized quadrupole moment rather than the discarded higher multipoles. A simple degree-counting rule determines which orders of distortion contribute to each multipole. As a consequence, the odd-parity component must be small compared with $α_{2}^2$ before it can be neglected. Finally, truncating the series in $β$ at its first $β$-dependent order reproduces the $\ell \leq 6$ multipoles to about $10^{-3}$ for $|β|,|α_{2}| \leq 1/8$.

</details>


### [130] [Horizon dynamical entropies and phase space topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32444)
*Matteo Bruno,Simone Speziale*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究由无穷维范数（infinite-dimensional norms）诱导的相空间拓扑（phase space topologies），用于解决零超曲面（null hypersurface）微分同胚对应的哈密顿生成元（Hamiltonian generators）的可积性问题，并比较黑洞动力学熵（dynamical entropy）的不同方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是解决与零超曲面微分同胚相关的哈密顿生成元的"可积性（integrability）"问题，以便在非定常（non-stationary）情形下比较替代面积的黑洞动力学熵的不同处方（prescriptions）。

Method: 采用无穷维范数诱导相空间拓扑的方法：首先对标量场（scalar field）构造一族范数，使其对不同极化（polarizations）分别提供可积性；随后考察该范数族在引力场（gravitational field）中的适用性。

Result: 对标量场而言，存在一族范数可对不同极化提供可积性；而引力场仅适用该族中的单一成员，即文献中已考虑过的 Sobolev 范数（Sobolev norm）。其他候选方案受阻的原因在于度规（metric）的半正定性（semi-positivity）。

Conclusion: 引力场情形下可积性唯一地指向 Sobolev 范数，而其余候选范数因度规半正定性而失效，这为黑洞动力学熵处方之间的比较提供了判据基础。

Abstract: We study phase space topologies induced by infinite-dimensional norms as a means to solve the problem of 'integrability' of Hamiltonian generators corresponding to diffeomorphisms of a null hypersurface, with the specific goal in mind to compare different prescriptions for the dynamical entropy of black holes, which replaces the area in non-stationary regimes. We first show that for a scalar field, there exists a family of norms that would provide integrability for different polarizations. We then show that a single member of this family applies to the gravitational field, and it is given by the Sobolev norm already considered in the literature. The obstruction to the other candidates lies in the semi-positivity of the metric.

</details>


### [131] [Analytic response functions of space-based gravitational-wave detectors to ultralight scalar-field dark matter under multiple types of TDI combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32563)
*Wenqiu Tang,Pan-Pan Wang,Yuezhe Chen,Rui Luo,Cheng-Gang Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了空间引力波探测器对超轻标量场暗物质在多种时间延迟干涉（TDI）组合下的响应函数解析表达式，可用于评估LISA、太极、天琴等任务对暗物质质量与耦合参数的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻标量场（ultralight scalar fields）是重要的暗物质候选者，其作为相干振荡场通过与被测质量的耦合可在空间引力波探测器（space-based gravitational-wave detectors）中留下特征信号，因此需要准确的响应函数描述。

Method: 从标量场与被测质量的耦合作用量（coupling action）出发，得到被测质量的附加位移与单链路多普勒响应（single-link Doppler response）；进而给出任意时间延迟干涉（time-delay interferometry, TDI）组合下响应函数的一般解析形式，并对最优组合A、E、T比较解析结果与蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）数值结果。

Result: 分析多类TDI组合的响应，依据低频行为将其划分为普通型与ζ型两类，并给出各组合的低频响应表达式；解析结果与数值结果在A、E、T组合上得到对比验证。

Conclusion: 所得响应函数解析表达式可用于评估LISA、太极（Taiji）、天琴（TianQin）等空间引力波任务对超轻标量场暗物质质量与耦合参数的探测灵敏度。

Abstract: Ultralight scalar fields are important candidates for dark matter. As coherently oscillating fields, they can leave characteristic signatures in space-based gravitational-wave detectors through their coupling to test masses. In this paper, we derive analytic expressions for the response functions of space-based gravitational-wave detectors to ultralight scalar-field dark matter under multiple types of time-delay interferometry (TDI) combinations. Starting from the coupling action between the scalar field and a test mass, we obtain the additional displacement of the test mass and the single-link Doppler response. We then give the general analytic form of the response function for an arbitrary TDI combination and compare the analytic and Monte Carlo numerical results for the optimal combinations A, E, and T. Finally, we analyze the responses of multiple types of TDI combinations, classify them into ordinary and ζ types according to their low-frequency behavior, and give the low-frequency response expressions of the individual combinations. The results can be used to evaluate the sensitivity of space-based gravitational-wave missions such as LISA, Taiji, and TianQin to the mass and coupling parameters of ultralight scalar-field dark matter.

</details>


### [132] [Toward Solving the Galactic Foreground Problem in Gravitational-Wave Astronomy with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32800)
*Bo Liang,Jinwen Deng,Minghui Du,Hanlin Song,Xilong Fan,Peng Xu,Ziren Luo,Lijing Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将“可分辨双星数目”定义为信噪比超过阈值的源数目，借助神经流（neural flows）与嵌套采样（nested sampling）在0.5–10 mHz范围内实现计数与分辨，并提出由数据本身设定的“贝叶斯混淆极限”（Bayesian confusion limit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波探测器将观测到数千万银河系双星，已分辨者构成不受尘埃遮蔽的超致密双星普查，未分辨者则构成毫赫兹以下频段的主要噪声；区分二者需要双星数目，而该数目因双星存在于任意振幅而定义不清。

Method: 通过设定信噪比阈值将源定义为可观测量，采用神经流（neural flows）在0.5–10 mHz的1微赫兹区间内计数，并用嵌套采样（nested sampling）进行分辨，可分辨间隔小于一个频率bin的双星对。

Result: 神经流在99%的1微赫兹区间内计数误差在一个以内；数据本身给出限制：源可分辨仅当其信号足以“支付”自身描述所需约20 nats，该偏差即使对理想跨维采样器（trans-dimensional sampler）亦存在。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯混淆极限（Bayesian confusion limit）类同成像中的衍射极限（diffraction limit），由数据而非方法决定，适用于射电巡天、脉冲星计时阵列与超分辨显微等任何弱源叠加问题；对未来空间探测器，该方法将银河系前景转化为具有解析选择函数的确定、可标定双星计数，可作为全局拟合与族群模型检验的基础。

Abstract: Space-borne gravitational-wave detectors will see tens of millions of Galactic binaries. Resolved, they would form a census of the Galaxy's ultracompact binaries unhindered by dust; unresolved, they are the dominant noise up to a few millihertz for massive black-hole binaries, extreme-mass-ratio inspirals and backgrounds from the early Universe. Separating the two requires the number of binaries, which is ill-defined because binaries exist at every amplitude. We make it an observable by defining a source as a binary above a signal-to-noise threshold. Neural flows count the binaries to within one in 99% of 1 microhertz intervals over 0.5-10 mHz, and nested sampling resolves them, even pairs closer than one frequency bin. The data themselves set a limit: a source is resolvable only if its signal pays for its own description, about 20 nats, which biases even ideal trans-dimensional samplers. Like the diffraction limit in imaging, this Bayesian confusion limit is set by the data rather than by the method, and it governs any superposition of weak sources, from radio surveys and pulsar timing arrays to super-resolution microscopy. For future space-borne detectors, the method turns the Galactic foreground into a definite, calibrated count of binaries with an analytic selection function, the basis for global fits and for tests of population models.

</details>


### [133] [An uncalibrated effective-one-body waveform model for intermediate-mass-ratio black hole binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32840)
*Angelica Albertini,Vojtěch Witzany*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种面向质量比 $q>10$ 的无自旋准圆轨道中等质量比双星（intermediate-mass-ratio binary）的旋近-并合-铃宕（inspiral-merger-ringdown）有效单体（effective-one-body, EOB）模型，动力学完全由解析后牛顿（post-Newtonian）结果构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等质量比双星（$q\sim10^2-10^4$）是波形建模中最具挑战性的源之一，现有建模手段在该区间精度与效率难以兼顾。

Method: 采用有效单体（effective-one-body）框架，构造旋近-并合-铃宕波形模型，其动力学仅基于解析后牛顿（post-Newtonian）结果；并实现优化的事后绝热（post-adiabatic）方案以加速波形生成。

Result: 对 $q>10$ 的数值相对论（numerical-relativity）模拟，$(2,2)$ 模的非忠实度（unfaithfulness）达 $10^{-4}-10^{-5}$；在 $q=10^4-10^5$ 的四年旋近中与二阶自力（second-order self-force）波形相位偏差小于一弧度；波形生成速度较直接积分完整运动方程快达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该模型以纯解析动力学实现了对中等质量比双星的高精度、高效率波形建模，可覆盖数值相对论与自力计算之间的质量比区间。

Abstract: Intermediate-mass-ratio binaries, with mass ratios $q=m_1/m_2\sim10^2-10^4$, are among the most challenging sources for waveform modeling. We present an inspiral-merger-ringdown effective-one-body model for nonspinning, quasicircular binaries with $q>10$, with dynamics built only from analytical post-Newtonian results. It reaches $(2,2)$ unfaithfulness of $10^{-4}-10^{-5}$ against numerical-relativity simulations with $q>10$ and subradian dephasing against second-order self-force waveforms over four-year inspirals at $q=10^4-10^5$. An optimized post-adiabatic implementation enables waveform generation up to two orders of magnitude faster than integrating the full equations of motion.

</details>


### [134] [Strong hyperbolicity of puncture-gauge formulations of gravitational theories with second order equations of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32853)
*Shunhui Yao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明修正CCZ4（mCCZ4）形式在爱因斯坦-高斯-博内（Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet）引力与四维四阶标量-张量理论中，于弱耦合及满足锥分离条件时具有强双曲性（strong hyperbolicity），并将标准CCZ4在广义相对论穿刺规范下的强双曲性推广到两个退化点之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于mCCZ4的强双曲性分析依赖围绕爱因斯坦极限的一阶微扰理论（first-order perturbation theory），仅覆盖主象征的近似结构，缺乏对完整主象征（full principal symbol）的严格论证。

Method: 将mCCZ4方程改写为形式上等价的二阶系统（second-order system），使其导数结构与Kovács–Reall修正调和形式（modified harmonic formulation）相仿，从而套用其协变特征分析（covariant characteristic analysis）与连续性论证，且无需假设特征值或特征向量对耦合解析依赖。

Result: 证明在耦合足够弱、规范参数满足锥分离条件（cone-separation condition）时，mCCZ4对两种理论均为强双曲；并表明广义相对论中的标准CCZ4在穿刺规范（puncture gauge）下，除两个缺陷退化（defective degeneracies）外亦强双曲。

Conclusion: mCCZ4的强双曲性可在完整主象征层面严格建立，为非线性穿刺规范数值模拟（nonlinear puncture-gauge simulations）的适定性提供理论依据，并厘清标准CCZ4的退化边界。

Abstract: We prove that the modified CCZ4 (mCCZ4) formulation, used in nonlinear puncture-gauge simulations of (d+1)-dimensional Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity and four-derivative scalar-tensor theory in four dimensions, is strongly hyperbolic for both theories at sufficiently weak coupling, provided that the gauge parameters are chosen to satisfy a cone-separation condition. The proof applies to the full principal symbol of the theories, extending previous analyses based on first-order perturbation theory about the Einstein limit. We establish strong hyperbolicity by recasting the mCCZ4 equations into a formally equivalent second-order system whose derivative structure resembles that of the modified harmonic formulation of Kovács and Reall. This allows us to adapt their covariant characteristic analysis and continuity argument, without assuming that individual eigenvalues or eigenvectors depend analytically on the coupling. We also show that standard CCZ4 in general relativity is strongly hyperbolic in puncture gauge away from two defective degeneracies.

</details>


### [135] [Geometrothermodynamics characterization of ABG-AdS black hole immersed by string clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32907)
*Sayed Farooq Ahmed Shah,Safia Taj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在准齐次几何热力学（quasi-homogeneous geometrothermodynamics）框架下研究含弦云（cloud of strings）的规则 ABG-AdS 黑洞，建立响应函数反常（response-function anomalies）与热力学曲率奇点（curvature singularities）之间的一一对应，并借助 Gibbs 自由能（Gibbs free energy）的燕尾结构（swallow-tail）刻画相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则黑洞（regular black holes）以平滑几何替代奇点核心，为研究热力学稳定性如何编码于时空几何提供了自然场景；但传统稳定性判据依赖热力学势（thermodynamic potential）的选择，缺乏势无关的刻画方式，故需发展 Legendre 不变（Legendre-invariant）的分析框架。

Method: 构建含弦云的规则 Ayón-Beato–García AdS（ABG-AdS）黑洞的准齐次几何热力学；证明熵（entropy）、电单极荷（electric monopole charge）与 AdS 半径满足广义准齐次性条件，作为广延变量，并由此在平衡流形（equilibrium manifold）上导出三族热力学度规（thermodynamic metrics），进而比较其曲率发散位置与响应函数的行为。

Result: 第一族度规的曲率在零温极端边界处发散，该处热容（heat capacity）为零，对应 Davies 一阶相变；第二族度规的曲率恰在热容发散处发散，对应 Davies 二阶相变。Gibbs 自由能呈燕尾结构，显示小/大黑洞（small/large black hole）一阶相变，其亚稳区（metastable region）由热容发散界定，并存在向热 AdS 的 Hawking–Page 型相变。

Conclusion: 弦云密度（string-cloud density）与电荷共同调控视界结构（horizon structure）、允许的热力学区域及稳定区间；准齐次几何热力学可在不偏好任何势的前提下，将热力学稳定性几何化，并精确对应各类相变。

Abstract: Regular black holes, whose singular cores are replaced by smooth geometries sourced by nonlinear electrodynamics, provide a natural setting to study how thermodynamic stability is encoded in spacetime geometry. Conventional stability criteria, however, depend on the choice of thermodynamic potential. Here we develop the quasi-homogeneous geometrothermodynamics of the regular Ayón-Beato--García anti-de Sitter (ABG-AdS) black hole surrounded by a cloud of strings, a Legendre-invariant framework in which no potential needs to be preferred. We show that the entropy, electric monopole charge and AdS radius act as extensive variables obeying a generalized quasi-homogeneity condition, and that the equilibrium manifold then carries three families of thermodynamic metrics. We establish an exact correspondence between response-function anomalies and curvature singularities: the first metric's curvature diverges on the zero-temperature extremal boundary, where the heat capacity vanishes, signaling Davies first-order transitions, whereas the second's diverges precisely where the heat capacity does, pinpointing Davies second-order transitions. The Gibbs free energy displays a swallow-tail structure that reveals a first-order small/large black hole transition, whose metastable region is bounded by these divergences of the heat capacity, together with a Hawking--Page-type transition to thermal AdS. The string-cloud density and the electric charge jointly control the horizon structure, the admissible thermal domain and the stability intervals.

</details>


### [136] [The Cosmological Constant as a Clock and the Vilenkin Wavefunction as a Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32987)
*Stephon Alexander,Marcell Howard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在协变形式的幺模引力（unimodular gravity）框架下处理量子引力（quantum gravity）中的时间问题，通过将宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）与全局四维体积（global four-volume）变量正则共轭，构建出时间依赖的惠勒–德维特方程（Wheeler–DeWitt equation）并求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力中"时间"的缺失（problem of time）是核心难题；作者希望借助幺模引力的协变表述，将宇宙学常数转化为可充当时钟（clock）的动力学变量，从而恢复时间演化。

Method: 采用协变幺模引力（covariant unimodular gravity）表述，使宇宙学常数与全局四维体积正则共轭，得到含时惠勒–德维特方程；构造与完整 Chern–Simons–Kodama 态（Chern–Simons–Kodama state）相关的精确、背景无关（background-independent）解，并在迷你超空间（minisuperspace）中考察演化。

Result: 在迷你超空间中证明幺正演化（unitary evolution）；Chern–Simons 泛函（Chern–Simons functional）提供内禀引力时钟；解表明 Vilenkin 波函数（Vilenkin wavefunction）是引力 Gamow 共振（gravitational Gamow resonance），其不可归一化性（non-normalizability）源于出射边界条件（outgoing boundary condition）；隧穿到膨胀分支的概率随正宇宙学常数增大。

Conclusion: 结果为宇宙波函数（wavefunction of the universe）提供了动力学解释，并澄清了边界条件（boundary conditions）在其构造中的作用。

Abstract: We address the problem of time in quantum gravity using a covariant formulation of unimodular gravity. As a result, the cosmological constant is canonically conjugate to a global four-volume variable, which serves as a clock and leads to a time-dependent Wheeler--DeWitt equation. We construct exact, background-independent solutions related to the full Chern--Simons--Kodama state and demonstrate unitary evolution in minisuperspace. The Chern--Simons functional also provides an intrinsic gravitational clock. These solutions reveal that the Vilenkin wavefunction is a gravitational Gamow resonance, in which its non-normalizability follows from the outgoing boundary condition. The tunneling probability into the expanding branch increases with the positive cosmological constant. These results give a dynamical interpretation of the wavefunction of the universe and clarify the role of its boundary conditions.

</details>


### [137] [Scalar-tensor interactions selected through their first-order thermodynamic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33033)
*David S. Pereira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶热力学（first-order thermodynamics）对标量-张量引力（scalar-tensor gravity）的限制能力，通过热传导与剪切黏性方程约束非线性Hessian相互作用，并揭示热力学可积性与动力学退化之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验一阶热力学能否作为筛选引力相互作用（gravitational interactions）的独立且互补判据，从热力学自洽性角度约束标量-张量引力中出现的非线性曲率与Hessian耦合项。

Method: 从包含非线性曲率依赖、Einstein张量-Hessian耦合及独立二、三次标量-Hessian相互作用的广义作用量出发，导出完整的等效流体（effective-fluid）量，并同时施加Eckart热传导与剪切黏性方程以约化算符基并确定几何剪切黏度（geometric shear viscosity）。

Result: 保留完整热传导定律可建立不预设温度与热导率的可积性条件（integrability condition），构造显式本构对并导出热演化；该条件化简标量-度规动力学结构，使协变退化（covariant degeneracy）排除剩余非线性相互作用，恢复已知的光速Horndeski子类；若退化仅限标量幺正规范（scalar-unitary gauge），则非线性热力学兼容相互作用仍可存活。

Conclusion: 结果建立了热力学可积性与动力学退化之间的对应关系，表明一阶热力学可作为筛选引力相互作用的独立且互补准则。

Abstract: We investigate the restrictive capability of first-order thermodynamics on scalar-tensor gravity. Starting from a general action containing nonlinear curvature dependence, an Einstein-tensor-Hessian coupling, and independent quadratic and cubic scalar-Hessian interactions, we derive the complete effective-fluid quantities. Simultaneously imposing Eckart's heat-conduction and shear-viscosity equations, while accounting for cancellations between interactions, substantially reduces the original operator basis and determines the geometric shear viscosity. Retaining the complete heat-conduction law, we establish an integrability condition that constrains the nonlinear Hessian interactions without prescribing the temperature and thermal conductivity. We construct explicit constitutive pairs and derive the associated thermal evolution, revealing contributions beyond the usual acceleration-aligned description. Remarkably, the thermodynamic integrability condition simplifies the scalar-metric kinetic structure such that imposing covariant degeneracy excludes the remaining nonlinear interactions, recovering the established luminal Horndeski subclass. Nonlinear thermodynamically compatible interactions nevertheless survive when degeneracy is restricted to the scalar-unitary gauge. These results establish a connection between thermodynamic integrability and dynamical degeneracy, demonstrating the potential of first-order thermodynamics as an independent and complementary criterion for selecting gravitational interactions.

</details>


### [138] [General-relativistic Vlasov disks in external magnetic fields around Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33053)
*Paweł Doruchowski,Tomasz Grewenda,David Kofroň,Patryk Mach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究处于外部磁场中的Kerr黑洞周围无碰撞气体的定态广义相对论动理学模型，重点给出薄盘电流的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画黑洞磁场环境中无碰撞气盘的定态动理学结构，尤其是赤道面上"剃刀薄盘"（razor-thin disk）中粒子分布与电流之间的关系。

Method: 采用广义相对论动理学（general-relativistic kinetic theory）框架，选取对应常正则能量（canonical energy）与常正则角动量（canonical angular momentum）的分布函数（distribution function），推导气体电流的解析表达式；并在Schwarzschild背景度规下推广至依赖能量与角动量的更一般分布函数，示例中考虑Wald磁层（Wald magnetosphere）。

Result: 获得了与气体相关的电流（electric current）的解析表达式；给出Wald磁层中薄盘的显式算例，该磁层描述渐近均匀磁场（asymptotically uniform magnetic field）。几何厚盘（geometrically thick disk）仅作简要讨论。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁场中Kerr黑洞周围无碰撞气盘提供了可解析处理的定态动理学描述，可用于分析薄盘电流分布及更一般分布函数情形。

Abstract: We investigate stationary general-relativistic kinetic models of a collisionless gas around the Kerr black hole immersed in an external magnetic field. The main focus is put on razor-thin disks confined to the equatorial plane, but geometrically thick disks are also briefly mentioned. We consider distribution functions corresponding to constant canonical energy and constant canonical angular momentum of particles within the disk and obtain analytic expressions for the electric current associated with the gas. More general solutions characterized by distribution functions depending on the energy and the angular momentum are obtained assuming the Schwarzschild background metric. In explicit examples, we investigate disks in the Wald magnetosphere, describing an asymptotically uniform magnetic field.

</details>

### [139] [Locality from Nonlocality: Symmetry Relocalization and Gauging of Lattice Dirac Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31366)
*Lei Su*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文论证空间非定域对称性（spatially nonlocal symmetries）并不必然导致非定域的规范动力学（gauge dynamics）。作者通过非定域正则变换（nonlocal canonical transformations）将自由格点狄拉克费米子（free lattice Dirac fermions）的若干精确非定域紧致对称性（compact symmetries）变为在位（onsite）形式，同时保留足够的哈密顿量定域性以实现规范化（gauging），从而在一维、二维、三维获得精确或涌现的规范定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为非定域对称性意味着非定域规范动力学，本文旨在反驳这一蕴含关系，并研究在何种格点结构、拓扑与反常（anomalies）条件下可将非定域对称性"再定域化"（relocalization）以实现定域规范化。

Method: 构造非定域正则变换使对称性在位化并保持哈密顿量定域性；一维结合玻色化（bosonization）识别动量绕转的T对偶（T-duality）并用非微扰范数预解界（norm-resolvent bound）证明有限化学势下的涌现动力学规范定域性；二维借助方格子π通量马约拉纳模型（π-flux Majorana model）与Kitaev蜂窝模型的对应及AIII/AII母体模型；三维导出精确的混合矢量-轴矢流通量响应并构造保持指数哈密顿量定域性的非定域映射。

Result: 一维：保持哈密顿量的变换交换在位矢量荷与非定域谱轴矢荷（spectral axial charges），揭示二者混合反常（mixed anomaly），T对偶对应宇称投影算符流向非可逆拓扑缺陷（noninvertible topological defect），并证明有限化学势下涌现规范定域性。二维：π通量马约拉纳模型与Kitaev模型共享单个狄拉克费米子与奇Pfaffian宇称，但支持尖锐及指数定域的紧致荷（后者具非平凡Chern拓扑），双味给出紧致U(2)、四元数不变量与渐近定域非阿贝尔规范化。三维：得到精确混合矢量-轴矢流通量响应，单模型支持具强四元数拓扑的指数定域矢量U(1)荷；对紧致SU(2)外尔双重态（Weyl doublet）给出禁止保持平移的高斯在位再定域化的no-go定理。

Conclusion: 非定域对称性可在保持足够哈密顿量定域性的前提下通过非定域正则变换再定域化并实现（渐近）定域规范化，但其可行性依赖维度、格点结构、拓扑与反常，且存在如SU(2)外尔双重态情形下的no-go限制。

Abstract: Spatially nonlocal symmetries need not imply nonlocal gauge dynamics. Nonlocal canonical transformations make certain exact nonlocal compact symmetries of free lattice Dirac fermions onsite while retaining sufficient Hamiltonian locality for gauging. Symmetry relocalization yields exact or emergent gauge locality in 1D, 2D, and 3D, subject to lattice structure, topology, and anomalies.
  In 1D, a Hamiltonian-preserving transformation exchanges onsite vector and nonlocal spectral axial charges of a half-filled chain, enabling local gauging of each and exposing their mixed anomaly. Bosonization identifies momentum-winding T-duality, with a parity-projected operator flowing to a noninvertible topological defect. A nonperturbative norm-resolvent bound proves emergent dynamical gauge locality at finite chemical potential, while a doubled Chern-insulator parent reveals the duality's bulk origin and superconducting extensions reveal hidden compact symmetries.
  In 2D, the square $π$-flux Majorana model shares the Kitaev honeycomb model's single Dirac fermion and odd Pfaffian parity but supports sharp and exponentially local compact charges, the latter with nontrivial Chern topology. Relocalization retains Schwartz locality for asymptotically local gauging, while AIII/AII parents clarify bulk charge origin and parity-anomaly inflow. Two flavors yield compact $U(2)$, a quaternionic invariant, and asymptotically local non-Abelian gauging.
  In 3D, an exact mixed vector-axial flux response is derived, while a distinct model supports an exponentially local vector $U(1)$ charge with strong quaternionic topology. A nonlocal map makes the charge and Kramers symmetry onsite while preserving exponential Hamiltonian locality. For a compact $SU(2)$ Weyl doublet, a no-go theorem forbids translation-preserving Gaussian onsite relocalization retaining Weyl content and Hamiltonian locality.

</details>


### [140] [Non-integer high harmonic generation from many-body nonadiabatic gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31383)
*Mingyao Guo,Luting Wang,Murali Suganya,Biqiong Yu,Martin Greven,Sritharan Krishnamoorthi,Raman Sankar,Yao Wang,David Hsieh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在铜酸盐Mott绝缘体Sr$_2$CuO$_3$中，中红外激发产生了巨大的蓝移、非整数高次谐波（HHG），其机制为多体时间门控效应抑制脉冲后沿的光发射，从而实现频域蓝移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体高次谐波（HHG）通常表现为锁定于驱动光子能量整数倍的刚性频率梳；但在强关联材料中，光场可在谐波产生的时间尺度上动态改变电子态，可能打破这一规律，本文即探究该可能性。

Method: 采用中红外激发铜酸盐Mott绝缘体Sr$_2$CuO$_3$，结合瞬态反射率（transient reflectivity）测量与受驱Hubbard模型（driven Hubbard model）的时间依赖精确对角化（exact diagonalization）模拟。

Result: 观测到巨大的蓝移、非整数高次谐波发射；实验与模拟共同支持多体时间门控（many-body temporal gating）机制，即脉冲后沿的光发射被抑制，导致辐射谐波在频域上蓝移。

Conclusion: 研究确立HHG发射能量可作为量子多体动力学（quantum many-body dynamics）的敏感探针，并为利用强场光谱学（strong-field spectroscopy）探索与操控强关联电子相铺平道路。

Abstract: High harmonic generation (HHG) in solids typically produces a rigid frequency comb locked to exact integer multiples of the drive photon energy. In strongly correlated materials, however, the light field can dynamically transform the electronic state on the timescale of harmonic generation itself, potentially violating this rule. Here, we report giant blueshifted, non-integer HHG in a cuprate Mott insulator Sr$_2$CuO$_3$ produced by mid-infrared excitations. Concurrent transient reflectivity measurements and time-dependent exact-diagonalization simulations of a driven Hubbard model support a many-body temporal gating mechanism that suppresses light emission on the trailing edge of the pulse, thereby blueshifting radiated harmonics in the frequency domain. Our findings establish HHG emission energy as a sensitive probe of quantum many-body dynamics and pave the way for exploration and manipulation of correlated electronic phases of matter with strong-field spectroscopy.

</details>


### [141] [Origin of quadrupolar order in the 5d$^1$ double perovskite Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31395)
*Dario Fiore Mosca,Leonid V. Pourovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过第一性原理（first principles）定量确定Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$中驱动四极矩有序（quadrupolar order）的各微观相互作用，并求解相应微观哈密顿量，从而统一描述其耦合的电子与晶格自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量实验与理论研究，Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$中四极矩有序的微观起源及各项相互作用的相对能量尺度仍未解决；尤其$z^2$四极矩的铁性有序（ferroic ordering）难以解释，超交换（superexchange）、经典四极矩-四极矩相互作用、振动耦合（vibronic couplings）及向高能级的虚激发（virtual excitations）的相对贡献缺乏定量界定。

Method: 研究从第一性原理出发确定上述全部相互作用，并求解由此得到的微观哈密顿量（microscopic Hamiltonian），进而分离各项个体贡献以厘清其来源。

Result: 计算所得基态定量重现了实验观测的四极矩有序参量（order parameters）及相伴随的结构畸变（structural distortions）。分离各项贡献后发现，铁性$z^2$有序源于线性-四次耦合（linear-quartic coupling），该耦合由向$J_{\mathrm{eff}}$=1/2双重态（doublet）的虚激发产生，并受振动相互作用驱动。

Conclusion: 研究确立了Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$中四极矩有序背后的微观相互作用层级（hierarchy），并为其耦合的电子与晶格自由度提供了统一的$ab\ initio$描述。

Abstract: Despite extensive experimental and theoretical studies of quadrupolar order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$, the microscopic origin and relative energy scales of the interactions driving it remain unresolved. In particular, the observed ferroic ordering of the $z^2$ quadrupole has remained difficult to explain, as the relative contributions of superexchange, classical quadrupole-quadrupole interactions, vibronic couplings, and virtual excitations to higher-energy levels have not been established quantitatively. Here, we determine all these interactions from first principles and solve the resulting microscopic Hamiltonian. The calculated ground state reproduces the experimentally observed quadrupolar order parameters and the associated structural distortions with quantitative accuracy. Disentangling the individual contributions, we find that ferroic $z^2$ order originates from a linear-quartic coupling, generated by virtual excitations to the $J_{\mathrm{eff}}$= 1/2 doublet and driven by vibronic interactions. Our results establish the hierarchy of microscopic interactions underlying quadrupolar order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ and provide a unified $ab \ initio$ description of its coupled electronic and lattice degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [142] [Ground state and thermal phase diagrams of the magnetized spin-$1/2$ XXZ antiferromagnet on the triangular lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31412)
*Owan Cho,Seungho Lee,Hyun-Yong Lee,SungBin Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用无穷密度矩阵重整化群（infinite DMRG）、无穷投影纠缠对算符（iPEPS）与指数张量重整化群（exponential tensor renormalization group）等方法，系统解决了三角晶格自旋-1/2 XXZ反铁磁体在磁场中的零温相图与热学相结构问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格磁场下自旋-1/2 XXZ反铁磁体是典型的阻挫量子磁体（frustrated quantum magnet），但其在一般各向异性下的基态相图及有限温度相结构尚未被充分理解。

Method: 采用无穷密度矩阵重整化群（infinite density-matrix renormalization group）、无穷投影纠缠对算符（infinite projected entangled-pair operator）与指数张量重整化群（exponential tensor renormalization group），并以精确对角化（exact diagonalization）作为辅助验证。

Result: 零温下获得从Ising极限到Heisenberg点的连续相图，发现非经典Ψ超固相（supersolid）在所有研究各向异性下均为饱和点下方的稳定相；在强易轴（easy-axis）区发现有限温度下的键向列相（bond-nematic phase），其间三个键方向自发不等价而无横向自旋相干或三子格序；硬核玻色子（hard-core-boson）映射揭示其源于热涨落破坏相位相干后空穴在局域上-上-下序的畴壁（domain wall）上聚集。

Conclusion: 研究结果呼吁重新审视三角晶格钴酸盐（如Na₂BaCo(PO₄)₂与K₂Co(SeO₃)₂）的高场态，并对高场量子磁体的相结构认识提出修正。

Abstract: The spin-1/2 XXZ antiferromagnet on the triangular lattice in a magnetic field is a paradigmatic frustrated quantum magnet, yet its ground-state phase diagram at generic anisotropy and its thermal phase structure remain incompletely understood. Here, using infinite density-matrix renormalization group, infinite projected entangled-pair operator, and exponential tensor renormalization group methods, supported by exact diagonalization, we resolve both issues. At zero temperature, we map the phase diagram continuously from the Ising limit to the Heisenberg point and find that the nonclassical $Ψ$ supersolid is a stable phase just below saturation at all anisotropies studied: it borders the one-third-magnetization up-up-down plateau directly in the strongly easy-axis regime and the semiclassical $V$ phase toward the Heisenberg point. In the strongly easy-axis regime, we uncover an intrinsically finite-temperature bond-nematic phase above the zero-temperature plateau--$Ψ$ boundary, in which the three bond directions become spontaneously inequivalent without transverse spin coherence or three-sublattice order. A hard-core-boson mapping reveals its origin: once thermal fluctuations destroy phase coherence, the field-doped holes settle on domain walls of the local up-up-down order, which are stabilized by quantum kink delocalization and aligned by its $Z_3$ registry. Our results call for reexamining high-field states in triangular-lattice cobaltates such as Na$_2$BaCo(PO$_4$)$_2$ and K$_2$Co(SeO$_3$)$_2$.

</details>


### [143] [High-density correlation energy of the electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31426)
*Miguel A. L. Marques,Paulo R. Pires,Stefano Pittalis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文重新计算了三维非极化电子气（unpolarized three-dimensional electron gas）关联能（correlation energy）高密度展开中 $r_s\ln r_s$ 项系数 $D$，得到 $-0.0077880(9)$ Ha，低于此前估计值 $-0.010$ Ha，并给出 $r_s^2$ 阶单对数与双对数系数的求和结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高密度展开系数被广泛用于密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）关联泛函（correlation functional）的近似构造，其中 $A$、$B$、$C$ 已知精确值，而 $D$ 需依赖图示微扰估计；重新审视 $D$ 的估计有助于提高相关近似的可靠性。

Method: 基于三类费曼图（Feynman diagram）估计 $D$：裸环图（bare rings）、含一个一阶交换插入的环图（rings with one first-order exchange insertion），以及含三个不同动量转移（momentum transfer）的三阶图；同时计算 $r_s^2$ 阶单对数系数 $E_1$ 的七项贡献与双对数系数 $E_2$ 的三项长波长（long-wavelength）贡献。

Result: 三类图之和为 $-0.0077880(9)$ Ha/电子，与早先的 $-0.010$ Ha 存在差异，主要源于 Carr 与 Maradudin 计算中交换环的耦合常数加权（coupling-constant weighting）。$E_1$ 的七项贡献合计 $(3.653 \pm 0.010)\times 10^{-3}$ Ha/电子；$E_2$ 的三项贡献合计 $α^2(36π^2 - 208\ln 2 - 221)/(192π^4) \approx -1.433\times 10^{-4}$ Ha/电子，其中 $α= (4/9π)^{1/3}$。

Conclusion: 在 $r_s = 0.5$ 处现有的扩散蒙特卡罗（diffusion Monte Carlo）能量尚不足以区分两种 $D$ 估计值，故修正后的 $D$ 值仍需更高精度数值数据加以检验。

Abstract: The high-density expansion of the correlation energy per electron of the unpolarized three-dimensional electron gas, $ε_c = A\ln r_s + B + C r_s\ln r_s + D r_s + \dots$, is used in many approximations to the correlation functional of density-functional theory. The coefficients $A$, $B$ and $C$ are known exactly. We revisit the three diagram classes used to estimate $D$, namely bare rings, rings with one first-order exchange insertion, and third-order diagrams with three distinct momentum transfers. Their sum is $-0.0077880(9)$ Ha per electron, compared with the earlier estimate of $-0.010$ Ha. The difference comes mainly from the coupling-constant weighting of the exchange rings in the calculation of Carr and Maradudin. At order $r_s^2$, seven contributions to the single-logarithm coefficient $E_1$ sum to $(3.653 \pm 0.010)\times 10^{-3}$ Ha per electron. The three long-wavelength contributions to the double-logarithm coefficient $E_2$ sum to $α^2(36π^2 - 208\ln 2 - 221)/(192π^4) \approx -1.433\times 10^{-4}$ Ha per electron, where $α= (4/9π)^{1/3}$. Available diffusion Monte Carlo energies at $r_s = 0.5$ cannot distinguish between the two estimates of $D$.

</details>


### [144] [A fermionic framework for quantum machine learning benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31458)
*Jérémie Gince,Ayana Sarkar,Stefanos Kourtis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出费米子机器学习（fermionic ML, FermiML）框架，利用可高效经典模拟的自由费米子系统构建量子机器学习（quantum ML, QML）模型的“费米子替身（fermionic surrogates）”，在同等条件下对QML与经典可模拟模型进行基准测试，并扩展至回归与大尺度分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QML模型的性能增益是否真正源自经典不可及的资源，抑或仅是架构带来的偶然副效应，缺乏公平可比的基准。作者希望构建与QML模型共享架构与归纳偏置、但可高效经典模拟的对照模型，以在同等条件下甄别QML的真实优势。

Method: FermiML以（参数化）量子比特旋转（qubit rotations）与纠缠门（entangling gates）为基本构件，涵盖核方法与神经网络、支持分类与回归、可扩展至真实数据集。基于此构建fQKS、fPQC等费米子替身，与量子厨房水槽（QKS）、瞬时量子多项式（IQP）及通用参数化量子电路（PQC）对比；并引入费米子保真度（fermionic fidelity）与Pfaffian协方差核（Pfaffian covariance kernels）及费米子能量分类器（fermionic energy-based classifier）。

Result: 在五个分类数据集、31种配置上，fQKS显著优于QKS且与IQP、PQC统计上不可区分；fPQC优于IQP和QKS，并在28种配置中与PQC持平。费米子能量分类器在图像数据上优于主流QML模型，在文本上胜出或接近，且整体接近经典基线。

Conclusion: 性能对架构高度敏感，但FermiML产生的费米子替身可挑战甚至超越无限制的QML对应模型；其费米子QML模型在真实数据集上达到与经典基线相当的规模，为QML增益归因提供了公平基准。

Abstract: We present a framework for building and benchmarking scalable machine learning (ML) models based on simulating free-fermion systems. Our fermionic ML (FermiML) framework is (i) versatile enough to encompass kernel methods and neural networks, (ii) applicable to classification and regression, and (iii) scalable to real-world datasets. FermiML models are quantum circuits sharing the building blocks of most quantum ML (QML) models: (parameterized) qubit rotations and entangling gates. This allows us to build fermionic surrogates that mimic the architecture, and hence the quantum resources and inductive bias, of any QML model while remaining efficiently classically simulable. We thus benchmark QML models against close fermionic counterparts on equal footing and ascertain whether a purported QML gain truly stems from classically inaccessible resources or is an accidental side-effect. Across 31 configurations from five classification datasets, we benchmark quantum kitchen sinks (QKS), instantaneous quantum polynomial (IQP) and generic parameterized quantum circuit (PQC) models against fermionic surrogates. fQKS significantly outperforms QKS and is statistically indistinguishable from IQP and PQC; fPQC outperforms IQP and QKS and ties PQC in 28 configurations. We also introduce fermionic fidelity and Pfaffian covariance kernels for regression and establish QML baselines for this harder task. Finally, we introduce a fermionic energy-based classifier scalable to large text and image datasets, which outperforms prominent QML models on images, beats or closely trails them on text, and stays close to classical baselines throughout. Our results show that while performance depends sensitively on architecture, FermiML yields fermionic surrogates that challenge or outperform their unrestricted counterparts and fermionic QML models that match the scale of classical baselines on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [145] [Strong Co-O exchange interaction in $P4/nmm$ KCoO$_2$ and its effect on spin wave and Néel temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31504)
*Ilteris K. Turan,Walter R. L. Lambrecht,Jerome Jackson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文采用线性响应方法计算KCoO₂（P4/nnm结构）中经典海森堡哈密顿量的交换相互作用，发现Co与其顶端O之间的交换作用最大且为铁磁性，并在平均场与随机相位近似下评估其对奈尔温度与自旋波谱的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在定量确定KCoO₂中各类交换相互作用（exchange interactions）的强度与符号，特别是识别主导性相互作用，并厘清其对磁有序温度与自旋激发的影响机制。

Method: 基于横向自旋磁化率（transverse spin susceptibility）导出的线性响应方法（linear response approach），在原子球内以正比于局域磁化强度的投影算符进行积分，并将全部原子视为磁性原子，从而计算经典海森堡哈密顿量（classical Heisenberg Hamiltonian）的交换参数。

Result: Co与顶端O之间的交换相互作用远大于其他相互作用，且为铁磁性（ferromagnetic）；该作用在平均场近似（mean field approximation）下显著提高临界温度，但在随机相位近似（random phase approximation）下则无此增强效果。

Conclusion: Co–O交换作用是KCoO₂中主导的磁相互作用，其对各近似层次下奈尔温度（Néel temperature）与自旋波谱（spin wave spectrum）的预测存在显著差异，表明结果的可靠性高度依赖于所采用的近似方法。

Abstract: The exchange interactions of a classical Heisenberg Hamiltonian
  in KCoO$_2$ in the $P4/nnm$ structure
  are calculated using a linear response approach, derived from the transverse
  spin susceptibility, integrated over atomic spheres using a projector proportional the local magnetization and including all atoms as magnetic atoms. The exchange interaction between Co and its apical O is found to be by far the largest interaction and ferromagnetic. Its effect on the Néel temperature and the spin wave spectrum are evaluated. The Co-O exchange is found to strongly enhance the critical temperature in the mean field approximation but not in the random phase approximation.

</details>


### [146] [Spin Hamiltonian, magnetic anisotropy and excitation spectrum in altermagnetic $α$-MnTe from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31548)
*Matteo Costa,Dario Fiore Mosca,Cesare Franchini,Leonid V. Pourovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于第一性原理构建了六角交替磁体（altermagnet）α-MnTe 的完整低能磁哈密顿量，发现易平面各向异性与 Γ 点磁振子能隙主要由经典偶极-偶极相互作用主导，而非传统单离子图像，并据此定量解释巨磁致伸缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-MnTe 兼具近室温共线磁序与强自旋劈裂电子结构，但其易平面各向异性、布里渊区中心（zone-center）磁振子能隙及异常大的磁致伸缩（magnetostriction）的微观起源长期缺乏解释。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT），并用力定理（force theorem）评估完整的各向异性层间交换、单离子项与经典偶极耦合，从第一性原理推导低能磁哈密顿量。

Result: 各向同性交换（含第十近邻耦合的交替磁劈裂）与非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）数据一致；偶极-偶极相互作用使 Γ 点磁振子劈裂增强三倍并与实验定量吻合，而 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 项虽量级不小却无实质贡献；该哈密顿量亦重现反节点方向的手性磁振子劈裂，并通过超交换（superexchange）的体积依赖定量解释巨磁体积耦合。

Conclusion: 易平面各向异性与 Γ 点磁振子能隙由经典偶极-偶极相互作用支配，修正了单离子主导的通常认识；单一哈密顿量可统一描述 α-MnTe 的交换、磁振子劈裂与磁致伸缩行为。

Abstract: The hexagonal altermagnet $α$-MnTe combines a near-room-temperature collinear order with a strongly spin-split electronic structure, yet the microscopic origin of its easy-plane anisotropy, its zone-center magnon gap and its anomalously large magnetostriction have remained unexplained. We derive the complete low-energy magnetic Hamiltonian of $α$-MnTe from first principles, combining density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory with a force-theorem evaluation of the full anisotropic intersite exchange, single-ion terms and classical dipolar couplings. The isotropic exchange, including the altermagnetic splitting of the tenth-neighbor coupling, agrees with inelastic neutron scattering data. Contrary to the common single-ion picture, we find that the easy-plane anisotropy and the magnon gap at $Γ$ are governed by classical dipole-dipole interactions, which enhance the zone-center magnon splitting threefold leading to quantitative agreement with experiment, while the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya term is irrelevant despite its size. The same Hamiltonian reproduces the chiral magnon splitting along antinodal directions, mirroring the electronic spin splitting, and quantitatively accounts for the giant magnetovolume coupling of $α$-MnTe through the volume dependence of superexchange.

</details>


### [147] [Adaptive ghost Gutzwiller dynamics of Hubbard model quenches across coupling strengths over extended times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31631)
*Yong-Xin Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出自适应含时鬼轨道Gutzwiller近似（adaptive td-gGA），通过按需增加辅助鬼轨道，以远低于固定轨道数的代价长时间追踪关联电子实时动力学；在Bethe晶格半满Hubbard模型相互作用淬火中，直至每自旋16个鬼轨道的结果在DMFT可及范围内与精确参考一致，强耦合下收敛时长可达DMFT数据的3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将关联电子实时动力学推进到更长时间、覆盖全部相互作用强度仍是未解难题；此前固定6个鬼轨道的td-gGA虽大体复现DMFT数据，却缺乏误差估计，无法支持超出已有参考数据的预测。

Method: 采用自适应含时鬼Gutzwiller近似（adaptive time-dependent ghost Gutzwiller approximation, td-gGA），在Gutzwiller近似中引入辅助鬼轨道（ghost orbitals），当动力学过程中累积的关联超过阈值时自动增加鬼轨道数目；以Bethe晶格半满Hubbard模型的相互作用淬火为测试，对照数值精确的非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT）。

Result: 每自旋最多16个鬼轨道时，自适应td-gGA在DMFT数据可及范围内均与之一致，涵盖弱耦合预热（prethermal）区与强耦合坍缩-复活（collapse-and-revival）区；强耦合下收敛时间达DMFT数据的3.5倍。两区交叉附近系统弛豫至与DMFT一致的热化值；按鬼轨道数目分辨动力学，可揭示Gutzwiller近似的尖锐动力学转变如何变为平滑交叉；延长时窗中振荡频率的重整化与二阶强耦合展开的预言相符。

Conclusion: 自适应td-gGA内置误差估计，为驱动与多轨道关联系统的延长时间动力学提供了可系统改进的途径，尤其在与张量网络（tensor-network）或量子求解器结合处理嵌入问题时具有前景。

Abstract: Following the real-time dynamics of correlated electrons to extended times, at all interaction strengths, remains an open challenge. We address it with an adaptive time-dependent ghost Gutzwiller approximation (td-gGA). The method extends the Gutzwiller approximation by auxiliary ghost orbitals. It adds more of them whenever the correlations that build up during the dynamics exceed a threshold. This is computationally far more efficient than keeping their number fixed. As a demonstration we take the interaction quench of the half-filled Hubbard model on the Bethe lattice. For this paradigmatic problem, numerically exact nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) serves as the reference. A previous td-gGA study with a fixed set of 6 ghost orbitals per spin largely reproduced the available DMFT data. It lacked, however, an error estimate that would support predictions beyond them. With up to 16 ghost orbitals per spin, adaptive td-gGA agrees with DMFT wherever DMFT data are available, from the weak-coupling prethermal regime to the strong-coupling collapse-and-revival regime. At strong coupling it remains converged up to three and a half times as long as the DMFT data. Near the crossover between the two regimes, the system relaxes to thermal values that agree with DMFT. Resolving the dynamics in the number of ghost orbitals shows how the sharp dynamical transition of the Gutzwiller approximation turns into this smooth crossover. In the extended time window at strong coupling, we observe that the oscillation frequency is renormalized as predicted by our second-order strong-coupling expansion. With its built-in error estimate, adaptive td-gGA offers a systematically improvable route to the extended-time dynamics of driven and multi-orbital correlated systems, especially when combined with tensor-network or quantum solvers for the embedding problem.

</details>


### [148] [Universal spectrum of weakly coupled quantum critical Ising chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31655)
*Hansen S. Wu,Ribhu K. Kaul*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用基于哈密顿量截断（Hamiltonian truncation）的精确对角化方法，研究缩放极限下弱链间耦合扰动的临界横场 Ising 链阵列的能谱。当链数增大时，证实存在两粒子连续谱以下的激发，可对应具有小修正的 E8 理论，并发现一个 E8 理论之外的 ψ 粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注弱链间耦合下临界横场 Ising 链阵列的缩放极限能谱，检验多链系统中是否出现单链问题所没有的激发，并考察其与 E8 理论的关系。

Method: 采用基于哈密顿量截断（Hamiltonian truncation）的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）算法，在缩放极限（scaling limit）下计算被弱链间耦合扰动的临界横场 Ising 链（transverse field Ising chains）阵列能谱，并随链数增大进行分析。

Result: 链数增大时确认两粒子连续谱（two particle continuum）以下存在激发，可与 Zamolodchikov E8 理论在小修正下对应；同时发现 E8 理论之外的新 ψ 粒子，其质量 Mψ 与最低 E8 质量 M1 之比为普适数值，数值计算得 Mψ/M1 = 1.819⋯（一维临界链阵列）；Mψ 随偏离临界点（detuning）的缩放依赖与 E8 质量定性不同。

Conclusion: 该新状态本质上涉及相邻链上的激发，因而在单链问题中无对应物；其独特质量缩放行为有助于在易轴磁性链材料的光谱实验中识别该粒子（ψ-particle）。

Abstract: We study the spectrum of arrays of critical transverse field Ising chains perturbed by a weak inter-chain coupling in the scaling limit, using an exact diagonalization algorithm based on Hamiltonian truncation. As the number of chains is made large, we confirm the existence of excitations below the two particle continuum that can be identified with the celebrated Zamalodchikov $E_8$ theory with small corrections. In addition, we find a distinct $ψ$-particle, with mass $M_\mathrmψ$ which is not contained in the $E_8$ theory. Its ratio to the lowest $E_8$ mass ($M_1$) is a universal number, which we have computed numerically to be $M_\mathrmψ/M_1 = 1.819\cdots$ for a one dimensional array of critical chains. We present evidence that this new state fundamentally involves excitations on neighboring chains, explaining why it has no analogue in the single chain problem. Interestingly the $M_\mathrmψ$ mass has a characteristic scaling dependence on detuning from the critical point that is qualitatively distinct from the $E_8$ masses, which can help identify it in spectroscopic experiments on easy-axis magnetic chain materials.

</details>


### [149] [Symmetry-dependent field-induced elastic renormalization in the quantum-critical medium-entropy alloy NiCoCr$_{0.8}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32254)
*Tadataka Watanabe,Mai Watanabe,Brian C. Sales*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用高精度超声声速测量研究量子临界中熵合金（medium-entropy alloy）NiCoCr$_{0.8}$ 的弹性响应：零场下三个弹性模量随降温单调硬化，而在沿 [110] 方向施加磁场时于低温分辨出量级约 $10^{-5}$ 的微小弹性重整化（elastic renormalization），且 $E_g$ 与 $A_{1g}$ 应变对称通道呈现相反的场响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学无序的巡游量子临界金属（itinerant quantum-critical metal）中，电子与磁性自由能对晶格应变（strain）的细微依赖难以探测，需要一种对对称性敏感的探针来分辨不同应变对称通道的响应。

Method: 采用高精度超声声速（ultrasound velocity）测量，测定立方 NiCoCr$_{0.8}$ 的三个对称独立弹性模量 $C_{11}$、$C_t=(C_{11}-C_{12})/2$ 与 $C_{44}$；在零场与沿 [110] 方向施加磁场的条件下对比其温度与场依赖行为，并将结果与已报道的量子临界费米液体（quantum-critical Fermi liquid）渡越（crossover）能标进行比较。

Result: 零磁场下三个弹性模量随降温单调硬化，无明显弹性软化或反常；沿 [110] 施加磁场时，低温下分辨出约 $10^{-5}$ 量级的系统性弹性重整化，其中四方 $E_g$ 模式 $C_t$ 随场增强而软化，而导出的体模 $A_{1g}$ 模式 $C_B$ 硬化。

Conclusion: 场诱导的弹性重整化在量子临界区即已出现，并连续演化穿过渡越区域，无尖锐弹性反常或阈值行为；对称性分辨的超声测量可作为探测化学无序巡游量子临界金属中应变依赖电子与磁自由能变化的灵敏手段。

Abstract: We investigate the elastic response of cubic NiCoCr$_{0.8}$, a quantum-critical medium-entropy alloy, by high-precision ultrasound velocity measurements. At zero magnetic field, the three symmetrically independent elastic moduli $C_{11}$, $(C_{11}-C_{12})/2=C_t$, and $C_{44}$ monotonically harden upon cooling without pronounced elastic softening or anomalies. In magnetic fields applied along [110], however, a minute but systematic elastic renormalization of the order of $10^{-5}$ is resolved at low temperatures. The tetragonal $E_g$ mode $C_t$ softens with increasing field, whereas the derived bulk $A_{1g}$ mode $C_B$ hardens, revealing an opposite field response between the two strain-symmetry channels. Comparison with the previously reported quantum-critical-Fermi-liquid crossover scale shows that the field-induced elastic renormalization develops already in the quantum-critical regime and evolves continuously across the crossover region, without a sharp elastic anomaly or threshold behavior. These results demonstrate that symmetry-resolved ultrasound provides a sensitive probe of subtle strain-dependent changes in the electronic and magnetic free energy of a chemically disordered itinerant quantum-critical metal.

</details>


### [150] [Curvilinear nanomagnets of non-trivial geometric topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32306)
*Vagson L. Carvalho-Santos,Luke A. Turnbull,Naëmi Leo,Oleksandr V. Pylypovskyi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本章探讨样品形状的拓扑（topology）等全局几何特征如何反映在曲线纳米结构（curvilinear nanoarchitecture）的磁化场（magnetization field）性质中，重点分析环形、球形及不可定向流形（non-orientable manifolds）等非平庸拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于超越已深入研究的一维等半径管状结构，理解具有非平庸拓扑的纳米结构中磁化场与几何拓扑之间的耦合关系，并将拓扑分析从纯理论概念推进为可实验表征且具实际相关性的课题。

Method: 方法上先简要介绍微分几何（differential geometry）与拓扑学，建立其与特定流形（manifold）上磁化矢量场性质的关联，再结合现代制备与表征技术，对拓扑结构异于扩展薄膜（extended films）的纳米结构开展理论与实验分析。

Result: 分析对象涵盖环形（toroidal）、球形（spherical）及不可定向流形（non-orientable manifolds）等具体体系，讨论其全局几何特征在磁化场性质中的体现（摘要未给出具体定量结果）。

Conclusion: 样品拓扑是决定曲线纳米结构中磁化场性质的关键因素；借助现代制备与表征手段，拓扑效应已成为可直接测量并具应用价值的物理问题，其中非等半径管及非平庸拓扑流形是本章关注的核心。

Abstract: In this Chapter, we focus on the question of how the global geometric characteristics given by the topology of the sample's shape are reflected in the properties of the magnetization field of the given curvilinear nanoarchitecture. We start with a brief introduction to differential geometry and topology, giving their connection to the specific properties of the vector field represented by the magnetization field defined at the specific manifold. Then we discuss modern fabrication and characterization techniques that make analysis of a sample's topology not only an interesting theoretical concept but also a directly accessible and practically relevant topic. The main attention of the Chapter is dedicated to the theoretical and experimental analysis of nanostructrures with the topology different from the extended films beyond the in-depth studied topic of tubes of the constant radius. In particular, toroidal, spherical, and non-orientable manifolds are considered.

</details>


### [151] [Contribution to the GdTe$_3$ magnetic phase diagram from electronic transport measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32401)
*A. A. Sinchenko,P. D. Grigoriev,A. Hadj-Azzem,E. Pachoud,E. Lorenzo,P. Monceau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过沿三个晶向的磁场下电阻测量，构建了GdTe₃三个零场反铁磁转变的磁相图，发现基面磁场下中间相的重入现象及自旋翻转（spin flop）转变，推测其与三重点（triple point）相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GdTe₃在零磁场下存在三个反铁磁（antiferromagnetic, AFM）转变（T_N1=12 K、T_N2=10.5 K、T₁=7 K），其磁相图尚未完整，需厘清各转变在磁场下的演化及与电荷密度波（charge density wave）等序的耦合关系。

Method: 采用电阻测量（resistance measurements）手段，在沿三个晶体学方向施加磁场的条件下追踪三个AFM转变，从而间接探测磁有序（magnetic ordering）行为。

Result: 基面（basal plane）内加场时观察到中间相的重入（re-entrance），可能源于T_N2反铁磁序与电荷密度波（及可能的铁轴序，ferroaxial order）之间的耦合；同时在某一温度出现自旋翻转（spin flop）转变，该温度推测收敛于约2.5-3.0 T、T介于T₁与T_N2之间的三重点（triple point）附近。

Conclusion: 结果补全了GdTe₃的复杂相图，并提出以输运测量（transport measurements）研究磁有序的方法，该技术可推广至其他化合物。

Abstract: Magnetic phase diagrams of the three antiferromagnetic (AFM) transitions of GdTe$_3$ that occur at zero magnetic field at $T_{N1}=12$ K, $T_{N2}=10.5$ K and $T_1=7$ K are investigated by means of resistance measurements in a magnetic field oriented along the three crystallographic directions. With the magnetic field applied in the basal plane we found re-entrance of an intermediate phase which may be a result of the coupling between the $T_{N2}$ AFM and the charge density wave with possibly a ferroaxial order. A spin flop transition is also observed at a temperature that presumably converges towards a triple point in vicinity of 2.5-3.0 T and $T$ between $T_1$ and $T_{N2}$. Our results help complete a complex phase diagram of GdTe$_3$ and advance a method for investigating magnetic ordering via transport measurements -- a technique applicable to many other compounds.

</details>


### [152] [Space-Time Point Defects as Probes of Criticality: Application to the Gross-Neveu-Heisenberg Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32775)
*Jakob Väth,Luis Thremer,Francesco Parisen Toldin,Fakher F. Assaad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在临界点（criticality）处，单点局域场（local field）不相关，但其诱导的调制以两倍标度维数（scaling dimension）衰减，从而将两点关联函数（two-point correlation function）转化为单点测量。该方法在辅助场量子蒙特卡罗（auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo）中实现对π通量哈伯德模型（π-flux Hubbard model）的狄拉克半金属—反铁磁莫特绝缘体转变（Dirac-semimetal to antiferromagnetic-Mott-insulator transition）分析，给出ν=1.10(3)、η_φ=1.09(4)，并指出等时结构因子（equal-time structure factor）不能用于提取临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界点处等时结构因子可能受背景贡献主导，传统基于两点关联函数提取临界指数（critical exponents）的方式可能失效，需寻求更精确的测量途径。

Method: 利用临界点局域场诱导调制随两倍标度维数衰减的性质，将两点关联函数测量转化为单点测量：一个算符插入玻尔兹曼权重（Boltzmann weight），另一个被测量。该方法在辅助场量子蒙特卡罗中实现，用于半填充π通量哈伯德模型，并配合有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling analysis）。

Result: 得到静态自旋磁化率（static spin susceptibility）与虚时位移关联（imaginary-time-displaced correlations）的高精度结果；临界指数估计为ν=1.10(3)与η_φ=1.09(4)，接近场论预测η_φ≈1。由于η_φ>1，临界处等时结构因子不随体系尺寸发散。

Conclusion: 该模型为N_f=2的Gross-Neveu-Heisenberg普适类（universality class）提供正则化；临界处等时结构因子在有序波矢处由背景贡献主导，不能用于提取临界指数；转变处自旋动力学强烈非相干，量子费舍尔信息（quantum Fisher information）保持有界。

Abstract: At criticality, a local field acting at a single point in Euclidean space-time is irrelevant and induces a modulation in space and time that decays with twice its scaling dimension. This turns two-point correlation functions into one-point measurements: one operator insertion enters the Boltzmann weight, the other is measured. For the local magnetization the relation is exact at any source strength. Implemented in auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo, the approach yields the static spin susceptibility and imaginary-time-displaced correlations with unprecedented precision. We apply it to the Dirac-semimetal to antiferromagnetic-Mott-insulator transition of the half-filled $π$-flux Hubbard model. This lattice model provides a regularization of the $N_f=2$ Gross-Neveu-Heisenberg universality class. A finite-size scaling analysis estimates the critical exponents as $ ν=1.10(3)$ and $η_φ=1.09(4)$, close to field-theory predictions of $η_φ\simeq1$. Since $η_φ>1$, the equal-time structure factor at criticality does not diverge with the system size. Hence, the equal-time structure factor at the ordering wave vector is dominated by background contributions and cannot be used for extracting critical exponents. Consistently, the spin dynamics at the transition is strongly incoherent and the quantum Fisher information remains bounded.

</details>


### [153] [Zone-folded Goldstone mode of the field-induced spin supersolid in the square-lattice $S=1$ antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32798)
*Sibin Yang,Pinaki Sengupta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过随机解析延拓（stochastic analytic continuation）量子蒙特卡洛数据，首次计算了方晶格 S=1 反铁磁体场诱导自旋超固态（spin supersolid）及其邻近相区的动态自旋结构因子，并指出"折叠的戈德斯通模（Goldstone mode）"是超固态的动力学指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该体系的场诱导自旋超固态此前仅由静态可观测量（如双峰横向结构因子）刻画，缺乏动力学层面的表征，因此需要计算动态自旋结构因子以区分超固态与邻近相区。

Method: 对 16×16 晶格、逆温度 β=32 的随机级数展开量子蒙特卡洛（stochastic-series-expansion quantum Monte Carlo）数据进行随机解析延拓（stochastic analytic continuation），分别在超固态、m_z=1/2 伊辛平台相（Ising plateau）与倾斜 XY 相（canted XY phase）各取一个磁场点计算动态结构因子。

Result: 超固态仅有一个戈德斯通模，棋盘格（checkerboard）折叠使其同时出现在 Q=(π,π) 与 q=(0,0) 两个波矢、两个自旋通道中，速度为 v=1.36±0.01；倾斜相只呈现该模式的一半，平台相则无，取而代之的是有能隙磁振子带（gapped magnon band）与双磁振子纵向连续谱（two-magnon longitudinal continuum）。在 Q 与 (0,0) 处，有限晶格谱由横向通道的转子塔（rotor tower）与纵向通道的伊辛二重态（Ising doublet）主导。

Conclusion: 通过与同批数据的刚度（stiffness）和均匀磁化率（uniform susceptibility）进行三项无参数比较（速度、声模权重、转子塔的热衰减），将解析延拓谱与原始蒙特卡洛可观测量关联起来，确认折叠的戈德斯通模为超固态的动力学指纹。

Abstract: The field-induced spin supersolid of the square-lattice $S=1$ antiferromagnet with single-ion and exchange anisotropy has so far been characterized only by static observables, among them a two-peak transverse structure factor. We compute its dynamic spin structure factors, and those of the neighboring phases, by stochastic analytic continuation of stochastic-series-expansion quantum Monte Carlo data on a $16\times16$ lattice at inverse temperature $β=32$, at one field in each of the supersolid, the $m_z=1/2$ Ising plateau and the canted $XY$ phase. The supersolid has a single Goldstone mode, which the checkerboard folds onto both the ordering wave vector $\mathbf{Q}=(π,π)$ and $\mathbf{q}=(0,0)$, so that it appears in both spin channels at both points with one velocity $v=1.36\pm0.01$. The canted phase shows half of this pattern and the plateau none, the latter carrying instead a gapped magnon band and a two-magnon longitudinal continuum. Exactly at $\mathbf{Q}$ and $(0,0)$ the finite-lattice spectra are governed by a rotor tower of the condensate in the transverse channel and an Ising doublet of the checkerboard in the longitudinal one, both present at $\mathbf{Q}$ in the supersolid.
