Three parameter-free comparisons with the stiffness and the uniform susceptibility of the same runs, for the velocity, the sound-mode weight and the thermal decay of the rotor tower, tie the continued spectra to raw Monte Carlo observables. The folded Goldstone mode is the dynamical fingerprint of the supersolid.

</details>


### [154] [Local Thermodynamic Reconstruction of Impurity Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32974)
*Hang Su*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出局部麦克斯韦方法（Local-Maxwell approach），从世界线量子蒙特卡洛（worldline QMC）的局域可观测量重构杂质熵（impurity entropy），避免对广延熵的单独估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间扩展的相互作用量子浴（interacting quantum bath）中局域杂质的热力学熵难以数值求解，且传统方法需分别估算广延熵（extensive entropy），代价高。

Method: 沿杂质耦合对局域可观测量的温度响应进行积分以重建杂质熵；对非负世界线展开且有界局域耦合的情形，给出杂质顶点计数（impurity vertex count）各阶阶乘矩（factorial moments）的浴尺寸无关界，并分析麦克斯韦估计量（Maxwell estimator）的方差行为。

Result: 横场 Ising（transverse-field Ising）基准复现精确自由费米子（free fermion）结果；相互作用 XXZ 链上统计效率较常规广延热力学积分（thermodynamic integration）提升达数百倍；三通道 XXZ 杂质结（impurity junction）中揭示冷却过程中的熵恢复及有限温度熵对比（entropy contrast）对耦合的非单调依赖。

Conclusion: 局域热力学重构（local thermodynamic reconstruction）是处理相互作用量子浴中杂质熵的高效途径。

Abstract: Resolving the thermodynamic entropy associated with a local impurity in a spatially extended interacting quantum bath remains a numerical challenge. We develop a Local-Maxwell approach that reconstructs the impurity entropy from local observables in worldline quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations. By integrating their temperature responses along the impurity coupling, the method avoids separate estimates of extensive entropies. For nonnegative worldline expansions with bounded local couplings, we establish bath-size independent bounds on all factorial moments of the impurity vertex count. For even vertex counts, the Maxwell estimator variance remains bounded as the coupling approaches zero. The transverse-field Ising benchmark reproduces exact free fermion results, while an interacting XXZ chain demonstrates up to several hundredfold improvement in statistical efficiency over conventional extensive thermodynamic integration. In a three-channel XXZ impurity junction, we reveal entropy recovery upon cooling and a nonmonotonic coupling dependence of the finite-temperature entropy contrast. These results establish local thermodynamic reconstruction as an efficient approach to impurity entropy in interacting quantum baths.

</details>


### [155] [Investigating competing order in the Kanamori-Hubbard model with the multiorbital multichannel fluctuating field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32983)
*Laura Torchia,Erik Linnér,Massimo Capone*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将多通道涨落场（MCFF）方法推广至多轨道体系，并在半填充双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型中考察电荷、自旋、轨道及自旋-轨道涨落的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCFF方法此前仅应用于单带体系，尚缺乏对轨道结构（orbital structure）所导致的电荷与自旋不稳定性（charge and spin instabilities）的描述能力。

Method: 将MCFF理论扩展至多轨道体系，纳入轨道结构；应用于半填充双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，含排斥性轨道内相互作用U及正、负Hund耦合J，并可选择性保留单一涨落通道以分离其贡献。

Result: 在J≈U附近出现电荷密度波（charge density wave）与反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）涨落的简单竞争；在J≈0附近、模型具有更高SU(4)对称性时，反铁磁、轨道与自旋-轨道涨落呈现更复杂的竞争。

Conclusion: 所提出的多轨道推广为探究轨道与自旋特征如何塑造竞争性集体涨落提供了计算成本低且高效的框架。

Abstract: The multichannel fluctuating field (MCFF) approach treats selected collective fluctuations in interacting quantum lattice models nonperturbatively, but has so far been applied only to single-band systems. Here, we extend MCFF theory to multiorbital systems, accounting for the orbital structure of charge and spin instabilities. We apply it to the half-filled two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model with repulsive intra-orbital interaction $U$, and both positive and negative Hund's coupling $J$, addressing the competing orders and their interplay. The ability to selectively retain individual fluctuation channels and control their interplay allows us to disentangle their respective contributions and identify the mechanisms underlying competing instabilities. The model features a simple competition between charge density wave and antiferromagnetic fluctuations in the vicinity of $J=U$, and a more intricate competition between antiferromagnetic, orbital, and spin-orbital fluctuations near $J=0$, where the model acquires a higher SU(4) symmetry. The proposed multiorbital extension thus provides a computationally inexpensive and efficient framework for exploring how orbital and spin character shape competing collective fluctuations.

</details>


### [156] [Signatures of Kitaev quantum spin liquid states in $α$-RuCl$_3$ revealed by nitrogen-vacancy spin-noise spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33085)
*Jitender Kumar,Sehrish Iqbal,Himani Saraswat,Benjamin Hammons,Tianlin Li,Ariel Smooha,Rupak Timalsina,Arnab K. Pariari,Sy-Hwang Liou,Xia Hong,Markus Hücker,Amit Finkler,Abdelghani Laraoui*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用金刚石氮-空位（NV）色心自旋噪声谱（spin-noise spectroscopy）对8–150 nm厚的α-RuCl₃纳米片进行局域、非侵入式探测，发现约100 K以下NV自旋-晶格弛豫时间T₁显著下降，指示GHz频段磁涨落增强；低温下较厚薄片呈现双指数弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-RuCl₃因键依赖磁相互作用、强自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与量子涨落被视为Kitaev量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid, QSL）的典型候选，但既有输运、中子散射、光学与隧穿谱测量多针对大尺度样品的全局特征，尚缺乏纳米尺度的局域非侵入式证据。

Method: 采用金刚石氮-空位（NV）色心自旋噪声谱，对厚度8–150 nm的α-RuCl₃纳米片在4.3–250 K温区内测量NV自旋-晶格弛豫时间（T₁），并与片外及8 nm薄片结果对照。

Result: 靠近α-RuCl₃薄片处，T₁在约100 K以下明显下降，表明GHz频段磁涨落增强，该温标与Kitaev相互作用能标（约8 meV）及低能分数化激发（fractionalized excitations）的出现一致；50 K以下，厚度大于90 nm的薄片出现双指数T₁弛豫，而片外与8 nm薄片为单指数，提示磁噪声环境趋于非均匀。

Conclusion: NV自旋噪声谱可作为α-RuCl₃纳米片中低能自旋动力学的局域、非侵入式探针，并为检验原子级薄QSL候选材料中分数化激发的预测噪声特征提供途径。

Abstract: Bond-dependent magnetic interactions, strong spin-orbit coupling, and quantum fluctuations in $α$-RuCl$_3$ make it an archetypical candidate for exotic Kitaev quantum spin liquid (QSL) states. While electronic transport, neutron scattering, optical and tunneling spectroscopies were used to probe global QSL signatures in large scale $α$-RuCl$_3$ samples, nanoscale evidence of local QSL behavior via a non-invasive experimental probe is still missing. Here, we use diamond nitrogen-vacancy (NV) spin-noise spectroscopy to probe QSL states in α-RuCl3 nanoflakes with thicknesses ranging from 8 to 150 nm over a temperature range of 4.3-250 K. Our measurements reveal a pronounced decrease in the NV spin-lattice relaxation time ($T_{1}$) below ~100 K in proximity to the $α$-RuCl$_3$ flakes, indicating enhanced GHz-frequency magnetic fluctuations. This temperature scale is consistent with the energy scale associated with Kitaev interactions (~8 meV) and the emergence of low-energy fractionalized excitations. Below 50 K, thicker (> 90 nm) flakes exhibit a distinct bi-exponential $T_{1}$ decay, unlike the single-exponential behavior observed for off-flake and thinner (8 nm) flake measurements, suggesting an increasingly heterogeneous magnetic noise environment. These results establish NV spin-noise spectroscopy as a local, non-invasive probe of low-energy spin dynamics in $α$-RuCl$_3$ nanoflakes and open a route to testing predicted noise signatures of fractionalized excitations in atomically thin QSL candidates.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [SPARK: Spectral Preconditioned Analytic Residual Kolmogorov Arnold Networks for incompressible Navier Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31834)
*Dathi Rajasekhar,Thien-Tam Nguyen,Trung Bao Le*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: SJ-KAN 通过切比雪夫谱基解析传播空间导数、线性压缩加单层 CKAN，显著降低 PINN 求解 NS 方程的微分成本与网络复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对物理信息神经网络（Physics-Informed Neural Networks, PINNs）求解纳维-斯托克斯（Navier-Stokes, NS）方程时自动微分（automatic differentiation, AD）计算导数成本高、网络结构复杂的问题。

Method: 输入坐标经切比雪夫基（Chebyshev bases）嵌入，其空间导数有闭式表达且微分矩阵在训练前对配点一次性计算；单线性层将基压缩为少量潜特征，再由单层非线性切比雪夫 KAN（CKAN）层映射到物理变量，空间导数以固定矩阵乘积与逐元素运算解析传播，AD 仅用于反向传播计算权重梯度。

Result: 在 Taylor-Green 涡与顶盖驱动方腔（Re = 100、1000、5000、10,000）上，三种变体精度与基于 AD 的 PIKAN 及标准 PINN 相当或更优，参数量最多减少约 47 倍，每轮训练最快约 8.2 倍；并在无参考数据、少于 9,000 可训练参数下求解 Re = 10,000 的方腔流。

Conclusion: 解析导数传播、小型谱输入层与单层 KAN 结合可构成比基于 AD 方法更快更简的求解器，且适用于高雷诺数流动。

Abstract: We propose Spectral Jet KAN (SJ-KAN) to reduce the cost of differentiation and the network complexity of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) for the Navier-Stokes (NS) equations. The input coordinates are embedded through Chebyshev bases whose spatial derivatives are known in closed form, and all differentiation matrices are computed once for the collocation points before training. A single linear layer compresses the Chebyshev bases into a few latent features, and a single nonlinear Chebyshev Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (CKAN) layer maps these features to the physical variables. Because the basis derivatives are known in advance and the compression is linear, the spatial derivatives in the NS equations are carried through the network analytically by a fixed sequence of matrix products and elementwise operations. Automatic differentiation (AD) is used only in backpropagation, to compute the gradients of the loss with respect to the network weights. We examine three variants of SJ-KAN (JetKAN, JetKAN-F, JetKAN-H), which differ in how the output layer maps the latent features to the physical variables. All variants are tested on the Taylor-Green vortex and on the lid driven cavity at Reynolds numbers Re = 100, 1000, 5000 and 10,000. The results are compared with a KAN that uses AD (PIKAN) and with a standard PINN. The SJ-KAN variants reach comparable or better accuracy than both, with up to 47 times fewer parameters and up to 8.2 times faster training per epoch. SJ-KAN also solves the lid driven cavity flow at Re = 10,000 without any reference data and with fewer than 9,000 trainable parameters. These results show that analytic derivative propagation, a small spectral input layer and a single KAN layer give a faster and simpler solver than methods based on AD, also for flows at high Reynolds numbers.

</details>


### [158] [Emergent cloud patterns from stochastic jump models of parcel microphysics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32596)
*Mickaël D. Chekroun,Ilan Koren,Honghu Liu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文提出云水与雨水异常的“空间跳跃过程”模型，并通过平均场闭合导出伪抛物型金兹堡-朗道方程（PGLE），揭示动态惯性与振幅依赖频移（twist）如何控制云的组织形态，如带状、细胞状与波状网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暖云（warm clouds）会组织成带状、细胞状和复杂波网络，但连接这些形态的动力学机制难以分离；同时，未解析的云滴微物理（parcel microphysics）与云组织之间存在衔接缺口，需要桥接模型。

Method: 在活化区构建云水与雨水异常的空间跳跃过程模型并进行平均场闭合（mean-field closure）；从液滴增长与水汽输送中消去快变的过饱和调整，得到屏蔽环境核（screened environmental kernel），耦合立方Stuart-Landau振荡律，对泊松云滴事件平均并闭合三阶矩，导出PGLE。

Result: PGLE分解为带正则化的非线性薛定谔哈密顿（Hamiltonian）部分与由守恒诺特荷（Noether charge）支配的伪抛物Allen-Cahn梯度部分，并给出开放系统液态水收支。精确稳定性判据显示动态惯性与twist通过调制失同步与共振边带增益控制不稳定性；数值积分显示增大twist使类波状带经缺陷增殖转变为类asperitas波，亚临界竞争使细胞状合并为卷状，随机异常可演化出持续细胞排斥尺度与类actinoform螺旋臂。

Conclusion: 跳跃模型识别出被忽略的涨落矩（fluctuation moments），可用于与大涡模拟（large-eddy simulations）标定对比，从而连接微物理与云组织形态。

Abstract: Warm clouds organize into bands, cells, and intricate wave networks, yet the dynamical mechanisms connecting these forms remain difficult to isolate. To bridge unresolved parcel microphysics and cloud organization, we formulate a spatial jump-process model of cloud-water and rain-water anomalies in an activated regime and analyze its mean-field closure. Eliminating fast supersaturation adjustment from droplet growth and vapor transport supplies a screened environmental kernel, which we couple to a cubic Stuart-Landau oscillatory law. Averaging over Poisson parcel episodes and closing third-order moments yields a pseudoparabolic Ginzburg--Landau equation (PGLE), whose mixed space--time derivative encodes dynamic inertia, slowing fine-scale relaxation. Unlike diffusive amplitude equations, the PGLE splits into a regularized nonlinear Schrödinger Hamiltonian sector and a pseudoparabolic Allen--Cahn gradient sector governed by a conserved Noether charge, alongside an open-system liquid-water budget separating conversion from vapor exchange, fallout, and triad-driven domain-mean oscillations. Exact stability criteria show how dynamic inertia and amplitude-dependent frequency shifts (twist) control modulational loss of synchrony and resonant cross-carrier sideband gain above a critical twist threshold. Numerical integrations show that increasing twist transforms undulatus-like bands into defect-rich asperitas-like waves via defect proliferation, while subcritical competition consolidates cellular patterns into rolls. Even without twist, random anomalies evolve into a persistent cellular exclusion scale and actinoform-like spiral arms, while 1D wave breakup reorganizes into a two-frequency combination-tone spectrum. The jump model identifies omitted fluctuation moments for calibration against large-eddy simulations.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Dynamical phase transition in Brownian motion via sharp large deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31453)
*Benjamin Budway,Makiko Sasada,Mykhaylo Shkolnikov*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明带漂移布朗运动（Brownian motion with drift）中存在动力学相变（dynamical phase transition）：以占据测度（occupation measure）的上尾大偏差事件为条件时，远尾与近尾分别导致两类截然不同的Langevin扩散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学文献近期预测了带漂移布朗运动中存在此类动力学相变，但缺乏严格的数学证明；该工作旨在以Gibbs条件化原理（Gibbs conditioning principle）为框架，为此现象提供严格论证。

Method: 在长时间区间上，对归一化占据测度在区间[-1,1]上的上尾大偏差事件施加条件化；通过建立一个新的、关于占据测度的尖锐大偏差结果（sharp large deviation result）来刻画条件化过程的行为。

Result: 远上尾条件下，条件化过程遵循由加性泛函（additive functionals）的Donsker-Varadhan变分公式所给出的Langevin扩散；近上尾条件下，条件化过程在部分时间区间上遵循另一Langevin扩散，随后表现为无条件过程。

Conclusion: 研究严格确立了该动力学相变的存在性，并给出了该大偏差结果的若干推论，同时讨论了向其他马尔可夫过程（Markov processes）与加性泛函的推广。

Abstract: We prove a dynamical phase transition in Brownian motion with drift, a phenomenon recently predicted in the physics literature. Specifically, when a Brownian motion with drift on a long time interval is conditioned on an upper large deviation event for the (normalized) occupation measure of the interval [-1,1], in the spirit of the Gibbs conditioning principle, a phase transition occurs. If one conditions on a large deviation event in the far upper tail, the conditioned process follows the Langevin diffusion suggested by the Donsker-Varadhan variational formula for additive functionals. In contrast, if one conditions on a large deviation event in the near upper tail, the conditioned process follows a different Langevin diffusion on a fraction of the time interval and subsequently behaves like the unconditioned process. We establish this phase transition via a new sharp large deviation result for the occupation measure, which is of independent interest. We also provide further corollaries of this result and discuss generalizations to other Markov processes and additive functionals.

</details>


### [160] [Mass gap for finite connections in supercritical percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31981)
*Alexander Fribergh,Alan Hammond*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究超临界 Bernoulli 键渗流（Bernoulli bond percolation）中以原点为基、最大高度落在固定宽度窗口内的有限开簇，证明其以正密度被更新点（renewal points）占据，并由此推出有限连通函数的精确 Ornstein–Zernike 渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画超临界渗流中条件化有限开簇的几何结构，并借此获得有限连通函数（finite connectivity）的尖锐逐点渐近及其修正项性质，这些在已有结果中尚未被精细建立。

Method: 核心工具是保持条件分布的滑动重抽样（slide resampling）：在给定 ℓ-高度处切断有限连接并将上部随机平移；通过对滑动操作沿正密度尝试高度的迭代版本进行细致分析，证明缺失更新点的概率指数小，从而得到不可约片段的质量间隙（mass gap）。

Result: 在条件化事件下，除概率随 u 指数小外，簇被更新点以正密度填充；由质量间隙与更新分解（renewal decomposition）、端点估计共同给出尖锐的逐点 Ornstein–Zernike 渐近及高斯型幂律修正，其乘性前因子为正且实解析，相关的等衰减边界与 Wulff 边界实解析且严格凸。

Conclusion: 滑动重抽样被证明是分析条件化渗流簇结构的有效手段，更新点丰度、质量间隙与渐近展开构成一条自洽的论证链条，并直接导出前因子与边界曲率的解析性与凸性结论。

Abstract: We consider finite open clusters based at the origin in Bernoulli bond percolation on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ for $d\geq3$ at a supercritical value $p>p_c(d)$, whose maximal height in the direction of a unit vector $\pmb{\ell}$ lies in a fixed-width window at distance $u$. Conditioned on this event, we prove that, except with probability exponentially small in $u$, such a cluster is populated at positive density by renewal points. Here, a renewal point $w$ is the only vertex of the cluster in the fixed-width band immediately below the height hyperplane through $w$.
  The main technical estimate is that, under this conditioning, the absence of a renewal point is exponentially unlikely. From it we derive an exponential mass gap for the irreducible pieces delimited by successive renewal points, and from the mass gap the stated renewal abundance. The mass gap, together with the renewal decomposition and endpoint estimates established here, yields a sharp point-to-point Ornstein--Zernike asymptotic for finite connectivity, with its Gaussian-type power-law correction. The multiplicative prefactor in this asymptotic is positive and real-analytic, and the associated equidecay and Wulff boundaries are real-analytic and strictly convex.
  The principal innovation of the article is the use of a slide resampling that preserves the conditioned law. The slide acts by cutting the long finite connection at a given $\pmb{\ell}$-height and relocating the upper piece by a random shift. The mass gap is obtained by a delicate analysis of an iterated version of the slide operation along a positive density of attempted heights.

</details>


### [161] [Reflection positivity of discrete and Brownian loop-soup occupation fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32453)
*Yves Le Jan*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文确定环汤顶点占据（loop-soup vertex occupations）保证反射正性（reflection positivity）的强度阈值集合：有限图上为正的 Wallach 集 $\W_r$，而无质量平面 Dirichlet 条带上的重整化占据场（renormalized occupation field）恰好对应半整数格 $\{0,\tfrac12,1,\tfrac32,\dots\}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射正性是连接概率模型与量子场论、算子理论的关键性质；环汤占据场在何种强度下满足该性质尚需精确刻画，尤其对连续平面区域的重整化场。

Method: 在具有秩为 $r$ 的半正定交叉电导矩阵（cross-conductance matrix）的反射对称有限图上，确定保证反射正性的强度集合；并将结论对无质量平面 Dirichlet 条带（massless planar Dirichlet strip）的重整化占据场取所有秩的交。

Result: 有限图上该集合为实 Wallach 集（real Wallach set）$\W_r$；对重整化平面条带占据场，精确集合为 $\bigcap_{r\ge1}\W_r=\{0,\tfrac12,1,\tfrac32,\dots\}$，即半整数格。

Conclusion: 研究给出环汤占据场反射正性强度的精确阈值刻画，表明连续平面情形下的容许强度恰为非负半整数，从而把有限图上的 Wallach 集结论推广为平面重整化场的显式刻画。

Abstract: The intensities guaranteeing reflection positivity of loop-soup vertex occupations on every reflection-symmetric finite graph with positive semidefinite cross-conductance matrix of rank $r$ form the real Wallach set $\W_r$. For the renormalized occupation field on the massless planar Dirichlet strip, the exact set is $\bigcap_{r\geq1}\W_r=\{0,\tfrac12,1,\tfrac32,\ldots\}$.

</details>


### [162] [The sharp uniqueness threshold for the antiferromagnetic Potts model on regular trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32635)
*Wei Wu,Yicheng Xu,Kuan Yang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文确定了无限根 $d$-叉树（rooted $d$-ary tree）与 $(d+1)$-正则树（regular tree）上 $q$-态反铁磁 Potts 模型（antiferromagnetic Potts model）Gibbs 测度唯一性（Gibbs uniqueness）的精确阈值：当且仅当 $p\geqslant p_c=\max\{0,1-q/(d+1)\}$（除 $(q,p)=(d+1,0)$ 外）时唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果尚未覆盖全部 $d\geqslant1$、$q\geqslant2$ 情形下的唯一性阈值，本文旨在补全并统一该阈值刻画。

Method: 基于树上的递推关系（tree recursion），构造一个凸势函数（convex potential），并证明该势在偏离均匀分布（uniform distribution）时严格下降。

Result: 证明当且仅当 $p\geqslant p_c$ 时存在唯一 Gibbs 态，唯一例外为 $(q,p)=(d+1,0)$；结合 Gu、Wu 与 Yang 的结果，在 $p_c>0$ 时给出精确的临界幂律（critical power law）。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了所有 $d\geqslant1$、$q\geqslant2$ 情形下反铁磁 Potts 模型的唯一性阈值刻画，并为临界幂律提供了完整结论。

Abstract: We study Gibbs uniqueness for the $q$-state antiferromagnetic Potts model on the infinite rooted $d$-ary tree and the infinite $(d+1)$-regular tree, where $d\geqslant1$ and $q\geqslant2$. An edge has weight $p\in[0,1]$ if its endpoints have the same color and weight $1$ otherwise. We prove that there is a unique Gibbs state if and only if $p\geqslant p_c:=\max\{0,1-q/(d+1)\}$, except when $(q,p)=(d+1,0)$, thus completes the uniqueness thresholds for all $d\geqslant1$ and $q\geqslant2$. Our proof use a convex potential for the tree recursion and show that it strictly decreases away from the uniform distribution. Together with the result of Gu, Wu and Yang, our theorem gives the exact critical power law whenever $p_c>0$.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [The Hidden Simplicity of the Classical Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31369)
*Yilber Fabian Bautista,Stefano De Angelis,David A. Kosower,Donal O'Connell,Matteo Sergola*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在探针极限下研究了二到二有质量标量振幅（massive-scalar amplitudes）在微扰论所有阶的经典极限（classical limit），发现多重发射与二到二经典振幅可按加权树（weighted trees）组织，从而给出递归计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二到二有质量标量振幅在所有微扰阶的经典极限，并寻求经典振幅中可由量子振幅因子化（factorization）导出的简洁结构，以理解经典动力学的组合学起源。

Method: 在探针极限（probe limit）下对多类理论（一族标量理论、电动力学（electrodynamics）、含半BPS物质的 \mathcal{N}=8 超引力（supergravity））计算振幅；利用量子振幅的常规因子化推导经典因子化，并用加权树（weighted trees）组织多重发射与二到二振幅，借助费曼 iε 规定（Feynman iε prescription）给出边界条件与新世界线格林函数（worldline Green's function）实现递归计算。

Result: 相关多重发射经典振幅满足简单的因子化关系，该经典因子化反映经典动力学的二阶性质，并在包括引力（gravity）在内的更一般理论中成立；多重发射与二到二经典振幅均可用加权树简洁组织，支持探针极限下所有阶的递归计算，探针轨迹表现为树的生成函数（generating function）。

Conclusion: 经典极限下丰富的经典动力学可由简单组合学（combinatorics）产生；加权树结构与新世界线格林函数为全阶计算提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We study the classical limit of two-to-two massive-scalar amplitudes at all orders of perturbation theory. We compute these amplitudes in the probe limit for several theories: a family of scalar theories, electrodynamics, and $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity with half-BPS matter. We show that related multiple-emission classical amplitudes obey a simple factorization derivable from the usual factorization of quantum amplitudes. The classical factorization, which reflects the second-order nature of classical dynamics, holds in more general theories --- including gravity. We further show that both the multiple-emission and the two-to-two classical amplitudes have a simple organization in terms of weighted trees. This allows a recursive computation to all orders in the probe limit. The probe's trajectory emerges as a generating function of trees. The required boundary conditions are given by the Feynman $i \varepsilon$ prescription, leading to a novel worldline Green's function. In our approach, the rich classical dynamics arises from simple combinatorics.

</details>


### [164] [Bootstrapping two-loop $β$-functions for bosonic and heterotic strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31372)
*Achilleas Gitsis,Falk Hassler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出以低能有效作用量的梯度流（gradient flow）方法，借助仅由对称性确定的耦合空间度规（metric），绕开直接计算，获得非线性σ模型（non-linear σ-model）的两圈β函数（β-functions）。所得结果为O(d,d)协变的两参数族，同时覆盖玻色弦与杂化弦（heterotic string）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过一圈（beyond one loop）时，非线性σ模型的β函数难以直接计算，需要一种不依赖逐阶直接微扰计算、且能推广到更高圈（higher loops）的替代途径。

Method: 将β函数视为低能有效作用量的梯度流，其耦合空间度规完全由对称性（symmetry）固定，从而构造出O(d,d)协变的两圈β函数两参数族；并应用于可积的η形变与λ形变（η- and λ-deformations）的重整化群（RG）流。

Result: 得到同时涵盖玻色弦与杂化弦的两圈β函数族；对η、λ形变给出两圈RG流；在杂化弦情形下，该修正量为玻色情形的二分之一。

Conclusion: 由于方法仅依赖对称性输入，为计算更高圈β函数提供了可行路线；同时揭示了杂化弦与玻色弦两圈修正之间的比例关系。

Abstract: Beyond one loop, $β$-functions of non-linear $σ$-models are hard to compute directly. We obtain them instead as a gradient flow of the low-energy effective action, whose coupling-space metric is fixed exclusively by symmetry. This gives a two-parameter family of O$(d,d)$-covariant, two-loop $β$-functions that covers the bosonic and heterotic string at once. Because only symmetry enters, the method opens a route to higher loops. As an application, we derive the two-loop RG flow of the integrable $η$- and $λ$-deformations; for the heterotic string, the correction is half of the bosonic one.

</details>


### [165] [A Kerr-black-hole worldline action to $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31377)
*Yilber Fabian Bautista,Mathias Driesse,Kays Haddad,Gustav Uhre Jakobsen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作建立了一个世界线有效作用量（worldline action），在物理后闵可夫斯基计数（post-Minkowskian counting）下正确重现了克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole）动力学至牛顿常数的五阶，涵盖至黎曼曲率张量（Riemann curvature tensor）二阶与自旋张量（spin tensor）四阶的算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在有效场论框架下精确描述自旋紧致天体与引力波的散射，需要构造能系统重现克尔黑洞动力学的世界线作用量，并厘清匹配所需的高阶曲率与自旋算符。

Method: 完成引力波被自旋紧致天体散射振幅（amplitude）的四圈（four-loop）有效场论计算，并将结果与线性黑洞微扰理论（linear black hole perturbation theory）的预言进行匹配。

Result: 成功匹配所需的曲率二次算符包括线性与三次自旋阶的时间反演反对称（time-reversal anti-symmetric）算符（刻画穿越黑洞视界（horizon）的吸收）以及保守的二次自旋算符。

Conclusion: 作为该作用量的直接应用，计算了散射事件中克尔黑洞自旋大小的领头阶变化，并在一般自旋取向下重现了质量的领头阶变化。

Abstract: We establish a worldline action which correctly reproduces Kerr-black-hole dynamics up to fifth order in Newton's constant in a physical post-Minkowskian counting. This encompasses operators up to second order in the Riemann curvature tensor and up to fourth order in the spin tensor. Doing so entails performing the full effective-field-theory computation of the amplitude for a gravitational-wave scattering off of a spinning compact object up to four-loop order, and matching the result to the predictions from linear black hole perturbation theory. Quadratic-in-curvature operators which must be added to match successfully include time-reversal anti-symmetric ones at linear and cubic spin orders, capturing absorption across the black hole horizon, and conservative quadratic-in-spin ones. As an immediate application of our action, we compute the leading-order change in spin magnitude, and reproduce the leading change in mass, for a Kerr black hole in a scattering event with a second compact body, considering a generic orientation of the black hole's spin.

</details>


### [166] [Circulating currents around orbifolded defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31391)
*Matthew M. Roberts*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究对象为具备五维轨形奇点（orbifold singularity）并提升至光滑超对称十维几何的全息单值性缺陷（holographic monodromy defect），其实现对五维底空间与五维球同时非平凡作用。计算表明即使在所有单值性关闭时缺陷仍具非零环流，对应 1/8-BPS 激发态；非平凡体拓扑使 A 型反常系数变大，B 型则可大可小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类几何近期在纺锤体紧致化（spindle compactification）中受到关注，并被解释为广义 Gukov-Witten 缺陷，因此有必要系统刻画其性质及与真空绝热连通性的差异。

Method: 在先前研究的、具有锥形超额角（conical excess angle）空间上的全息单值性缺陷基础上取轨形商（orbifold），构造出提升后光滑超对称的十维几何，并解析计算单点函数（one-point function）与反常系数作为单值性及轨形数据的函数。

Result: 缺陷与空的反德西特真空（anti-de Sitter vacuum）非绝热连通。出乎意料的是，即便所有单值性均关闭，缺陷仍呈现非零环流（circulating current），区别于与真空绝热连通的缺陷，因而对应 1/8-BPS 激发态。与非平凡体拓扑的缺陷比较，A 型反常系数总是更大，B 型系数则可能更大或更小。

Conclusion: 轨形单值性缺陷构成一类不平凡的全息缺陷态，兼具非绝热性与自发环流特征，并表现出拓扑依赖的反常系数层级，为纺锤体紧致化中的广义 Gukov-Witten 缺陷图像提供了定量支撑。

Abstract: We investigate holographic monodromy defects with five dimensional orbifold singularities which lift to smooth and supersymmetric ten dimensional geometries. Such geometries have been of recent interest in spindle compactifications and have been interpreted as generalized Gukov-Witten defects. They are constructed by taking an orbifold of previously considered holographic monodromy defects on a space with a conical excess angle, and are not adiabatically connected to the empty anti-de Sitter vacuum. The the orbifold action acts nontrivially both on the five dimensional base as well as the five-sphere. We calculate one-point functions and anomaly coefficients as a function of the monodromies and orbifold data. Surprisingly, we discover that defects exhibit nonzero circulating currents even when all monodromies are turned off, unlike defects adiabatically connected to the vacuum, and therefore correspond to 1/8-BPS excited states. Comparing defects with nontrivial bulk topology we find that the A-type anomaly coefficient is always larger than those with trivial topology, while the B-type coefficient can be either larger or smaller.

</details>


### [167] [The Schwarzschild-Kerr crossover of high overtone quasinormal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31394)
*Yasuyuki Hatsuda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 慢转黑洞在高阻尼准正规模（quasinormal spectrum）上与史瓦西近似显著偏离；频率偏移量由一个六次振子（sextic oscillator）谱给出，并通过常微分方程与可积模型的对应关系导出，统一了史瓦西的 log 3 与奇异 Kerr 系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢转黑洞虽接近非旋转黑洞，但其高阻尼准正规模并不接近；史瓦西近似仅在 overtone 数约为 χ^{-4/3}（χ 为无量纲自旋）的范围内成立，需确定该尺度上的频率偏移规律。

Method: 基于波函数边界条件推导该尺度上的频率偏移，并给出误差估计；借助常微分方程与可积模型（integrable models）之间的对应关系，将其归结为一个六次振子的谱问题。

Result: 该尺度上的频率偏移是一个普适函数（universal function），由六次振子的谱确定；结果可回收史瓦西极限的 log 3，并对奇异的 Kerr 系数（singular Kerr coefficients）加以组织。

Conclusion: 慢转黑洞高阻尼准正规模的偏移具有普适性，可由六次振子谱刻画，从而在史瓦西近似失效区给出超越该近似的统一描述。

Abstract: A slowly rotating black hole is close to a nonrotating one, but its highly damped quasinormal spectrum is not. The Schwarzschild approximation holds only up to overtone numbers of order $χ^{-4/3}$, where $χ$ is the dimensionless spin. We find that the frequency shift at this scale is a universal function, derived from the wave boundary conditions with an error estimate. It is given by the spectrum of a sextic oscillator, via the correspondence between ordinary differential equations and integrable models. It recovers the Schwarzschild $\log 3$ and organizes the singular Kerr coefficients.

</details>


### [168] [Refined Rényi Entropy from Replica-Geometry Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31398)
*Qiang Wen,Haocheng Zhong,Yiwei Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构造了一类依赖复本指标 n 的显式测地线图族，在保持链路最大纠缠的前提下复现了全息共形场论（holographic conformal field theory）中宇宙膜（cosmic brane）导致的精细 Rényi 熵（refined Rényi entropy）n 依赖性，从而弥补标准全息张量网络（holographic tensor network）纠缠谱平坦的结构缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准固定图结构、最大纠缠链路的全息张量网络其主导纠缠谱是平坦的，Rényi 熵不依赖复本指标（replica index），与全息共形场论中张力性宇宙膜造成的 n 依赖精细 Rényi 熵形成鲜明反差。因此刻画这种复本依赖性是检验张量网络能否描述全息纠缠的重要判据。

Method: 构造显式的复本依赖测地线图（replica-dependent geodesic graphs）族：对每个复本指标 n，测地线以相同的局域各向同性相交测度从复本化边界发出，并完美铺砌所选复本几何，使相交计数的 n 依赖来源于几何本身，同时链路仍保持最大纠缠；随后在这些图上构建张量网络模型，通过计数跨越线程（crossing threads）复现精细 Rényi 熵的宇宙膜处方。

Result: 该张量网络模型通过交叉线程计数复现了宇宙膜的精细 Rényi 熵处方；其跨复本端点密度（cross-copy endpoint density）在 CFT₂ 中与加性线性组合方案（additive linear combination proposal）下的边界重构一致，构成总熵之外的额外检验；在高维情形还给出了该密度的解析大间隔衰减律（large-separation decay law）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在保留最大纠缠链路的条件下，通过几何化的复本依赖测地线图即可在张量网络中再现全息纠缠的复本依赖特征，为全息对偶（holographic duality）机制的张量网络描述提供了超越总熵的检验途径。

Abstract: Holographic tensor networks are a central route to understanding the mechanism behind the holographic duality, but standard fixed-graph constructions with maximally entangled links face a structural obstacle. Their leading entanglement spectra are flat, so their Rényi entropies do not depend on the replica index. This is in sharp contrast with holographic conformal field theories, where a tensionful cosmic brane makes the refined Rényi entropy $n$-dependent. Capturing this replica dependence is therefore an important test of tensor-network descriptions of holographic entanglement. We construct an explicit family of replica-dependent geodesic graphs that reproduces this behavior while keeping the links maximally entangled. For each replica index $n$, geodesics are emitted from the replicated boundary with the same local, isotropic intersection measure. They perfectly tessellate the selected replica geometry, so the $n$-dependence of their intersection counts comes from the geometry. Tensor-network models on these graphs reproduce the cosmic-brane prescription for refined Rényi entropy by counting crossing threads. Their cross-copy endpoint density also agrees, in CFT$_2$, with a boundary reconstruction within the additive linear combination proposal, providing a check beyond the total entropy. In higher dimensions, we further obtain an analytic large-separation decay law for this density.

</details>


### [169] [Elliptic spin Ruijsenaars--Schneider model via the Poisson reduction of the affine Heisenberg double](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31405)
*Gleb Arutyunov,Lukas Hardi,Davide Polvara*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文通过仿射Heisenberg double（affine Heisenberg double）的Poisson约化（Poisson reduction）构造了含动力学非均匀性的椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars–Schneider模型的哈密顿结构，并在有限自由度实现中与项链箭图（necklace quiver）的椭圆Coulomb分支（elliptic Coulomb branch）结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有自旋Ruijsenaars–Schneider模型的Heisenberg double方法仅覆盖双曲（hyperbolic）情形，需推广至椭圆（elliptic）情形，并为近期从箭图Coulomb分支得到的哈密顿结构提供独立推导。

Method: 将仿射Heisenberg double用仿射振子流形（affine oscillator manifold）扩展后进行Poisson约化；求解动量映射约束（moment map constraint），导出不变Lax算子（invariant Lax operator）与自旋变量的Poisson代数；再通过将不变振子在仿射参数的不同点取值并局域化获得有限自由度实现。

Result: 有限自由度实现要求这些取值点为动力学变量，使其与自旋变量的非平凡Poisson括号抵消导数项，从而保证Poisson括号封闭；所得Poisson括号与哈密顿量与项链箭图椭圆Coulomb分支的结果一致。

Conclusion: 该构造给出椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars–Schneider模型哈密顿结构的独立推导，并建立了Poisson约化与Coulomb分支之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We construct the Hamiltonian structure of the elliptic spin Ruijsenaars--Schneider model with dynamical inhomogeneities by Poisson reduction of the affine Heisenberg double extended by an affine oscillator manifold. This construction extends the Heisenberg double approach to spin Ruijsenaars--Schneider models from the hyperbolic to the elliptic case. We solve the moment map constraint and derive the Poisson algebras of the invariant Lax operator and spin variables. A realization with finitely many degrees of freedom is obtained by evaluating and localizing the invariant oscillators at distinct points of the affine parameter. Consistency requires these points to be dynamical so that their nontrivial Poisson brackets with the spin variables cancel derivative terms that would otherwise obstruct closure of the Poisson bracket on finitely many degrees of freedom. The resulting Poisson brackets and Hamiltonian coincide with those recently obtained from the elliptic Coulomb branch of the necklace quiver. Our construction thus provides an independent derivation of this Hamiltonian structure and establishes a direct link between Poisson reductions and Coulomb branches.

</details>


### [170] [Flat Space Entanglement from Wedge Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31414)
*Robert C. Myers,Bruno Sivilotti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将楔形全息（wedge holography）与受控的 AdS 平坦极限（flat limit）相结合，研究三维平坦空间中的全息纠缠熵（holographic entanglement entropy）；结果显示所得结果虽具有二维 Carrollian CFT 纠缠熵的函数形式，但中心荷（central charge）取 c_M=0、c_L→∞，且极值面并不趋于 Song 等人提出的摆动面（swing surfaces）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验渐近平坦三维引力的候选全息对偶——二维 Carrollian CFT——能否通过 AdS/CFT 的平坦极限正确再现纠缠熵，并澄清极值面与既有摆动面构造之间的关系。

Method: 把由两张 AdS₃ 膜（branes）界定的 AdS₄ 楔形区域取受控平坦极限，得到类似 Randall-Sundrum 的双平坦膜构型；并进一步引入引力 Chern-Simons 项（gravitational Chern-Simons term）以考察其对中心荷的贡献。

Result: 二维角点 CFT（corner CFT）中区间的纠缠熵在极限下变为膜零无穷（null infinity）处区间的纠缠熵，其函数形式与二维 Carrollian CFT 一致；但推断的中心荷为 c_M=0、c_L→∞，加入 Chern-Simons 项只产生类 c_M 贡献，极限下仍为 c_L→∞、c_M→0。相应极值面（extremal surfaces）不趋近摆动面，而是坍缩到沿零无穷的零射线（null rays）。

Conclusion: 作者论证上述两个特征对 AdS/CFT 的平坦极限具有普遍性（generic），其根源在于纠缠熵是依赖域（domain of dependence）的属性，以及真空中区间情形下因果楔（causal wedge）与纠缠楔（entanglement wedge）相等。

Abstract: We study holographic entanglement entropy in three-dimensional flat space by combining wedge holography with a controlled flat limit of AdS end-of-the-world branes. The limit takes a wedge of AdS$_4$ bounded by two AdS$_3$ branes to a Randall-Sundrum-like configuration of two flat branes, and the entanglement entropy of an interval in the two-dimensional corner CFT becomes that of an interval at null infinity of the branes. The result has the functional form of the entanglement entropy of a two-dimensional Carrollian CFT, the proposed holographic dual of gravity in asymptotically flat three-dimensional spacetimes. However, the inferred central charges yield $c_M=0$ and $c_L\to\infty$ in the flat limit. Adding a gravitational Chern-Simons term produces a $c_M$-like contribution, but we still find $c_L\to\infty$ and $c_M\to0$ in this limit. Moreover, the corresponding extremal surfaces do not approach the swing surfaces proposed by Song and collaborators. Instead, they collapse onto null rays along null infinity. We argue that both features are generic for flat limits of AdS/CFT. They follow from entanglement entropy being a property of the domain of dependence and from the equality of causal and entanglement wedges for intervals in the vacuum.

</details>


### [171] [A spin chain as a deformed conformal field theory: an ODE/IM approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31417)
*Sujay K. Ashok,Nicolò Primi,Roberto Tateo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文探讨 ODE/IM 对应（ODE/IM correspondence）能否推广到有限长度 $N$ 的可积自旋链，并在扭曲 XXZ 链的 Lee–Yang 标度区（Lee–Yang scaling sector）中，借助三次振子（cubic oscillator）与广义 $T\bar T$ 型耦合，给出能量与局域守恒荷（local conserved charges）的 $1/N$ 展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODE/IM 对应原本联系连续情形的常微分方程与可积量子场论，本文旨在检验其是否可延伸至有限长度 $N$ 的可积自旋链，从而为有限尺寸修正提供解析描述。

Method: 分析特定各向异性与 twist 下的扭曲 XXZ 链，聚焦 Lee–Yang 标度区；将有限尺寸修正视为由无关算符（irrelevant operators）形变的 Lee–Yang 共形场论（conformal field theory），用 ODE/IM 对应把 Bethe 根分布两边缘各描述为一个处于广义 Gibbs 系综（generalised Gibbs ensemble）中的手征性，并用三次振子以 $1/N$ 级数确定其系数；再将两手征间的耦合识别为广义 $T\bar T$ 型相互作用并自洽求解。

Result: 得到能量与局域守恒荷的无自由参数 $1/N$ 级数展开；化学势（chemical potentials）依赖 $N$，且两手征通过有限尺寸效应相互移动对方化学势；结果在基态与一个激发态上与 Bethe ansatz 验证一致。

Conclusion: ODE/IM 对应可有效推广至有限长度可积自旋链的 Lee–Yang 标度区，有限尺寸效应可被表述为手征系综间的广义 $T\bar T$ 型耦合，并给出与 Bethe ansatz 相符的解析展开。

Abstract: The ODE/IM correspondence relates ordinary differential equations to integrable quantum field theories in the continuum. We ask whether it extends to integrable spin chains of finite length $N$. We analyse the twisted XXZ chain at a special anisotropy and twist, focusing on states in its Lee--Yang scaling sector. Their finite-size corrections are expected to come from the Lee--Yang conformal field theory deformed by irrelevant operators, and with the help of the ODE/IM correspondence we make this deformation explicit for the energy and the local conserved charges. Near each of the two edges of the distribution of the Bethe roots, the chain behaves as one chirality of the Lee--Yang theory in a generalised Gibbs ensemble, whose chemical potentials depend on $N$. The ODE/IM correspondence describes each of these ensembles by a cubic oscillator, whose coefficients we determine as series in $1/N$. The two chiralities are not independent: finite-size effects couple them, so that the charges of each shift the chemical potentials of the other. This turns out to be a generalised $T\bar T$-type interaction. Solving this coupling self-consistently, we obtain the energy and the local conserved charges of the chain as series in $1/N$, with no free parameters. We verify these series against the Bethe ansatz, for the ground state and for an excited state.

</details>


### [172] [Topological Phase Transition of $ F_{α}{}^{γ} F^{αβ} F_{β}{}^{η} \ F^{λμ} R_{γηλμ} $ AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31433)
*Mehdi Sadeghi,Faramarz Rahmani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在扩展相空间中研究含非最小电磁-曲率耦合项的四维带电反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）黑洞，结合常规临界性判据与热力学拓扑（thermodynamic topology）分析其临界行为与平衡态全局结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非最小电磁-曲率相互作用可能修正黑洞解及其热力学性质，而仅依赖霍金温度（Hawking temperature）的常规临界性判据只能给出局域信息，需引入热力学拓扑以刻画平衡态构型的全局结构。

Method: 将耦合视为一阶微扰，构造修正黑洞解并在扩展相空间（extended phase space）中导出热力学量；分别在正则系综（canonical ensemble）与巨正则系综（grand canonical ensemble）下用临界性条件考察常规临界行为，再用热力学拓扑计算绕数（winding number, W）。

Result: 正则系综下出现两个区域：非临界区 W=+1，临界区 W=0。巨正则系综下按电势分为三个区域：低电势与中电势区均为 W=+1，但常规临界点仅出现在中电势区；高电势区 W=0 且无常规临界行为。

Conclusion: 热力学拓扑提供了对平衡态构型的全局刻画，可补充基于温度的常规临界性分析；单独的全局绕数不能唯一决定常规临界点是否存在。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic criticality and topological properties of four-dimensional charged anti-de Sitter black holes in the presence of a nonminimal electromagnetic-curvature interaction of the form \(F_α{}^γF^{αβ}F_β{}^ηF^{λμ}R_{γηλμ}\). Treating the coupling perturbatively to first order, we construct the corrected black hole solutions and derive their thermodynamic quantities in the extended phase space. Conventional critical behavior is examined through the criticality conditions for the Hawking temperature in both canonical and grand canonical ensembles. We then use thermodynamic topology to investigate the global structure of equilibrium configurations and its dependence on the thermodynamic parameters. In the canonical ensemble, two distinct regimes emerge: a noncritical region characterized by a total winding number \(W=+1\), and a critical region with \(W=0\). In the grand canonical ensemble, three distinct regimes are identified according to the electric potential. Interestingly, the low- and intermediate-potential regions share the same topological number, \(W=+1\), although conventional critical points occur only in the intermediate-potential region. The high-potential region, by contrast, is characterized by \(W=0\) and exhibits no conventional critical behavior. Our findings show that thermodynamic topology provides a global characterization of equilibrium configurations that complements conventional temperature-based criticality analysis, and that the global winding number alone does not uniquely determine the existence of conventional critical points.

</details>


### [173] [The Relaxation of Quantum Near-Extremal Black Holes. (I) Microcanonical States, Pruning of Quasinormal Modes, and the Aretakis Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31459)
*Roberto Emparan,Sameer Murthy,Stefano Trezzi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文解决了近极端黑洞（near-extremal black hole）晚期弛豫中经典准正规模（quasinormal modes）无穷塔与黑洞能谱矛盾的难题：引入当能量低于某标度 $E_b$ 时变得显著的量子涨落，导致每个经典模式分裂为实部由 $E_b$ 设定的一对极点，从而将模式塔截断为有限个模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图像下近极端黑洞晚期弛豫由无穷多个纯阻尼准正规模描述，但高阶泛音（overtones）的衰减率远大于黑洞高于极端性的能量 $E$，这要求存在低于极端质量的黑洞态，构成一个疑难（puzzle）。同时极端黑洞的 Aretakis 不稳定性也源自经典喉区（throat）的标度不变性，需要理解量子效应如何修正它。

Method: 计算固定能量黑洞中具有共形维数（conformal dimension）$Δ$ 的探针的推迟格林函数（retarded Green's function），纳入当 $E$ 低于标度 $E_b$ 时变大的量子涨落，分析各经典模式分裂为实部由 $E_b$ 决定的一对极点，并依据黑洞可及能谱判断哪些极点构成共振（resonance）。

Result: 每个经典模式分裂为实部由 $E_b$ 设定的一对极点；受扰黑洞作为不稳定量子系统衰减，仅当极点能量处于可及能谱内才成为准正规模。模式塔被截断为有限个模式，衰减均快于 $Δ^2E_b$，且当 $E=Δ^2E_b/2$ 以下无模式剩余。对一般 $Δ$，响应先按 $t^{-2Δ}$ 衰减，最终按 $t^{-3/2}$ 衰减；若 $E\ggΔ^2E_b$ 则中间存在指数衰减阶段。Aretakis 增长作为喉区标度不变性的体现，在 $t\sim1/E_b$ 时终止，届时视界导数仅增长到有限值。

Conclusion: Aretakis 不稳定性的无界增长是先在晚期极限之前取半经典极限（semiclassical limit）所造成的假象，这两个极限在量子力学中不可交换。量子涨落破坏了经典喉区的标度不变性，使模式塔有限化并使视界导数增长趋于有限。

Abstract: The late-time relaxation of a near-extremal black hole is classically governed by an infinite tower of purely damped quasinormal modes. This poses a puzzle: the higher overtones decay at rates much larger than the energy $E$ of the black hole above extremality, so they would need black hole states below the extremal mass. We resolve it by including the quantum fluctuations that become large when $E$ falls below a scale $E_b$. We compute the retarded Green's function of a probe of conformal dimension $Δ$ in a black hole of fixed energy, and find that each classical mode splits into a pair of poles with real parts set by $E_b$. The perturbed black hole decays as an unstable quantum system, and a pair is a resonance, i.e., a quasinormal mode, only if its energy lies within the accessible black hole spectrum. This prunes the tower to finitely many modes, all damped faster than $Δ^2E_b$, and below $E=Δ^2E_b/2$ none is left. For generic $Δ$, the response of the black hole decays first as $t^{-2Δ}$ and finally as $t^{-3/2}$, with an exponential stage in between if $E\ggΔ^2E_b$.
  The same quantum effects bear on the Aretakis instability of extremal black holes, in which transverse derivatives of a perturbation grow without bound on the horizon. Like the tower of modes, it originates in the scale invariance of the classical throat, which the quantum fluctuations break. The $t^{-2Δ}$ decay, which is the boundary counterpart of the Aretakis growth, ends by $t\sim1/E_b$, when the throat ceases to be classical, and by then horizon derivatives have grown only to a finite value. Their unbounded growth is an artifact of taking the semiclassical limit before the late-time one, two limits that in quantum mechanics do not commute.

</details>


### [174] [Exact Scalar and Fermionic Propagators in a Uniform Electric Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31528)
*Emerson B. S. Corrêa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过将 Ritus 本征函数技术与 Schwinger 固有时形式结合，在四维欧氏空间中构造了恒定均匀外电场下带电标量与费米子场的精确传播子，并保持四维动量表示，最终重新导出 Schwinger 的费米子对产生率经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Ritus 方法（Ritus method）在处理费米子时依赖坐标限制，且 Schwinger 方法（Schwinger's proper-time formalism）的完整推导较为繁琐；本文旨在建立一种混合分析框架，避免上述限制并获得更简洁的精确传播子（propagator）表达。

Method: 构建结合 Ritus 本征函数技术（Ritus eigenfunction technique）与 Schwinger 固有时形式（Schwinger's proper-time formalism）的分析性混合框架；在谱求和（spectral summation）后保留四维动量表示（four-dimensional momentum representation），并通过适当变量替换分离 Laguerre 多项式求和后出现的平移非不变分量，得到平移不变核。

Result: 成功得到恒定均匀外电场下带电标量场与费米子场的精确传播子，其保持四维动量表示且背景场强度不变；作为一致性检验，重新导出了 Schwinger 关于费米子对产生率（pair production rate）的经典表达式。

Conclusion: 该混合框架将 Ritus 基结构与固有时方法的算符表述相联系，无需传统完整推导与坐标限制，为外电场中量子场传播子的精确求解提供了可行途径，并由对产生率结果验证了其正确性。

Abstract: By implementing an analytical hybrid framework that combines the Ritus eigenfunction technique with Schwinger's proper-time formalism, we construct exact propagators for charged scalar and fermionic fields in a constant, homogeneous, external electric field in four-dimensional Euclidean space. A central advantage of this approach is that the resulting propagators retain a four-dimensional momentum representation after the spectral summation, connecting the structure of the Ritus basis with the operator formulation of the proper-time method, without requiring the full traditional derivation of Schwinger's approach and the coordinate restrictions originally associated with the Ritus method for fermions. The translationally non-invariant component emerging after the Laguerre-polynomial summation is isolated through a suitable change of variables, yielding translationally invariant kernels and maintaining the background field strength unchanged. As a consistency check, we successfully rederive Schwinger's seminal expression for the fermionic pair production rate.

</details>


### [175] [Radiation from periods: Bremsstrahlung functions, wound loops, latitudes and integral constraints in ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31555)
*Michelangelo Preti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: ABJM 理论中 1/2BPS 与 1/6BPS Wilson 线的 Bremsstrahlung 函数可由局域化矩阵模型的谱曲线统一、闭环地表出；其中费米型函数为第二类周期（单个超几何函数），玻色型函数为周期之比且恒较小，关键新观测量"缠绕速度"等于 't Hooft 耦合减去四倍费米型函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1/2BPS 与 1/6BPS Wilson 线的 Bremsstrahlung 函数刻画缺陷对微小形变的响应，但缺乏统一、闭环的解析描述；本文意在寻找能同时涵盖二者的几何结构。

Method: 以局域化矩阵模型（localisation matrix model）的谱曲线（spectral curve）作为单一几何对象，通过其对数周期（logarithmic periods）与带两个电荷的周期（periods with two charges）提取各量，并引入新观测量"缠绕速度"（winding velocity）。

Result: 谱曲线以闭环形式同时给出 1/2BPS 与 1/6BPS 的 Bremsstrahlung 函数，以及任意复缠绕数与 framing 下的平面缠绕 Wilson 环与 latitude Wilson 环；费米型函数为满足二阶微分方程、具有准模性（quasimodular）的第二类周期（单个超几何函数），玻色型函数为周期之比，且在任意耦合下均较小。

Conclusion: 辐射沿缺陷流（defect flow）从玻色线向费米线增长；缠绕速度同时是缠绕 Wilson 环的缠绕导数与 latitude 的曲率，并确定 1/2BPS 线上倾斜算符（tilt operators）的一个新积分四点函数，从而成为缺陷共形场理论的数据；所有结果在规范群秩不等时可推广至 ABJ 理论。

Abstract: The Bremsstrahlung functions of the 1/2BPS and 1/6BPS Wilson lines of ABJM theory measure their response to small deformations. We show that a single geometric object, the spectral curve of the localisation matrix model, captures both of them in closed form, together with the planar wound and latitude Wilson loops at any complex winding and framing. The fermionic function turns out to be a period of the second kind: a single hypergeometric function, which solves a second-order differential equation and is quasimodular. The bosonic one is not a period but a ratio of periods. At every coupling it is the smaller of the two, so radiation grows along the defect flow from the bosonic to the fermionic line. The key role is played by a new observable, the winding velocity, which turns out to be the 't Hooft coupling shifted by four times the fermionic function. It is at the same time the winding derivative of the wound loop and the curvature of the latitude, and the curve captures it in both incarnations through its logarithmic periods and through its periods with two charges. It is also a datum of the defect conformal field theory on the 1/2BPS line, since it fixes a new integrated four-point function of the tilt operators. All results extend to ABJ theory when the ranks of the gauge groups are not equal.

</details>


### [176] [New relations of tree-level string amplitudes from the massless prescription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31580)
*Chen Huang,Yi-Xiao Tao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在树图层次（tree level）揭示了场论振幅（field-theory amplitudes）的弦修正（string corrections）与弦振幅（string amplitudes）之间的新关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注场论振幅的弦修正与低阶弦振幅之间是否存在联系，并尝试从无质量顶点算符（massless vertex operators）的指定方式出发寻找此类关系。

Method: 基于顶点算符的无质量指定（massless prescription of vertex operators）进行推导，比较 $(m+1)$ 点场论振幅的弦修正与 $m$ 点弦振幅在树图（tree level）下的对应关系。

Result: 发现 $(m+1)$ 点场论振幅的弦修正与 $m$ 点树图弦振幅之间存在新的关系式（relations）。

Conclusion: 无质量顶点算符指定可作为连接场论振幅弦修正与低阶弦振幅的有效出发点，为两类振幅间的结构对应提供了新证据。

Abstract: We discover new relations between the string corrections to $(m+1)$-point field-theory amplitudes and $m$-point string amplitudes at tree level from the massless prescription of vertex operators.

</details>


### [177] [All the Positive Energy Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31587)
*Lorenzo Ricci,Marc Riembau,Minho Son*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一个系统性框架，用于获得任意自旋源（sources of arbitrary spin）多点能量关联函数（multi-point energy correlators）的完整正性界（positivity bounds），并以正算符族与线性规划相结合给出外边界与内边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多点能量关联函数在高能碰撞中可观测，但对其允许取值空间的约束尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其缺少对任意自旋源与高阶关联函数的完整正性界。

Method: 约束被组织为一族正算符（positive operators）$\mathscr{E}^{(N,k)}$（$k=0,\ldots,\lfloor N/2\rfloor$）；外边界（outer bounds）通过排除违反这些算符正性的关联函数得到，内边界（inner bounds）则通过线性规划（linear programming）构造自洽对撞机事件集合（ensembles）得到，两者随数值精度提高而收敛。

Result: 该方法可在低点关联函数确定后刻画允许的高点关联函数；示例包括由 LEP 的单点与两点关联函数测量推导 QCD 中三点能量关联函数（three-point energy correlator）的多极矩（multipoles）界。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一套系统、可收敛的正性界方法，将低点与高点能量关联函数联系起来，并在 QCD 现象学中具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a framework to systematically obtain the full set of positivity bounds on multi-point energy correlators for sources of arbitrary spin. The constraints are organized in a family of positive operators $\mathscr{E}^{(N,k)}$, with $k=0,\ldots,\lfloor N/2\rfloor$. Our approach provides outer bounds, which exclude correlators that violate the positivity of $\mathscr{E}^{(N,k)}$, and inner bounds, obtained by constructing ensembles of consistent collider events via linear programming. Both methods converge to each other as numerical precision increases. This approach, in particular, characterizes the allowed higher-point correlators once lower-point correlators are determined. We illustrate this through several examples, including bounds on multipoles of the three-point energy correlator in QCD derived from measurements of one- and two-point correlators at LEP.

</details>


### [178] [The Eigenfunction Method to Calculate the Klein--Gordon Propa-gator in an Inhomogeneous Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31630)
*Emerson B. S. Corrê,Mateus G. Lima,Suellen. C. Q. Arruda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文采用本征函数法（Eigenfunction method，即 Ritus 方法）在四维欧氏空间（four-dimensional Euclidean space）中计算了带指数衰减因子（exponential damping factor）\(b\) 的外磁场下克莱因–戈尔登传播子（Klein–Gordon propagator），并将其应用于指数衰减磁场下相互作用玻色子系统的质量参数（mass parameter）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在处理指数衰减型外磁场中标量场的传播子计算问题，并考察该类背景场对相互作用玻色子系统质量参数的影响。

Method: 采用本征函数法（Ritus 方法），将标量场传播子用平面波（plane waves）与拉盖尔多项式（Laguerre polynomials）展开表示，并分析相应微分算符的本征值。

Result: 微分算符的本征值在 \(\mathrm{xy}\) 平面上通过朗道能级（Landau levels）体现出量子化特征；所得传播子被进一步用于计算指数衰减磁场下相互作用玻色子系统的质量参数。

Conclusion: 本征函数法可有效给出指数衰减磁场中标量传播子的显式表达，且该结果可用于研究背景磁场对相互作用玻色子系统质量参数的修正。

Abstract: In this paper, we use the Eigenfunction method (Ritus' method) to calculate the Klein--Gordon propagator under an external magnetic field with an exponential damping factor $b$, in a four-dimensional Euclidean space. We write the propagator of scalar field in terms of plane waves and Laguerre polynomials. Also the eigenvalues associated to differential operator show the quantization in the $\mathrm{xy}$-plane through the Landau levels. We apply the calculated propagator to the mass parameter of an interacting boson system under an exponentially decaying magnetic field.

</details>


### [179] [The Matrix in Pieces: Complete BFSS Scattering at Finite Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31649)
*Mai Qi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究 B F S S 矩阵量子力学（matrix quantum mechanics）中矩阵块分离时形成的复合粒子散射通道，证明了在固定有限秩 $N\ge 2$ 下物理相对哈密顿量的粒子通道渐近完备，波算子（wave operators）值域为绝对连续子空间，散射算子（scattering operator）在计入内部束缚态时酉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个矩阵块分离时，连接它们的模式出现增长的激发间隙（excitation gap），而超对称（supersymmetry）抵消了主导真空能，使处于内部束缚态的块可作为复合粒子散射。作者希望严格论证这些粒子通道在散射意义下的完备性。

Method: 证明需同时控制内部平坦方向与块间相对运动两个尺度：以矩阵半径的逆幂控制内部平坦方向上的导数，用增长的间隙压制块间激发；再借助传播估计（propagation estimates），按块秩（block rank）归纳处理逐次分裂（successive breakups）。

Result: 对每个固定有限秩 $N\ge 2$，粒子通道对物理相对哈密顿量渐近完备（asymptotically complete）；入、出波算子的值域均等于绝对连续子空间（absolutely continuous subspace），奇异连续子空间（singular-continuous subspace）为零，故散射算子在每个组分可占据任意内部束缚态时酉。

Conclusion: 对零能组分的归一化入射态，未进入零能通道的出射概率，等于产生至少一个具有正内部能量组分的包容概率（inclusive probability）；即散射的缺失概率被正能激发过程完全解释。

Abstract: As two matrix blocks separate in Banks-Fischler-Shenker-Susskind matrix quantum mechanics, the modes connecting them develop a growing excitation gap. Supersymmetry cancels their leading vacuum energy, so blocks in internal bound states can scatter as composite particles. We prove that these particle channels are asymptotically complete for the physical relative Hamiltonian at each fixed finite rank $N\ge 2$. Because a block can itself break up as its center moves away from the others, the proof must control motion on both scales. Inverse powers of the matrix radius control derivatives along internal flat directions, while the growing gap suppresses excitations between blocks. Propagation estimates then allow us to resolve successive breakups by induction on the block rank. The incoming and outgoing wave operators each have range equal to the absolutely continuous subspace, and the singular-continuous subspace vanishes. The scattering operator is thus unitary when each constituent can occupy any of its internal bound states. For a normalized incoming state of zero-energy constituents, the outgoing probability missing from the zero-energy channels equals the inclusive probability of producing at least one constituent with positive internal energy.

</details>


### [180] [Second-order localization and intersecting O6-planes in scale-separated AdS vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31660)
*Aravind Aikot,Timm Wrase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文构造了在 \(T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2)\) 上尺度分离 AdS\(_4\) 真空的二阶周期局域化修正，并验证其满足完整场方程与超对称条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶修正中不同 O6-平面族（O6-plane families）的贡献只是简单相加，而相交族之间的相互作用要进入二阶才出现，可能阻碍解的延续（obstruction），因此需要显式构造二阶修正以检验该解是否自洽。

Method: 在带质量的 IIA 型超引力（massive type IIA supergravity）框架下，显式求解二阶的翘曲因子（warp factor）、膨胀子（dilaton）、完整内空间度规（internal metric）及所需形式场（form fields），并采用对奇异源项（singular source）的通用正则化（regularization）检验场方程与紧致空间解条件；用纯旋量形式（pure-spinor formalism）核对超对称条件。

Result: 给出了包含相交族相互作用项的显式二阶解，二阶度规出现非对角分量（off-diagonal components）；在共同正则化下验证了全部场方程与紧致性条件，且二阶场满足在保留阶数内可及的超对称条件。

Conclusion: 二阶局域化修正不阻碍尺度分离 AdS\(_4\) 真空的延续，该解在保留阶数内自洽并保持超对称。

Abstract: We construct explicit periodic second-order localization corrections to scale-separated AdS$_4$ vacua of massive type IIA supergravity on $T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2)$. At first order, the corrections from different O6-plane families simply add. The interactions between intersecting families first enter at second order and could obstruct the continuation of the solution. We find explicit solutions for the warp factor, dilaton, full internal metric and required form fields, including the terms generated by these interactions. The metric at second order contains off-diagonal components. With a common regularization of the singular source terms, we verify the full field equations and the conditions required for a solution on a compact space. Using the pure-spinor formalism, we find that the second-order fields satisfy the supersymmetry conditions accessible at the retained orders.

</details>


### [181] [$AdS$ Leading Regge Trajectory from String Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31678)
*Minjae Cho,João Gomide,Jaroslav Scheinpflug,Xi Yin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在闭弦超弦场论（superstring field theory）框架下，通过求解线性化运动方程，计算了纯 Ramond-Ramond（RR）通量背景下若干超弦背景中领头 Regge 轨迹（leading Regge trajectory）的 AdS 质量，并给出至 RR 通量二阶的结果；其中 AdS₅ 与 AdS₄ 情形与对偶规范理论的可积性（integrability）结果完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有全息对偶（holographic duality）检验多依赖超对称保护量（supersymmetry-protected quantities），缺乏对非保护量的独立验证，因此需要构造可由两侧独立计算且可直接比较的可观测量。

Method: 以背景 RR 通量（RR flux）为展开参数，在闭超弦场论中求解线性化运动方程，将微扰展开推进至二阶，从而确定 AdS₅×S⁵、AdS₄×CP³ 以及 AdS₃×S³×M₄（M₄ = S³×S¹ 与 T⁴）中领头 Regge 轨迹的 AdS 质量。

Result: AdS₅ 与 AdS₄ 情形的结果与对偶规范理论可积性方法所得结果完全一致；AdS₃ 情形（含 M₄ = S³×S¹ 与 T⁴ 两种内部流形）亦给出相应二阶质量结果。

Conclusion: 该一致性构成对超出超对称保护范围的全息对偶的非平凡检验（nontrivial check），表明弦场论微扰计算可作为检验 AdS/CFT 对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）的有效途径。

Abstract: We determine the $AdS$ mass of the leading Regge trajectory in superstring backgrounds $AdS_5\times S^5$, $AdS_4\times\mathbb{CP}^3$, and $AdS_3\times S^3\times{\cal M}_4$ with pure Ramond-Ramond (RR) flux for both ${\cal M}_4=S^3\times S^1$ and ${\cal M}_4=T^4$, to the second order in the expansion with respect to the background RR flux by solving the linearized equations of motion in closed superstring field theory. The $AdS_5$ and $AdS_4$ results are in a complete agreement with those obtained from the integrability approach to dual gauge theories, thereby providing a nontrivial check of the holographic duality beyond supersymmetry-protected quantities.

</details>


### [182] [BPS baryonic tubes in chiral perturbation theory minimally coupled with electrodynamics and a novel crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31719)
*Fabrizio Canfora,Martín Garrido,Pablo Pais*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在手征微扰理论（chiral perturbation theory, CPT）中构造了一类新的（3+1）维一阶 BPS 系统，其解自动满足二阶场方程，描述沿管身携带手征电流的重子管（baryonic tubes）。与已有解析解不同，这些 BPS 解的能密度峰值位置可任意分布；比较表明，低重子数密度时旧解能量更优，高密度时新 BPS 管更受青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中重子管的解析描述具有能密度峰值位置固定的局限，作者希望构造一类更一般、且能自动满足场方程的重子管解，并厘清不同重子数密度下各类重子管构型的能量优劣。

Method: 在（3+1）维手征微扰理论中推导新的一阶 BPS 系统（其解自动为二阶场方程的解），并分析 CPT 领头项，即最小耦合到电动力学的 SU(2) 非线性 sigma 模型（non-linear sigma model）；所得重子管可沿 z 轴均匀或调制，电磁场及其对重子管的反作用（back-reaction）可解析处理。

Result: 获得一类精确解，其能密度峰值位置任意（与已有解中峰值位置固定形成定性区别）；决定能量优劣的关键参数为重子数密度（baryonic charge density）：低密度下旧解更有利，高密度下新 BPS 管更有利；并讨论了低、高重子密度两个区域中电磁场分布的差异。

Conclusion: BPS 性质赋予重子管能密度峰值位置的自由度，从而在高重子数密度区间提供能量更优的构型；电磁场及其反作用可在该框架内解析处理，手征场势能项（potential terms）的分析见附录 B。

Abstract: We derive a novel first-order BPS system (whose solutions are automatically solutions of the second-order field equations) in chiral perturbation theory (CPT) in $(3+1)$ dimensions. These BPS configurations describe baryonic tubes carrying a chiral current along the tubes themselves. Such baryonic tubes can be either homogeneous or modulated along the $z$-axis. We analyze the leading term in CPT, which is the $SU(2)$ non-linear sigma model minimally coupled to electrodynamics. These exact solutions are qualitatively different from the available analytic description of baryonic tubes since, due to their BPS nature, the positions of the energy-density peaks can be arbitrary. In contrast (in the case of the known solutions in the literature), the energy-density peaks' positions are fixed. We compare these two types of baryonic configurations; the relevant parameter that determines which one is energetically favored is the baryonic charge density: for low densities, the old solutions are favorable, while for high densities these novel BPS tubes are energetically favored. The inclusion of the electromagnetic field (together with the corresponding back-reaction on the baryonic tubes) can also be treated analytically. We discuss the differences in the electromagnetic field distribution between the two regions (low and high baryonic density). The analysis of potential terms for the chiral field is detailed in Appendix B.

</details>


### [183] [Hadron structure in $1+1$ dimensions from the infinite Hamiltonian lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31750)
*Ross Dempsey*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出在无穷空间格点上计算（1+1）维规范理论中强子矩阵元的框架，并应用于有质量 Schwinger 模型（massive Schwinger model），在 θ=0 处用 DLCQ 校验，进而研究 θ≠0 时强子的部分子分布函数与引力形状因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子内部结构由确定动量强子态之间的算符矩阵元刻画，但 θ≠0 情形下现有方法（如 DLCQ）不适用，需要新的计算框架。

Method: 在无穷空间格点（infinite spatial lattice）上构建计算（1+1）维规范理论矩阵元的框架，并以有质量 Schwinger 模型为对象，用离散光锥量子化（discretized lightcone quantization, DLCQ）在 θ=0 处进行基准对照。

Result: 计算得到部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions）与引力形状因子（gravitational form factors）：当 m 大于临界质量 m_c 且从下方趋近 θ=π 时，二者均显示强子变为大尺度、松散束缚态的特征；当 θ=π 并从下方趋近 m=m_c 的二维 Ising 临界点（2D Ising critical point）时，引力形状因子由自由玻色子（free boson）转变为自由 Majorana 费米子（free Majorana fermion）的形式。

Conclusion: 该格点框架可推广至 DLCQ 不适用的 θ≠0 区域，并揭示 θ 与质量参数驱动的强子结构相变特征。

Abstract: The internal structure of a hadron is encoded in matrix elements of operators between hadron states of definite momentum. In this paper, we develop a framework for computing such matrix elements in $(1+1)$-dimensional gauge theories by working on an infinite spatial lattice. We apply the method to the massive Schwinger model. We benchmark our method against discretized lightcone quantization (DLCQ) at $θ= 0$, and then use the lattice to study hadrons at $θ\neq 0$, where DLCQ does not apply. We compute parton distribution functions and gravitational form factors, and both show signatures of hadrons becoming large, loosely-bound states when we set $m$ greater than the critical mass $m_c$ and approach $θ= π$ from below. Moreover, we see the gravitational form factor change from that of a free boson to that of a free Majorana fermion when we set $θ= π$ and approach the 2D Ising critical point at $m=m_c$ from below.

</details>


### [184] [A cubic theory of gravity with quasitopological-like properties for compactified cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31758)
*Vicente Aguilar,Fabrizio Canfora,Vicente García,Alex Giacomini,Julio Oliva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 $D=7$ 的三次曲率不变量组合中加入爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量，经三维常曲率内空间紧化后可在四维给出健康的宇宙学演化；该三次理论被唯一确定，并表现出准一阶约束、线性稳定性与奇点推迟等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是寻找能在紧化后产生健康四维宇宙学演化的高维高阶曲率修正，并考察其场方程阶数、稳定性及对广义相对论（general relativity, GR）中奇点问题的改善。

Method: 方法为构造 $D=7$ 的三次曲率不变量组合并加入爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量（Einstein-Hilbert action），在三维常曲率内空间上紧化；分析四维空间平坦、均匀各向同性宇宙与半径 $b(t)$ 内空间的 warped product 上的场方程阶数，并做线性扰动稳定性分析与非线性数值演化。

Result: 对一般背景场方程为四阶，但在该 warped product 上标度因子 $a(t)$ 与 $b(t)$ 的约束为一阶、其余方程为二阶，且该性质唯一筛选出三次理论；静态 de Sitter 解的稳定性与内空间曲率符号共同确定高阶曲率耦合的符号，依赖空间坐标的线性标量扰动方程至多含两个时间导数。

Conclusion: 数值演化表明，在正裸宇宙学常数 $Λ_0$ 与双曲紧致空间情形下，增大高阶曲率耦合可将 GR 在该拟设下出现的奇点形成推至未来。

Abstract: We identify a cubic combination of curvature invariants in dimension $D=7$ that when added to the Einstein-Hilbert action, yields a healthy cosmological evolution in four dimensions after compactification on a constant-curvature internal space of dimension three. For generic backgrounds it leads to fourth-order field equations, nevertheless, on a warped product of a spatially flat, homogeneous and isotropic four-dimensional universe with a constant-curvature space of radius $b(t)$, the constraint is of first order in the scale factor $a(t)$ and in $b(t)$, while the remaining equations are of second order. Requiring this property uniquely singles out the cubic theory. We study the solutions describing a four-dimensional de Sitter Universe in direct product with an internal space of stabilized radius and evaluate its linear stability under time-dependent perturbations, showing that for a given sign of the curvature of the internal space, stability of the static solution fixes the sign of the higher curvature coupling. We also argue that linearized scalar perturbations depending on the spatial coordinates obey equations with at most two time derivatives. Finally, we numerically evolve the homogeneous and isotropic system non-linearly and present evidence that for a positive bare cosmological term $Λ_0$ and a hyperbolic compact space, the singularity formation present in GR in this ansatz is pushed into the future by increasing the higher-curvature coupling.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond the Thermodynamic-Volume Limit in Holographic Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31761)
*Di Wu,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大黑洞极限下研究全息复杂度（holographic complexity）的热力学体积标度律（thermodynamic-volume scaling law）的有限尺寸修正，针对奇数维等自旋 Myers-Perry-AdS 黑洞的形成复杂度（complexity of formation，CV 猜想）给出解析描述，并识别出一个使主导有限尺寸修正消失的临界视界比（Critical Horizon Ratio, CHR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大黑洞区域中全息复杂度服从热力学体积标度律，但趋向该极限的有限尺寸行为尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白并考察有限尺寸效应能否成为探测全息结构的新手段。

Method: 在 complexity=volume（CV）方案下，对奇数维等自旋 Myers-Perry-AdS 黑洞的形成复杂度做解析计算，导出有限尺寸修正的领头项，并解析确定临界视界比（CHR）及其维度依赖。

Result: 领头有限尺寸修正保留了对状态的依赖，而该依赖在极限的热力学体积律中并不存在；该修正在 CHR 处变号，此处一般性领头有限尺寸贡献消失、收敛到体积律相应加速。CHR 的维度依赖被解析给出，大维极限下趋于一个有限非极端值，并带有可计算的首个有限维修正。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸趋近过程可提供探测全息结构的新手段，值得在不同全息复杂度实现中进一步研究，并最终在对偶场论（dual field theory）中加以刻画。

Abstract: In the large black hole regime, holographic complexity obeys a thermodynamic-volume scaling law, while the finite-size approach to this limit remains unresolved. In this Letter, we develop an analytic description of finite-size corrections to this scaling for complexity of formation in the complexity=volume (CV) proposal of odd-dimensional equal-spinning Myers-Perry-AdS black holes. We find that the leading finite-size correction retains state dependence that is absent from the limiting thermodynamic-volume law. This correction changes sign at a distinguished Critical Horizon Ratio (CHR), where the generic leading finite-size contribution vanishes and convergence to the thermodynamic-volume law is correspondingly accelerated. We further determine the dimension dependence of the CHR analytically, showing that it approaches a finite nonextremal value at large dimension with a calculable leading finite-dimensional correction. These results show that the finite-size approach can provide a new probe of holographic structure, motivating its exploration across different realizations of holographic complexity and its eventual characterization in the dual field theory.

</details>


### [186] [NS5-branes and the black hole/string transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31945)
*Asad Fayiz,Emil J. Martinec*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在弦论（string theory）中BTZ黑洞形成阈值附近，将6维BPS超引力解（BPS supergravity solutions）的构造推广至包含五膜（fivebrane）的规范激发，并研究D弦孤子（D-string soliton）的能量学与"小弦"（little string）退禁闭相变的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BTZ黑洞形成起点附近存在由略微分离的基本弦（fundamental strings）与Neveu-Schwarz五膜（NS5-branes）作为源的6维BPS超引力解大系综；为更完整刻画该区域物理，需纳入五膜的规范自由度，并理解向黑洞相转变时退禁闭的非阿贝尔自由度（non-abelian degrees of freedom）。

Method: 将既有几何构造加以推广，加入五膜的规范激发（gauge excitations），在此基础上分析D弦孤子的能量学（energetics），并与小弦的退禁闭相变（deconfinement transition）相比较。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果；所述工作建立了包含规范激发的扩展几何框架，并将D弦孤子的能量行为与小弦退禁闭相变联系起来。

Conclusion: 五膜规范激发可被纳入BTZ黑洞形成前沿的6维BPS超引力解构造；小弦被视为向黑洞相转变中退禁闭的自然非阿贝尔自由度，D弦孤子能量学为该相变提供了研究途径。

Abstract: Near to the onset of BTZ black hole formation in string theory is a large ensemble of 6d BPS supergravity solutions sourced by slightly separated fundamental strings and Neveu-Schwarz fivebranes. We extend the construction of these geometries to include gauge excitations of the fivebranes, and then study the energetics of D-string solitons in relation to the deconfinement transition of little strings, which are the natural non-abelian degrees of freedom that deconfine in the transition to the black hole phase.

</details>


### [187] [M5-branes wrapped on topological disks from maximal 7D gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31947)
*Patharadanai Nuchino,Parinya Karndumri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在七维最大规范超引力（maximal gauged supergravity）中，针对规范群 $CSO(p,q,5-p-q)$，构造了 $AdS_5 \times \Sigma$（$\Sigma$ 为拓扑圆盘）的新型超对称解，并给出其全息解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从十一维超引力在 $H^{p,q}\circ T^{5-p-q}$ 上的一致截断（consistent truncation）出发，探索七维规范超引力中带边界的 $AdS_5$ 解，以刻画四维 SCFT 及其中的余维二缺陷（codimension-2 defect）。

Method: 构造并分析具有 $SO(2)\times SO(2)$、$SO(2)\times SO(3)$ 和 $SO(2)_{\textrm{diag}}$ 对称性的解（来自 $SO(5)$、$SO(3,2)$、$SO(4,1)$ 规范群）；对 $S^4$ 截断已知的情形，将解提升（uplift）至十一维并计算对偶四维 $N=1$ SCFT 的全息中心荷（holographic central charge）。

Result: $SO(2)\times SO(3)$ 对称解保留 16 个超荷（supercharge），其余解仅保留 8 个；部分解对偶于中心荷有限的四维 $N=1$ SCFT，多数解则描述六维 $N=(2,0)$ 理论中的余维二缺陷。

Conclusion: 所构造的解族为四维 $N=1$ SCFT 与六维 $N=(2,0)$ 理论中的缺陷提供了统一的全息描述，并给出 $SO(5)$ 规范群下相应解的全息中心荷。

Abstract: We study new supersymmetric $AdS_5 \times Σ$ solutions for $Σ$ being a topological disk from the maximal gauged supergravity in seven dimensions with $CSO(p,q,5-p-q)$ gauge groups. These gauged supergravities can be obtained from consistent truncations of eleven-dimensional supergravity on $H^{p,q} \circ T^{5-p-q}$. We find a number of solutions with $SO(2)\times SO(2)$ and $SO(2)\times SO(3)$ symmetries from $SO(5)$ and $SO(3,2)$ gauge groups as well as $SO(2)_{\textrm{diag}}$ symmetric solutions from $SO(5)$ and $SO(4,1)$ gauge groups. Solutions with $SO(2)\times SO(3)$ symmetry preserve $16$ supercharges while the other solutions preserve only $8$ supercharges. Although some of the solutions give a holographic description of the dual $N=1$ SCFTs in four dimensions with finite central charges, most of the solutions describe codimension-2 defects within $N=(2,0)$ theories in six dimensions. For solutions with $SO(2)\times SO(2)$ and $SO(2)_{\textrm{diag}}$ symmetries from $SO(5)$ gauge group for which a complete truncation ansatz of eleven-dimensional supergravity on $S^4$ is known, we also uplift the solutions to eleven dimensions and compute the holographic central charge of the dual four-dimensional $N=1$ SCFT with $SO(2)_{\textrm{diag}}$ symmetry.

</details>


### [188] [Entanglement structure in conformal Y-junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32062)
*Yan Liu,You-Jie Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文以全息（holographic）与自由场模型研究共形 Y 型结（conformal Y-junctions）中纠缠对能量传输的限制，提出由互信息（mutual information）定义的成对有效中心荷（pairwise effective central charge），并发现需要引入三体信息（tripartite information）或 L-熵（L-entropy）修正才能恢复能量传输上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面纠缠系数（interface entanglement coefficient）这一概念此前主要用于两区域情形，其在多分支共形结中对能量传输的约束能力尚不清楚，本文旨在检验成对纠缠量能否给出能量传输（energy transmission）的上界。

Method: 从互信息出发定义成对有效中心荷；对全息结构造分支 RT 处方（branched RT prescription）并检验互信息的单调性（monogamy of mutual information）；同时用自由玻色子（free boson）与 Dirac 费米子结进行对照，并结合解析极限与数值计算检验各类修正假设。

Result: 成对有效中心荷并不总能界定相应的能量传输；全息情形下加入含有限三体信息的修正可恢复该上界，且系数由极限饱和确定。自由玻色子与 Dirac 费米子结同样违反成对界限，但三体修正失效，因三体信息分别为正或为零；解析与数值结果转而支持基于 L-熵的平方根修正，该量在本文采用的全息“完整腿”处方中为零。

Conclusion: 结果表明多分支共形结中的能量传输约束需由多体信息量（multipartite information）刻画，文中讨论了存在一般共形结下统一多体信息上界的可能性，但尚未给出确定结论。

Abstract: We study how entanglement constrains energy transmission in conformal Y-junctions using holographic and free-field models. We define pairwise effective central charges from mutual information as a natural extension of the interface entanglement coefficient. For holographic junctions, we formulate a branched RT prescription and show that the resulting entropies satisfy monogamy of mutual information. We find that the pairwise effective central charge does not always bound the corresponding energy transmission, but a correction involving finite tripartite information restores the bound, with a sharp coefficient determined by limiting saturation. Free-boson and Dirac-fermion junctions also violate the pairwise bound, but the tripartite correction fails because the tripartite information is positive or vanishing, respectively. Analytic limits and numerical results instead support a candidate square-root correction based on L-entropy, which vanishes in the holographic complete-leg prescription considered here. We discuss the possibility of a common multipartite information bound in general conformal junctions.

</details>


### [189] [Resolvent reconstruction of minimal strings beyond KdV II: D series models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32150)
*Wasif Ahmed,Joydeep Naskar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文借助将 Johnson 微分方程方法（以 Part I 中约化 KP 梯队的重构形式）推广到 D 系列极小弦（D-series minimal strings），解释了具有相同中心荷与共形谱曲线的三态 Potts 引力与四临界 Ising 引力，在加入手柄（handle）或边界（boundary）后振幅如何产生差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三态 Potts 引力与四临界 Ising 引力共享中心荷与共形谱曲线，仅凭谱数据无法区分二者；因此需要确定在引入手柄或边界后，是什么机制导致其振幅不同。

Method: 将 Johnson 的微分方程方法（differential-equation approach）以重构形式推广至 D 系列极小弦；其空间算符为带非局域 Pfaffian 项的伪微分铅笔（pseudo-differential pencil），经典能量在零动量处出现第二个极点；由两个极点处的正则性（regularity）唯一确定量子修正，普通弦方程提供这些修正，普通 D 流（D flows）以与 tau 函数一致的方式添加边界。

Result: 在亏格一（genus one）时，闭弦自由能可由铅笔表示为临界值速度的对数之和；两理论的差异源于曲线原点的处理（D 理论将其计为两个正则方向）以及每一点与其反射点的配对。该原点贡献被追踪至亏格三的自由能、单孔环面（one-holed torus）与环面单点振幅；在初级范围（primary range）内，后者重现了由世界面数值方法对两个系列所 conjectured 的公式。

Conclusion: D 系列极小弦的铅笔形式给出了量子修正的唯一确定方案，并表明原点贡献与点—反射配对是区分两理论振幅的关键；所得振幅在初级范围内与既有数值猜想一致，支持该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Three-state Potts gravity and tetracritical Ising gravity have the same central charge and the same conformal spectral curve. What distinguishes their amplitudes once a handle or a boundary is added? We answer this question by extending Johnson's differential-equation approach, in the reconstruction form developed in Part I for the reduced KP hierarchies, to D-series minimal strings. Their spatial operator is a pseudo-differential pencil with a nonlocal Pfaffian term, and the classical energy acquires a second pole at zero momentum. Regularity at both poles fixes the quantum corrections uniquely, ordinary string equations supply them, and the ordinary D flows add boundaries in a way that matches the tau function. At genus one the closed free energy follows from the pencil as a sum of logarithms of critical-value velocities. The two theories differ through the origin of the curve, which the D theory counts as two canonical directions, and through the pairing of every point with its reflection. We follow the origin contribution through the free energies to genus three, the one-holed torus and the torus one-point amplitudes, and in the primary range the latter reproduce the formulas conjectured from worldsheet numerics for both series.

</details>


### [190] [Holographic Entanglement Entropy of Network CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32224)
*Zhi-Rong Deng,Jie Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将 AdS/NCFT 对应（AdS/NCFT correspondence）从爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）与高斯-博内引力（Gauss-Bonnet gravity）推广到更高阶的洛夫洛克引力（Lovelock gravity），并在更复杂网络结构中研究全息纠缠熵（holographic entanglement entropy, HEE）与引力耦合系数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在爱因斯坦引力与高斯-博内引力（即一阶与二阶洛夫洛克引力）框架下考察定义于网络上的共形场论（conformal field theory）的引力对偶，因而有必要推广至更高阶洛夫洛克引力及更复杂网络结构。

Method: 推导三阶洛夫洛克引力（third-order Lovelock gravity）中广义 Ryu-Takayanagi（RT）曲面的极值条件（extremal conditions）与连接条件（junction conditions），并说明其可自然推广至任意高阶洛夫洛克理论；随后以一个含单节点三分支的网络模型进行数值计算，并分析多种网络构型下的 HEE。

Result: 数值结果给出网络全息纠缠熵与引力耦合系数之间的关系；跨构型分析表明，影响网络 HEE 的主要结构特征是线状网络（line-shaped networks）内部节点（internal nodes）的存在，且网络 HEE 随内部支路数量或深度的增加而减小。

Conclusion: AdS/NCFT 框架可拓展至任意高阶洛夫洛克引力；网络 HEE 主要由内部节点是否出现决定，并随内部支路数目与深度的增加而单调下降。

Abstract: Miao et al. \cite{Guo:2025sbm, Guo:2026prm} proposed the AdS/NCFT correspondence, which explores the gravity dual of conformal field theories defined on networks. While previous studies investigated this model within Einstein and Gauss-Bonnet gravity (first- and second-order Lovelock gravity), in this paper, we generalize the framework to higher-order Lovelock gravity and more complex network structures. We derive the extremal and junction conditions for generalized Ryu-Takayanagi (RT) surfaces in third-order Lovelock gravity, which can be naturally extended to arbitrary higher-order Lovelock theories. Focusing on a network model comprising one node and three branches, we numerically investigate the relationship between the network holographic entanglement entropy (HEE) and gravitational coupling coefficients. Furthermore, by analyzing the HEE across various network configurations, we find that the primary structural feature affecting the network HEE is the presence of internal nodes within line-shaped networks. Our results indicate that the network HEE decreases as the number or depth of internal branches increases.

</details>


### [191] [D0-brane bound states in Matrix theory: nonperturbative bounds on matrix extent, noncommutativity and spatial shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32333)
*Tetsuyuki Muramatsu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 BFSS 模型中，假设存在归一化的物理 BPS 态，利用超对称性导出该 D0-膜束缚态的矩阵尺度、非对易性与空间形状的非微扰上界：矩阵尺度小于某个显式尺度固定比例的概率随 $N^2$ 指数压低，相对非对易性期望为 $O(1/N)$ 量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵理论（Matrix theory）中，单个十一维超引力子（supergraviton）对应 D0-膜的 BPS 束缚态，但其量子态的矩阵尺度与几何结构缺乏非微扰约束，需要从超对称性出发给出严格界限。

Method: 在 BFSS 模型框架下，假设存在归一化的物理 BPS 态，借助超对称性（supersymmetry）推导对矩阵尺度（matrix extent）、非对易性（noncommutativity）与空间形状（spatial shape）的非微扰（nonperturbative）界限；并在 Yang-Mills 变量下以 't Hooft 耦合标定尺度，用无量纲对易子比值刻画相对非对易性。

Result: 矩阵构型小于显式尺度固定比例的概率随 D0-膜数 $N$ 的平方指数压低；迹归一化尺度的量级为 't Hooft 耦合的三次方根。相对非对易性期望被 $O(1/N)$ 量级的量所界定，超过该量足够大固定倍数的概率在大 $N$ 下趋于零。若在大 $N$ 下矩阵尺度集中到少于九个固定主方向，则相对参考尺度的总尺度需依概率发散，且相对非对易性须在每一固定有限矩（阶数至少为一）上被强于 $1/N$ 地压低。

Conclusion: 所得界限适用于包含非对角矩阵自由度的完整量子波函数，从而约束 D0-膜束缚态的量子结构以及矩阵理论中可能的大 $N$ 矩阵几何。

Abstract: In Matrix theory, a single eleven-dimensional supergraviton is associated with a BPS bound state of D0-branes. Assuming the existence of a normalized physical BPS state, we derive nonperturbative bounds on its matrix extent, noncommutativity and spatial shape from supersymmetry in the BFSS model.
  The probability of matrix configurations smaller than a fixed fraction of an explicit extent scale is exponentially suppressed in the square of the number $N$ of D0-branes. In Yang-Mills variables, this scale for the trace-normalized extent is of order the cube root of the 't Hooft coupling. Relative noncommutativity, measured by a dimensionless commutator ratio, has an expectation bounded by a quantity of order the inverse of $N$, and its probability of exceeding a sufficiently large fixed multiple of the inverse of $N$ vanishes at large $N$.
  Concentration of the matrix extent along a fixed number of principal directions fewer than nine at large $N$ requires the total extent relative to a corresponding reference scale to diverge in probability. It also requires relative noncommutativity to be suppressed more strongly than the inverse of $N$, in every fixed finite moment of order at least one.
  These bounds apply to the full quantum wave function, including off-diagonal matrix degrees of freedom, and constrain the quantum structure of D0-brane bound states and the possible large-$N$ matrix geometries in Matrix theory.

</details>


### [192] [The DBI critical electric field in stationary non-diagonal geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32378)
*Leila Shahkarami*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一般稳态非对角（non-diagonal）几何中的全息施温格效应（holographic Schwinger effect）临界电场问题，指出DBI（Dirac-Born-Infeld）实条件应以局部正交标架（orthonormal frame）下测得的电场表述，并给出该标架下的普适临界值 \(E_{\mathrm{proper},c}=T_F\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非对角背景中，场强（field strength）的坐标分量未必对应纯电场，因此需要明确临界电场应以何种方式定义，以推广全息施温格效应的判据。

Method: 以一般稳态非对角几何为背景，将DBI实条件改写为局部正交标架下的电场表述，并通过全息势（holographic potential）分析中势垒消失的条件对临界场进行独立验证；以平面极限（planar limit）和整体旋转 Myers–Perry–AdS 几何作为示例。

Result: 正交标架下的临界场取普适值 \(E_{\mathrm{proper},c}=T_F\)，而其坐标表达式依赖背景几何；两种方法（DBI实条件与势垒消失）给出的临界场一致。

Conclusion: 该方案为稳态非对角几何中的全息施温格效应提供了普适的临界场判据，且示例表明不同度规混合（metric mixing）类型均可纳入同一框架。

Abstract: We investigate the critical electric field for the holographic Schwinger effect in general stationary non-diagonal geometries.
