We show that, in such backgrounds, the DBI reality condition must be formulated in terms of the electric field measured in a local orthonormal frame, since the coordinate components of the field strength need not represent a purely electric field in that frame. The critical field measured in the orthonormal frame takes the universal value \(E_{\mathrm{proper},c}=T_F\), while its coordinate expression depends on the background geometry. The resulting critical field is independently confirmed by the holographic potential analysis, through the disappearance of the potential barrier. We further illustrate the general prescription using the planar limit and the global rotating Myers--Perry--AdS geometry, which exhibit different types of metric mixing.

</details>


### [193] [Holographic Spread Complexity of a Probe Particle with General Initial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32427)
*Zekun Qian,Zhehan Li,Jia Tian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究大质量粒子探测整体AdS₃时空的全息扩散复杂度（holographic spread complexity），构建适配哈密顿量将体测地线映射为边界Krylov动力学对，揭示复杂度与守恒手征荷之间的非线性编码关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有全息图像中复杂度增长率与体正则动量之间存在简单对应（$\dot{C}\sim p_\rho$），但该关系在一般初始数据下是否成立尚不清楚，需系统检验并刻画其修正。

Method: 构造适配哈密顿量（adapted Hamiltonian），把每条体测地线（bulk geodesic）映射为边界Krylov动力学对（Krylov dynamical pair），其动力学仅由守恒手征荷（chiral charges）决定；并通过多种微扰展开（perturbative expansions）系统分析非线性编码。进一步考察对称分辨扩散复杂度（symmetry-resolved spread complexity）与手征扩散复杂度（chiral spread complexity）两种精细化变体，并直接量子化有效AdS₂理论。

Result: 对一般初始数据，原对应关系$\dot{C}\sim p_\rho$失效，复杂度及其增长率均非线性依赖于体Noether荷（Noether charges）。对称分辨版本恢复了体径向$\pi$-周期性（$\pi$-periodicity）；手征版本具有精确闭式（closed form），在小角动量下趋近标准复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过对有效AdS₂理论的直接量子化，给出以失效函数（lapse function）表述的扩散复杂度新全息解释，表明复杂度与体守恒荷之间为非线性编码关系。

Abstract: We investigate the holographic spread complexity of a massive particle probing global AdS$_3$ with general initial data. By constructing an adapted Hamiltonian, we map each bulk geodesic to a boundary Krylov dynamical pair, whose dynamics are governed solely by the conserved chiral charges. We find that, for generic initial data, the original identification $\dot{C}\sim p_ρ$ fails; instead, both the complexity and its growth rate depend nonlinearly on the bulk Noether charges. We systematically analyze this nonlinear encoding via several perturbative expansions. We further investigate two refined variants: the symmetry-resolved spread complexity, which restores the bulk radial $π$-periodicity, and the chiral spread complexity, which admits an exact closed form approaching the standard complexity at small angular momentum. Finally, by directly quantizing an effective AdS$_2$ theory, we obtain a new holographic interpretation of spread complexity in terms of the lapse function.

</details>


### [194] [Romans Mass from a $\mathrm{GL}(1,\mathbb{R})$ Topological Field Theory with Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32437)
*Euihun Joung,J. A. Rosabal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个十一维拓扑场论（topological field theory），其边界模式为含 Romans 质量的 IIA 型超引力（type IIA supergravity）的伸缩子（dilaton）与形式场；体作用量为双重形式（doubled formalism）超代数的 Chern–Simons 泛函，其边界约化在壳外（off shell）给出 Romans 作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在十一维拓扑框架中统一描述 IIA 型超引力的场内容，特别是为 Romans 质量（Romans mass）提供不依赖于十维势（potential）的几何与规范结构解释，并厘清伸缩子与费米宇称翻转的边界角色。

Method: 采用双重形式超代数的 Chern–Simons 泛函作为体作用量，由十维中不可见的两个括号（brackets）补全；将各场强（含 Romans 质量）视为零形式的边界值并变分，度规仅通过边界项进入。

Result: Romans 质量作为零形式的边界值，其运动方程为无十维对应的十一形式高斯定律（Gauss law）；边界项将 GL(1,ℝ) 规范对称破缺为 O(1)，伸缩子成为 GL(1,ℝ)/O(1) 上的边缘模式，而翻转 Romans 质量的 (-1)^{F_L} 成为规范对称。

Conclusion: 平坦联络（flat connection）的和乐（holonomies）实现为伸缩子与 (-1)^{F_L} 的单值变换（monodromies），经典上由 D8-膜电荷固定；对偶于 O(1) 和乐的超曲面可终止于反射七膜（reflection seven-branes）。由于无自对偶场，约化在壳外给出 Romans 作用量，而同一体作用量的其他边界条件给出无质量 IIA 型及对偶表述。

Abstract: We construct an eleven-dimensional topological field theory with boundary whose edge modes are the dilaton and form fields of type IIA supergravity, including the Romans mass. The bulk action is the Chern--Simons functional of the superalgebra of the doubled formalism, completed by two brackets that are invisible in ten dimensions; it is unique up to normalization, contains no potentials, and the metric enters only through a boundary term. The Romans mass is the boundary value of a zero-form, like the other field strengths: it is varied, and its equation of motion is an eleven-form Gauss law with no ten-dimensional counterpart. The boundary term breaks the $GL(1,\mathbb{R})$ gauge symmetry to $O(1)$: the dilaton is the edge mode on $GL(1,\mathbb{R})/O(1)$, and $(-1)^{F_L}$, which reverses the Romans mass, becomes a gauge symmetry. The holonomies of the flat connection are monodromies of the dilaton and of $(-1)^{F_L}$, fixed classically by the D8-brane charges when the mass is nonzero, and the hypersurface dual to the $O(1)$ holonomy can end on reflection seven-branes. Since no field is self-dual, the reduction gives Romans' action off shell, while other boundary conditions of the same bulk give massless type IIA and dual formulations.

</details>


### [195] [Entanglement island and Page curve for one-sided rotating Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32438)
*Soumen Pari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用岛公式（island prescription）研究由引力坍缩（gravitational collapse）形成的单侧渐近平坦旋转Kerr黑洞的信息佯谬（information paradox），分析了旋转对岛边界、Page时间与扰乱时间的影响，并分别讨论了非极值与极值情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单侧渐近平坦旋转Kerr黑洞的信息佯谬尚待检验，尤其旋转如何影响岛（island）的出现及其对霍金辐射熵的修正需要明确。

Method: 将视界附近的标量场（scalar field）动力学约化为有效二维理论（effective two-dimensional theory），在岛公式框架下计算非极值与极值Kerr黑洞的岛边界位置、Page时间（Page time）和扰乱时间（scrambling time）。

Result: 非极值情形下，岛的出现抑制了晚期霍金辐射熵（Hawking radiation entropy）的无界增长并恢复幺正的Page曲线（Page curve）；岛边界依赖于截断面（cutoff surface）位置，这与永恒Kerr黑洞的结果不同；极值情形下晚期纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）趋于极值黑洞的Bekenstein-Hawking熵。

Conclusion: 黑洞几何（black hole geometry）在决定纠缠熵行为中起重要作用，岛公式为单侧旋转Kerr黑洞的信息佯谬提供了半经典（semiclassical）解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate the information paradox for one-sided asymptotically flat rotating Kerr black holes formed through gravitational collapse using the island prescription. Near the horizon, the dynamics of a scalar field can be described by an effective two-dimensional theory, which provides the framework for our analysis. We study how rotation affects the location of the island boundary $\partial I$, the Page time, and the scrambling time, treating non-extremal and extremal Kerr black holes separately. For non-extremal black holes, the emergence of an island prevents the unbounded growth of Hawking radiation entropy at late times and restores the unitary Page curve. We further find that the island boundary depends on the location of the cutoff surface, in contrast to the behaviour found for eternal Kerr black holes. In the extremal case, the late-time entanglement entropy of Hawking radiation approaches the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy of the extremal black hole. Our results demonstrate the important role of black hole geometry in determining the behaviour of entanglement entropy and suggest that the island prescription provides a semiclassical resolution of the information paradox for one-sided rotating Kerr black holes.

</details>


### [196] [Differential Equations for Feynman Integrals from Higher Residue Pairings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32544)
*Giacomo Brunello,Vsevolod Chestnov,Ben Page*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出直接由多元交点数（multivariate intersection numbers）构造费曼积分微分方程的方法，无需纤维化（fibration）。通过Saito高阶留数配对（higher residue pairings）在临界点的局域化及δ→0极限下的扇区分解，得到以未正则化扇区数据表出的闭式公式，并在无质量平面四点梯形族上给出四圈新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构造费曼积分微分方程通常依赖分部积分恒等式（integration-by-parts identities），并常借助纤维化处理多元问题；该文旨在绕开这一途径，直接从交点数出发建立微分系统。

Method: 利用交点数配对的大ε展开（ε为维数正则化参数），其由Saito高阶留数配对给出，局域于势的临界点并具有多元闭式。在Lee-Pomeransky表示中需引入坐标超平面上的解析正则化参数δ；在假定正则化临界点关于δ解析的前提下，证明δ→0时临界点按被夹紧传播子（pinched propagators）集合组织成扇区，并推导前导与次前导高阶留数配对的闭式。

Result: 所得公式完全以未正则化扇区数据表示，足以重建至多关于ε线性的微分系统（包括典范系统（canonical form））。将该方法应用于至多四圈的无质量平面四点梯形族（ladder families），得到其典范微分方程，其中四圈结果为新的。

Conclusion: 多元交点数结合高阶留数配对可系统化地导出费曼积分微分方程，且无需分部积分恒等式；该方法在四圈梯形族上验证有效并产生新结果，显示其在高圈计算中的实用潜力。

Abstract: We develop a method for constructing differential equations for Feynman integrals directly from multivariate intersection numbers, without fibration. The large-$\varepsilon$ expansion of the intersection pairing, with $\varepsilon$ the dimensional regulator, is given by Saito's higher residue pairings, which localise at the critical points of a potential and admit closed multivariate formulae. In the Lee-Pomeransky representation, applying these formulae to Feynman integrals requires analytic regulators $δ$ on the coordinate hyperplanes, and the physical limit $δ\to 0$ is not manifest. Assuming that the regulated critical points are analytic in $δ$, we show that, as $δ\to 0$, they organise into sectors labelled by sets of pinched propagators. We derive closed formulae for the leading and first subleading higher residue pairings in this limit, written entirely in terms of unregulated sector data. These suffice to reconstruct differential systems at most linear in $\varepsilon$, including canonical ones, bypassing integration-by-parts identities. We apply the method to the massless planar four-point ladder families up to four loops and obtain their canonical differential equations, which are new at four loops.

</details>


### [197] [Inflation at strong mixing as a connection problem: effective field theory, Stokes phenomenon and the cosmological collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32612)
*Sebastian Garcia-Saenz,Yizhou Lu,Sébastien Renaux-Petel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究宇宙暴涨期间大质量场与曲率扰动强混合的情形，通过求解连接问题（connection problem）并结合逆混合展开，给出含粒子产生（particle production）效应的宇宙对撞机信号（cosmological collider signal）的紧凑解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单场有效场论（EFT）在低能下只能描述有限内容，其在早期真空定义与晚期观测量之间留下未定的相位与真空选择问题，且粒子产生超出了该 EFT 的所有阶，需要一套超越 EFT 的系统方法来刻画。

Method: 采用不依赖精确解的通用方法求解连接问题；以逆混合量（inverse mixing）作渐近展开，把 EFT 作为领头阶并系统控制修正，再由色散关系的复分支点处的 Stokes 现象（Stokes phenomenon）与运动方程的反射对称性（reflection symmetry）结合正则对易关系，精确确定所有指数小效应。

Result: 逆混合展开确定了 EFT 的真空并固定其无法自定的相位，且能描述任意时刻的重模；粒子产生由 Stokes 现象给出，在旋转基下化为质量为重模一半的粒子的通常产生过程；据此得到宇宙对撞机信号的紧凑解析公式，其指数压低为通常 Boltzmann 压低的一半，直接对应减半的粒子质量。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需能标分离与重模的绝热性（adiabaticity），而反射对称性在 de Sitter 空间的许多系统中成立，因此预期该途径可推广到本文情形之外，提供物理理解与简洁公式。

Abstract: We study a massive field strongly mixed with the curvature perturbation during inflation, in the regime where the latter is described at low energies by a single-field effective field theory (EFT) with a quadratic dispersion relation. What this EFT captures, and what lies beyond it, are encoded in a connection problem, which relates the solutions that define the vacuum at early times to those that describe observables at late times. We solve it with a general method that does not rely on an exact solution. An expansion in the inverse mixing, keeping both fields, yields the EFT as its leading order, with corrections under systematic control. It selects the vacuum of the EFT, fixes a phase that the EFT cannot determine on its own, and describes the heavy mode at all times. Particle production lies beyond all orders of this asymptotic expansion. We show that it arises from a Stokes phenomenon at the complex branch points of the dispersion relation and that, in a rotating basis of the two fields, it reduces to the familiar production of particles with half the heavy mass. A reflection symmetry of the equations of motion, combined with the canonical commutation relations, then determines all exponentially small effects exactly, and with them the solution of the connection problem, which combines particle production with a conversion between light and heavy quanta. Together with the expansion, these exact results give a compact analytical formula for the cosmological collider signal, whose exponential suppression, half of the usual Boltzmann one, directly reflects the halved mass of the produced particles. The expansion only requires a separation of scales and the adiabaticity of the heavy mode, while the reflection symmetry holds for many systems in de Sitter space. We therefore expect this approach to bring physical understanding and simple formulas well beyond the present case.

</details>


### [198] [Holographic Mutual Information in an Anisotropic Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32637)
*Mehri Rahimi,Masoumeh Tavakoli,Behrouz Mirza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在规范/引力对偶（gauge/gravity duality）框架下研究各向异性强耦合等离子体（anisotropic strongly coupled plasma）中的全息互信息（holographic mutual information），揭示能量密度与压强的方向依赖性对相关结构相图的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息互信息可作为探测强耦合等离子体相关结构的工具，但各向异性背景下的方向性效应尚不明确，论文旨在厘清温度、各向异性与几何构型如何共同影响子系统间的关联。

Method: 在各向异性等离子体的全息模型中，分别构造平行（parallel）与垂直（perpendicular）条带构型，分析温度、各向异性、条带长度及间隔距离对互信息的影响，并比较等长与非等长条带情形。

Result: 等长条带下，升温抑制互信息并缩小相图中相关区域；平行构型呈单调行为，垂直构型因能量密度与压强的竞争而表现更复杂；非等长条带呈相似的定性特征。

Conclusion: 能量密度与压强的方向依赖性是互信息行为及相结构中非平庸方向效应的关键来源。

Abstract: We investigate holographic mutual information in an anisotropic strongly coupled plasma within the framework of gauge/gravity duality. Considering both parallel and perpendicular strip configurations, we analyze the effects of temperature, anisotropy, strip lengths, and separation distance on the correlation structure between subsystems. For strips with equal lengths, we show that increasing temperature suppresses mutual information and reduces the size of the correlated region in the phase diagram. We find that the parallel configuration exhibits a monotonic behavior. However, the perpendicular configuration displays a more complex behavior resulting from the competition between the effects of energy density and pressure. Similar qualitative features are observed for strips with unequal lengths. Our results suggest that the directional dependence of the energy density and pressure plays an important role in the behavior of mutual information and may explain the nontrivial directional effects observed in the phase structure.

</details>


### [199] [The Wilson-loop formulation of Yang-Mills at finite mode truncation: a Level-Two structural bypass of the Gribov ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32690)
*Yu. N. Berdinsky*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在有限模式截断下实现了杨-米尔斯泛函积分的“二级绕行（Level-Two bypass）”：以Wilson圈W(C)为基本变量构造圈空间上的积分，从而完全避开规范场、规范轨道、规范固定与Faddeev-Popov行列式，并用Lean 4对九条命题进行无sorry的形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未固定（unfixed）实时Henstock-Kurzweil泛函积分虽消除了规范面选择，但规范轨道仍是表述中的对象，属于对Gribov歧义（Gribov ambiguity）的一级绕行；本文旨在进一步在截断层面彻底移除规范相关概念。

Method: 将Wilson圈作为基本变量，使构型空间成为圈数据空间；对九条陈述在Lean 4中机器检验（不含sorry、admit及新公理），涵盖圈代数性质、作用量在plaquette Wilson变量下的仿射多项式形式、可观测量的柱性、Henstock-Kurzweil可积性、积分对规范固定数据的独立性，以及一级与二级积分对圈可观测量的相符性。

Result: 证明了圈代数的交换性、共轭封闭性与规范不变性（含SU(2) Mandelstam恒等式）；振荡被积函数在每个有界圈空间盒上Henstock-Kurzweil可积且取Lebesgue值；规范副本对应圈表述中的同一点；单plaquette SU(2)检验完整成立并给出闭式2J_1(β)/β；SU(N) Mandelstam关系以协变迹形式导出；有限M积分射影相容，其极限作为线性泛函而非测度存在。

Conclusion: 工作严格限于有限模式截断，未声称连续极限、质量间隙（mass gap）、禁闭（confinement）或Makeenko-Migdal方程；Giles重构定理仅作引用，并如实记录每条证明的终止边界。

Abstract: The unfixed real-time Henstock-Kurzweil functional integral for truncated Yang-Mills removes the choice of a gauge surface, but gauge orbits remain objects of the formulation: that is a Level-One bypass of the Gribov ambiguity. This paper carries out a Level-Two bypass at finite mode truncation: the Wilson loops W(C) are taken as the fundamental variables, so that the configuration space is a space of loop data and no gauge field, gauge orbit, gauge-fixing condition or Faddeev-Popov determinant occurs anywhere in the formulation. Nine statements are proved and machine checked in Lean 4 without sorry, without admit and without new axioms: the loop algebra is commutative, conjugation-closed and gauge invariant, with bounded generators and the SU(2) Mandelstam identity; the truncated Yang-Mills action is an affine polynomial in the plaquette Wilson variables; loop-expressible observables are cylindrical; the oscillatory integrand is Henstock-Kurzweil integrable on every bounded loop-space box for every calibration, with the Lebesgue value; the integral is independent of gauge-fixing data and gauge copies are the same point of the loop formulation; for loop observables the Level-Two integral with respect to the push-forward measure coincides with the Level-One unfixed integral; the one-plaquette SU(2) check is proved in full, including the closed form 2J_1(beta)/beta; the SU(N) Mandelstam relations are derived in covariant trace form; and the finite-M integrals are projectively compatible, their limit existing as a linear functional, not a measure. No continuum limit, mass gap, confinement or Makeenko-Migdal equation is claimed; the Giles reconstruction theorem is cited only. The honest report records exactly where each proof stops.

</details>


### [200] [Baker-Akhiezer functions for Koornwinder-van Diejen integrable system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32744)
*Liudmila Bishler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文厘清 Chalykh 针对经典根系（classical root system）$BC_n$ 提出的 Baker-Akhiezer 函数形式体系，并将其推广定义到整族 $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$ 仿射根系（affine root systems）上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koornwinder-van Diejen 可积系统（integrable system）是三角 Ruijsenaars 系统在根系 $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$ 上的对应物，其本征函数为对称 Koornwinder 多项式，在参数特定取值下等于 Chalykh 为 $BC_n$ 引入的 Baker-Akhiezer 函数在 Weyl 群上的求和。作者希望阐明该形式体系并推广至其他仿射根系。

Method: 在参数 $q$、$t$ 及 Koornwinder（Askey-Wilson）参数 $a,b,c,d$ 的框架下，将 Chalykh 的构造解释并推广，对 $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$ 族中的全部根系定义 Baker-Akhiezer 函数，并给出若干显式表达式。

Result: 对 $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$ 族内所有根系均给出 Baker-Akhiezer 函数的定义，并呈现了一些显式的函数表达式；参数的不同特化（specializations）将该系统与同族的其他经典根系相联系。

Conclusion: Chalykh 的形式体系可自然地推广到 $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$ 族的其他仿射根系，这为统一处理 Koornwinder-van Diejen 系统及其相关本征函数提供了框架。

Abstract: The Koornwinder-van Diejen integrable system is a counterpart of the trigonometric Ruijsenaars integrable system (associated with the root system $A_n$) for the root system $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$. It depends on the standard Macdonald parameters $q$ and $t$ as well as the Koornwinder (Askey-Wilson) parameters $a$, $b$, $c$, $d$. Different specializations of the parameters connect it to the other classical root systems from the $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$ family.
  The eigenfunctions of the Koornwinder-van Diejen integrable system are the symmetric Koornwinder polynomials; for certain values of the parameters $t$, $a$, $b$, $c$, $d$ they coincide with the sum over the Weyl group of the Baker-Akhiezer functions introduced by O.A.\,Chalykh for the classical root system $BC_n$. The main idea behind this paper is to elucidate Chalykh's formalism and apply it to other affine root systems. We define Baker-Akhiezer functions for all root systems in the $(C_n^\vee, C_n)$ family. We also present some explicit expressions for Baker-Akhiezer functions.

</details>


### [201] [Cosmological Constant Screening from Quantum Horizon Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32839)
*Chong-Sun Chu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通过视界自由度（horizon degrees of freedom）的量子混沌（quantum chaos）来屏蔽宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）的微观机制，核心是将引力修饰软模（gravitationally dressed soft mode）的布朗扩散（Brownian diffusion）所生成的玻色熵与费米部分子（parton）微观态熵相加，作为总热力学熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）的观测值远小于量子场论估计，缺乏微观动力学解释；本文动机是在量子视界矩阵模型（quantum-horizon matrix model）框架内，为红外屏蔽（infrared screening）提供微观机制。

Method: 在 arXiv:2406.01466 的量子视界矩阵模型与 arXiv:2608.21178 的 Schwarzschild–de Sitter 衔接（junction completion）中，识别出趋近 de Sitter 引力的无质量最小耦合标量的引力修饰软模；假设有限记忆（finite-memory）力谱具有非零规则低频权重，考察动量弛豫后软模的布朗扩散及其熵产生。

Result: 软模的玻色粗粒化熵（coarse-grained entropy）补充费米部分子微观态的背景熵，二者之和可等同于反作用因果视界（backreacted causal horizon）的 Gibbons–Hawking 熵；扩散区间的熵产生导致有效宇宙学常数的红外屏蔽，且熵–方差系数（entropy–variance coefficient）与 Kitazawa 等人的结果一致。

Conclusion: 视界自由度的量子混沌可为宇宙学常数屏蔽提供微观机制；软模的辐射抬升（radiative lifting）可能终止屏蔽区间，构成该机制的退出通道。

Abstract: We propose a microscopic mechanism for screening the cosmological constant through quantum chaos of the horizon degrees of freedom. We show that in a recently proposed quantum-horizon matrix model arXiv:2406.01466, the Schwarzschild--de Sitter junction completion arXiv:2608.21178 supports a gravitationally dressed soft mode that approaches the massless minimally coupled scalar of de Sitter gravity. Interactions among the microscopic horizon degrees of freedom provide a mechanism for force relaxation. Assuming a finite-memory force spectrum with nonzero regular low-frequency weight, the gravitationally dressed soft mode undergoes Brownian diffusion after momentum relaxation. The resulting bosonic coarse-grained entropy supplements the background entropy arising from fermionic parton microstates. We propose that their sum defines the total thermodynamic entropy, which can be identified with the Gibbons--Hawking entropy of the backreacted causal horizon. Entropy generation during the diffusion regime then leads to infrared screening of the effective cosmological constant, with a universal entropy--variance coefficient agreeing with that of Kitazawa and collaborators. Radiative lifting of the soft mode provides a possible mechanism for terminating the screening regime.

</details>


### [202] [The Four-Loop Ladder Integral Family for the QED Electron Self-Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32854)
*Felix Forner,Andreas von Manteuffel,Christoph Nega,Lorenzo Tancredi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本工作计算了量子电动力学（Quantum Electrodynamics, QED）中电子自能（electron self-energy）四圈梯形积分族（four-loop ladder integral family）的全部主积分（master integrals），结果以迭代积分形式给出，涉及椭圆曲线（elliptic curve）与卡拉比-丘三维流形（Calabi-Yau threefold）几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四圈电子自能是电子反常磁矩（anomalous magnetic moment, g-2）平面贡献的高阶组成部分，其结果包含非多重对数（non-polylogarithmic）常数，需要完整主积分集才能精确刻画。

Method: 采用正则微分方程（canonical differential equations）方法，将结果表示为若干积分核上的迭代积分（iterated integrals），这些核与日出椭圆曲线（sunrise elliptic curve）的不同参数化及一个 Calabi-Yau 三维流形相关；为实现 ε-因子化（ε-factorization）引入大量额外超越函数（transcendental functions），并显式讨论了若干有趣扇区（sector）的 canonical basis 构造。

Result: 给出了 p²=∞ 与 p²=m² 处至权重七（weight seven）的局部级数展开与数值边界常数，并通过数值匹配（numerical matching）获得多重对数常数的解析表示；该单一积分族的 on-shell 边界值已覆盖四圈电子 g-2 平面贡献中全部非多重对数常数，且借助数值传输（numerical transport）得到全 p² 范围内的精确数值结果。

Conclusion: 该积分族在几何结构上足够丰富，可涵盖四圈 g-2 平面贡献所需的全部非多重对数常数，为高阶 QED 精确计算提供了完整的主积分基础。

Abstract: We compute the complete set of master integrals of the four-loop ladder integral family contributing to the electron self-energy in Quantum Electrodynamics. We obtain the results through canonical differential equations and write them as iterated integrals over integration kernels related to different parametrizations of the sunrise elliptic curve and a Calabi-Yau threefold. The rich structure of geometries and couplings between them requires the introduction of a large number of extra transcendental functions to reach $ε$-factorization. We discuss explicitly how to find a canonical basis for a selection of particularly interesting sectors. Additionally, we compute local series expansions and numerical boundary constants at $p^2=\infty$ and $p^2=m^2$ up to weight seven, and for the polylogarithmic constants we find analytical representations by numerical matching. The on-shell boundary values of this single family already span all non-polylogarithmic constants entering the planar contribution to the four-loop result for the anomalous magnetic moment $(g-2)$ of the electron. We obtain precise numerical results across all values of $p^2$ using numerical transport.

</details>


### [203] [Fisher information in the flux landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32901)
*Martin Carrascal,Radu Tatar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用 Fisher 信息度量（Fisher Information Metric）量化 Type IIB Calabi-Yau orientifold 紧化中通量超势（flux superpotential）的精细调节（fine-tuning），发现其 Fisher 矩阵唯一非零本征值可分解并给出超势上界；在具体双模 orbifold 模型中，通量整性（flux integrality）与 D3-膜 tadpole 条件将 12 维通量格（flux lattice）截断为 8 维，使该本征值增长显著减缓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦真空通量超势的精细调节（fine-tuning）缺乏定量刻画工具，本文旨在引入统计几何量给出可计算的调节程度度量。

Method: 将背景通量（background fluxes）视为模型参数、超势模量（magnitude of the superpotential）视为物理观测量，构造 Fisher 信息矩阵；并在 P^4(1,1,1,6,9) 中 18 次超曲面的双模 orientifold 大复结构极限下，结合通量整性与 D3-brane tadpole 条件约束容许通量方向。

Result: Fisher 矩阵存在唯一非零本征值，可从上方界定超势，并分解为 axio-dilaton、复结构（complex structure）部分与受模量及超势相位限制的因子；规范对偶标架下它随耦合常数倒数线性增长、随复结构 saxion 三次增长。受限到容许方向后，该本征值或趋于常数、或至多线性增长，远慢于全晶格情形。

Conclusion: 通量整性与 D3-brane tadpole 条件实质性削弱了通量超势的精细调节，Fisher 信息度量为弦紧化中调节程度的定量评估提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We quantify the fine-tuning of the flux superpotential in Type IIB Calabi-Yau orientifold compactifications using the Fisher Information Metric. Treating the background fluxes as model parameters and the magnitude of the superpotential as the physical observable, we show that the Fisher matrix admits a unique non-zero eigenvalue that bounds the superpotential from above and factorises into axio-dilaton, complex structure components and a bounded factor depending on both moduli and the phase of the superpotential. In the canonical duality frame and over the full flux lattice, this eigenvalue grows linearly with the inverse string coupling and cubically with the complex structure saxions. Specialising to a two modulus orientifold of the degree 18 hypersurface in $\mathbb{P}^4(1,1,1,6,9)$ at large complex structure, we combine the flux integrality with the D3-brane tadpole condition to show that several flux components, including the one driving this growth, are forced to vanish across deep moduli space patches, truncating the twelve-dimensional flux lattice to an eight-dimensional sublattice. The Fisher eigenvalue restricted to these admissible flux directions either approaches a constant or grows at most linearly, much more slowly than over the full lattice.

</details>


### [204] [Holographic ensembles and giant gravitons at the Leigh--Strassler fixed point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33072)
*Jesse van Muiden,De-liang Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了 N=1 Leigh-Strassler 共形场论（CFT）的有限秩配分函数，在正则系综与巨正则系综两种框架下给出显式计算，并以体（bulk）超引力一侧的 on-shell 作用量（on-shell action）、单圈行列式（one-loop determinant）和 D3-brane 巨引力子（giant graviton）单圈配分函数分别重现微扰与非微扰结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息量子场论（holographic quantum field theory）中有限秩可观测量（finite-rank observables）是检验弦论与 M 理论（string and M-theory）的严格手段，但在非最大超对称（non-maximal supersymmetry）情形下计算困难，故需针对具体 N=1 模型开展研究。

Method: 利用 Leigh-Strassler CFT 的相互作用费米气体（interacting Fermi-gas）描述，分别在固定秩 N 的正则系综（canonical ensemble）与固定化学势 μ、对 N 求和的巨正则系综（grand canonical ensemble）中显式计算配分函数，并与 IIB 型超引力（type IIB supergravity）Kaluza–Klein 模的 on-shell 作用量及单圈行列式展开结果进行对比。

Result: 两种系综下的大 N 与大 μ 微扰展开（perturbative large-N and large-μ expansions）均可由 on-shell 作用量与 Kaluza–Klein 模的一圈行列式重现，前提是加入依赖系综的边界项（ensemble-dependent boundary terms）；此外，D3-brane 巨引力子的一圈配分函数与边界配分函数的主导非微扰修正（leading nonperturbative correction）精确吻合。

Conclusion: 结果支持近期关于有限秩全息配分函数可由超引力体理论重现的提议，并表明巨引力子单圈贡献对应边界理论的非微扰效应，为 N=1 全息对偶的一致性提供了显式检验。

Abstract: Finite-rank observables in holographic quantum field theories provide stringent tests of string and M-theory, but remain challenging to compute when supersymmetry is non-maximal. In this paper, we study the finite rank partition function of the $\mathcal N=1$ Leigh-Strassler CFT, which admits an interacting Fermi-gas description. We compute the partition function explicitly in two ensembles: the canonical ensemble at fixed rank $N$, and the grand canonical ensemble, where $N$ is summed over while its conjugate chemical potential $μ$ is held fixed. In line with recent proposals, we find that the perturbative large-$N$ and large-$μ$ expansions in both ensembles are reproduced by the on-shell action and one-loop determinant over Kaluza--Klein modes of type IIB supergravity, with appropriate ensemble-dependent boundary terms. Furthermore, the one-loop partition function of a D3-brane giant graviton precisely matches the leading nonperturbative correction to the boundary partition functions.

</details>


### [205] [Anomalies and the Berry Phase: From $π^0\toγγ$ to the geometry of the fermion vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33121)
*Spenta R. Wadia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以历史视角综述手征反常（chiral anomaly）研究脉络：从π⁰→γγ之谜及Adler–Bell–Jackiw工作到Wess–Zumino–Witten（WZW）项，阐明其从量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics）中的推导，并将其诠释为一种Berry相位（Berry phase）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理手征反常的来龙去脉，并在量子色动力学框架下说明WZW项的物理起源，同时揭示其与Berry相位及流代数（current algebra）结构的深刻联系，从而统一理解反常、对称性破缺与重子性质。

Method: 历史回顾与有效场论推导相结合：通过依次积掉有能隙的非阿贝尔规范场（non-Abelian gauge fields）和有能隙的夸克（quarks），将费米子行列式（fermion determinant）分解为依赖于正规化方案的模量与由反常固定、普遍的相位（即WZW项）；并借助重子孤子（baryon soliton）的集体坐标及1+1维中基于欧拉角（Euler angles）的显式联络分析。

Result: WZW项对重子孤子的集体坐标施加U(1)约束，使重子波函数为单极谐函数（monopole harmonics），并确定其自旋、统计与味多重态（flavour multiplets）；该相位即Berry相位——流代数的Schwinger项是手征场位形空间上U(1)联络的曲率，WZW项为其和乐（holonomy，即Berry–Wilson环路）；1+1维中该联络表现出量子化电荷等于流代数能级（level）的狄拉克单极（Dirac monopole）。

Conclusion: 手征反常与WZW项可从QCD有效作用量自然导出，并可统一理解为Berry–Wilson环路的几何相位；该几何框架将反常、重子量子数与流代数结构相联系，并在1+1维给出显式对应。

Abstract: We review the chiral anomaly from a historical perspective, from the $π^0\toγγ$ puzzle and the work of Adler, Bell and Jackiw, to the Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) term. We then describe how the WZW term was motivated from quantum chromodynamics in 1983-85. Integrating out the non-Abelian gauge fields, that have a gapped spectrum, gives a Nambu-Jona-Lasinio type theory of quarks, and integrating out the quarks, which are gapped by chiral symmetry breaking, gives the WZW model. The fermion determinant splits into a regulator-dependent modulus, which gives the dynamics of mesons, and a universal phase, which is fixed by the anomaly and is the WZW term. Restricted to the collective coordinates of the baryon soliton, the WZW term imposes a U(1) constraint that makes the baryon wave functions monopole harmonics and fixes their spin, statistics and flavour multiplets. We then explain that this phase is a Berry phase: the Schwinger terms of current algebra are the curvature of a U(1) connection on the space of chiral field configurations, and the WZW term is its holonomy, which we call a Berry-Wilson loop. In 1+1 dimensions we write the connection explicitly in terms of Euler angles, exhibiting a Dirac monopole whose quantised charge is the level of the current algebra.

</details>


### [206] [Scalar perturbations in extremal Reissner-Nordström geometry: new methods at work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33123)
*Giorgio Di Russo,Davide Fioravanti,Francesco Olivi,Marco Rossi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究沿圆形轨道绕四维极端 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞（extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole）运动的粒子所发射的标量辐射（scalar radiation），计算了标量波携带至无穷远与穿越事件视界（event horizon）的能量通量以及标量自作用力（scalar self-force），并以后牛顿展开（post-Newtonian expansion）形式给出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极端黑洞背景下粒子轨道运动产生的标量辐射与自作用力，可为检验黑洞时空中波传播的计算方法及与已有数值结果对照提供依据；同时考察源自量子可积性（quantum integrability）的新解析方法是否适用。

Method: 求解径向波动方程（radial wave equation）时采用并检验两种解析方法：一是源自量子可积性的 kink 方法（kink method），二是 Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi 方法；随后计算能量通量与标量自作用力并作后牛顿展开。

Result: 能量通量（energy fluxes）结果与文献中已有数值结果一致；两种解析方法所得结果完全吻合（perfect agreement）。

Conclusion: 结果的一致性表明 kink 方法可作为研究黑洞时空中波传播（wave propagation in black-hole spacetimes）的互补工具（complementary tool）。

Abstract: We study scalar radiation emitted by a particle moving along a circular orbit around a four-dimensional extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole. In particular, we calculate the energy fluxes carried by scalar waves to infinity and across the event horizon, as well as the scalar self-force acting on the particle, and express all these quantities as post-Newtonian expansions. Our results for the energy fluxes are in agreement with previous numerical findings in the literature. To solve the radial wave equation, we employ and test two distinct analytic approaches: the first is the so-called \textit{kink method}, a novel alternative stemming from quantum integrability and the second is the Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi method. The perfect agreement of the results highlights the potential of the kink method as a complementary tool for studying wave propagation in black-hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [207] [The double copy and perturbative teleparallel gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33146)
*Georg Stettinger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在远平行引力（teleparallel gravity）框架下分析胶子与引力子振幅之间的双拷贝（double copy）关系，构造能重现双拷贝壳上振幅的有效拉格朗日量，并发现特定参数选择下与微扰远平行引力精确等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双拷贝procedure在壳上振幅层面已较成熟，但从拉格朗日量（Lagrangian）视角理解仍不透明；作者希望借助远平行引力的表述使双拷贝结构更为清晰。

Method: 基于最小假设建立有效拉格朗日量（effective Lagrangian），通过直接计算三点与四点振幅（three- and four-point amplitude），在展开至四次阶（quartic order）时将拉格朗日量约束至少数实参数。

Result: 该双拷贝拉格朗日量在四阶展开下仅由少数实参数约束；对某组特定参数选择，其与微扰远平行引力（perturbative teleparallel gravity）精确等价。

Conclusion: 结果表明远平行表述可能有助于从拉格朗日量层面更透明地呈现双拷贝结构。

Abstract: In this paper we analyze the double copy between gluon and graviton amplitudes within the context of teleparallel gravity. We first set up an effective Lagrangian based on minimal assumptions which reproduces the on-shell amplitudes stemming from the double-copy procedure. By straightforwardly computing the three- and four-point amplitude, this double-copy Lagrangian can be constrained up to a few real parameters when expanding to quartic order. For a specific choice of parameters, we find an exact equivalence with perturbative teleparallel gravity. This suggests that the teleparallel formulation might be well suited to make the double copy more transparent from a Lagrangian viewpoint.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [A colored look at anyons and parastatistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31841)
*Francesco Toppan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了颜色李（超）代数（color Lie (super)algebras）在物理学中的最新进展，指出该框架不仅能描述两类超出玻色子/费米子的仲统计（parastatistics），还可为其提供物理判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颜色李（超）代数自1978年由Rittenberg与Wyler提出后长期缺乏物理应用；近期研究才证明其与物理的相关性，作者因而整理这些新进展以推动对仲统计与辫子统计（braid statistics）问题的"颜色视角"重新审视。

Method: 采用综述性梳理的方法：介绍颜色结构的基本定义——由阿贝尔群（abelian groups）分次、括号满足广义雅可比恒等式（generalized Jacobi identities），并将其应用于两类仲统计体系的描述。

Result: 颜色框架可描述并给出物理判据的两类对象为：i) 通过置换群（permutation group）交换、可存在于任意空间维度D的仲粒子；ii) 通过辫子群（braid group）交换、仅存在于D≤2空间的任意子（anyons）。

Conclusion: 颜色李（超）代数为仲统计与辫子统计问题提供了新的"颜色视角"（colored look），本文总结的相关进展构成了该方向更新的研究图景。

Abstract: Color Lie (super)algebras were introduced in 1978 by Rittenberg and Wyler as generalizations of ordinary Lie (super)algebras. These color structures, graded by abelian groups, are defined by brackets which satisfy generalized Jacobi identities. Only recently the relevance of color Lie (super)algebras to physical applications has been pointed out and proved. The color framework not only describes, but also provides physical signatures for two broad types of parastatistics (particles beyond ordinary bosons/fermions): i) paraparticles which are exchanged via the permutation group and can exist in any space dimension D and ii) anyons (exchanged via the braid group), which exist up to D=2 space dimensions. This paper presents an update of the recent advancements which motivate a brand new "colored look" at questions involving parastatistics and braid statistics.

</details>


### [209] [On the computation of Green's function in layered elastic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31852)
*Heng Yuan,Wenzhong Zhang,Bo Wang,Wei Cai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于矩阵基表示的傅里叶域递推格式，用于计算三维层状介质中时谐弹性波的格林函数，计算代价与层数呈线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维层状介质弹性波格林函数计算在多层情形下代价高、不稳定，难以支撑层状介质弹性波散射的分析与高效数值模拟，故需构造可递推、线性代价且数值稳定的求解格式。

Method: 在保持横向旋转对称的前提下，基于傅里叶域（Fourier domain）的矩阵基表示（matrix basis representation），结合经典界面反射-透射理论（interface reaction-transmission theory），将格林函数（Green's function）解耦为SH波与P-SV波，并用五个标量函数递推求解。

Result: 该格式对总层数具有线性计算代价，且与震源和接收点所在层位无关；所得结果构成稳定的数值方法，可直接用于空间域中的汉克尔变换（Hankel transform）求值。

Conclusion: 该递推格式为层状介质中弹性波散射的分析与高效数值模拟奠定了基础，兼具稳定性与线性复杂度。

Abstract: We develop a recursive scheme for the computation of Green's function for time-harmonic elastic wave in three-dimensional layered media, based on a matrix basis representation in the Fourier domain and a classic interface reaction-transmission theory. With transversal rotational symmetry preserved, the scheme decouples the Green's function into SH waves and P-SV waves using five scalar functions, which are recursively solved at linear cost with respect to the number of total layers, regardless of the layers the source and target particles locate in. The resulting scheme leads to a stable numerical method ready for Hankel transform evaluation in the spatial domain, constituting a foundation for the analysis and efficient numerical simulation of elastic wave scattering in layered media.

</details>


### [210] [Cantor spectrum at a fixed Diophantine frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31904)
*Rui Han,Wilhelm Schlag,Fan Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明对任意固定 Diophantine 频率，在 Lyapunov 指数为正的区域，单频解析准周期 Schrödinger 算子（quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators）的谱呈 Cantor 结构；证明改进了间隙构造法，避免消除双共振与三共振，并借助 IDS 的 Hölder 连续性等新工具导出矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 Goldstein 型间隙构造（gap construction）为证明谱的 Cantor 结构需消除双共振与三共振（double and triple resonances），论证繁琐；本文旨在给出避开该消除步骤的新证明路径，并覆盖任意固定 Diophantine 频率、正 Lyapunov 指数（Lyapunov exponents）的情形。

Method: 沿用 Goldstein 型间隙构造的总策略但去除共振消除；引入积分态密度（integrated density of states, IDS）的 Hölder 连续性（Hölder continuity）以排除有限体积本征值参数化斜率过小的能量，结合 Dirichlet 行列式零点数关于体积的线性界（linear bound）与鸽笼论证（pigeonholing）得到本征值间距下界；再由两条斜率相反的平移 Rellich 函数（Rellich functions）交叉产生局部极大，其本征函数边界值指数小，配合有限体积本征值分离迫使 DOS 质量向极大处集中，而加速度（acceleration）常值区间上 Lyapunov 指数解析、DOS 为 Lipschitz，最终在极大附近导出矛盾。

Result: 对每个固定 Diophantine 频率、在正 Lyapunov 指数区域，谱集被证明为 Cantor 集（Cantor set），即谱中存在大量空隙（gaps）；证明同时给出本征值间距的定量下界与 DOS 在相关区间上的集中性刻画。

Conclusion: 谱的 Cantor 结构可在不消除双、三共振的前提下成立；IDS 的 Hölder 连续性、Dirichlet 行列式零点线性界与加速度常值区间上 DOS 的 Lipschitz 性构成关键工具。

Abstract: We establish that the spectrum of one-frequency analytic quasiperiodic Schrodinger operators exhibits Cantor structure in the regime of positive Lyapunov exponents for every fixed Diophantine frequency. The proof follows the general strategy of the gap construction by Goldstein and the second author, but avoids the elimination of double and triple resonances used in the original argument. A novel ingredient is the use of Holder continuity of the integrated density of states (IDS) to exclude energies at which eigenvalue parametrizations in finite volume have small slopes. We also use a linear bound in the volume on the number of zeros of Dirichlet determinants, and a pigeonholing argument to obtain lower bounds on the distances between eigenvalues. The crossing of two translated Rellich functions with opposite slopes produces a local maximum whose eigenfunctions have exponentially small boundary values. Together with finite volume eigenvalue separation, this forces the DOS mass to concentrate near the maximum. However, on an interval of constancy of the acceleration, the Lyapunov exponent is analytic, and hence the DOS is Lipschitz there. This ultimately leads to a contradiction near the maximum.

</details>


### [211] [Field-Dependent Kinetics, Exact Kinks, and a Moduli-Space Route from Self-Dual Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31971)
*Ji Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究1+1维带场依赖动能系数的标量场模型，通过逆问题构造出让给定tanh扭结（kink）精确解成立的六次势（sextic potential），并给出能量闭式、真空曲率关系、可容许性判据与谱稳定性数值证据；同时指出该变形模型一般不可积，与自对偶杨-米尔斯（SDYM）的正确联系应经由模空间（moduli space）途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机来自自对偶杨-米尔斯（self-dual Yang-Mills, SDYM）理论作为可积方程生成器的角色，用以考察场依赖动能系数的标量场模型及其与非可积变形的关系。

Method: 方法包括推导Euler-Lagrange方程、行波约化（traveling-wave reduction）与首次积分，求解逆问题：给定动能结构与预设的tanh扭结，确定使其精确成立的势；其中空间二次变形配正则时间部分给出六次势，并结合Bogomolny型表述、真空展开、自伴涨落算子（fluctuation operator）与数值谱分析。

Result: 主要结果有：(i) 与奇参数无关的闭式扭结能量，但该参数使能量密度非对称；(ii) 将谱与真空处动能函数相联系的真空曲率关系；(iii) 关于速度族的两个命题及一个引理，表明单一速度数据仅探测动能函数的一种组合；(iv) 统一的可容许性分析，空间动能函数的正性控制双曲性、扭结上的势界与真空质量；(v) 自伴涨落算子具闭式势、渐近为真空质量、存在精确平移零模，数值谱在可容许区域无负模。

Conclusion: SDYM的精确约化给出可积方程，而该变形模型一般不可积、不能作为精确约化；正确桥梁是模空间途径——SDYM约化为Bogomolny方程，其BPS单极子与二重子（dyon）动力学产生场依赖动能项，任意光滑一维模空间度规二阶展开即得本文的二次模型，分离二重子的Taub-NUT度规给出显式实现；六次势在具体规范理论模动力学中的完整识别仍为开放问题，被列为研究计划。

Abstract: We study scalar-field models in 1+1 dimensions with field-dependent kinetic coefficients, motivated by self-dual Yang-Mills (SDYM) theory as a generator of integrable equations. We derive the Euler-Lagrange equation, traveling-wave reduction, and first integral, and solve the inverse problem: given a kinetic structure and a prescribed tanh kink, which potential makes it exact? A quadratic spatial deformation with a canonical temporal sector yields a sextic potential. We obtain: (i) a closed-form kink energy with a Bogomolny-type formulation, independent of the odd parameter, which nonetheless shapes the energy density asymmetrically; (ii) vacuum curvature relations tying the spectrum to the kinetic function at the vacua; (iii) two propositions on speed families, plus a lemma showing single-speed data probe only one combination of the kinetic functions; (iv) a unified admissibility analysis in which positivity of the spatial kinetic function controls hyperbolicity, the potential bound along the kink, and the vacuum masses; (v) a self-adjoint fluctuation operator with a closed-form potential whose asymptotes are the vacuum masses, an exact translation zero mode, and numerical spectra with no negative mode on the admissible region, evidence for spectral stability. Exact SDYM reductions yield integrable equations, whereas the deformed model is generically non-integrable and cannot be an exact reduction. The correct bridge is the moduli-space route: SDYM reduces to the Bogomolny equations, whose BPS monopole and dyon dynamics produce field-dependent kinetic terms; any smooth one-dimensional moduli metric expands, to second order, to the quadratic model studied here, and the Taub-NUT metric of well-separated dyons gives an explicit realization. Full identification of the sextic potential within a specific gauge-theory moduli dynamics remains open and is formulated as a research program.

</details>


### [212] [Exact transport exponents for random mosaic operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32132)
*Fumihiko Nakano,Xu Xia*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文确定“二期随机马赛克算子（period-two random mosaic operators）”的指数时间平均输运指数，给出对所有非零有限支撑态成立的精确公式 $\beta_f^{\pm}(q)=\max\{0,2/3-\nu_H(f)/(3q)\}$，且不要求分布具有密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究交替结构中随机势（偶数位零势、奇数位 iid 势）的量子输运（quantum transport）行为，旨在刻画不同初始态下矩增长幂次随阶数 $q$ 的变化，并厘清零能解与格点可除性对输运的抑制作用。

Method: 结合概率上的“概率为一事件”论证与确定性上下界：通过保留零能处奇异归一化（singular normalization）中两个多项式源通道的消没阶（vanishing orders），获得匹配的预解式界（resolvent bounds）；同时给出对任意有界正构型的确定性下界。

Result: 对每个有界、严格正、非常数的单点分布律（single-site law），在概率为一的事件上，所有非零有限支撑态 $f$ 与所有 $q>0$ 满足 $\beta_f^-(q)=\beta_f^+(q)=\max\{0,2/3-\nu_H(f)/(3q)\}$；大矩指数恒为 $2/3$，而两子晶格（sublattices）上的点源二阶矩增长幂分别为 $1$ 与 $1/3$。

Conclusion: 输运指数由整数 $\nu_H(f)$（由被 $H$ 整除性及与有界零能解（zero-energy solution）的抵消决定）完全刻画，二阶矩结果与高矩结果可统一表述；结论不依赖分布律的密度假设。

Abstract: We determine the exponentially time-averaged transport exponents of period-two random mosaic operators, with zero potential on the even sites and iid potential on the odd sites. For every bounded, strictly positive, nonconstant single-site law, on one event of probability one, $β_f^-(q)=β_f^+(q)=\max\{0,2/3-ν_H(f)/(3q)\}$ for all nonzero finitely supported states $f$ and all $q>0$. The positive odd integer $ν_H(f)$ is determined by divisibility by $H$ and cancellation against the bounded zero-energy solution. In particular, the large-moment exponent is $2/3$ for every such state, while point sources on the two sublattices have second-moment growth powers $1$ and $1/3$. We obtain matching resolvent bounds by retaining the vanishing orders of two polynomial source channels through the singular normalization at zero energy. We also prove deterministic lower bounds for every bounded positive configuration. No density assumption is imposed on the law.

</details>


### [213] [Group classification of a class of systems of nonlinear diffusion equations. I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32136)
*Oleksandr A. Voloshyn,Mykola I. Serov,Olena O. Vaneeva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究一类(1+1)维非线性扩散方程组的李对称，求出等价代数与最大李不变代数的核，证明恰有九个不等价子类存在李对称扩张，并对第一个子类完成穷尽群分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性扩散方程组在李对称（Lie symmetry）意义下的群分类尚不完整，需厘清该类系统的等价代数结构及可产生对称扩张的子类划分。

Method: 计算该类(1+1)维非线性扩散方程组的等价代数（equivalence algebra）及最大李不变代数（maximal Lie invariance algebra）的核，并据此进行子类划分与群分类（group classification）。

Result: 证明恰好存在九个不等价子类（inequivalent subclasses），其对应扩散系统可容许李对称扩张；并给出第一个子类的完全群分类。

Conclusion: 该结果刻画了此类非线性扩散方程组的对称结构骨架，为后续求解与约化提供分类基础。

Abstract: We study the Lie symmetries of a class of systems of (1 + 1)-dimensional nonlinear diffusion equations. The equivalence algebra and the kernel of the maximal Lie invariance algebras of this class are found. We prove that there exist exactly nine inequivalent subclasses of this class for which the corresponding diffusion systems admit Lie symmetry extensions. We also carry out the exhaustive group classification of the systems of nonlinear diffusion equations from the first subclass.

</details>


### [214] [The polarization bundle of a bending ray: nested projections and helicity Chern numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32213)
*Didier Felbacq,Emmanuel Rousseau*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 将弱各向异性介质中光线传播的偏振问题几何化为复向量丛上的联络与曲率问题：由复化Levi-Civita联络的曲率同时导出Rytov旋转、Pancharatnam–Berry相位与陈数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱各向异性（possibly absorbing）介质中传播光线的偏振演化，需要一个统一的几何框架来刻画Rytov旋转（Rytov rotation）与Pancharatnam–Berry相位及其拓扑性质。

Method: 在方向球（direction sphere）S^2上的秩二复向量丛（rank-two complex vector bundle）E_⊥ 中，将偏振态视为 Z=CP(E_⊥) 的点；把 C^3 的平坦导数投影到 E_⊥ 得到联络 D（S^2 的复化Levi-Civita联络），再将 D 投影到偏振线丛上得到 tautological 线丛的联络，并计算其曲率在垂直、水平与混合分量上的形式。

Result: 该线丛的曲率在垂直部分为 iω_FS（Fubini–Study形式）、水平部分为 i s_3 ω（ω为S^2面积形式，s_3为圆偏振Stokes参数）、混合部分为零；Rytov旋转、Pancharatnam–Berry相位及陈数（Chern numbers）c_1(L_±)=∓2 均由该曲率得出。

Conclusion: 限制到螺旋度截面（helicity sections）上，该曲率给出全局光滑圆偏振Jones向量（circular Jones vector）存在的阻碍，尽管圆偏振态本身是全局定义的。

Abstract: We consider a ray propagating in a weakly anisotropic, possibly absorbing medium. The transverse planes of the propagation directions form a rank-two complex vector bundle $E_\perp$ over the sphere $S^2$ of directions. The polarization states are the points of $Z=\mathbb{CP}(E_\perp)$. Projecting the flat derivative of $\mathbb{C}^3$ onto $E_\perp$ gives a connection $D$, the complexified Levi-Civita connection of $S^2$, whose holonomy is the Rytov rotation. Projecting $D$ onto a polarization line gives the connection of the tautological line bundle over $Z$, whose holonomy is the Pancharatnam--Berry phase. For the horizontal distribution defined by $D$, the curvature of this bundle is $iω_{\mathrm{FS}}$ on the vertical part, $i s_3\,ω$ on the horizontal part, and zero on the mixed part. Here $ω_{\mathrm{FS}}$ is the Fubini--Study form, $ω$ the area form of $S^2$, and $s_3$ the circular Stokes parameter. The Rytov rotation, the Pancharatnam--Berry phase and the Chern numbers $c_1(L_\pm)=\mp 2$ are all obtained from this curvature. Restricted to the helicity sections, it gives the obstruction to a globally smooth circular Jones vector, although the circular polarization states are globally defined.

</details>


### [215] [Compressional-shear wave interference and wave suppression at exceptional-point gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32219)
*Gabriel Núñez,William J. Parnell,Raphaël C. Assier*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用传递矩阵方法结合 Bloch 定理，揭示层状弹性介质中由奇异点（exceptional points, EPs）界定的频率禁隙源于压缩波与剪切波场的相消干涉，并指出对应波数满足类布拉格条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂弹性材料中奇异点对波传播的影响已被广泛研究，但其存在的物理机制仍不清楚，本文旨在给出奇异点的直接物理解释。

Method: 采用传递矩阵（transfer matrix）表述并结合 Bloch 定理，分析层状介质中压缩模式与剪切模式的耦合与干涉行为。

Result: 发现由奇异点界定的禁隙起源于压缩波场与剪切波场之间的相消干涉（destructive interference）；相应波数与原胞厚度满足类布拉格条件（Bragg-like condition），使总波场成为驻波（standing wave）；在有限层状介质中，该机制表现为禁隙内两种模式均无法传播。

Conclusion: 上述结果为奇异点提供了直接的物理解释，将禁隙与两波场的干涉及驻波形成联系起来。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) in complex elastic materials signify the coalescence of compressional and shear modes and bound a frequency gap where waves become evanescent. While their effects on wave propagation have been extensively studied, the physical mechanisms underlying their existence remain elusive. In this Letter, employing a transfer matrix formulation combined with Bloch's theorem, we demonstrate that the gaps bounded by EPs in layered media originate from destructive interference between the compressional and shear wave fields. We further reveal that the corresponding wavenumbers satisfy a Bragg-like condition with the unit cell thickness, causing the total wave field to become stationary, forming a standing wave. Finally, a finite layered medium is considered, where this mechanism is shown to manifest as forbidden wave propagation for both modes in the gap. These results provide a direct physical interpretation of EPs.

</details>


### [216] [Correlation functions of 2D critical models via a KPZ relation for conformal blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32485)
*Gefei Cai,Shengzhi Jin,Xin Sun,Baojun Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 KPZ 关系扩展至关联函数层面，利用中心电荷互补（c 与 26-c）的 Virasoro 共形块之间的积分反演关系，从 Liouville 量子引力的量子对应量推出二维临界系统的盘面关联函数，并可处理此前未解的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ 关系（Knizhnik–Polyakov–Zamolodchikov relation）原本用于从 Liouville 量子引力中的标度指数推得二维临界系统的标度指数，但其在关联函数层面的推广仍有限，且部分模型缺乏求解手段。

Method: 建立中心电荷互补（complementary central charges）c 与 26-c 的 Virasoro 共形块（Virasoro conformal blocks）之间的积分反演关系（integral inversion relation），据此把关联函数的 KPZ 关系推广到更一般情形。

Result: 在多个模型中，由量子对应量结合 KPZ 关系导出两点盘面关联函数（2-point disk correlation）；临界环路模型（critical loop models）的结果与已知结论一致，并覆盖此前未解的情形，如 Fuzzy Potts 模型。

Conclusion: 该方法与共形自举（conformal bootstrap）互补，为二维临界系统的关联函数计算提供了新的通用途径。

Abstract: The Knizhnik--Polyakov--Zamolodchikov (KPZ) relation allows scaling exponents of two-dimensional (2D) critical systems to be derived from their counterparts in Liouville quantum gravity. We extend this framework to correlation functions based on an integral inversion relation between Virasoro conformal blocks with complementary central charges $c$ and $26-c$. We demonstrate this method in various models by deriving the 2-point disk correlation from their quantum counterpart and the KPZ relation. Results on critical loop models were previously known, but our method is robust enough to include many scenarios previously unsolved, such as the Fuzzy Potts model. Our approach is complementary to the conformal bootstrap.

</details>


### [217] [On Lipschitz equicontinuity of random Schrödinger operators on lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32521)
*Jan Fischer,David Hasler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上的随机薛定谔算子（random Schrödinger operators），证明对一稠密的单站点概率分布（single site probability distributions）集合，态密度（density of states）关于耦合强度（coupling strength）一致有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅给出研究对象与目标命题；从选题看，态密度对耦合强度的一致有界性属于随机算子谱理论中的定量刻画问题。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上的随机薛定谔算子框架下，针对一个稠密的单站点概率分布集合建立态密度的一致上界估计。

Result: 证明了对稠密的单站点概率分布集合，态密度在耦合强度上一致有界（uniformly bounded）。

Conclusion: 态密度关于耦合强度的一致有界性在一类稠密的单站点分布中成立，摘要未进一步给出推论或推广讨论。

Abstract: We consider random Schrödinger operators on $\mathbb{Z}^d$. We prove that the density of states is uniformly bounded in the coupling strength for a dense set of single site probability distributions.

</details>


### [218] [Krylov dynamics of deformed oscillator chains: localization, ballistic spreading, and indeterminacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32694)
*Tunde Joseph Osunmusanmi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要将 Krylov 复杂度（Krylov complexity）与半直线量子行走、正交多项式理论对应，研究 $(p,q)$-变形振子生成的 Krylov 链，按变形参数分出有界、弹道增长、$t^2$ 增长与 Jacobi 算子非本质自伴四类区域，并指出最后一种情形下复杂度需附加无穷远边界条件才有定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出 Krylov 与 spread complexity 可映射为以 Lanczos 系数为跃迁幅的半直线量子行走，从而把 Krylov 动力学等价于正交多项式与 Jacobi 算子理论；作者借此框架考察 $(p,q)$-变形振子所张成的一大类 Krylov 链，以统一刻画不同增长行为及算子自伴性对动力学可定义性的影响。

Method: 以双参数 $(p,q)$-变形振子构造 Krylov 链，该链可插值于 Heisenberg–Weyl 链、双标度 SYK 的 $q$-振子链与 Lanczos 系数指数增长链之间。对变形位移链，将 spread complexity 识别为平均激发数，其变化率对应变形动量求积，二阶导数为 $\ddot C_K=2\omega^2\langle[a,a^\dagger]\rangle$，并借助逐阶 Lanczos 系数（即 Jacobi 算子矩阵元）分析矩问题的确定性。

Result: 变形参数划分四个区域：$\max(p,q)<1$ 时复杂度有界，属代数本身导致的局域化；$p=1,\ q<1$ 时呈弹道增长，渐近速度由 theta 型级数精确给出；$p=q=1$ 时为 $t^2$ 增长；$\max(p,q)>1$ 时 Jacobi 算子非本质自伴、矩问题不确定，Lanczos 系数不再决定动力学，奇偶截断分别收敛到两个反射对称的 $N$-极值解，奇支携带闭式权重的零能原子，自伴扩张给出早期重合、Krylov 波前抵达无穷后才分离的一族复杂度曲线。

Conclusion: Krylov 复杂度在 $\max(p,q)>1$ 区域只有在指定无穷 Krylov 指标的边界条件后才被定义；变形换向子沿 Krylov 链充当“力”，平均变形量控制早期增长。附录给出正交多项式流程及其在可精确求解链上的校验。

Abstract: Krylov and spread complexity map quantum dynamics onto a quantum walk on a half-line whose hopping amplitudes are the Lanczos coefficients, so that Krylov dynamics is the theory of orthogonal polynomials and Jacobi operators. We use this correspondence to study Krylov chains generated by the two-parameter $(p,q)$-deformed oscillator, which interpolates between the Heisenberg--Weyl chain, the $q$-oscillator chain of double-scaled SYK, and chains with exponentially growing Lanczos coefficients. For the deformed displacement chain the spread complexity is the mean excitation number, its rate is the deformed momentum quadrature, and its acceleration is $\ddot C_K=2ω^2\langle[a,a^\dagger]\rangle$, so the deformed commutator acts as a force along the Krylov chain and the mean deformation controls early-time growth. The deformation parameters separate four regimes. For $\max(p,q)<1$ the complexity stays bounded, a localization produced by the algebra alone. For $p=1$, $q<1$ it grows ballistically, with an exact asymptotic velocity given by a theta-type series. At $p=q=1$ it grows as $t^2$. For $\max(p,q)>1$ the Jacobi operator is not essentially self-adjoint, the moment problem is indeterminate, and the Lanczos coefficients no longer determine the dynamics. Even and odd truncations converge to two different evolutions, the two reflection-symmetric N-extremal solutions of the moment problem, the odd one carrying a zero-energy atom of closed-form weight; the self-adjoint extensions yield a family of complexity curves that coincide at early times and separate once the Krylov wavefront reaches infinity. Krylov complexity in this regime is thus defined only once a boundary condition at infinite Krylov index is specified. The orthogonal-polynomial procedure and its checks on exactly solvable chains are given in the appendices.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Morphogenic necking of surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31360)
*Farzan Vafa,Puskar Mondal,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文提出一个将形态发生素（morphogen）组织与表面几何耦合的内禀（intrinsic）框架，通过“平滑与形态发生素诱导变形相竞争”的最小几何模型解释薄表面走向拓扑转变（topological transition）的颈部收缩机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面形态发生（surface morphogenesis）跨越从亚细胞囊泡、细胞到器官与整体生物体的多个尺度，且常涉及拓扑变化，因此需要能描述薄表面拓扑转变的合适数学框架。

Method: 构建一个内禀几何框架，将形态发生素分布与表面几何耦合；提出平滑效应与形态发生素诱导变形相竞争的最小几何模型，并建立一个标量场与表面度规（surface metric）耦合的简单动力学模型。

Result: 模型揭示两种颈部收缩机制：形态发生素陡峭过渡处的梯度驱动（gradient-driven）收缩，以及局部形态发生素峰值附近的曲率驱动（curvature-driven）收缩。在简单轴对称（axisymmetric）例子中冻结形态发生素分布时，梯度驱动路径给出精确的代数收缩，曲率驱动路径给出局部指数收缩。

Conclusion: 形态发生素空间组织与表面几何的内禀耦合可定量调控表面形变，且当平滑与变形间的平衡被打破时，表面可经颈部收缩逼近拓扑转变；两类收缩路径具有不同的定量收缩律。

Abstract: Surface morphogenesis occurs across biological scales, from subcellular vesicles and cells to visceral organs, tissue organoids, and whole organisms. Since morphogenesis often involves changes in topology, a natural question is that of appropriate mathematical descriptions that provide an approach to topological transitions of thin surfaces. Here, we suggest a general intrinsic framework coupling morphogen organization to surface geometry. We propose a minimal geometric model in which smoothing competes with morphogen-induced deformation to regulate shape, while loss of this balance can bring a surface toward a topological transition via neck constriction. A simple dynamical model coupling a scalar field to the surface metric reveals two mechanisms: gradient-driven constriction at steep morphogen transitions and curvature-driven constriction near localized morphogen peaks. For frozen morphogen profiles in simple axisymmetric examples, the gradient-driven route gives exact algebraic contraction, while the curvature-driven route gives local exponential contraction.

</details>


### [220] [Three-phase contact lines as distinct molecular adsorption environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31404)
*Fabio Staniscia,Matej Kanduč*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过分子动力学模拟证明，三相接触线（three-phase contact line）并非两界面的几何交集，而是一个对两亲分子具有更强亲和力的独立热力学吸附环境，其吸附随体系尺寸缩小而愈发重要并可显著改变线张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触线广泛存在于液滴、弯月面、毛细桥及多孔介质等多相体系中，但低维边界上的分子吸附（molecular adsorption）长期缺乏研究，其热力学性质尚不明确。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），考察疏水基底上接触线对不同结构两亲分子（amphiphiles）的吸附行为，并分析线吸附与面吸附的标度关系及随烷基链长的变化。

Result: 稀溶液区线吸附与面吸附呈普适标度关系，高浓度下行为依赖分子结构；接触线亲和力随烷基链（alkyl chain）长度指数增长，导致更低浓度即出现拥挤，对痕量杂质极为敏感；线、面、体相布居随体系尺寸标度不同，小尺度下线吸附显著改变分子分配。

Conclusion: 接触线富集可预测使线张力（line tension）降低，降幅与其本征量级相当，可能对异相成核（heterogeneous nucleation）产生重要影响；分子吸附应被视为三相接触线的基本属性。

Abstract: In wetting and multiphase systems, three-phase contact lines form where liquid, solid, and gas phases meet, creating effectively one-dimensional boundaries with distinct molecular environments. Despite their ubiquity in droplets, liquid menisci, capillary bridges, porous materials, and confined multiphase systems, molecular adsorption at these lower-dimensional boundaries has remained largely unexplored. Here, we use molecular dynamics simulations to show that the contact line on a hydrophobic substrate is not merely the geometric intersection of two interfaces but a distinct thermodynamic adsorption environment with a substantially stronger affinity for amphiphilic molecules than either neighboring interface. The investigated amphiphiles of different molecular structures exhibit a common scaling relation between line and surface adsorption in the dilute regime, while their behavior becomes molecule-specific at higher concentrations. Furthermore, contact-line affinity increases exponentially with alkyl-chain length, leading to crowding at progressively lower concentrations and making contact lines extraordinarily sensitive to trace amphiphilic impurities. Because line, surface, and bulk populations scale differently with system size, contact-line adsorption becomes increasingly important at small length scales, where it can substantially alter molecular partitioning. Contact-line enrichment is predicted to reduce the line tension by an amount comparable to its intrinsic magnitude, with potentially substantial consequences for heterogeneous nucleation. Our results establish molecular adsorption as a fundamental property of three-phase contact lines with implications for wetting and multiphase systems across length scales.

</details>


### [221] [Fluids under tension: dynamics, mechanics, and structure of sparse active adhesive networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31838)
*Alex T. Grigas,M. Lisa Manning*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文研究张力下稀疏力学网络（sparse mechanical networks）如何在不断裂塌缩的情况下实现流动，指出需要主动活动（activity）抵消聚集不稳定性（clustering instability）且黏附解绑（adhesive unbinding）需足够随机化；不同活动机制（自驱动爬行与力偶极）导致截然不同的集体动力学、密度均匀性与力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏力学网络在张力下随键断裂易发生凝胶化/聚集不稳定性（gelation/clustering instabilities），通常无法在不塌缩的前提下重构；然而近期发现若干生物材料能在保持张力与均匀性的同时流动，本文旨在揭示产生这种“张力下流体”所需的关键要素。

Method: 通过模型研究探讨实现张力下流动的关键成分，并系统比较不同实现细节（活动类型、解绑随机性）对材料动力学、结构与力学的影响；具体对比以力单极（force monopole）实现的自驱动爬行（self-propelled crawling）模型与以力偶极（force dipole）表示的相互拉扯模型。

Result: 活动必须抵消聚集不稳定性，且黏附解绑须足够随机，否则活动被抵消并使系统进入静息吸收态（quiescent absorbing state）。爬行模型驱动长波长集体运动，产生反超均匀密度（anti-hyperuniform density）；力偶极模型驱动无标度集体运动（scale-free collective motion）并给出更均匀的密度。两模型均表现出奇异力学：单键应力仅通过键周转（bond turnover）弛豫，而全局张力从不弛豫；爬行模型中网络结构弛豫快于应力模式，表明材料在粒子位置重构时仍保留应力状态记忆（memory）。

Conclusion: 研究表明可通过分析涌现材料性质来区分生物系统中的活动机制，并可在可重构活性物质（reconfigurable active matter）系统中设计奇异的涌现性质。

Abstract: Sparse mechanical networks under tension are inherently prone to gelation/clustering instabilities as bonds break, so they typically cannot reconfigure without collapsing. Subverting this expectation, several biological materials have recently been found to maintain tension and uniformity while flowing. Here, we identify key ingredients needed to generate this fluid under tension and explore how details of the implementation affect ma- terial dynamics, structure, and mechanics. We find that activity must counteract the clustering instability, and that adhesive unbinding must be sufficiently stochastic so that it does not cancel out the activity and drive the system to a quiescent absorbing state. We find that the nature of the activity dramatically changes the material properties and glassy dynamics: self-propelled crawling, implemented as a force monopole, drives collective motion over long wavelengths, resulting in anti-hyperuniform density, whereas a force dipole representing par- ticles pulling on each other drives scale-free collective motion and more uniform density. Both models exhibit exotic mechanics: stresses in each bond relax only through bond turnover, but the global tension never relaxes. In addition, in the crawling model the structure of the network relaxes faster than the pattern of stresses, sug- gesting the material retains a memory of its stress state even as the particle positions reconfigure. Together, this work shows that we can distinguish between activity mechanisms in biological systems by analyzing emergent materials properties, and design exotic emergent properties in reconfigurable active matter systems.

</details>


### [222] [Antiferroelectric Fluids with Hierarchical Polar Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31959)
*Hiroya Nishikawa,Hina Saito,Tetsu Katayama,Atsuko Nihonyanagi,Dennis Kwaria,Hiroyuki Inuzuka,Junxi Liu,Go Watanabe,Fumito Araoka*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究报道了一类同源系列液晶中由末端烷基链增长与极性核心相互作用竞争所产生的调制型反铁电（antiferroelectric, AFE）序，并识别出两种二维（2D）AFE柱状晶格结构，提出层级结构模型以解释其相序列与超结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电向列相（ferroelectric nematic, NF）的发现推动了沿分子长轴具有极性序的流体研究，但其反铁电对应物研究较少，因此需要探究AFE序的形成与调控机制。

Method: 在同源系列中研究末端烷基链延长与极性核心-核心相互作用之间的竞争，结合多重锐利X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）峰分析与分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟。

Result: 该系列表现出伴随SmC型鳍状（cybotactic）关联的调制AFE序，AFE相可延续至末端烷基链含17个碳原子的同系物；降温过程中出现三种不同相序列，形成两类二维AFE晶格，XRD显示矩形与斜方晶格结构，支持其归类为AFE柱状相。

Conclusion: 提出层级结构模型：局部取向一致的极性分子占据极性柱内有限宽度的薄区段，柱占据二维晶格位点且相邻柱极化方向相反；该框架可解释不同前驱相序列，并将柱间堆积与AFE超结构联系起来。

Abstract: The discovery of the ferroelectric nematic (NF) phase has accelerated research on fluids exhibiting polar order along the molecular long axis. However, their antiferroelectric (AFE) counterparts remain less explored. Here, we investigate how terminal alkyl-chain elongation competes with polar core-core interactions in a homologous series. This series exhibits modulated AFE order accompanied by SmC-type cybotactic correlations, with the AFE phase persisting in homologs bearing terminal alkyl chains of up to 17 carbon atoms. Three distinct phase sequences on cooling lead to two types of two-dimensional (2D) AFE lattices. Multiple sharp X-ray diffraction (XRD) peaks reveal rectangular and oblique lattice structures, supporting their classification as AFE columnar phases. XRD analysis and molecular dynamics (MD) simulations support a hierarchical structural model in which locally aligned polar molecules occupy thin segments of finite width within polar columns. The columns occupy the sites of a 2D lattice, with neighboring columns polarized in opposite directions. This model provides a structural framework for understanding the distinct precursor phase sequences and links packing between columns to the resulting AFE superstructures.

</details>


### [223] [Covalent Welding of Two-Dimensional Molecular Sheets into Ultra-Stiff, Super-Tough Polymeric Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32291)
*Xiaoli Gong,Guohu Luo,Jiaxin Wu,Jiayu Chen,Zhengqiao Yin,Zhipeng Yan,Jingran Chen,Bowen Tang,Le Tang,Yuxiao Wu,Guanling Wu,Li Peng,Qihang Wang,Yinglong Hu,Yue Zhu,Yuan Yu,Keyan Liu,Xiaozhou Liu,Huanhuan Wu,Yakai Lin,Yifei Xu,Zhiliang Pan,Hao Ma,Man Li,Hongjun Zhang,Dacheng Wei,Youpeng Zhang,Yao Fu,Yongxiang Hu,Yuwen Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作通过将外围预接枝柔性连接链的二维聚芳酰胺（polyaramid）片层在固态下共价焊接，构建出二维高取向分子网络（2D highly oriented molecular network, 2D HOMN），在片层间实现高效载荷共享与能量耗散，从而把二维分子片层的优异性能传递至宏观尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高分子由零维单体（zero-dimensional monomers）构建，应力仅沿离散的一维链传递；受构象熵（conformational entropy）驱动，链在三维空间中卷曲，阻碍有效应力传递并限制极限力学性能。二维分子片层（2D molecular sheets）具有本征面内刚性，但现有组装体中片内应力传递高效、片间传递薄弱，优异性能难以在宏观尺度体现。

Method: 以柔性连接链（flexible linkers）预接枝于二维聚芳酰胺片层外围，再通过固态共价焊接（solid-state covalent welding）将组装后的片层连接，形成片层间共价连通（intersheet covalent connectivity）的二维高取向分子网络。

Result: 焊接薄膜的模量与强度分别达 69.8 和 3.9 GPa，可与 Kevlar 29 纤维相当；冲击韧性（impact toughness）较 Kevlar 复合材料高出最多一个数量级。片层间共价连接还使该材料对气体分子与离子不可渗透（impermeable），并具备抗辐射性与高效热输运能力。

Conclusion: 研究将二维分子基元（2D molecular motifs）确立为异质单体构建单元（heterogeneous monomeric building blocks），并为构筑多功能聚合物网络提供了通用策略，突破了传统一维链范式对宏观力学性能的限制。

Abstract: Since the inception of macromolecular science, polymers have been constructed from zero-dimensional monomers, a paradigm that funnels all stress through discrete one-dimensional chains. Driven by conformational entropy, polymer chains inevitably coil in three-dimensional space, precluding effective stress transfer, thereby compromising the ultimate mechanical performance. Two-dimensional (2D) molecular sheets, with intrinsic in-plane rigidity, could in principle circumvent this constraint. Yet in existing assemblies, where stress transfers efficiently within each molecular sheet but poorly between them, their exceptional properties fail to translate to the macroscale. Here, we demonstrate a 2D highly oriented molecular network (2D HOMN), an ultra-stiff, super-tough polymeric material constructed by the solid-state covalent welding of assembled 2D polyaramid sheets with flexible linkers pre-grafted at their peripheries. Enabled by efficient intersheet load sharing and energy dissipation, the welded films rival the modulus and strength of Kevlar 29 fibers, reaching 69.8 and 3.9 GPa, with impact toughness up to an order of magnitude beyond Kevlar composites. This designed intersheet covalent connectivity further renders the 2D HOMN impermeable to gas molecules and ions, resilient to radiation and efficient in thermal transport. This work establishes 2D molecular motifs as heterogeneous monomeric building blocks and provides a general strategy for constructing multifunctional polymeric networks.

</details>


### [224] [Neural-network-inferred initial guesses for photoelastic force inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32312)
*Jr-Jiun Lin,Jih-Chiang,Tsai*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文提出接触区域力推断（Contact-Region Inference of Forces, CRIF）方法，用卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network）从单个接触附近的图像直接推断力矢量，为光弹圆盘（photoelastic disks）接触力反演的迭代拟合提供初始猜测，并在合成与实验图像上验证其优于 PeGS 流程中的 $G^2$ 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光弹圆盘接触力反演（contact force inversion）已成为颗粒研究（granular research）的标准工具，但迭代收敛正确与否关键取决于力矢量的初始猜测，传统方法（如 PeGS 中的 $G^2$）在初始猜测质量上存在不足。

Method: 训练卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network），以单个接触附近区域的图像为输入推断对应力矢量，即 CRIF 方法；并将其扩展至具有内置轴对称残余应力（axisymmetric residual stress）的圆盘情形，例如圆盘浸没于流体时常见的情况。

Result: 在合成圆盘图像测试中，相较广泛采用的 PeGS 流程里的 $G^2$ 方法，CRIF 显著提升拟合精度，尤其对接触数多、含高阶条纹（fringe orders）大力的情况；在实验堆积（experimental packing）上，以 CRIF 为初值的拟合在强接触处满足力互易性（force reciprocity），而 $G^2$ 初值的拟合不满足。

Conclusion: 神经网络推断的初始猜测可实质改善光弹颗粒接触力反演的准确性与物理一致性，并可推广到含残余应力的实验场景。

Abstract: The inversion of contact forces between closely packed photoelastic disks has become a standard tool in the granular research community. The process iteratively fits the image generated by a forward model to the experimental image, and the correct convergence of the iteration depends critically on the initial guess of the force vectors. In this work, we train a convolutional neural network to infer a force vector from the image in the vicinity of individual contacts. This method, called Contact-Region Inference of Forces, or CRIF, provides the initial guess for the iteration. When tested with synthetic disk images, our approach substantially improves the accuracy of fitting results in comparison to that from the $G^2$ method in the widely adopted PeGS pipeline [Rev. Sci. Instrum. 88, 051808 (2017)], particularly for disks with many contacts and large forces involving high fringe orders. In addition, we extend the method to disks with a built-in axisymmetric residual stress, which often arises when photoelastic disks are submerged in fluids. Finally, on an experimental packing, fits started from the CRIF guess satisfy force reciprocity at strong contacts where fits started from $G^2$ do not.

</details>


### [225] [X-ray induced structural and dynamical changes in supramolecular polymer networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32349)
*Niels C. Gießelmann,Yasothaa Thavayogarajah,Tobias Eklund,Louisa E. Kraft,Isabell Zick,Yizhi Liu,Fiona Berner,Felix Lehmkühler,Fabian Westermeier,Mostafa Ahmadi,Sebastian Seiffert,Katrin Amann-Winkel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究考察不同剂量率下X射线辐照对金属超分子四臂聚乙二醇（tetra-PEG）网络的影响，结合WAXS/SAXS与X射线光子相关谱发现辐照诱导预结晶式重构（伴随水排出）及复杂动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线散射（X-ray scattering）研究软物质（soft matter）时，束流诱导效应（beam-induced effects）通常是关键但常被忽视的因素，因实验多假设体系未受扰动；本文旨在厘清X射线对具有瞬态交联（transient interlinkage）与自修复能力的金属超分子（metallo-supramolecular）tetra-PEG网络的影响。

Method: 在依赖剂量率（dose rate）的条件下，采用广角与小角X射线散射（wide- and small-angle X-ray scattering）探测微观与介观（micro- and mesoscale）结构，并用X射线光子相关谱（X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy）表征动力学。

Result: 观察到聚合物链在多个长度尺度上发生重构，表明电离辐射（ionizing radiation）诱导了预结晶（pre-crystallization）步骤，并伴随水从网络中排出；动力学表现为先快后整体减慢的复杂过程，双时间相关函数（two-time correlation functions）呈振荡行为，反映网络中多重动力学群体（dynamic populations）的形成。

Conclusion: X射线会显著改变金属超分子tetra-PEG网络的结构与动力学，故此类散射实验需重视束流诱导效应；辐照下的重构与动力学异质性为理解该类自修复材料在束流中的行为提供了依据。

Abstract: Beam-induced effects are key considerations in X-ray scattering studies of soft matter. Because these experiments typically aim to probe the unperturbed system, the structural and dynamic changes induced by the beam often remain underexplored. In the study at hand, the influence of X-rays on metallo-supramolecular tetra-arm poly(ethylene glycol) (tetra-PEG) networks is studied in dependence of the dose rate. Based on their transient interlinkage, such materials have the ability to reconnect and self-heal mechanical damage. Here, wide- and small-angle X-ray Scattering were employed to understand the effect of X-rays on the micro- and mesoscale structure of such polymer networks. Dynamical effects were probed using X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy. A restructuring of the polymer chains in the network is observed over several length scales, indicating a pre-crystallization step induced by the ionizing radiation, accompanied by expulsion of water from the network. In conjunction, a complex dynamical behavior is observed, consisting of an initial fast process, followed by an overall slow-down during the restructuring process. The two-time correlation functions exhibit an oscillatory behavior related to the development of multiple dynamic populations in the polymer network.

</details>


### [226] [Odd Viscosity-Driven Phase Separation in Chiral Active Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32535)
*Xiaoyi Wu,Tzer Han Tan,Hongbo Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出双流体流体力学理论，将奇黏性推广至多组分体系，指出跨宇称破缺黏性使均匀混合态失稳，而各同手性畴内的奇黏性控制界面稳定性与动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向自旋的活性粒子二元混合物能自发分离为同手性畴（homochiral domains），但这一稳健去混合（demixing）机制此前理解不足，作者旨在揭示其流体力学根源。

Method: 建立双流体流体力学理论（two-fluid hydrodynamic theory），把奇黏性（odd viscosity）推广到多组分系统；引入跨宇称破缺黏性（cross parity-violating viscosities），使一个手性组分的应力与另一组分的应变率耦合；并结合对应力与速度关联的微观测量估计黏性。

Result: 跨宇称破缺黏性可使均匀混合物失稳，而同手性畴内的奇黏性决定界面稳定性与动力学；由微观关联得到的黏性估计可预测自发去混合及随后的畴粗化（domain coarsening）。

Conclusion: 奇黏性被确认为一种编程手性活性混合物相行为与畴形貌（domain morphology）的途径，为理解与设计此类体系的去混合提供理论框架。

Abstract: Binary mixtures of active particles spinning in opposite directions can spontaneously separate into homochiral domains, yet the mechanism behind this robust demixing remains poorly understood. Here, we establish a two-fluid hydrodynamic theory that generalizes odd viscosity to multicomponent systems. We show that cross parity-violating viscosities, which couple the stress of one chiral species to the strain rate of another, can destabilize the homogeneous mixture, while odd viscosity within each homochiral domain governs the interfacial stability and dynamics. Microscopic measurements of stress and velocity correlations yield viscosity estimates that predict spontaneous demixing followed by domain coarsening. These findings identify odd viscosity as a route to programming phase behavior and domain morphology in chiral active mixtures.

</details>


### [227] [Impact of Taylor-Aris dispersion on chemical patterns in confined channel flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32737)
*Yanis Baouche,Ramita Mondal,Christina Kurzthaler*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文利用不变流形（invariant manifold）方法将细长通道内的对流-扩散-反应方程降为一维模型，发现大佩克莱数（Péclet number）下经典图灵判据（Turing criterion）可被反转，通道流动既能触发亦能破坏化学图案形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学图案源于物种间的非线性相互作用，但受限通道流动如何影响图案形成尚缺乏系统认识；本文以Brusselator作为典型的激活-抑制（activator-inhibitor）模型研究这一问题。

Method: 采用适用于细长通道的不变流形方法，将对流-扩散-反应（advection-diffusion-reaction）方程降维至一维，物种以平均流速输运、分子扩散系数替换为经典Taylor-Aris弥散系数（dispersion coefficient）；并基于佩克莱数、通道细长度（slenderness）与物种扩散系数比给出相图（phase diagram），同时以直接数值模拟（direct numerical simulation）验证。

Result: 解析结果表明，在大佩克莱数下经典图灵判据可发生反转：即使分子扩散系数违反经典图灵条件，通道流动仍可触发图案形成，反之亦可破坏已有图案。数值模拟证实了上述预测，并显示平流条纹图案（其波长可由约化理论预测）及振荡动力学（oscillatory dynamics）。

Conclusion: 流体动力流动（hydrodynamic flow）可改变化学混合过程，从而深刻影响通道几何中的图案形成；约化理论为图案波长与相图提供了定量预测。

Abstract: Chemical patterns form through non-linear interactions between different species. We study how confined channel flows affect pattern formation using the Brusselator as a paradigmatic activator-inhibitor model. An invariant-manifold method for slender channels reduces the advection-diffusion-reaction equation to one spatial dimension, with species transported at the mean flow velocity and molecular diffusivities replaced by the classical Taylor-Aris dispersion coefficients. We obtain the phase diagram as a function of the Péclet number (i.e., the relative strength of advection to diffusion), the channel slenderness, and the diffusivity ratio of the species. Our analytical results demonstrate that the classical Turing criterion can become inverted at large Péclet numbers and, thus, channel flow can trigger pattern formation even when the molecular diffusivities violate the classical Turing condition, and conversely destroy pre-existing patterns. Both predictions are confirmed by direct numerical simulations, which show advected stripe patterns, whose wavelength can be predicted by our reduced theory, as well as oscillatory dynamics. These findings highlight that hydrodynamic flows can modify chemical mixing and thereby profoundly affect pattern formation in channel geometries.

</details>


### [228] [Snap-Through Symmetry Breaking in Multicellular Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32832)
*Martí Planasdemunt-Hospital,David Oriola*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文以两相黏弹性固体液滴为最小模型，揭示弹性-毛细竞争可将流体液滴经典的连续浸润（engulfing）转变改为不连续的突变式转变，并据此解释类原肠胚（gastruloid）自发对称破缺的体积分数阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多细胞体系中对称破缺（symmetry breaking）的起始受何种物理机制控制仍不清楚，类原肠胚（gastruloids）提供了自发分化与对称破缺的实验原型，需要可解析的最小理论模型。

Method: 构建两相黏弹性固体液滴（two-phase viscoelastic solid drop）作为组织极化（tissue polarization）的最小模型，分析弹性-毛细（elastocapillary）竞争对经典液滴 engulfing 转变的影响，并用依赖于液滴尺寸与相体积分数的铺展系数（spreading coefficient）描述该转变。

Result: 纯黏性极限下对称破缺为连续发生；引入体弹性（bulk elasticity）后转变为不连续的、突变式（snap-like）转变，可视为一种由尺寸与体积分数调控的浸润转变（wetting transition）。应用于类原肠胚时得到对称破缺的临界体积分数（critical volume fraction）。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一类普适的弹性-毛细转变（generic elastocapillary transition），使双相液滴（biphasic drops）能够自发对称破缺，并为发育时序（developmental timing）的稳健性提供自组织机制。

Abstract: Symmetry breaking is a fundamental process in biological organization. Yet the physical mechanisms that control its onset in multicellular systems remain poorly understood. Motivated by gastruloids -- multicellular aggregates in which cells differentiate and spontaneously break symmetry -- we study a two-phase viscoelastic solid drop as a minimal model of tissue polarization. We show that elastocapillary competition fundamentally alters the classical engulfing transition of fluid drops. While symmetry breaking occurs continuously in the purely viscous limit, bulk elasticity instead leads to a discontinuous snap-like transition. We demonstrate that this behavior can be understood as a wetting transition governed by a spreading coefficient which depends on the drop size and phase volume fraction. Applying our model to gastruloids, we find a critical volume fraction for symmetry breaking, providing a self-organized mechanism for robust developmental timing. Our results identify a generic elastocapillary transition that enables spontaneous symmetry breaking in biphasic drops.

</details>


### [229] [Geometry and topology of DNA defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32850)
*A. Fassino,E. Carlon,D. Marenduzzo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合几何建模、原子尺度模拟与真实DNA结构分析，提出以“骨架—碱基极向量”与描述单链碱基三维取向的四元数构成复合序参量，对DNA缺陷进行定量刻画，并揭示其类型、序列依赖性与动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA缺陷（DNA defects）常由损伤、蛋白结合或RNA聚合酶等分子酶作用产生，但缺乏对缺陷及其动力学的定量几何表征，研究旨在建立几何与生物化学之间的联系。

Method: 采用几何方法、原子级模拟（atomistic simulations）与真实DNA结构分析相结合；以骨架到碱基的极向量（backbone-to-base polar vector）和捕获单链碱基三维取向的四元数（quaternion）构成复合序参量（composite order parameter），并对施加弹性形变的DNA开展偏置分子动力学模拟（biased molecular dynamics simulations）。

Result: 识别出三类缺陷：极向量翻转（flips of the polar vector）、碱基堆叠缺陷（stacking defects of bases）以及二者兼具的复合缺陷（composite defects）；三类缺陷均出现在真实DNA结构中并具有功能相关性。模拟显示缺陷的形成与动力学强烈依赖序列和形变类型。

Conclusion: 研究在几何与生物化学之间建立了联系，所提框架还可用于识别RNA分子及核小体阵列（nucleosome arrays）中的缺陷。

Abstract: DNA defects arise frequently, either as a result of damage, protein binding or the action of molecular enzymes such as an RNA polymerase. Here we combine geometry, atomistic simulations and an analysis of real DNA structures to provide a quantitative characterisation of DNA defects and their dynamics. The composite order parameter describing defects geometrically is a backbone-to-base polar vector, together with a quaternion capturing 3D base orientation in a single strand. There are three types of defects: flips of the polar vector, stacking defects of bases, and composite defects encompassing both. All types of defects feature in realistic structures for DNA and have functional relevance. Additionally, biased molecular dynamics simulations of DNA with an imposed elastic deformation form flip or stacking defects whose abundance and dynamics strongly depend on sequence and type of deformation. Our results provide a link between geometry and biochemistry, and could also be applied, for instance, to identify defects in RNA molecules and nucleosome arrays.

</details>


### [230] [Long-Range Hydrodynamic Communication in Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32867)
*Abhishek Thakur,Bibaswan Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文证明在无任何几何偏置的情况下，边界约束的 Marangoni 游泳体集群（flock）所产生的水动力流可驱动置于圆形域中心的对称被动转子（passive rotor）发生自发单向旋转，从而提供一种无接触的远程机械驱动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性物质（active matter）中的定向输运通常依赖棘轮、齿轮等几何不对称结构对活性粒子随机运动进行整流，本文旨在探索一条无需几何偏置即可产生持续机械运动的全新路径。
