Method: 实验上采用位于圆形域中心的对称被动转子与边界约束的 Marangoni 游泳体集群，借助粒子图像测速（particle image velocimetry, PIV）表征集体流场，并结合一个最小流场叠加模型（minimal flow superposition model）进行分析。

Result: 单个游泳体因瞬态水动力回流（hydrodynamic backflow）会产生间歇性力矩反转，而增大集群规模可形成相干环流（coherent circulation），稳定转子动力学；PIV 显示集体流在宏观尺度上传递动量，将局部活性驱动力转化为稳健的机械输运。

Conclusion: 反向力矩事件的抑制被识别为活性游泳体与被动体之间水动力耦合（hydrodynamic coupling）的集体特征，由此提出一种活性物质中无接触远程机械驱动机制。

Abstract: Directed transport in active matter is typically achieved by introducing geometric asymmetry, such as ratchets or gears, that rectify the stochastic motion of active particles. Here we demonstrate a fundamentally different route in which persistent mechanical motion emerges without any geometric bias. A symmetric passive rotor placed at the center of a circular domain undergoes spontaneous unidirectional rotation driven solely by the hydrodynamic flow generated by a boundary-confined flocks of Marangoni swimmers, despite the absence of direct physical contact. While individual swimmers produce intermittent torque reversals arising from transient hydrodynamic backflow, increasing the size of the flock establishes coherent circulation that stabilizes the rotor dynamics. Particle image velocimetry (PIV) reveals that the collectively generated flow transmits momentum over macroscopic distances, converting local active forcing into robust mechanical transport. Together with a minimal flow superposition model, our results identify the suppression of reverse-torque events as a collective signature of hydrodynamic coupling between active swimmers and a passive body, providing a contact-free mechanism for remote mechanical actuation in active matter.

</details>


### [231] [Confinement-induced Transitions of Chiral Active Particles and Dumbbells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32972)
*Mike Brandt,Falko Ziebert,Carsten Littek*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，用 Martin-Siggia-Rose 路径积分（Martin-Siggia-Rose path integral）与 Fokker-Planck 方程（Fokker-Planck equation）两类方法描述谐振阱（harmonic trap）中手性活性布朗粒子（chiral Active Brownian Particle, cABP）与手性活性布朗哑铃（chiral Active Brownian Dumbbell, cABD）的随机动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对手性活性粒子在受限环境中的随机描述需求，需要一个能同时给出精确时间关联函数（correlation functions）与稳态概率分布（steady-state probability distribution）的自洽理论框架。

Method: 以被动布朗运动（passive Brownian motion）为参考态，将活性以自推进速度（self-propulsion speed）的微扰级数纳入 MSR 路径积分形式，用于计算精确含时关联函数；并采用 Fokker-Planck 方程求解稳态概率分布。

Result: 揭示了从低活性/强约束下的阱中心分布到高活性/弱约束下的环状分布（ring-shaped distribution）的转变，以及手性（活性角速度，active angular velocity）的影响；对哑铃体系出现一系列此类转变，关联函数可反映粒子的构象（conformation）与取向（orientation）信息。

Conclusion: 两种方法的联合框架被确立为研究随机活性粒子体系（stochastic active particle systems）的有力工具。

Abstract: We present a coherent framework for the stochastic description of a chiral Active Brownian Particle (cABP) and a chiral Active Brownian Dumbbell (cABD) in a harmonic trap. On the one hand, we employ the Martin-Siggia-Rose path integral formalism, using the passive Brownian motion as a reference state around which activity is accounted for as a perturbation series in the self-propulsion speed. This method is suited best to calculate the exact time-dependent correlation functions. On the other hand, we use the Fokker-Planck equation, which works best to obtain the steady-state probability distribution. We discuss in detail the transitions from a trap centered distribution for low activity/strong confinement to a ring-shaped distribution for high activity/low confinement and the effect of chirality, i.e. the active angular velocity. For the dumbbell, a series of such transitions occurs and the correlation functions yield information about the particle's conformation and orientation. This establishes the combined framework as a powerful tool to investigate stochastic active particle systems.

</details>


### [232] [Polar Streams through Active Condensation in Flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33007)
*Lauritz Hahn,Xinyu Dou,Ziqi Zhou,Jie Zhang,Marin Bukov,Ricard Alert*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 利用自驱动Janus胶体二聚体实验、粒子模拟与粗粒化流体力学理论，发现成群运动（flocking）可相分离为极性流（polar streams），其机制为“活性向列凝聚（active nematic condensation）”：密集区极性与向列序增强，驱动粒子进一步流入，揭示向列序对成群结构的塑造作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性物质（active matter）中成群运动与自驱动诱导相分离（motility-induced phase separation）通常被视为两类独立范例现象，二者能否共存并相互作用尚不清楚。

Method: 采用自驱动Janus胶体二聚体（Janus colloid dimers）实验，结合基于粒子的模拟（particle-based simulations）与通过粗粒化（coarse-graining）获得的流体力学理论进行分析。

Result: 实验观察到成群可相分离形成极性流（polar streams）——沿流长轴成群运动的细长密集粒子团簇，区别于成群转变附近已知的横向带（transverse bands）；机理为活性向列凝聚：密集区产生更强的极性与向列序（nematic order），导致粒子流进一步涌入密集区。

Conclusion: 提出成群可凝聚为流的普适机制，表明成群结构可受标准Toner-Tu方程通常未考虑的向列序塑造；更广泛地说明活性物质中集体运动与相分离不仅可共存，还可协同作用。

Abstract: Two of the most paradigmatic phenomena in active matter are flocking, where particles align to move collectively, and motility-induced phase separation, where self-propulsion causes particles to condense even without attractive interactions. Here, using experiments on self-propelled Janus colloid dimers, we show that flocks can phase separate to form polar streams. These structures are elongated dense clusters of particles that flock along the stream's long axis, in contrast to the well-known transverse bands that form close to the flocking transition. We use particle-based simulations and hydrodynamic theory obtained through coarse-graining to identify the underlying mechanism, which we call active nematic condensation: Denser regions of the flock develop stronger polar and hence nematic order, which leads to further particle fluxes into the dense region. These results reveal a generic mechanism whereby flocks can condense into streams. Our findings uncover that flocks can be shaped by nematic order, which is usually not accounted for in the standard Toner-Tu equations. More broadly, our work shows that collective motion and phase separation can not only coexist but also cooperate in active matter.

</details>


### [233] [A model colloidal gel former for the measurement of inter-particle forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33091)
*Rui Cheng,Kristian Thijssen,Abraham Mauleon Amieva,Tanniemola B. Liverpool,Malcolm A. Faers,Robert L. Jack,C. Patrick Royall*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究改进了胶体乳液模型体系，通过调控密度失配与折射率衬度实现三维共聚焦成像，并结合计算机模拟定量刻画耗竭凝胶中的液滴间作用力、堆积几何及局域应力分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有胶体颗粒坐标追踪技术成熟，且近期乳液体系可测定液滴间接触与压缩力，但需要同时抑制重力沉降并获得高质量三维成像，以将实验体系可靠映射至模拟并研究局域结构与应力的关系。

Method: 调控乳液液滴与溶剂之间的密度失配（density mismatch）和折射率衬度（refractive index contrast），并将体系细致映射到计算机模拟（computer simulation）；在耗竭凝胶（depletion gels）中量化液滴间作用力与堆积几何，并由模拟给出液滴间吸引力及应力张量（stress tensor）等系综测量结果，同时实现改进的局域应力实验测量。

Result: 模拟提供了液滴间吸引力及应力张量等系综量，实验获得的局域应力可与模拟中对应量直接比较；发现局域刚性最小能量团簇（locally rigid minimum energy clusters）与凝胶网络中较高应力的区域相关联。

Conclusion: 密度失配与折射率衬度的协同调控使乳液体系成为可与模拟定量对照的三维胶体模型平台，并揭示局域刚性结构与凝胶应力不均匀性之间的关联。

Abstract: Tracking of particle coordinates in colloidal model systems is an established technique. Recently, an emulsion system has been introduced where not only the coordinates, but also contacts and compressive forces between the droplets can be determined [Dong <em>et al. J. Chem. Phys.</em> <strong>156</strong> 214907 (2022). Here we develop this system to control both the density mismatch and refractive index contrast between the emulsion droplets and the solvent. The former is important in minimizing sedimentation due to gravity and the latter is necessary for 3d imaging with confocal microscopy. By mapping our system carefully to computer simulation, we quantify interdroplet forces and packing geometries in depletion gels and probe the relationship between local structure and compressive stress. The simulations also provide ensemble measurements of the attractive part of the force between droplets and related quantities such as the stress tensor. Finally, we implement an improved measure of local stress in our experiments, which can be directly compared with the corresponding quantity in simulations. We find that locally rigid minimum energy clusters are associated with regions of higher stress in the gel network.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Tunable first-order transitions and magnetic hysteresis in Bernal pentalayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31376)
*Wentian Zheng,Jiawei Hu,Yuxuan Yao,Fang Xie,Zihan Wang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Qimiao Si,Yonglong Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在Bernal堆叠五层石墨烯（pentalayer graphene）中证明子带（subband）自由度可作为调控能谷/自旋味（flavor）对称性破缺相变的手段，通过局域可压缩性测量与输运测量揭示子带朗道能级交叉处的一阶相变及其可调磁滞行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子系统中强库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions）可自发破缺自旋与能谷等味简并，产生具有磁滞切换（hysteretic switching）的一阶相变，对存储器件有潜在价值。多能带系统的子带自由度或可参与此类相变并通过位移场（displacement field）调控其磁滞行为，但此前缺乏实验验证。

Method: 在Bernal堆叠五层石墨烯中，利用局域可压缩性测量（local compressibility measurements）分辨完全破缺对称性的朗道能级（Landau-level）谱及不同子带朗道能级交叉时发生的一系列相变，并结合磁场下的霍尔响应（Hall responses）测量磁滞特性，同时考察位移场对磁滞的调控作用。

Result: 局域可压缩性测量分辨出完全对称性破缺的朗道能级谱，以及子带朗道能级交叉时强相互作用重新分配味占据的一系列相变；这些相变在磁场下表现出磁滞霍尔响应，证实其一阶性质，且磁滞的幅度与符号可由位移场调控，与可压缩性测量确定的子带朗道能级序一致。

Conclusion: 该工作确立多能带系统中的子带自由度为工程化磁滞切换的资源，并表明局域热力学测量（local thermodynamic measurements）能够将微观认识与量子材料（quantum materials）的宏观响应联系起来。

Abstract: In two-dimensional electron systems, strong Coulomb interactions can spontaneously lift flavor degeneracies such as spin and valley, producing first-order transitions with hysteretic switching of interest for memory devices. The subband degree of freedom in multiband systems may be viewed as a flavor that not only participates in such transitions but also enables control of the hysteretic behavior, as a displacement field can shift the energy difference between subbands. Here, we demonstrate this principle in Bernal-stacked pentalayer graphene, whose low-energy spectrum comprises a monolayer-like Dirac sub-band together with light and heavy bilayer-like sub-bands. Local compressibility measurements resolve a fully symmetry-broken Landau-level spectrum and a sequence of phase transitions that occur when Landau levels of different subbands cross, at which strong interactions redistribute the occupation among flavors. We show that these transitions exhibit hysteretic Hall responses in magnetic field, confirming their first-order character, and that the magnitude and sign of the hysteresis are tuned by the displacement field, as expected from the subband Landau-level ordering determined in compressibility measurements. Our work establishes the subband degree of freedom in multiband systems as a resource for engineering hysteretic switching, and highlights how local thermodynamic measurements can link microscopic insights to macroscopic responses in quantum materials.

</details>


### [235] [Moiré-enabled polariton control in large-angle twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31423)
*Bogdan Borodin,Xuanpu Zhang,Christopher Yang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Cyprian Lewandowski,Petr Stepanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用双曲等离激元-声子极化激元（hyperbolic plasmon-phonon polaritons, HP³）探测双层石墨烯从 AB 堆叠到 5.5° 扭转角（twist angle）的动力学电子响应，发现大扭转角 TBG 中存在由中心与远端莫尔带（remote moiré bands）杂化调控的窄密度窗口，其极化激元波长可实现近 90% 的门电压可调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, TBG）的认识多集中于魔角（magic angle）附近的强关联平带与大扭转角下近乎解耦的狄拉克锥（Dirac cones）两个极限区间，处于中间的过渡区域缺乏研究。该工作旨在填补这一空白，并探索大扭转角 TBG 的动力学电子响应与可调性。

Method: 采用双曲等离激元-声子极化激元（HP³）作为探针，测量双层石墨烯从 AB 堆叠至 5.5° 扭转角范围内的动态电子响应；并结合连续模型（continuum-model）与电动力学计算对实验现象进行解释。

Result: 实验识别出一个 HP³ 波长强烈依赖门电压的窄密度窗口：当掺杂抑制朗道阻尼（Landau damping）并出现相干石墨烯等离激元（graphene plasmon）时窗口开启；在 4° 以上出现第二个交叉点并使窗口关闭，且该交叉点随扭转角系统性移动。计算表明该交叉源于中心与远端莫尔带（moiré bands）的杂化，削弱了德鲁德响应（Drude response）的密度依赖性，总波长变化接近 90%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，远端莫尔带在远离魔角区域仍能重塑 TBG 的集体电动力学行为，并将大扭转角 TBG 确立为门电压可重构中红外极化激元（mid-infrared polaritons）与材料腔（material cavities）的平台。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) is commonly viewed through two limiting regimes: strongly correlated flat bands near the magic angle and nearly decoupled Dirac cones at large twist angles. Here we explore the intermediate regime. Using hyperbolic plasmon-phonon polaritons (HP$^{3}$) to probe the dynamical electronic response, we investigate bilayer graphene from AB stacking to $5.5^\circ$ TBG. We identify a narrow density window in which the HP$^{3}$ wavelength becomes strongly gate tunable. The window opens when doping suppresses Landau damping and a coherent graphene plasmon emerges. It closes at a second crossover, which appears only above $4^\circ$ and shifts systematically with twist angle. Continuum-model and electrodynamic calculations trace this second crossover to hybridization between the central and remote moiré bands, which weakens the density dependence of the Drude response. The resulting polaritonic tunability is concentrated within a twist-angle-selected density window and produces total wavelength changes approaching $90\%$. Our results demonstrate that remote moiré bands reshape the collective electrodynamics of TBG far from the magic-angle regime and establish large-angle TBG as a platform for gate-reconfigurable mid-infrared polaritons and material cavities.

</details>


### [236] [Excess noise and electron-phonon energy transfer in Au wires: Length and temperature effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31519)
*Yuxin Wan,Dale T. Lowder,Liyang Chen,Manasi Mandal,Gavin Joyce,Douglas Natelson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在 3–50 K 温度范围、亚微米至 30 μm 长度的扩散 Au 纳米线上测量偏置电流依赖的噪声，以厘清电荷散粒噪声（shot noise）与电子自加热、声子主导噪声抑制之间的竞争关系，并指出高温高偏置下简单电子-声子能量损失模型不足，需引入界面热导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温介观扩散导线（mesoscopic diffusive wire）被电流驱动偏离平衡时会出现超出平衡 Johnson-Nyquist 涨落的过剩噪声，其形式取决于导线穿越时间与电子-电子、电子-声子非弹性能量转移速率的相对大小，故需实验厘清这一竞争。

Method: 测量 SiO2/Si 衬底上扩散 Au 纳米线的噪声随偏置电流的变化，线长为亚微米至 30 μm，温度为 3 K 至 50 K，以绘制散粒噪声、电子自加热交叉及声子主导噪声抑制之间的相互作用图谱。

Result: 定性上符合预期：随线长与衬底温度增加，电子-声子散射重要性上升，散粒噪声（shot noise）受到抑制。但在长导线的高偏置、高温极限下，已知在几开尔文以下定量有效的简单电子-声子能量损失模型会低估过剩噪声；该偏差在 SiO2/Si 上比裸 Si 更严重。

Conclusion: 即使在相对较高的温度下，热通道中仍须考虑界面热导（interfacial thermal conduction）；衬底界面结构对高温噪声行为有显著影响。

Abstract: As a mesoscopic diffusive wire at low temperatures is driven out of equilibrium by a current, excess noise is observed compared to the equilibrium Johnson-Nyquist fluctuations. The expected form of the excess noise varies depending on the relative rates of wire traversal, electron-electron inelastic energy transfer, and electron-phonon inelastic energy transfer. We measure noise as a function of bias current in diffusive Au nanowires on SiO2/Si with lengths ranging from sub-μm to 30 μm and temperatures from 3 K to 50 K, to map out the interplay between charge shot noise and the crossover to electron self-heating and phonon-dominated noise suppression. As expected qualitatively, shot noise is suppressed with increasing wire length and substrate temperature, as electron-phonon scattering is of increasing importance. In long wires, the simple model of electron-phonon energy loss known to be quantitatively effective below a few Kelvin underpredicts the excess noise in the high bias, high temperature limit. This issue is more severe in wires on SiO2/Si than on bare Si, implying that interfacial thermal conduction must be considered in the thermal path even at comparatively high temperatures.

</details>


### [237] [Interface and film engineering for improved spin-charge interconversion in sputtered GeTe nanodevices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31816)
*S. Teresi,T. Frottier,M. Culot,W. Savero-Torres,P. Sgarro,M. Wissmann,A. Kandazoglou,F. Osana,J. Lagrave,D. Térébénec,J. Paterson,A. Oysel Mestre,O. J. Mouawad,E. Chevilley,N. Bernier,P. Noé,M. Bibes,F. Hippert,J. -P. Attané,L. Vila,M. Cosset-Chéneau*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在溅射GeTe基铁电自旋轨道（FESO）纳米器件中实现室温自旋-电荷互转换，特征尺寸小至100 nm，互转换输出信号从3 mΩ提升至24 mΩ，超过同尺寸铂的报道值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋逻辑器件有望实现超低功耗非易失存内计算，规避冯·诺依曼瓶颈；但磁电/铁电自旋轨道（MESO/FESO）器件所需的spin-charge interconversion材料须同时具备大输出信号与耐纳米尺度集成的特性，而碲化物在加工中易引入缺陷、性能退化。

Method: 采用溅射GeTe构建图形化FESO纳米器件；在CoFe电极与GeTe之间引入Ti界面层，并使用结晶质量更优的GeTe薄膜，以提升互转换输出信号。

Result: 实现室温spin-charge interconversion，特征尺寸低至100 nm；输出信号由3 mΩ提升至24 mΩ，超过同等器件尺寸下铂（Pt）的报道值。

Conclusion: 结果表明可大规模沉积的GeTe是自旋逻辑器件的可行材料平台，界面质量与薄膜结晶度是优化性能的关键调控因素。

Abstract: Spin-logic devices promise non-volatile logic-in-memory computing with ultra-low power consumption, offering a route to circumvent the von Neumann bottleneck. Magnetoelectric and ferroelectric spin-orbit (MESO/FESO) devices, however, require spin-charge interconversion materials that combine a large output signal with properties that survive nanoscale integration. Tellurides are particularly fragile in this respect, as processing readily introduces defects that degrade their spin-charge interconversion properties. Here we demonstrate room-temperature spin-charge interconversion in patterned FESO nanoscale devices based on sputtered GeTe, with feature sizes down to 100 nm. By introducing a Ti interfacial layer between the CoFe electrode and GeTe, and by using GeTe films of improved crystalline quality, we increase the interconversion output signal from 3 mΩ to 24 mΩ, exceeding values reported for platinum at comparable device size. These results establish large-scale-deposited GeTe as a viable material platform for spin-logic devices and identify interface quality and film crystallinity as key levers for optimizing their performance.

</details>


### [238] [Moiré-Induced Interlayer Electron-Phonon Coupling in Graphene/hBN Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31896)
*Dasol Kim,Zhengguang Lu,Tonghang Han,Guorui Chen,Tianyi Han,Jixiang Yang,Bing Cheng,Zhenxian Liu,Christopher Homes,G. L. Carr,Jeong Min Park,Qihang Zhang,Lili Jiang,Hongyuan Li,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Pablo Jarillo-Herrero,Feng Wang,Long Ju*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文首次在石墨烯基莫尔超晶格中直接观测到源于莫尔电子激发与声子耦合的共振，表现为hBN面外光学声子的显著吸收峰与Fano共振干涉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）深刻影响材料的电学与光学性质；在莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）中，面外振动可调制莫尔势并增强层间EPC，但此前缺乏对莫尔电子激发与声子耦合共振的直接观测。

Method: 采用傅里叶变换红外光电流光谱（Fourier-transform infrared photocurrent spectroscopy），探测hBN面外光学（out-of-plane optical, ZO）声子，并通过位移场（displacement field）依赖测量及不同石墨烯层数与扭转构型器件进行对照验证。

Result: 检测到名义上“沉默”的hBN ZO声子表现为显著吸收峰，并与莫尔平带（moiré flat-band）激发干涉形成特征Fano共振；位移场依赖测量证实该特征源于与莫尔电子连续谱的耦合，而非简单能带折叠（zone folding）。

Conclusion: 该增强的电子-声子耦合为理解与调控超导（superconductivity）等新兴量子现象及设计新型光电器件提供了途径。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling (EPC) plays a vital role in the electrical and optical properties of materials, ranging from superconductivity to charge dynamics. In moiré superlattices, out-of-plane vibrations can modulate the moiré potential and enhance interlayer EPC. Here we report the first direct observation of a resonance arising from coupling between moiré electronic excitations and phonons in graphene-based moiré superlattices. Using Fourier-transform infrared photocurrent spectroscopy, we detect the nominally silent hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) out-of-plane optical (ZO) phonon as a pronounced absorption peak. The phonon interferes with moiré flat-band excitations to form a characteristic Fano resonance. Displacement field-dependent measurements confirm that the feature originates from coupling to the moiré electronic continuum rather than simple zone folding. We further observe similar resonances in devices with different graphene layer numbers and twist configurations. This enhanced EPC provides a pathway for understanding and controlling emerging quantum phenomena, such as superconductivity, and engineering novel optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [239] [Environmentally assisted transport in a diamond chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32027)
*Pavel S. Muraev,Andrey R. Kolovsky,Dmitrii N. Maksimov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究金刚石链（diamond chain）两端耦合马尔可夫库（Markovian reservoirs）的量子输运，在均匀退相位（dephasing）与每格通量Φ的磁场下求得Φ=0和Φ=π的精确解析解；发现Φ=π的全带平坦（all-bands-flat）区中退相位破坏Aharonov-Bohm囚禁（Aharonov-Bohm caging），产生环境辅助量子输运（environment-assisted quantum transport）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干动力学在全带平坦条件下会导致完全局域化，输运被抑制；因此需要探索耗散环境能否作为调控平坦带体系输运的有效途径。

Method: 构建边界连接马尔可夫库的金刚石链模型，施加均匀退相位与穿透每格通量Φ的外磁场，针对Φ=0与Φ=π两种情形推导精确解析解。

Result: 在Φ=π的全带平坦区，退相位破坏Aharonov-Bohm囚禁，从而出现环境辅助量子输运；Φ=0与Φ=π情形均由解析解刻画。

Conclusion: 将平坦带（flat-band）体系耦合至耗散环境，是在相干动力学本身导致完全局域化的情形下控制输运的可行途径。

Abstract: We study quantum transport in a diamond chain coupled to Markovian reservoirs at its boundaries. The system is subject to uniform dephasing and an external magnetic field threading a flux $Φ$ per plaquette. We derive exact analytical solutions for $Φ=0$ and $Φ=π$. We show that in the all-bands-flat regime at $Φ=π$, dephasing destroys Aharonov-Bohm caging, leading to environment-assisted quantum transport. Our results demonstrate that coupling a flat-band system to a dissipative environment provides a viable route for controlling transport in regimes where coherent dynamics alone would result in complete localization.

</details>


### [240] [Towards determination of intrinsic spin properties in MAPbI$_3$ crystals using spin noise spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32115)
*M. Vladimirova,S. Cronenberger,L. Doyenette,D. R. Yakovlev,B. Turedi,M. V. Kovalenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用背向与前向散射构型下的自旋噪声光谱（spin noise spectroscopy）研究 MAPbI₃ 单晶中载流子的自旋动力学，揭示电子与空穴不同的局域化程度及其空间分布的不均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需厘清 MAPbI₃（甲基铵铅碘）单晶中电子与空穴自旋弛豫的主导机制及载流子的空间分布，而现有认识尚不明确，故采用对不同散射构型敏感的自旋噪声光谱加以区分研究。

Method: 在背向散射（back-scattering）与前向散射（forward-scattering）两种几何构型下测量自旋噪声光谱，比较不同温度（低至 T ≈ 4 K）与不同激发功率下的自旋噪声谱线宽度及零场频率移动。

Result: 低温下空穴自旋弛豫由与核自旋的超精细相互作用（hyperfine interaction）主导，谱线随温度展宽可归因于局域空穴的热激活跃迁（thermally activated hopping），由此估算的空穴局域化长度与超精细去相位速率及零场频移一致；电子自旋动力学则无法仅用超精细相互作用解释，即使最低激发功率下仍受探测光影响，且自旋弛豫随功率增加而减慢。

Conclusion: 背向与前向散射信号对比表明载流子空间分布不均匀：电子优先位于晶体表面附近，弱局域化（自旋弛豫较慢）的空穴主要远离表面，强局域化空穴则居于两者之间。

Abstract: Spin noise spectroscopy in back- and forward-scattering geometries is used to study the spin dynamics of charge carriers in a MAPbI$_3$ (MA = methylammonium) single crystal. The spectra reveal electrons and holes with different degrees of localization. At low temperatures ($T \approx 4$~K), hole spin relaxation is dominated by the hyperfine interaction with nuclear spins. The broadening of the spectra with increasing temperature can be attributed to thermally activated hopping of localized holes. From the thermal activation energies, we estimate hole localization lengths that are consistent with both the hyperfine-induced dephasing rates and the zero-field frequency shifts measured at low temperature. By contrast, the electron spin dynamics cannot be explained by the hyperfine interaction alone: even at the lowest excitation powers, it is affected by the probe light, and we observe a slowing down of spin relaxation with increasing power. Comparison of the back- and forward-scattered spin noise signals indicates that the spatial distribution of carriers is inhomogeneous: electrons are located preferentially near the crystal surface, weakly localized holes (characterized by slower spin relaxation) are found mostly away from the surface, and strongly localized holes are somewhere in between.

</details>


### [241] [Topological Narrow Bands in Twisted Rhombohedral Multilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32229)
*Alejandro Jimeno-Pozo,Danna Liu,Francisco Guinea,Pierre A. Pantaleón*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出，扭转单层-$N$层菱面体石墨烯（twisted monolayer-$N$-layer rhombohedral graphene）的两条中心莫尔带（moiré bands）源于扭转界面处单层狄拉克锥（Dirac cone）与莫尔布里渊区（moiré Brillouin zone）对角处菱面体表面态（rhombohedral surface state）的杂化；层数$N$与位移场（displacement field）可协同调控能带平坦度、波函数极化与陈数层级（Chern-number hierarchy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期扭转菱面体石墨烯实验观测到由菱面体层数决定的高陈数层级（high-Chern-number hierarchy），但缺少将理想手性图像（ideal chiral picture）与真实电子结构联系起来的微观描述，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过分析两条中心莫尔带的起源（单层狄拉克锥与菱面体表面态的杂化），并构建投影表面理论（projected surface theory），说明界面隧穿（interfacial tunneling）如何经由菱面体堆垛中的虚拟传播（virtual propagation）被修饰。

Result: 随$N$增大，中心莫尔带逐渐变窄；反转位移场可将波函数权重在扭转界面与远端表面之间转移，并在谱隔离（spectrally isolated）区间使下带在$\mathcal{C}=N$与$\mathcal{C}=-1$之间切换。理论还解释了场选陈数层级及实空间电荷织构（charge textures）反复出现的三重结构。

Conclusion: 层数与位移场构成石墨烯莫尔异质结（graphene moiré heterostructures）中能带平坦度、空间极化与拓扑性质的互补纳米尺度调控手段。

Abstract: Moiré graphene multilayers provide an atomically thin platform for engineering narrow topological bands through stacking, twist, and electrostatic control. Recent experiments on twisted rhombohedral graphene have revealed a high-Chern-number hierarchy determined by the number of rhombohedral layers. A microscopic description of this hierarchy requires connecting the ideal chiral picture to the realistic electronic structure. Here, we show that the two central moiré bands of twisted monolayer-$N$-layer rhombohedral graphene originate from hybridization between a monolayer Dirac cone and a rhombohedral surface state located at opposite corners of the moiré Brillouin zone. Increasing $N$ progressively narrows these bands, whereas reversing the displacement field transfers their wave-function weight between the twisted interface and the remote surface. In spectrally isolated regimes, this redistribution switches the lower band between Chern numbers $\mathcal{C}=N$ and $\mathcal{C}=-1$. A projected surface theory shows how interfacial tunneling is dressed by virtual propagation through the rhombohedral stack, explaining the field-selected Chern hierarchy and the recurring threefold structure of the real-space charge textures. These results establish layer number and displacement field as complementary nanoscale controls of band flatness, spatial polarization, and topology in graphene moiré heterostructures.

</details>


### [242] [Periodic and quasiperiodic structures produced by microphase separation in one-component simple liquids with competing interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32273)
*Lorenzo Agosta,Alfredo Metere,Mikhail Dzugutov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该观点文章综述了单组分简单液体中基于短程吸引-长程排斥（SALR）势的微相分离（microphase separation）分子动力学模拟进展，表明此类模拟可重现嵌段共聚物与液晶的介观相，并可作为超材料（metamaterial）模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性相互作用（competing interactions）引发的阻挫（frustration）会抑制宏观相分离，转而形成微观尺度的分离图案。研究者关注 SALR 简单液体模型，源于不同体系中竞争相互作用所产生的调制结构（modulated structures）具有表面上的普适性；简单粒子模型便于理论处理并可借助粒子模拟触及实验难以达到的区域。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟（molecular-dynamics simulation）研究具有短程吸引与长程排斥（short-range attraction and long-range repulsion, SALR）对势的单组分简单液体，并综述该方向的近期进展。

Result: 模拟重现了嵌段共聚物（block copolymers）与液晶（liquid crystals）中典型的介观相（mesophases），说明真实液体组成粒子的复杂性并非其形成的必要条件；同时产生了兼具概念意义与技术价值的新型周期形貌（periodic morphologies）。

Conclusion: 单组分 SALR 液体的微相分离模拟可生成丰富介观结构，所模拟的介观相可作为超材料模板，其性质由拓扑（topology）决定。

Abstract: Competing interactions can generate frustration which suppresses macroscopic-scale phase separation, superseding it with separation patterns on the microscopic scale. This phenomenon, referred to as microphase separation, produces thermodynamically stable mesoscopic-scale periodic patterns in systems of different physical nature. The studies of this effect follow two paradigms. First, in block copolymers, the macroscopic-scale separation of chemically distinct components is prevented by the covalent bonds between them. In the other, the microscopic-scale separation is produced in simple single-component liquids by the competition between a short-range attraction and a long-range repulsion (SALR) in the pair potential, the separation occurring between a dense phase and a gas. The interest in the simple liquid models with SALR interaction was inspired by the apparent universality of the modulated structures generated by competing interactions in different systems. Simple particle models are tractable by theory and thereby make it possible to discern the microscopic mechanisms of pattern formation; particle-based simulations give access to systems beyond the reach of experiments. In this Perspective we review the recent advances in the molecular-dynamics simulation of microphase separation in single-component simple liquids using SALR potentials. We show that these simulations reproduce the mesophases typical of block copolymers and liquid crystals, which demonstrates that the complexity of the constituent particles of real liquids is not a necessary condition for their formation, and that they produce novel periodic morphologies of both conceptual interest and technological relevance; we discuss how simulated mesophases can serve as templates for metamaterials whose properties are governed by topology.

</details>


### [243] [Angular Momentum Hall Effect of Axial Ferrons in Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32331)
*Xiyin Ye,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出单轴铁电体中的“轴向铁子（axial ferrons）”，指出太赫兹软模（ferrons）同样具有热霍尔与角动量霍尔效应，并揭示其双通道竞争机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子热霍尔与角动量霍尔效应已在非铁电绝缘体中被认识，但位移型铁电体的太赫兹软模（即铁子，ferrons）是否具有同类效应尚属开放问题。

Method: 在单轴铁电体中构建具有极化-动量锁定（polarization-momentum locking）的轴向铁子图像，分析自辐射磁场导致的模式杂化与温度梯度诱导的模式量子相干两条竞争通道，并进行数值计算。

Result: 纵向温度梯度可在无外场下驱动横向角动量流；面外磁场开启热霍尔响应。两条通道贡献相反的横向角动量流，面外磁场破坏极化-动量锁定并产生极化霍尔电流；在 LiNbO$_3$ 与范德华铁电体中效应显著。

Conclusion: 边界磁化或极化积累可翻转相邻磁体或铁电体，为未来以热电流实现低功耗磁电功能提供可能。

Abstract: Phonon thermal and angular-momentum (AM) Hall effects are known in non-ferroelectric insulators, yet whether terahertz soft modes of displacive ferroelectrics, i.e., ``ferrons", share them remains open. We report axial ferrons that hold polarization-momentum locking in uniaxial ferroelectrics and show that both effects exist: a longitudinal temperature gradient drives a transverse AM flow without any field, whereas an out-of-plane magnetic field switches on the thermal Hall response. Two competitive channels emerge for the AM Hall effect: self-radiated magnetic fields hybridize two branches into circular modes whose momentum-locked Berry curvature deflects opposite magnetic moments to opposite edges, while temperature-gradient-induced mode quantum coherence adds a magnetic-moment dipole that leads to an opposite transverse AM current. An out-of-plane magnetic field breaks the exact polarization-momentum locking and yields a polarization Hall current. Numerical calculations show these effects are significant in both LiNbO$_3$ and van der Waals ferroelectrics. Edge magnetization or polarization accumulation can switch adjacent magnets or ferroelectrics, enabling low-power magnetoelectric functionality by thermal current in the future.

</details>


### [244] [Reconfigurable Chiral Phonons and Their Selection in Asymmetrically Stacked Graphene MoirMoiré Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32466)
*Yi-Fei Zhang,Ting-Ting Wang,Zhong-Jie Wang,Miao-Ling Lin,Yue Wang,Zi-Jian Zhong,Xue-Lu Liu,Heng Wu,Lin-Shang Chen,Yan-Meng Shi,Jin-Yang Lou,Lin-Han Li,Jia-Liang Xie,Rui Mei,Ti-Ying Zhu,Li-Fa Zhang,Jiang-Bin Wu,Ping-Heng Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用螺旋分辨拉曼光谱（helicity-resolved Raman spectroscopy）研究非对称堆叠石墨烯莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattices），观测到手性层间剪切声子与层内光学声子的圆偏振度（degree of circular polarization），并通过扭转方向、堆叠次序、激发能量、扭转角及栅压实现可逆调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性声子（chiral phonons）携带角动量，为调控声子介导的凝聚态现象提供新手段，但其按需产生与确定性控制仍具挑战性，本文旨在寻找可重构的生成与调控平台。

Method: 采用螺旋分辨拉曼光谱（helicity-resolved Raman spectroscopy）探测非对称堆叠石墨烯莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattices）中的手性声子；建立手性声子拉曼张量模型（Raman-tensor model）解释机理，并结合激发能量、扭转角与栅压调控进行系统测量。

Result: 手性层间剪切声子（interlayer shear phonons）与层内光学声子（intralayer optical phonons）均表现出有限的圆偏振度；模型将其归因于界面 C2 对称性破缺（C2-symmetry breaking）与范霍夫奇点区（van Hove singularity region）内的拉曼共振。反转扭转方向或堆叠次序可使圆偏振度符号反转；调节激发能量与扭转角可将层间剪切声子圆偏振度调控至 −1 至 1 的近全范围，栅致层间电势重分布（interlayer potential redistribution）还可实现显著且可逆的原位调制。

Conclusion: 非对称堆叠石墨烯莫尔超晶格可作为产生与调控手性声子的通用平台，对光电子学（optoelectronics）与量子技术（quantum technologies）具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Chiral phonons, lattice vibrations carrying angular momentum, offer a new handle for controlling phonon-mediated phenomena in condensed matter, yet their on-demand generation and deterministic control remain challenging. Here we show, using helicity-resolved Raman spectroscopy, that asymmetrically stacked graphene moiré superlattices provide a reconfigurable platform for chiral phonons. We observe a finite degree of circular polarization for both chiral interlayer shear phonons and intralayer optical phonons. A chiral-phonon Raman-tensor model attributes this response to interfacial $C_{2}$-symmetry breaking together with Raman resonance within the van Hove singularity region. Reversing either the twist sense or the stacking order inverts the degree of circular polarization through the spatial transformations linking the corresponding stacking geometries. By tuning the excitation energy and twist angle, we achieve nearly full-range control of the degree of circular polarization of interlayer shear phonons, from $-1$ to $1$, while gate-induced interlayer potential redistribution enables substantial and reversible in situ modulation. These results establish asymmetrically stacked graphene moiré superlattices as a versatile platform for generating and controlling chiral phonons, with implications for optoelectronic and quantum technologies.

</details>


### [245] [Modulations of the photoswitching performances of azobenzene-based molecular devices by the π-conjugated spacers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32611)
*Laurette-Apolline Jerro,Imen Hnid,Imane Arbouch,Ali Yassin,David Guérin,Alexis Elias,Colin van Dyck,Lionel Sanguinet,Jérôme Cornil,Stéphane Lenfant,Philippe Blanchard,Dominique Vuillaume*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究探讨π共轭间隔基（π-conjugated spacer）对金属-间隔基-偶氮苯-金属分子结电导与光开关性质的影响，发现噻吩/苯基噻吩间隔基的顺/反电导比约1,000，而联噻吩间隔基约500，并通过DFT与NEGF理论解释顺式构型下分子-电极耦合能增大是电导提升的主因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: π共轭间隔基是调控分子结电导与光开关特性的关键要素，但不同间隔基结构如何影响偶氮苯（azobenzene）分子结的开关比与异构化动力学尚需系统性比较，尤其是分子-电极耦合与轨道能级的作用机制不明确。

Method: 制备含噻吩（thiophene）、苯基噻吩（phenylthiophene）、联噻吩（bithiophene）及乙炔噻吩（acetylene thiophene）间隔基的偶氮苯基金属-分子结，在给定光照波长与强度及单批次样品条件下测量电导开关比与反-顺异构化动力学，并结合密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）与非平衡格林函数（Non Equilibrium Green's Functions, NEGF）进行理论计算。

Result: 噻吩与苯基噻吩间隔基分子结的顺/反电导比约1,000，联噻吩约500且开关速率更低；最高占据分子轨道（highest occupied molecular orbital, HOMO）能级受间隔基性质与偶氮苯构象影响较弱；顺式电导大幅提升归因于分子与顶电极间电子耦合能增加，理论计算与实验定性一致；乙炔噻吩间隔基则导致结果重复性差与不可预测。

Conclusion: 间隔基结构决定了顺/反耦合能差异：噻吩、苯基噻吩与联噻吩间隔基下顺式的顶部苯环相对顶电极呈平躺取向，从而增强耦合；而含碳-碳三键的间隔基会破坏结果的可重复性与可预测性，表明sp杂化间隔基不适用于此类器件。

Abstract: The nature of the π-conjugated spacer is a key element to tune the electrical conductance and optical switching properties of metal-spacer-azobenzene-metal molecular devices. We report that the azobenzene-based molecular junctions with a thiophene or a phenylthiophene spacer exhibit a high cis/trans conductance ratio (ca. 1,000), while those with a bithiophene spacer have a lower conductance switching ratio (ca. 500) at the stated conditions (light wavelength and intensity) and for one batch of samples (out of two). Similarly, the two former molecular junctions have quite similar trans-to-cis isomerization kinetics, while the latter has a lower switching rate. The energy level of the highest occupied molecular orbital, which controls the electron transfer in the molecular junction, is weakly influenced by the nature of the spacer, nor by the conformation of the azobenzene moieties. The large increase in the conductance of the molecular junction with azobenzene in the cis-state is explained by an increase in the electronic coupling energy between the molecule and the top electrode. Theoretical studies by Density Functional Theory and Non Equilibrium Green's Functions are qualitatively consistent with the experiments. We find a consistent structure-property trend, showing that the higher molecule/electrode coupling energy for the cis-state is explained by the top benzene ring of the azobenzene lying flat with respect to the top electrode for the thiophene, phenylthiophene and bithiophene spacers. On the contrary, we observe that a spacer with a carbon-carbon triple bond (acetylene thiophene) induces a large irreproducibility and unpredictability of the results.

</details>


### [246] [Electrons in a hard-wall cylinder with helicoidal metric: time-reversal pairing, cubic anticrossings, and twist-activated dipole transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32619)
*Humberto Belich,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究螺旋度规（helicoidal metric）中标量薛定谔方程在轴向硬壁（Dirichlet walls）约束下的能谱与跃迁性质，给出规范适配的变分表示，并据此修正前期模型的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献多将轴向导数替换为行波因子（traveling-wave factor），但在硬壁腔中该近似不成立；本文旨在建立包含硬壁边界条件的严格模型，并重检早期论文的结论。

Method: 将哈密顿量写为厄米耦合子带矩阵（coupled-subband matrix），构造精确的规范适配变分表示（gauge-adapted variational representation），结合微扰论与数值计算，并以偶极求和规则（dipole sum rules）和电流收敛性作验证。

Result: 有限半径下扭曲修正领头项为二次且在临界长径比处变号；无界径向域中则与|Ω_g|成线性。m扇区内径向—轴向共振可导致带隙Δ_gap/E_R=1.648775…|Ω_gR²/L|³的避免交叉；无扭曲时禁戒的横向偶极跃迁获得与扭曲平方成正比的振子强度，R/L=2时领头系数为0.0559408010。

Conclusion: 硬壁边界条件显著改变螺旋度规中的能谱与跃迁规律；文中给出标量实现所需的逆质量张量（inverse-mass tensor），并在修正模型下重新审视早期论文[Opt. Quantum Electron. 58, 113 (2026)]的各项陈述与图表。

Abstract: We study the scalar Schrödinger equation in a helicoidal metric when the axial direction is closed by Dirichlet walls built into the operator domain. With hard walls, the axial derivative cannot be replaced by a traveling-wave factor, and in a rotationally symmetric spinless cavity, time reversal makes the levels with azimuthal numbers $m$ and $-m$ exactly degenerate. We write the Hamiltonian as a Hermitian coupled-subband matrix and give an exact gauge-adapted variational representation. For a finite radius, the leading twist correction is quadratic, and changes sign at a critical aspect ratio; for an unbounded radial domain, it is linear in $|Ω_g|$. Retuning the aspect ratio through an isolated radial--axial resonance within one $m$ sector opens an avoided crossing with gap $Δ_{\mathrm{gap}}/E_R=1.648775\ldots|Ω_gR^2/L|^3$ to leading order. A transverse dipole transition forbidden without twist acquires an oscillator strength quadratic in the twist, with leading coefficient $0.0559408010$ for $R/L=2$ from perturbation theory. Dipole sum rules and the convergence of the currents serve as checks on the numerics. We also give the inverse-mass tensor required for a scalar realization and re-examine each statement and figure of the earlier article [Opt. Quantum Electron. \textbf{58}, 113 (2026)] within the corrected model.

</details>


### [247] [Intrinsic longitudinal Hall effect in ferroaxial metals with high-order rotation axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32709)
*Eleni Chatzikyriakou,Stepan Tsirkin,Fernando Peñaranda,Oscar Pozo,Ivo Souza,Fernando de Juan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出在纵霍尔效应（longitudinal Hall effect）中，高对称铁轴（ferroaxial）晶体可利用洛伦兹力（Lorentz force）贡献消失的特性孤立观测量子霍尔修正，并预测Ni₃P（σᴴₓzₓ = 28.4 S/m/T）为理想候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非磁金属低场霍尔电阻率（Hall resistivity）中存在由场修正贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与本征磁矩产生的内禀量子贡献，但通常被经典洛伦兹力贡献掩盖，难以单独测量。

Method: 针对磁场位于霍尔平面内时铁轴晶体出现的纵霍尔效应，分析含3、4、6重旋转轴的八个铁轴群（ferroaxial groups）中洛伦兹力贡献为零的对称性条件，并整理高次旋转轴候选金属清单，对代表性候选进行第一性原理（first principles）计算。

Result: 确认八个高对称铁轴群的纵霍尔效应中洛伦兹力贡献消失；而此前所有已测实例均属于对称性较低的其余五个铁轴群，信号由洛伦兹力主导。第一性原理计算识别Ni₃P的σᴴₓzₓ = 28.4 S/m/T，为可无歧义测量内禀纵霍尔效应的有前景材料。

Conclusion: 高对称铁轴金属为孤立测量内禀纵霍尔效应提供了可行平台，Ni₃P被预测为最具前景的候选材料。

Abstract: The low-field Hall resistivity of non-magnetic metals has an intrinsic quantum contribution that derives from the field-corrected Berry curvature and intrinsic magnetic moment of the Bloch states near the Fermi surface. However, its effect is usually overshadowed by the classical Lorentz-force contribution. Here we show that for the longitudinal Hall effect, which occurs in ferroaxial crystals when the applied magnetic field lies in the Hall plane, the Lorentz force contribution vanishes for the eight ferroaxial groups with a 3,4 or 6-fold rotation axis. This provides an opportunity to measure the quantum correction in isolation, but so far all measured instances of the effect fall in the remaining five ferroaxial groups of lower symmetry, where the Lorentz force dominates the signal. We compile a list of candidate ferroaxial metals with such high-order rotation axis and compute the Hall effect from first principles for representative candidates, identifying Ni$_3$P with $σ^{\rm H}_{xzx} = 28.4$ S/m/T as a promising material to unambiguously measure the intrinsic longitudinal Hall effect.

</details>


### [248] [Recovery of Extended Modes from the Non-Hermitian Skin Effect Due to Local Nonlinearity on a Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32783)
*Kazuki Sone,Tomonari Mizoguchi,Yasuhiro Hatsugai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文揭示边界单键非线性（single-bond nonlinearity）可使非厄米紧束缚模型（non-Hermitian tight-binding model）中原本局域化的趋肤模式恢复为扩展模式（extended modes），从而挑战了非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect）源于体带拓扑的常规认识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米趋肤效应通常被认为由体带（bulk bands）的非平庸拓扑导致，但其在非线性效应下的鲁棒性仍是不明朗的问题，本文即针对此展开研究。

Method: 提出并分析一维最小模型（minimal model），仅在边界格点引入跳跃项（hopping term）的非线性相位移动（nonlinear phase shift）；同时提出一种可能的光子学方案（photonic setup），用于以稳态（steady state）形式动态观测该非线性诱导的扩展模式。

Result: 边界处的单键非线性能够使非厄米紧束缚模型恢复扩展模式，表明局域化行为依赖于非线性边界条件。

Conclusion: 研究结果通过揭示非厄米趋肤效应对非线性边界条件的依赖，对“趋肤效应源于体带拓扑”的传统观念提出质疑。

Abstract: Localization in continuum spectra known as the non-Hermitian skin effect is widely believed to be a consequence of nontrivial topology of bulk bands. However, the robustness of the non-Hermitian skin effect against nonlinear effects is still a nontrivial problem. We here reveal that single-bond nonlinearity on a boundary can recover extended modes in a non-Hermitian tight-binding model. We propose and analyze a one-dimensional minimal model, where we introduce a nonlinear phase shift of a hopping term only at boundary sites. We also propose a possible photonic setup to dynamically observe the nonlinearity-induced extended modes as a steady state. Our results thus challenge the conventional notion of the non-Hermitian skin effect by revealing its dependence on nonlinear boundary conditions.

</details>


### [249] [Classification of spin space groups and crystal magnetic orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33099)
*Zheng Zhang,Peiyuan Wang,Y. X. Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出基于平移完整（translation holonomy）的物理框架，用于枚举与分类自旋空间群（spin space groups, SSGs），并据此将磁序归为17种普适自旋—晶格类型，进而预测四类新物理现象并构建数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSGs代数枚举方法抽象，掩盖了分类结果的物理含义，且未能建立SSGs与实空间磁序之间的直接对应关系。

Method: 以空间平移群在自旋同态（spin homomorphism）下的像——即平移完整（translation holonomy）——为基础；利用SO(3)中三类阿贝尔像（abelian images）划分出三种基本平移类型，并由平移完整约束点群元素对应的自旋操作，从而按物理层级完整枚举SSGs。

Result: 平移完整将SSGs与磁序归为17种普适自旋—晶格类型（universal spin-lattice types），各有明确的布拉格峰几何与中子衍射极化特征；框架直接预测四项普适现象：特定类型的精确磁化补偿、非对易布里渊区（noncommuting Brillouin zones）出现的精确群论判据、八极矩（octupoles）消失的新型3Q磁序、以及反射交错共面自旋晶格保护的严格共线动量空间自旋织构。

Conclusion: 该框架在物理上组织已知磁序并具备预测能力，同时按此物理层级构建了自旋空间群数据库（spin space group database）。

Abstract: Spin space groups (SSGs) describe magnetic crystals in the limit of negligible spin-orbit coupling, where spatial and spin rotations decouple. Although algebraic enumerations of SSGs exist, their abstract mathematical approaches obscure the physical meaning of the resulting classifications, leaving the direct correspondence between SSGs and real-space magnetic orders unestablished. Here, we present a physically motivated framework for enumerating and classifying SSGs based on their translation holonomy---the image of the spatial lattice translation group under the spin homomorphism. The three classes of abelian images in $SO(3)$ divide SSGs into three basic translation types. Translation holonomy constrains the spin operations associated with point-group elements, yielding a complete enumeration of SSGs organized by a physical hierarchy. Within this framework, translation holonomy classifies SSGs and magnetic orders into 17 universal spin-lattice types, each with unambiguous Bragg-peak geometries and polarization signatures in neutron diffraction. Beyond organizing known magnetic orders, this framework directly predicts four universal physical phenomena: (i) exact magnetization compensation enforced by certain spin-lattice types; (ii) an exact group-theoretic criterion for the emergence of noncommuting Brillouin zones; (iii) a new type of 3Q magnetic order with vanishing octupoles; and (iv) the realization of strictly collinear momentum-space spin textures protected by reflection-staggered coplanar spin lattices. We also construct a spin space group database organized according to this physical hierarchy.

</details>


### [250] [Topological insulators on site-eliminated, projected, and patterned fractal lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33155)
*Aiden Mains,Danile Salib,Christopher Leong,Bitan Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文将分数（Hausdorff）维度的分形晶格视为母晶体的几何后裔，通过三种互补方法在康托集、六角雪花、谢尔宾斯基地毯和门格尔海绵上实现了SSH、Haldane、Qi-Wu-Zhang及强Z2拓扑绝缘相，拓展了分形晶格中拓扑态的实现途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在将整数维度母晶体中的标准拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）推广至分数维分形晶格，并系统比较多种构造方法的可行性与适用范围，以补充和扩展此前在地毯上实现Qi-Wu-Zhang拓扑的工作。

Method: 提出三种方法：(a) 格点消除法（site elimination），直接移除不属于分形网络的母格点构造分形哈密顿量；(b) 投影/重整化法（projection or renormalization），积掉或投影掉相应格点得到有效哈密顿量；(c) 图案化分形晶格（patterned fractal lattices），保留母格点但在分形网络及其补集上赋予质量参数两个拓扑不同的值。随后通过数值对角化及拓扑不变量/局域标记计算，并用无能隙边界模式验证体边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）。

Result: 三种方法均在康托集（Cantor set）上实现Su-Schrieffer-Heeger拓扑；六角雪花（hexaflake）上用方法(a)(b)实现Haldane拓扑；谢尔宾斯基地毯（Sierpiński carpet）上用方法(c)实现Qi-Wu-Zhang拓扑；在拼贴门格尔海绵（tiled Menger sponge）上，三种方法均实现强Z2拓扑绝缘相。

Conclusion: 结果表明分形晶格可作为承载多种标准拓扑相的通用平台，不同构造方法各有适用场景，并显著扩展了此前仅在地毯上用(a)(b)实现Qi-Wu-Zhang拓扑的结论。

Abstract: Identifying fractal lattices of fractional (Hausdorff) dimension as geometric descendants of parent crystals, we showcase the successful realization of canonical topological insulators in one-, two-, and three-dimensional crystals on their self-similar descents using three complementary approaches, starting from lattice-regularized models in integer dimensions. (a) In the first approach, we construct the fractal Hamiltonian by removing all parent-lattice sites that do not belong to such a network (method of site elimination). (b) In the second one, we integrate or project out the same set of sites to obtain an effective Hamiltonian for the fractal lattice (method of projection or renormalization). (c) In the third approach, we retain the parent-crystal lattice intact but spatially pattern the on-site mass parameter, which controls the topology of the parent insulating system, such that it assumes two topologically distinct values on the fractal network and its complement (patterned fractal lattices). By numerically diagonalizing the resulting Hamiltonians and computing appropriate topological invariants or local markers, corroborating the bulk-boundary correspondence through gapless modes at outer or inner boundaries, we establish Su-Schrieffer-Heeger topology on the Cantor set using all three approaches, Haldane topology on the hexaflake [using (a) and (b)] and Qi-Wu-Zhang topology on the Sierpiński carpet [using (c)], and strong $Z_2$ topological insulating phases on the tiled Menger sponge from methods (a), (b), and (c). These findings complement and substantially extend the previously reported realization of Qi-Wu-Zhang topology on the Sierpiński carpet using methods (a) and (b) [Phys. Rev. B 110, L241302 (2024)].

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [Entanglement between distant spins in a polarized magnet forms a $d$-wave clover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32190)
*Alan Zhang,Ye Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）使极化磁体成为虚自旋翻转对（virtual spin-flip pairs）的源，这些虚对可纠缠远处自旋；在自旋-1/2 方格铁磁体（square-lattice ferromagnet）中，领头阶（leading order）下证明纠缠呈 d 波反平方律（d-wave inverse-square law），幅度为自旋波（spin-wave）幅度的 1.376 倍，纠缠区域为三叶草形（clover-shaped），且每个自旋的纠缠完全为两两形式（pairwise）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示自旋轨道耦合如何将极化磁体转变为虚自旋翻转对的来源，并由此使空间上分离的自旋产生纠缠。耦合对称性决定角度分布模式，而翻转残留在约自旋轨道角倒数（inverse spin-orbit angle）的距离上终止纠缠。

Method: 针对自旋-1/2 方格铁磁体，在领头阶（leading order）框架下进行解析证明，考察由自旋轨道耦合诱导的虚自旋翻转对所产生的远距离自旋纠缠。

Result: 证明得到 d 波反平方律（d-wave inverse-square law），纠缠幅度为自旋波（spin-wave）幅度的 1.376 倍；纠缠区域呈三叶草形（clover-shaped）；在无 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 项（Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya term）时结果精确成立；且每个自旋的纠缠完全为两两形式（pairwise）。

Conclusion: 四个量子比特（qubit）即可在现有误差率（error rates）下检验 d 波符号（d-wave signs），为该理论预言提供可行实验验证途径。

Abstract: Spin-orbit coupling makes a polarized magnet a source of virtual spin-flip pairs. These pairs entangle distant spins: the symmetry of the coupling fixes the angular pattern, and the flips they leave behind end it at a distance of order the inverse spin-orbit angle. For a spin-1/2 square-lattice ferromagnet we prove, at leading order, a d-wave inverse-square law, 1.376 times the spin-wave amplitude, a clover-shaped entangled region, exact without the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya term, and that each spin's entanglement is entirely pairwise. Four qubits can test the d-wave signs at present error rates.

</details>


### [252] [The role of composition rules in an axiomatic derivation of quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32237)
*Erkka Haapasalo,Pekka Lahti,Juha-Pekka Pellonpää*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文重新审视量子力学信息论公理化中“局部可区分性（local discriminability）”与“纯化（purification）”两条公理，通过形式化复合规则为联合态空间与效应空间的张量积结构提供更严谨的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D'Ariano 等人的公理化中，复合系统理论直接假定为张量积（tensor product）结构，但缺乏对形式复合规则（formal composition rules）的细致研究，即局部效应如何嵌入复合效应空间、联合态如何约化到子系统。该文旨在弥补这一基础性缺口，并延续 Matolcsi（1975）、Aerts 与 Daubechies（1978）及 Beltrametti–Cassinelli（1981）的工作。

Method: 形式化定义局部测量与局部可区分性公理，并重写纯化公理为更弱的形式；同时分析态与效应空间的形式复合规则，考察嵌入映射（embedding map）与投影张量积（projective tensor product）、单射张量积（injective tensor product）的关系，并讨论多分复合（multipartite composition）公理及结合性（associativity）要求。

Result: 经重构后，态空间的嵌入映射可通过局部态空间的投影张量积分解（factors through），效应空间的嵌入映射可通过局部效应空间的单射张量积分解，从而更接近恰当的联合态与效应张量积结构；弱化形式的纯化公理被认为对未来公理化工作具有潜在价值。

Conclusion: 将复合系统建立在更严谨的形式复合规则之上，可加强局部可区分性与纯化公理的表述基础，并为联合态与效应空间的真正张量积结构以及多分复合、结合性问题的处理提供更合适的框架。

Abstract: Local discriminability and purification are two key axioms in an information-theoretic axiomatization of quantum mechanics as proposed by G. M. D'Ariano et al. (Quantum Theory from First Principles, 2017). Both of them refer to compound systems, the theory of which is rather directly assumed to be given by the tensor product structure. This structure, though well understood, should, however, be based on a more careful study of formal composition rules describing how local effects can be embedded into the compound effect space and how to reduce joint states onto subsystems. In that we follow the earlier work of T. Matolcsi, (Acta Sci. Math., 1975), and D. Aerts and I. Daubechies, (Helvetica Physica Acta, 1978), as summarized, e.g., in The Logic of Quantum Mechanics, 1981, by E. Beltrametti and G. Cassinelli. We also establish axioms of local discrimination stating that compound states can be told apart by local measurements the notion of which we also formally define. The purification axioms first introduced as axioms of quantum mechanics by D'Ariano et al. are also rewritten in a weaker form which we believe could be fruitful for future axiomatizations. These reformulations bring us closer to proper tensor product structures for joint state and effect spaces, so that the embedding map for states factors through the projective tensor product of the local state spaces and the embedding map for effects factors through the injective tensor product of the local effect spaces. We also discuss the case of multipartite composition axioms and associativity properties required in these settings.

</details>


### [253] [Ground-State Chiral Quantum Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31370)
*Matteo Massaro,Adolfo del Campo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出“基态手性量子团簇（ground-state chiral quantum clusters）”这一自束缚多体基态，可实现平衡态下的持续电流，为平衡态量子时间晶体（quantum time crystals）提供理论与可解微观实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子时间晶体（quantum time crystals）将晶序概念推广至时间维度；非平衡版本已通过周期性驱动实现，但平衡态对应物仍难以实现，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建描述该类态的最小场论（minimal field theory），并在环上吸引型任意子 Lieb-Liniger 模型（attractive anyonic Lieb-Liniger model）中给出精确微观实现。

Result: 在该模型中，基态密度-密度关联函数（density-density correlator）展现 Talbot 效应（Talbot effect）：孤子状分布（soliton-like profile）周期性弥散与复现，同时沿环无限漂移，从而在平衡态实现持续运动（persistent motion）。

Conclusion: 平衡态下可存在支持持续电流的自束缚基态，为平衡态量子时间晶体提供了候选实现，并讨论了可能的实验探测方案。

Abstract: Quantum time crystals are states of matter that spontaneously break time translation symmetry, extending the notion of crystalline order to time. While their out-of-equilibrium version has been successfully realized by periodic driving, the equilibrium counterpart remains elusive. Here we introduce "ground-state chiral quantum clusters": self-bound many-body ground states supporting a persistent current. We provide a minimal field theory describing these states, and an exact microscopic realization via the attractive anyonic Lieb-Liniger model on a ring. In the latter, the ground-state density-density correlator exhibits the Talbot effect, with a soliton-like profile that periodically disperses and revives while drifting indefinitely along the ring, realizing persistent motion at equilibrium. Possible experimental probes are discussed.

</details>


### [254] [Augmented Imaginary-Time Evolution of Neural Quantum States with Implicit Time Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32236)
*Maksymilian Kliczkowski,Thomas D. Kühne,Fedor Šimkovic,Prachi Sharma,Carlos L. Benavides-Riveros,Werner Dobrautz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将隐式欧拉步与伽辽金投影结合，提出隐式随机重配置方案，并据此构造隐式增强虚时演化（AITE），在神经量子态优化中实现大而稳定的步长与能量误差的渐近二次收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解量子多体系统基态是物理与量子化学的核心难题；虚时演化（ITE）收敛通常仅为渐近式，而变分方法（尤其基于随机重配置优化的神经量子态）受变分流形几何、ITE收敛性质与有限步显式积分稳定性制约，亟需加速且稳定的优化途径。

Method: 先结合隐式欧拉步（implicit Euler step）与伽辽金投影（Galerkin projection）推导隐式随机重配置（SR）方案，以允许大而稳定的步长；再基于该方案构造隐式版本的增强虚时演化（AITE），并在变分投影与有限步积分条件下分析其收敛性，同时考察有限采样情形。

Result: 隐式SR方案显著优于传统方法；隐式AITE的加速效应在变分投影与有限步积分下依然保持，能量误差呈渐近二次收敛；在有限采样下隐式方案仍带来稳定增益，且隐式AITE的优势随条件增强而愈发明显。

Conclusion: 利用能量景观的局部几何信息可加速神经量子态基态优化；隐式积分框架在理论上保证了加速性与二次收敛，并在实际有限采样场景下具备一致优势，为NQS优化提供了稳定高效的新路径。

Abstract: Finding the ground states of quantum many-body systems remains a central challenge in physics and quantum chemistry. Imaginary-time evolution (ITE) offers a natural, widely used route, but its convergence is typically asymptotic. Variational approaches restrict this evolution to parameterized manifolds, with neural quantum states (NQS) providing flexible representations commonly optimized via stochastic reconfiguration (SR). Their performance depends on the geometry of the variational manifold, the convergence properties of ITE, and the stability of finite-step explicit (forward) integrators. By exploiting local geometric information of the energy landscape, the recently introduced augmented imaginary-time evolution (AITE) can, in principle, reach the ground state in finite imaginary time. To exploit the accelerated convergence of AITE in NQS optimization, we first combine an implicit Euler step with Galerkin projection to derive an implicit SR scheme that permits large, stable steps and substantially outperforms conventional methods. We use it to formulate an implicit version of AITE and show that its acceleration persists under variational projection and finite-step integration with asymptotically quadratic convergence of the energy error. With finite sampling, implicit schemes yield consistent gains, and implicit AITE becomes increasingly advantageous.

</details>


### [255] [Electron spin relaxation and coherence times of silicon vacancies in 4H-SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32451)
*Irene Donà,Michele Segantini,Gianluca Marcozzi,Nguyen Tien Son,Takeshi Ohshima,Ha Duong Ngo,Boris Naydenov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用电子顺磁共振（EPR）表征了4H-SiC中硅空位色心（V1、V2，S=3/2）在80–300 K的电子自旋性质，测得自旋浓度、相干时间T₂与弛豫时间T₁，并指出退相干主要源于核自旋与电子自旋扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石与碳化硅（SiC）中的色心是量子比特（qubit）与量子传感的有力候选，SiC因与现有半导体工艺兼容而尤具吸引力，需系统表征其硅空位色心的自旋性质。

Method: 采用电子顺磁共振（electron paramagnetic resonance, EPR），包括连续波EPR测量与T₁、T₂测量，在T=80–300 K范围内针对中心谱线与卫星谱线开展分析。

Result: 获得自旋浓度c_V1=1.47±0.3×10¹⁴、c_V2=5.6±1.12×10¹³、c_X=1.03±0.21×10¹³ spins/cm³；卫星跃迁的T₁与T₂长于中心跃迁，在80 K时最大值分别为42.8±1.3 ms与0.708±0.004 ms。

Conclusion: 卫星谱线具有更长的弛豫与相干时间，退相干（decoherence）主要由核自旋扩散与电子自旋扩散（spin diffusion）导致。

Abstract: Color defect centers carrying electron spins in solid crystals like diamond and silicon carbide (SiC) are promising candidates for qubits and quantum sensors, with SiC material being particularly interesting due to its compatibility with the existing semiconductor technology. Here we investigated the electron spin properties of silicon vacancy centers (V1 and V2) both electron spin $S = 3/2$ in 4H-SiC in the temperature range $T=80-300$~K using electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR). The EPR spectrum shows central and satellite lines, where the former ones originate from V1 and V2 centers, as well as from unknown defect center X. The analysis of continuous wave EPR measurements revealed a spin concentration of $c_{\mathrm{V1}}=1.47\pm0.3\times10^{14}$, $c_{\mathrm{V2}}=5.6\pm1.12\times10^{13}$ and $c_{\mathrm{X}}=1.03\pm0.21\times10^{13}$ spins/cm$^{3}$. Additionally, electron spin coherence $T_2$ and relaxation times $T_1$ have been measured on the central and on the satellite spectral lines, resulting in longer $T_1$ and $T_2$ for the satellite transition, compared to the central one, with maximum values of $42.8\pm1.3$~ms and $0.708\pm0.004$~ms respectively at $T=80$~K. We reveal, that decoherence is mainly caused by nuclear and electron spin diffusion.

</details>


### [256] [Trotterized hybrid qubit-boson simulation of the Jaynes-Cummings-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32895)
*Nabaraj Khatri,Dilip Ojha,Rajib Tripathi,Kismat K. C,Ashish Khanal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于Bosonic Qiskit与矩阵乘积态（MPS）后端设计Trotter化量子线路，对Jaynes-Cummings-Hubbard（JCH）模型的实时动力学进行模拟，涵盖单极化激元输运、双激发扇区及双分纠缠熵分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JCH模型可描述耦合光腔中光与物质的相互作用，是研究光-物质量子动力学的框架；作者希望验证混合量子比特-玻色子线路结合矩阵乘积态方法研究光-物质晶格动力学的可行性，并为更复杂的相互作用与开放光-物质系统提供数值基准。

Method: 采用Trotter化量子线路（Trotterized quantum circuit），借助Bosonic Qiskit与矩阵乘积态（Matrix Product State, MPS）后端模拟JCH模型的实时动力学；将单极化激元（polariton）输运的格点占据与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）结果对比，并在硬核光子（hard-core photon）约束下的双激发（two-excitation）扇区进一步模拟，考察激发输运与对称性质，最后分析双分纠缠熵（bipartite entanglement entropy）对腔间光子跃迁强度（photon-hopping strength）的依赖。

Result: 单极化激元输运的格点占据与精确对角化结果进行了基准比对；双激发扇区中考察了激发输运与对称性质；并给出双分纠缠熵随腔间光子跃迁强度的变化关系。摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，用矩阵乘积态模拟的混合量子比特-玻色子线路可用于研究光-物质晶格动力学，并为将混合模拟方法推广至更复杂的相互作用与开放光-物质系统提供了数值基准。

Abstract: The Jaynes-Cummings-Hubbard (JCH) model describes the interaction of light and matter in coupled optical cavities and provides a framework for studying their quantum dynamics. In this work, we design a Trotterized quantum circuit to investigate the real-time dynamics of the JCH model using Bosonic Qiskit and a Matrix Product State (MPS) backend. We simulate single-polariton transport and benchmark site occupations against exact diagonalization results. We then extend the simulations to the two-excitation sector under a hard-core photon constraint, examining excitation transport and symmetry properties. Finally, we study the dependence of bipartite entanglement entropy on the inter-cavity photon-hopping strength. The results illustrate how hybrid qubit-boson circuits, simulated with matrix product states, can be used to study light-matter lattice dynamics. This work provides a numerical benchmark for extending hybrid simulation approaches to more complex interacting and open light-matter systems.

</details>


### [257] [Dirac Propagation for Simulating Fermionic Observables under Particle-Number-Conserving Unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32902)
*G Arnav Naidu,Matteo D'Anna,Adrián Pérez-Salinas,Jannes Nys,Juan Carrasquilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出“狄拉克传播（Dirac propagation）”这一经典模拟方法，用于在粒子数守恒幺正演化下计算费米子体系的局域可观测量，并给出严格可截断的算符展开界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用费米子（interacting fermions）的动力学模拟是物理学与化学中的核心计算难题，亟需可行的经典模拟框架。

Method: 基于算符展开（operator expansion）的紧凑表示，证明权重超过总粒子数的字符串可在不影响最终期望值的前提下精确丢弃；并给出相互作用门（interacting gates）的显式表达式，同时精确处理粒子数守恒的费米子高斯（Gaussian）与克利福德（Clifford）幺正操作。

Result: 在固定粒子数下，该精确截断（exact cutoff）使保留字符串数随模式数呈多项式增长，且对相互作用动力学同样成立。

Conclusion: 该框架为费米子多体动力学模拟及费米子量子电路（fermionic quantum circuits）的经典基准测试（classical benchmarking）提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Simulating the dynamics of interacting fermions remains a central computational challenge across physics and chemistry. We introduce Dirac propagation, a classical method that simulates local observables in fermionic systems subject to particle-number-conserving unitaries. We show that, in the operator expansion, strings with weights above the total particle number can be discarded without incurring any error in the final expectation value. This exact cutoff bounds the number of retained strings polynomially in the number of modes at fixed particle number, including for interacting dynamics. The formalism combines a compact representation with explicit expressions for interacting gates and exact treatment of number-conserving fermionic Gaussian and Clifford unitaries. This framework offers an approach to simulating fermionic many-body dynamics and classically benchmarking fermionic quantum circuits.

</details>


### [258] [Robust growth of $α$-tantalum on sapphire at room temperature using atomically thin titanium seed layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33112)
*Anand Kamlapure,Lucas Hak,Kishore Kumar Thiyagarajan,Hozanna Miro,Marc Beekman,Matvey Finkel,Leonardo DiCarlo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出以超薄 Ti 种子层（seed layer）在室温下于蓝宝石（sapphire）衬底上生长 α 相钽（α-Ta）薄膜的稳健路线，并将其微波损耗性能与高温生长、Nb 种子层方案进行对比，获得与当前最优水平相当的 TLS 限制品质因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钽基超导电路（superconducting circuits）因低微波损耗（microwave loss）受到关注，但高质量 α-Ta 薄膜的可重复生长困难，因其性质对生长条件高度敏感，亟需拓宽可及沉积参数空间（deposition parameter space）的可靠工艺。

Method: 采用超薄 Ti 种子层在蓝宝石衬底上室温生长 α-Ta，并与无种子层高温生长、室温 Nb 种子层生长两种方案对比；通过结构与表面表征，以及在量子区（quantum regime）测量的共面波导（coplanar-waveguide, CPW）谐振器量化微波损耗，并进行变温测量。

Result: 所得 CPW 谐振器的两能级系统（two-level-system, TLS）限制品质因子（quality factor）与相似几何结构的 Ta-on-sapphire 器件当前最优水平相当；变温测量显示高温生长薄膜及经延长缓冲氧化物刻蚀（buffered-oxide etch）处理的薄膜存在额外温度激活损耗（temperature activated loss）。

Conclusion: 超薄种子层工程（seed-layer engineering）可作为实现可重复、低损耗 α-Ta 薄膜的可靠替代路线，并提示需进一步研究主导 Ta 相形成的界面机制（interfacial mechanisms）。

Abstract: Tantalum (Ta) based superconducting circuits have attracted attention for their low microwave loss. However, reproducible growth of high-quality $α$-phase Ta films remains challenging because film properties are highly sensitive to growth conditions. We present a robust, room-temperature route for $α$-Ta growth on sapphire substrates using an ultrathin Ti seed layer, which significantly widens the accessible deposition parameter space. We compare this approach to Ta films grown at high temperature without a seed layer and at room temperature with a Nb seed. We assess films using structural and surface characterization and quantify microwave loss with coplanar-waveguide (CPW) resonators measured in the quantum regime. We obtain two-level-system (TLS)-limited quality factors comparable to the state of the art for Ta-on-sapphire CPW resonators with similar geometries. In addition, temperature-dependent measurements reveal that high-temperature-grown films and films subjected to extended buffered-oxide etch treatment exhibit additional temperature activated loss, highlighting the role of growth route and post-processing in microwave performance. Our results establish ultrathin seed-layer engineering as a reliable alternative route to reproducible, low-loss $α$-Ta films and motivate closer study of the interfacial mechanisms that govern Ta phase formation.

</details>

### [259] [Emission regimes and characteristic energy scales in reverse-biased 4H-SiC Schottky barriers: unified field-emission formulas and energy-dependent tunneling effective mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32146)
*Tetsuo Hatakeyama*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文以费米-狄拉克供给与WKB透射的电流积分框架分析4H-SiC肖特基势垒二极管的反向漏电流，给出三角形势垒与抛物形势垒下的闭式场发射（FE）与热电子场发射（TFE）表达式，并通过Airy能量统一界定热电子-隧穿转变及TFE/FE边界。所提表达式与WKB电流偏差在15%和2%以内，并借助能量相关隧穿质量将Ti/4H-SiC数据的拟合残差由0.17降至0.11-0.12 dex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Padovani-Stratton公式在计算4H-SiC肖特基势垒二极管反向漏电时与WKB基准存在显著偏差，且TFE公式的适用性与TFE/FE转变判据缺乏对任意势垒形状通用的表述，因此需要更精确且可推广的解析模型。

Method: 采用含费米-狄拉克供给（Fermi-Dirac supply）与WKB透射（WKB transmission）的电流积分方法，以界面场与临界场$F_c$之比确定TFE鞍点深度，引入Airy能量刻画热电子-隧穿转变及TFE/FE边界（推广为$c_1 k_B T \approx 1$），并推导三角形势垒与抛物形势垒的闭式FE与TFE表达式，再与Ti/4H-SiC实测数据比对。

Result: 对抛物形势垒，Padovani-Stratton公式给出的电流为WKB电流的1.4-7.9倍（FE，$10^{19}$ cm$^{-3}$）与2.5-3.6倍（TFE，低掺杂层，300-433 K），而本文表达式分别吻合至15%与2%以内。应用于Ti/4H-SiC数据（30-160 °C，50-950 V）时，基于复能带结构的能量相关隧穿质量（energy-dependent tunneling mass）使残差由0.17降至0.11-0.12 dex。

Conclusion: 所提基于WKB电流积分的闭式表达式较Padovani-Stratton公式更准确地描述4H-SiC肖特基势垒二极管的反向漏电，Airy能量可作为统一判据推广至任意势垒形状；能量相关隧穿质量可经由Franz两带色散（Franz two-band dispersion）在器件仿真中实现。

Abstract: Reverse leakage in 4H-SiC Schottky barrier diodes is analyzed using a current integral with Fermi-Dirac supply and WKB transmission. The ratio of the interface field to a critical field $F_c$ sets the depth of the thermionic-field-emission (TFE) saddle point. The Airy energy distinguishes the thermionic-tunneling crossover, the validity of the TFE formula, and the TFE/field-emission (FE) boundary, which generalizes to $c_1 k_B T \approx 1$ for arbitrary barrier shapes. Closed-form FE and TFE expressions are given for triangular and quadratic barriers; for the latter, the Padovani-Stratton formulas give 1.4-7.9 times (FE, $10^{19}$ cm$^{-3}$) and 2.5-3.6 times (TFE, low-doped layers, 300-433 K) the WKB current, whereas the present expressions agree within 15% and 2%. Applied to Ti/4H-SiC data (30-160 °C, 50-950 V), an energy-dependent tunneling mass from the complex band structure reduces the residual from 0.17 to 0.11-0.12 dex; it can be implemented in device simulation via the Franz two-band dispersion.

</details>
